Bissimillahi rahmanin rahim

Assalamu alaikum.

Ya ubangiji kabani ikon rubuta daidai acikin wannan gajeran labarin da zanbayar Allah kaba mutane ikon ammafani da halaye masu kyau da suke ciki kasa su watsar da munanan da yake ciki, kasan cewar d'an Adam ajizine ba lallai labarina ya nishid'antar da wasu ba amma ina rokon duk wanda na bakanta masa rai sakamakon wannan labarin ya yafemun.


Masoya na nice dai takunnan Mmn Ashfaq nagode da d'unbin soyayyan ku gareni, bansan inada masoya haka ba saida na sauka a online darirrikan mutane sukayita kirana domin jin lafiya ta, to Alhamdulillahi gani nadawo muku da sabon littafina mai suna TAFEE JININA fararrar labari ne wanda ya faru a cikin garin Bauchi, ina fatan masu hali munana da suke cikin littafin Allah ya basu ikon gyara halin su masu kyawawan ciki kuma Allah yasa su dad'a akan wanda suke dashi, ina fatan wannan gajeran labarima zai nishadantar da fadakar daku ayi karatu lfy.



🅿️1\2
Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un! Allahumma ajirni fimusubati, Allah munason sa amma kifimu sonsa Malam Allah ya jikanka da rahama ubangiji ya raya abunda muka aifa, kafin ta share hawayen ta tace Faisal tashi kaje gidan Baffan ku Tukur kace mishi Allah yayiwa Malam rasuwa sannan kabiya gidansu Goggo Larai itama ka fad'a mata"

Wanda aka kira da Faisal yana kuka ya tashi ya futa sannan ta dubi dayan d'anta tace yi hakuri Fa'izu kamishi addu'a danshi yake bukata a garemu yanzu, tashi kaje gidan Sarkin kasuwa da gidan su Malam Hamza ka fad'a musu Allah yayiwa Malam rasuwa, shima ya tashi ya futa yana kuka, saida taga futansu kafin ta saki kuka maicin rai afili tace ashe haka mutuwan miji yake da zafi wayyo rayuwa Allah ka dubemu ta tashi taje ta dauko wayanta Nokia mai torchi ta lalimo number y'an uwanta ta shaida musu rasuwan Malam kafin ta kikkira wa'inda takeda number su suma, kafin kace mai gidan ya cika da mutane kowa ya tausaya musu tare da yaban halin Malam din.

Faisal yana shiga gidan Baffa Tukur kukan shi ya karu Baffa Tukur yana daki ya juyo kaman kuka a tsakar gida da sauri suka futo da matar sa har yana tuntube da buta ganin Faisal yana kuka shima saiya saka kuka yana cewa shikenan wanda yafimu sonsa ya dauki abunsa! Allah ya jikanka Lawan ayyukan ka nagari subika kai kade nake gani naji dadi kaima katafi kabarni, matansa ne tayita basu hakuri kafin suka futa suna tafi yana kiran yan uwansu da suke kauyen dunduma.

Zasu wuce ta kofan gidan su Goggo Larai, Faisal yace Baffa bazanje na fad'a mata" kai kawai Baffa Tukur ya iya daga masa, da sallama ya shiga gidan ya sameta tana surfen gero a tsakar gida tana ganinsa ta tsaya da dakan da takeyi tace yau kuma da wanne akazo mun?

Idonsa na zubar da hawaye muryan shi a shake irinna wanda yaci kuka yace "Allah yayiwa Malam rasuwa"

Mai makon tayi salati sai tace ahayye kace ya tafi yabar matar so, to mu da aka mayar damu Matan k'i zamu hadu da masoyan mu.

Bai iya cigaba da tsayuwa ba danji yake maganan ta zai iya fasa masa zuciya ya juyo ya futa da gudu, a zaure yaga Baffa Tukur yana jiransa kasan cewar yaji duk abunda take fad'a ya girgiza masa kai ido na zubar da hawaye shi kansa yasan dan uwansa baiyi dace da macen kwarai ba, suka kama hanyan komawa gida.

Suna shiga gidan yayi dakin da gawan yake hankali a tashe, yayi kokarin danne kukansa kafin yama dan uwansa addu'an samun rahman Ubangiji ya koma gefe ya zauna ido na zubar da hawaye.


Mai unguwa yazo ya tsaya a gefen Umma Hajara yace kiyi hakura Hajara ki mishi addu'a shiyafi bukata a wajen mu a halin yanzu, yasake gyara tsayuwan sa yace kin sanar da abokiyar zamanki rasuwan mijin naku?

Cikin kuka tace eh natura Faisal ya fad'a mata dazu, amma naga shiru har yanzu bata shigo ba, mai anguwa yace Allah ya kyauta, Malam Hamza ya futo daga dakin da gawan yake yace Hajara zaki iya masa wanka?

Tace "zan iya Malam amma tare da taimakon ka"

Yace masha Allah kafin ya dubi su Faisal yace kun amince nama ma'aifinku wanka?

Suka daga masa kai halamun amincewa kafin yace bissimillah Hajara suka shiga dakin a tare daga shi sai ita da Bappa Tukur suke masa wanka, ana mishi wanka Goggo Larai ta shigo da y'ayanta tana hura hanci tace ai dole a turamun mutuwarsa tunda yanzu ya gama amfani amma lokacin da yake raye an kamashi an killace to meye amfanin sanar dani tunda ni banida amfani.

Sanin halinta da mutanen anguwan sukayi yasa ba wanda ya kulata, sai y'ayanta ne suke tace tayi shuru ta daina magana duk da suma suna cikin tashin hankalin rasa ma'aifin su kowa a wajen yana kuka amma banda Goggo Larai wanda taketa cika tana batsewa, har aka gama masa wanka kafin aka kira iyalansa sukazo sukayi bankwana, amma fur Goggo Larai taki shiga dan tace ba taga amfanin ban kwanan da zasuyi ba.

Haka akazo aka ratsa dashi ta cikin yan uwa da abokan arziki kowa kagani a wajen mutuwan Malam ya girgiza shi, akaje aka masa sallah aka kaishi gidansa na gaskiya ( Allahu Akbar Allah kasa muyi kyakykyawan karshe, wanda zamu tafi ana fad'an abubuwan alkairinmu  Ameen ya Allah ).

A kofan gidan Umma Hajara ake karb'an zaman makokin, duk wanda ya shigo sai yayi Allah wadar da hali irinna Goggo Larai.

Bayan anyi addu'an uku Goggo Larai tasamu yan uwan Malam tace inada bukatan a raba mana gado yau ko gobe "

Malam Hamza da mamaki da al'ajabi suka rufeshi saiya kasa magana idonsa yanata tsiyayan hawaye yace "haba Larai yaushe akayi daren har gari ya waye yaufa dududu kwanan Malam uku ne da barin gidan duniyan nan amma ki fara mana maganan rabon gado.. bata bari ya gama maganan saba ta tari nunfashin sa da cewa "ko yanzu Malam yabar duniyan inada hakkin tumulun takaba ta akansa dan haka dole kucire mun nawa dana y'ayana ehe.

Umma Hajara da take gefe tanajin duk abunda suke cewa ta taso tace " Malam Hamza dan Allah kuyi hakuri kuyi abunda tace koba komai an sauk'e wa Malam nauyin da yake kansa"

Baffa Tukur yace "banji mamakin abunda Larai take cewa ba dan nasan zatayi abunda yafi hakama."

Malam Hamza ya jinjina kai kafin yace shikenan zamu duba zuwa gobe insha Allahu, yanzu Larai ki tafi gidan ku zamu nemai ki.

Ta daga murya kaman mai shirin isarda sako zuwa waje tace ai idan kunga na futa a gidan nan to ku tabbatar kun gama rabon nanne, karku mayar dani shashasha mana duk da banyi arabi da boko ba amma kwakwalwa ta tanaja.

Suka watse sukabar ta a wajen kowa da kunan abunda tayi a zuciyar sa, y'ayanta ne suka taso suka sameta....


Mmn Ashfaq

Bissimillahi rahmanin rahim

Barkanmu da Juma'a

🅿️3\4
Ibarahim yace haba Goggo wannan ai abun kunya zaki bar mana ace uwarmu tana masifan rabon gado tun ran ukun Ma'aifin mu, da wani ido kikeso mu kalli jama'a... Jummai ta cafke da cewa wannan abun kunyan dame yayi kama fissabilillahi ai saiki bari idan anyi arba'in saikiyi maganan gado amma ace mutane na cike a gidan ki bude baki kina daga murya kowa yanajin abunda kike fad'a.

Karamar yarta mai suna Hafsa tace bangane mai kuke nufi ba, kunwani zo kunsata a gaba kaman iyayenta, meye laifinta don tayi maganan hakkin ta da namu wlh ban taba zaton bakuda wayo ba sai yau, kuna ganin yadda Umma Hajara tayi baka baka a cikin gidan nan kaman ba gidan ubanmu ba, idan muka zuba ido nan gaba cewa zatayi Malam yabata gidan ita da y'ayanta kyauta.

Goggo Larai tace yi shuru yar albarka ke kadaice kika fahinci abunda na hango kibarsu suma da sannu zasu gane manufa ta.

Ibrahim yace mun fahinci duk abunda kike nufi Goggo munfi son kibari sai angama watsewa sai muta da maganan, amma idan kikaci gaba dayi a cikin bainan jama'a za'a futa damu, sai kiga kowa na tsoron auran y'ayanki.

Goggo Larai tace sai yanzu na fahince ku, kuma bazan sake magana a gaban kowa ba, bari naga gudun ruwan su idan sunki rabawa saina nuna musu ni Larai ba kanwan lasa bane.
Ku tashi mu shiga dakin mu da mukabar shi da, yanzu kam mundawo kenan zama daram ba wanda ya isa yace mu futa, ta dubi Ibrahim tace kaje gidan mu ka kwaso mana tsummo karan mu duka!

Ya amsa da to kafin suka tashi suka tafi dakinta da tagudu ta barshi, dakin yasha kura kasancewar ba mai shiga ciki suka zage sukayi gyara, shi kuma ya tafi ibo musu kayan,

Washe gari Baffa Tukur ya kira Malam Hamza sukayi kasafin yadda rabon gadon zai kasace, kasancewar gida kawai Malam ya mutu ya bari, jinyan da yayi ya cinye duk gundun azzikin sa da kadarorin daya mallaka a duniya, shiyasa rabon gadon bazai bada wahala ba, suka kira alkali yazo ya duba gidan d'akuna takwas ne ciki da falo guda biyu sai dakuna hudu da bayan gida da kicin sai filin tsakar gida, kuma iyalan sane kawai magadan da iyayensa duk sun mutu.

Ranan bakwai aka tarasu kafin Malam Hamza ya fara bayani da cewa "Alhamdulillah mungode wa Allah daya bamu ikon saukewa dan uwanmu nauyinsa da wuru da fatan zaku bamu hadin kai?"

Goggo Larai tace idan kunyi adalci ba, kunsan dai nice babba ni malam ya fara aura kuma y'ayana sune manya a gidan nan dole rabona yafi yawa ehe.

Alkali yayi gyaran murya yace kowa yasan gado Allah ne ya raba da kansa bawai anabin Babba da yaro bane, dan haka zan rabasa kaman yadda yazo a Qur'anic, nan ya musu bayanin matan sunci dakunan su, sannan dan Goggo Larai yaci daki daya y'ayanta Matan sunci daki daya, sai y'ayan Umma Hajara kowa ya mallaki daki d'addaya.

Nan Goggo Larai tayi tsalle tace abunda bazai tab'a yuyuwa ba kenan taya za'aba Faisal da Fa'izu d'aki d'aki sannan aba Balki da Hafsa daki d'aya kuma rabonsu yazo daidai da Ibrahim da yake Babba a gidan.

Ran alkali ba karamun baci yayi ba ya dubeta yace "idan rabon be miki ba mun baki nan da wata uku ki daukaka k'aran zuwa wani kotun" yana kaiwa nan suka tashi suka futa, Umma Hajara ma taja y'ayanta suka koma daki suna Allah wadar da hali irinna Larai.

Goggo Larai kam saida ta gama zage zagenta kafin suka futa daga gidan ita da y'ayanta, Balkisu ce kawai taki binsu dan ita halinta yasha bambam dana Goggo Larai da yan uwanta, taje tasamu Umma Hajara tace "Umma kiyi hakuri da halin Goggo Larai muyita mata addu'a har Allah yasa ta dawo daidai, ba komai yake dawainiya da itaba sai rashin karatu, kiyi hakuri kina mata uzuri."

Umma Hajara tayi murmushi cikin soyayya da kulawa ta dubi Balkisu tace karki damu y'ata, banasa damuwan Larai a zuciya ta dan nariga dana saba da halinta, kuma kullun ina mata addu'a Allah ya bata ikon gyara halinta" daganan suka tab'a hira da yan uwanta kafin ta tashi ta koma dakinsu gudun kar Goggo  Larai tazo ta sameta anan.

Goggo Larai suna futa basu tsaya ko inaba sai gidansu koda taje ta sanarwa iyayenta irin rabon da aka musu Maman tane kawai ta goyi bayanta kasancewar ma'aifinta nada ilimin addini yasa ya zauna yayi kokarin fahintar da ita amma ganin bazata fahince shiba yasa ya koreta a gidan.

Har wata ukun yayi bata samu kudin dazata daukaka k'ara ba, dole yasa ta hakura amma tadau alwashin saita hana Umma Hajara samun farin cikin rayuwan ta.

Umma Hajara kuwa tunda ta kammala takaban ta tanemi aikatau, takoyi sa'a tasamu da kudin aikin take biya wa y'ayanta kudin makaranta, ba karamun wahala take shaba wajen ganin ta inganta rayuwar y'ayanta.

Kullun garin Allah ya waye sai Goggo Larai da y'ayanta sun sakasu gaba da zagi amma basu kulasu, saukin da tasamu Balki  da Hafsa sunyi aure yanzu.

Karatun su Faisal yayi nisa inda Faisal yanzu yake 300L a university Fa'izu kuma ya gama secondary har yanzu ba'a samu kudin dazai tafi yaci gaba da karatu ba.

Ganin yana da kokari Baban abokinsa ya biya masa yayi diploma, inda Faisal ya had'a degree dinsa, suna zaune duka ba wanda yasamu aikin yi.

Mai anguwa yazo ya samu Umma Hajara yace mata anata cika police maizai hana su gwada sa'an su!

Cikin farin ciki tace nagode mai unguwa yanzu waye d'aya daga cikinsu kake ganin zai cika form din?

Mai anguwa yace "tunda Faisal shine Babba kuma yafi nisan karatu inaga shi ya cika kawai.

Ta mishi godiya koda suka dawo ta sanar dasu ba karamun farin ciki sukayi ba ya tattara komai dangane da karatunsa yakaiwa mai unguwa.

Kafin saka mako ya futo Faisal ya kwanta ciwo, kafansa ya kumbura ko tafiya baya iyayi, suna kwance a asibity sakamako ya futo cikin sa'a kuwa Faisal yana daga cikin wa'inda aka iba.

Hankalinsu  ba karamun tashi yayi ba Faisal yace Umma baza muyi asaran wannan daman ba inaga Fa'izu ya tafi a madadina kawai kinga muna kama inba wanda yasanmu ba bazai gane bani bane.

Sukayi na'am da shawaran sa bayan sati biyu suka tafi training ranan dazai tafi yasha kuka tare da ma dan uwansa addu'an samun sauki, Umma Hajara ta rakasa da addu'a samun nasara tare da kariyan ubangiji.


Mmn Ashfaq

JINJINA BANGIRMA GAREKU
Abubaka AK Saraki
Hassan ATK

Bissimillahi rahmanin rahim

🅿️5\6
Cikin kwarewa da fasaha Fa'izu yake gudanar da training dinsa, lokaci kankani ya shiga zukatan masu horar dasu ganin ya mayar da hankalinsa kan abunda ya kawo sa, sab'anin wasu daga cikin su.

Lokacin da Goggo Larai taji Fa'izu ya tafi training saida tayi karamun hauka,  Ibrahim da kishi ya cika masa zuciya yace Goggo inaga sai kinje har gida kin takawa mai anguwa burki domin naga shine munafikin da yake had'a komai, ina nine Babba a gidan nan meyasa bazai fara turani ba, saiya tura Faisal dayake Allah ba azzalumin bawansa bane saiya cire mana hakkin mu akansu, ya kwanta ciwo shine bai masa ba tsaban munafunci sai suka tura Fa'izu karamun k'anina a boye, to kitashi kije kija masa kunne kice masa ya hanzarta cewa adawo da Fa'izu na maye gurbin sa inba haka ba sai nasa ankashe shi ko kuma na kashe shi da hanuna.

 Goggo Larai tace kabar komai a hanuna ina zuwa yanzu ta futa ta barshi yana huci, har gida taje ta samu mai anguwa tace "mai anguwa nazo na tambayeka ne!

Yace "inajin ki  Larai"

Taci gaba da cewa "inaso ka fad'a mun waye Babba a y'ayan Malam?

Ganin kaman da bala'i tazo masa yasa yayi shuru baice mata komai ba, dan abun kunya ne ace yana fad'a da mace.

Tace "ina tambayan ka kayi mun shuru, to bari kaji wlh ko kunki ko kunso dole Ibrahim shine Babba a gida Malam, kuma ina maija maka kunne tun wuri kaje kasa a dawo da Fa'izu, Ibrahim ya maye gurbin sa, inba haka ba wlh na rantse maka rayuwanka tana cikin sark'ak'iya dan daf kake da barin duniyan.

Har tagama ta futa baice mata komai ba, tana futa ya daga waya ya sanar da d'ansa duk yadda sukayi da Goggo Larai.

Goggo Larai ta koma gida tana bawa Ibrahim labarin yadda sukayi da mai unguwa, suka kwashe da dariya tace damma baka wajen da kaga yadda idonshi ya futo danace masa zansa a kashe shi.

Ibrahim yace ainaso nabiki in fara koya masa hankali, amma bari mubasu yau zuwa gobe muga zaiyi abunda muka umurce sa, kafin Goggo Larai tayi magana sukaji sallaman yan sanda, Ibrahim ya kwashe da dariya yace aikinki yayi kyau Goggo nah kinga harya aikata abunda muka saka shi.

Jin abunda suke cewa yasa yan sandan sukace "munason magana daku amma ba anan ba"

Goggo Larai tace aiko bakuzo ba mu zamuzo, ta shige daki ta tattaro duk kudin da take dashi ta zuba a jaka, ta futo tana kaga duk abunda nake dashi na ibo saboda ba dad' mutum ya tafi horo ba kudi a hanunsa, kuma kudinka ma zaisa ka siye duka masu muku horo ta inda zaka futo da babban girma, wuce muje inyaso daga baya inna dawo zan biyoka da kayan ka."


Suna zuwa Polish station aka wurgasu bayan kanta, kafin akace Goggo Larai tacire hijabinta shi kuma Ibrahim suka cire masa kaya aka barshi daga shi sai gajeran wanda da singileti.

Goggo Larai tace tun daganan zasu fara training din, kuma naga nima kun k'wace duk abunda nazo dashi har takalmina, ni meya had'ani da aikin yan sanda da zaku ajiyeni anan?

Daya daga cikin sune ya bige mata baki yace Baba idan baki mana shuru ba zan miki dukan tsiya anan.

Baya ta koma tana k'unk'uni kafin ta kalli Ibrahim tace ka dube shi dakyau domin idan ka futo a kanshi nakeso kafara hukunci."

Ya girgiza mata kai yace "kiyi shuru kawai Goggo harna yanke irin hukuncin dazan masa bayan mun futo daga training idan beyi wasa bama saina koresa a aiki."

Ganin zasu cikasu da surutu yasa aka kaisu cell wasa wasa saida suka kwashe sati biyu a rufe kullun sau daya ake basu abinci, duk sun galabaita sun futa hayyacinsu, Balkisu da Hafsa basusan abunda yake faruwa ba kasancewar sai sufi wata biyu basuzo wajen Goggon ba.

Umma Hajara kuma suna asibiti tana jinyan danta hankali kwance, dan yanzu Alhamdulillah kafan ya fara sab'ewa sai nauyin da yayi kawai dan har yanzu bai fara takawa ba.

Yau suka cika kwana goma sha tara a rufe ranan ne mai unguwa yazo yasa aka futo dasu ya dubeta yace "Larai har yanzu banga masu kasheni ba kuma bansa andawo da Fa'izu an d'auki d'anki ba.

Ta sake baki tana kallonsa tace bangane me kake nufiba mai unguwa, dama kai kasa aka kamamu amma zalin.....Ibrahim yayi saurin to she mata baki yana cewa kiyi shuru Goggo karki sake sakamu a cikin wani matsalan.

Ya dubi mai unguwa yace "dan girman Allah dan darajan iyayenka kayi hakuri ka yafe mana wlh itama barazana ne kawai, bawai ta fad'a dan zata iya aikatawa bane."


Mai unguwa yayi murmushi kafin yace "yaushe ka dawo bakinta ban saniba kabari ta fad'a mun yaushe zatazo ta kasheni, sai naje nayi shirin karban bakin da zata turo mun"

Goggo Larai da sai yanzu ta fahinci abunda yake nufi ta saka kuka tace kayi hakuri mai unguwa kamana rai wlh bazan sake ba wannan ma kuskuran harshe ne, ni yanzu ko cewa kayi karmu sake futowa kofan gida bazamu sake ba duk umurnin daka kindaya mana shi zamubi.


Yaron mai unguwa yace Baba me kake so ayi musu yanzu?



Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️ 7\8
Saida mai unguwa ya gyara tsayuwan sa ya dubi Ibrahim dakyau yace in bason zuciya ba Ibrahim kaida ko primary baka gama ba taya za'a turaka aikin d'an sanda, ina me baka shawara kacire son zuciya da jahilci irinta ma'aifiyarka inba haka ba wlh rayuwar ka zata kare a dana sani, in kanaso na sama maka aiki ka koma makaranta dan yanzu zamani yazo mana da cigaba kala kala, akwai makarantu na manya ( adult education ) kala kala sai wanda ka zab'a kayi karatu nikuma namaka alkawarin indai ina raye zan sama maka aikin da zaka rike kanka harma da kannan ka, ke kuma Larai kinga kin fara girb'an abunda kika shuka tun yanzu, Allah ya jikan Malam a gabana yasha fad'a miki  yana guje miki rana irinta yau ranan da bakida daman da zaki gyarata, gashi bamuje ko inaba kin fara gani, to wannan ya isheki ishara idan kinga dama ki gyara halinki ko kici gaba zakiga saka mako duk abunda kika shuka.


Ya dubi dansa yace "ofisa nagama zaka iya sallaman su yanzu"

Wanda aka kira da ofisa yace "Babah ofishin dan sanda ba kaman ka shiga gida ka futa bane, dole saimun rubuta report akansu sannan a rubuta takaddan yar jejeniyan duk abunda ya sameka nan da shekara hamsin sune."

Mai unguwa yayi dariya yace Hamisu ofisa taya zankai shekara hamsin gaba, amma bazan hanaka aikin kaba kuyi duk inda ya dace, nan take akayi rubutu Goggo Larai da Ibrahim suka sa hanu, kafin mai unguwa yayi tafiyan sa.

Tace sai kubani kudina da kayana ko, tunda an gama mana muguntan"

Hamisu ya dubeta yace "lallai Matan nan zuciyar ki ta kone amma ina miki gudun ranan da zata had'a da gangan jikin ki su kone kurumus ranan nadaman ki bazai taba yi miki amfani ba" ya wuce office dinsa ya barta da yaran sa a wajen dan yana da tabbacin zasuyi maganinta kafun ta futa.

Suna ganin Ogansu ya shiga kofur Emanuel yace mai kikace?

Ganin ba imani a fuskan su yasa taja bakinta tayi shuru dama tana magana ne dan taga yaron mai unguwa a wajen ne.

Suka dauko jakanta suka bude ganin kudi yasa sukayi dariya kafin suka futo dashi naira dubu arba'in da uku suka kirga, saida suka kasafta kudin kafin suka wurga mata jakanta sukace mun cire kudin belinku dan haka ku iyaba kayanku zaku iya tafiya.

Itadai Goggo Larai bata da bakin magana tayi shuru tare da tattara kayan su suka tafi tana ganin sunbar wajen, ta saka kuka tana tsine musu, tace wlh nafi shekara goma ina tara kudin nan koda auran au Hafsa yazo kin tab'ashi nayi, nace kowa mijinta ya mata kayan daki domin su marayune, shine wa'innan tsinannun suka kwashe mun kudina.

Ibrahim sai zare ido yakeyi yace "Goggo kiyi shuru kibari sai munje gida mayi maganan amma anan sunada na'uran daukan hoto in kikaci gaba da magana wlh daukanki zasuyi su mayar dake ciki suyita gana miki azaba."

Kafin tayi magana sukaga wani dan sanda ya futo da alama kaman wajen da suke yake nufowa, kafin ta sake juyowa ta nemi Ibrahim ta rasa awaje, itama bata tsaya b'ata lokaci ba ta kwasa da gudu hartana tuntub'e.

Dan sandan da yaga inda suke gudu ya kwashe da dariya yace da badon ina wajen akayi case dinku ba, da sai nabiku na kamo ku dan kunnuna halamun rashin gaskiya, yaci gaba da tafiyan sa yana dariya.

Goggo Larai a tsakar gida ta tadda Ibrahim yana ta meda nunfashi yana ganin an shigo da gudu ya tashi zai tsallake Katanga yana cewa yallabo wlh mun tuba.

Cikin haki Goggo Larai tace "kai da Allah nice fa"

Kafin ya sauko suka zauna suna mayar da nunfashi sunkai tsawan awa daya suna sauke nunfashin wahala kafin suka dawo dai²

Ibrahim ya dubeta yace "yanzu Goggo munaji muna gani su Faisal zasuyi arziki su barmu gaskiya ki nemo mana mafuta."

Saida ta nunfasa kafin tace karka damu suyi arzikin mugani, amma nadau alkawarin bazasu taba samun kwanciyan hankali ba indai ina raye, kuma kasa a ranka sai kayi arzikin da ko a mafarki basu isa suyi irinsa ba, da mai unguwa yake cewa wani saida boko tukunna ya sama maka aiki, shima yaje ya rike aikin sa mutane miliyon nawane sukayi arziki a duniyan nan ba tare da sunsan ABCD ba, yasa ido arzikin mu yananan zuwa, haka tayita daura danta akan turban da bazai kaishi da aihuwan da mai idoba.


Yau Faisal ya fara taka kafan sa wanda yayi daidai da gama training dinsu Fa'izu, ganin kafan ta samu sauki sosai yasa likitan ya sallamesu, suna dawowa mai unguwa yazo ya musu barka da samun saukin sa, kafin yake sheda musu nanda kwana 2 Fa'izu zasuyi bikin kammala horansu kuma a wajen za'a rantsar dasu, sunji dadi sosai dajin bayanin mai unguwa.

Tun ranan suka fara hada kayan da zasu raba ranan taron, Baffa Tukur ma ba'a barshi a baya ba dan yayi bakin k'ok'arin sa a matsayinsa na mara karfi ya basu dubu hamsin yace suyi hidiman dashi Faisal saida yayi kukan dadi, ya sakejin son Baffan su da kaunan sa ya shiga zuciyar sa, dan ba karamun kulawa yake nuna musu ba.

Bayan futan sa yace Umma ya kamata na futa ko dako nayi nasamu na siya miki nama kuma nakara wani abun akan kudin da muka had'a dan so nake dan uwana yaji dadin abunda zamu masa sosai.

Umma Hajara tace haba Faisal yau dinnan mu futo a asibiti sannan ka kace zaka futa neman kudi, ka kwantar da hankalinka dan akwai kudin dana tara da muke asibiti kasan duk wanda yazo dubaka saiya bamu kyautar kudi, shine nayita tarawa kaga gashi zai mana amfani.

Yayi murmushin farin ciki yace "Alhamdulillah Allah ya sake rufa mana asiri, amma bazan futa na mike k'afana kozanji dadi"

Ta mishi addu'a kafin ya futa, yana futa yayita zaga gari daidai kofan wani kamfani yaga mai kamfanin yanata masifa wai y'an dokonsa  sun tafi sunbar masa kayan sa yanzu da ansace masa fa.

Da sauri Faisal yaje ya tsuguna ya gaishe shi, yace Alhaji kayi hakuri kadaina fad'a inka biye na mutane sai su saka maka ciwon zuciya suja gefe suna kallonka suna dariya, kuma ka zamo me yawan ma mutane uzuri saboda bakasan meyasa suka tafi sukabar maka kayan ka anan ba, yanzu haka wani abune ya kidimasu kabari idan suka dawo saikaji dalilinsu, sannan in bazaka damuba zan gwada shigar maka da kayan dan naga kaman basu da yawa a karamun motane.

Alh Sulaiman ya dubesa dakyau haka kawai yaji ya kwanta masa arai yace "yaro mai sunanka?

"Faisal"

"Anya zaka iya sauke kayan nan kuwa?"

"Zan iya Alh insha Allahu"

Alh Sulaiman da kansa ya shiga motan yana mikowa Faisal kayan yana shigarwa, a haka har suka gama bayan sun kammala Alh Sulaiman ya dubesa yace "sannu yaro Allah ya maka albarka, yanzu nawane kudinka?'

Faisal daya koma gefe yana mayar da nunfashin gajiya ya taso yana "cewa Ameen, amma Alh kabani duk abunda kaga ya dace dan bansan nawa ake sauke kaya ba, wannan ne karona na farko"

Alh Sulaiman yace wani sana'a kakeyi yanzu?

Yace "bana sana'an komai yau aka sallamoni a asibiti, nakai tsawon wata shida ina jinya"

Alh Sulaiman yace subbahanallahi yaro meyasa kayi irin wannan aiki, yasa hanu a aljihun sa ya ciro kudi ko kirgawa beyi ba ya bashi kafin ya dauko katin sa ya mika masa yace ina fatan kayi karatu.

Cikin jin dadi da nuna farin cikinsa a bayyane yace nagode sosai Alh Allah ya kara girma da azziki, nayi karatu dan har bautan kasa ma nayi."

Alh Sulaiman ya jinjina kai yace idan kaje gida ka kirani a waya amma kafin ka kirani kafara turomun message da sunanka tukunna, daga nan sukayi sallamah.

Yabar Alh Sulaiman da tausayinsa a zuciyar sa.

Faisal saida ya biya wajen masu sayarda gasheshshen kaza ya siya guda uku biyu na Umman sa daya nasa kafin ya taho gida.

A daki ya samu Ummah ta kwanta tana tunanin duniya, ya shiga da sallama a bakinsa, ta amsa cikin kulawa kafin tace "ina ka shiga tun d'azu kabarni da kewanka"

Yayi murmushin kafin ya zauna ya bata labarin duk abunda ya faru, ta nuna masa  b'acin ranta kafin ta daura da mishi nasiha akan ya daina daurawa kansa damuwa.

Saida ta gama masa fad'a kafin ya bata hakuri, ya ciro daurin naman ya mika mata kafin shima ya ciro nasa, yace "Ummah naga bazan iya siyo biyu bane saina had'a dana Fa'izu kinga wannan nawa ke kuma saikici harda na Fa'izu, Allah sarki dan uwana ina kewanka sosai wlh, Umma har gani nake kaman jibi bazaiyi naje naganshi ba, dan a duniyan nan bayan ke banida wanda nakeso sama dashi.

Ita kanta Umma Hajara tasan Faisal nasan dan uwansa, shakuwace mai karfi tsakanin su tun suna yara, a zuciyar ta tayi addu'an Allah ya kawar da abunda zai raba mata kan y'ay'anta har abada, sukayita cin naman su suna hiran yadda zasu gudanar da taron da za'ayi narantsar dasu Fa'izu.

Yau ya kama taron Fa'izu tun asuba yake raba ido, kowani lungu da sako idonsa nakai bayida burin daya wuce yaga ma'aifiyar sa da dan uwansa abun alfaharinsa, daga idon dazaiyi ya hango wani yanata masa murmushi.....



Mmmn Ashfaq

🅿️9\10
Ya tab'a wanda yake kusa dashi yace "Yusuf kaga wancan sunyi mungun kama da Faisal dina da badan nasan yana gadon asibiti baya iya tafiya ba dasai nace shine, amma Allah yayi kama anan wannan mutumin kamansu ya baci da d'an uwana sosai wlh, ganin yaci gaba da mishi murmushi yanzu harda d'aga masa hanu yasa ya sake kura masa ido kafin ya tabbatar Faisal dinsane.

Ai tuni ya manta ajere suke za'a fara rantsar dasu ya kwaso da gudu bai tsaya ko inaba sai a kirjin d'an uwansa yana kukan farin cikin ganin sa a tsaye a kan kafafun sa.

Yace kaine a tsaye akan k'afan ka Alhamdulillah Allah nagode maka daka nunamun warakan dan uwana.

Lokacin da ya kwaso da gudu yan sandan wajen har zasuyi magana, shugaban su ya d'aga musu hanu suka koma suka tsaya a inda suke.

Faisal ya buga bayansa yace Fa'izu ka karya doka ko koma idan an gama zamuyi magana, sai yanzu ya tuna ya karya doka da sauri ya koma yana sunkuyar da kansa kasa.

Dawowan sa bada jimawa ba aka fara gudanar da taron, sukayi rantsuwa akan zasuyi aiki tsakaninsu da Allah kuma aikinsu shine sama da ahalinsu, bayan komai ya gudana Fa'izu yayita kiran abokansa na wajen yana nuna musu yan uwansa, baki ya sake ganin yadda akazo da yawa domin shi, bai dad'a jin mamaki ba saida yaga yadda Umma take ciro kayan rabo tanaba mutane azuciyar sa sai Allah Allah yakeyi agama taron sukoma gida ya tambayi Ummah a ina suka samu kudin da sukayi irin wannan hidiman, kwata² ya manta da wasu su Ibrahim shiyasa dabai gansu a wajen ba koya tambayesu, amma yaji mamakin ganin Balkisu a wajen.

Itama taga kallon mamakin da yake mata, sai tace nima nazo tayaka murnane dan uwana ba ruwana da shirmen su Goggo Larai.

Yayi murmushi kafin yace nagode sosai Balkisu Allah yabar zuminci, daganan y'an uwa sukayita tayashi murna.

Bayan kowa ya watse  Ummah da Faisal suka taho gida shi kuma Fa'izu sai washe gari ya dawo, yana shigowa da Ibrahim suka faracin karo ya fadad'a fara'ansa yace "sannu Ibrahim"

Wani mungun kallo Ibrahim ya watsa masa gawani kwalolon bakin ciki daya tokare masa nunfashi yace "tsaban rashin tarbiya ka dubeni ko kunya babu wani wai Ibrahim, to bari kaji ko kunki ko kunso ni aka fara aihuwa a gidan nan, kuma arziki daga sama ake farawa, kaga bata yadda y'an d'akinku zasu rigamu arziki, domin girma akebi dole sainayi kafin na sammuku.

Baki Fa'izu ya sake yana kallon sa, Faisal ne dayaji hayaniya yaki karewa a zauran ya futo domin ganin me yake faruwa, jin abunda Ibrahim yake fad'a yasa yaje ya rungume dan uwansa yace "haba Fa'izu meyasa zaka tsaya kana sauraron wannan shirman maganan, har kabari yake k'ok'arin cusa maka bak'in ciki wuce mutafi"

Ya dubi Ibrahim yace daga Qur'anic har zuwa hadisai babu aya ko nafsin da yace arziki girma takebi, munsan dai Ubangiji yana d'aga wanda yaso alokacin dayaga dayaso, Ibrahim ina baka girmane bawai bawai dan ka girmenin da zan girmamaka ba, kaje ka tambayi wanda suka sani kwana uku kacal kabani ba shekara ukuba dan hanka daga yanzu muzuba nidakai wanda ya fasa ba uwace ta aifesa ba, wlh duk wanda bayason zaman lafiya a rayuwar sa ya tabamun Fa'izu na ananne zan nuna masa ni Faisal ina kyalesa ne kawai.

Fa'izu yace haba dan uwana karka bari wasu suyi nasaran rabamu da tarbiyan da Umma tayita k'ok'arin bamu tun muna yara mana, karka manta mu ba jahilai bane muci gaba da hakuri da duk wanda Allah ya hadamu dasu koda sun kasance jahilai nesu, kwana nawame munbar musu gidan ma ka duba yanayin mu mana dukda mu bamu alfahari da abunda Allah ya bamu, amma mu Allah baiyi mu acikin jinsin wahalallu ba domin a wadace muke tunda Allah ya bamu wadatar zuciya da farin jinin Al'umma.

Suka kwashe da dariya tare da tafawa suna sake rungumar junansu, Faisal yace aiko akwai wani labarin da nakeso na baka d'an uwana zo muje daga ciki, suka wuce cikin farin ciki sukabar Ibrahim baki sake yana bak'in cikin abunda suka fad'a masa.

Suna shiga ciki suka tadda Umma tana kwance a daki duk da taji abunda sukayi Ibrahim, aranta taji dadi yau y'ayanta sun fara kwatan y'ancin su, amma a fili saita nuna kaman batasan mai sukayi ba, tace oyoyo autan Umman sa sannu da zuwa shugowan yaushe.

Sunji dadi da Umma batasan abunda ya faruba, dan basusan abunda zai b'ata mata rai yanzu kwata², cikin fara'a yace saukan yanzun nan Ummanah, na sameku lafiya?

Cikin so da kaunan y'ayanta tace lafiya qalau autana, kafin ta zuba musu abinci a tire d'aya suka wanke hanu suka faraci, bayan sun kammala ya dubesu yace "wlh Ummah nayi kewan ku sosai, gashi kullun inna tuna dan uwana yana kwance a asibiti ba lafiya nayita kuka, sai an hadu anata lallashina kafin nayi shuru.

Ya sake gyara zaman sa dakyau yace dan uwana yanzu ai nagama duk abunda zanyi sauran ka karbi aikin ka tunda Allah ya baka lafiya yanzu?

Cikin rashin fahinta Faisal yace aikina kuma wani aiki kenan Fa'izu?

Yace "aikin danaje nayi maka training dinsa mana"

Faisal ya daure fuska yace "dama Fa'izu akwai abunda zai dawo mallakata da banaka ba, gaskiya baka tab'a batamun rai irin na yauba, ko bayan raina ka sake cewa aikina kake amfani dashi ban yafe maka ba, yanzu banida burin da yawuce kafara aikin ka cikin koshin lafiya da gaskiya, sanna kuma kayi kokarin sanin manya a wajen saboda su canja maka suna cikin sauki.

Ummah ta musu addu'a tare da daurewan had'in kansu har abada, Faisal yaba Fa'izu labarin mutumin daya sauke masa kaya kafin yasa hanu a aljihunsa ya ciro kudi da katin da number mutumin yake yace kaga kudin daya bani da number, dama na ajiye kudin ne dan nasan idan ka dawo zaka nemesu, kuma inaso muje har dunduma muyiwa y'an uwa godiya na irin halaccin da suka nuna mana.

Fa'izu ya nuna farin cikin sa sosai na yadda dan uwansa yake nuna kulawan sa akansa, sannan yace "ya kamata ka tashi muje mu samu mutumin nan ko akwai aikin dazai iya sama mana.

Faisal yace to bari nafara tura masa sakon dayace na tura masa, ya ciro wayar sa Tacno ( Ashoben kwarawa ) yayi typing din massage ya tura masa ko second goma mekyau baiyi ba kiran Alh Sulaiman ya shigo wayan sa cikin girmamawa ya daga wayan ya gaishe shi bayan sungaisa.

Alh Sulaiman yace haba Faisal kabarni da tunanin ka, tun ranan nake jiran kiranka amma shuru saiyau ka tuna dani"

Yace hidiman kaninane ya rike ni Alh amma kayi hakuri dan Allah "

Alh Sulaiman yace ba komai idan bazan takura kaba kazo ka sameni akamfani na yanzu ina jiranka.
Ba komai Alh ganinan zuwa tare da kanina ma dan shima yace yanaso yazo ya tayani godiya bisa Alkairin daka mun.

Yace ba komai daganan sukayi sallama, ya dubi Umma yace "Umma zamuje mu sameshi ki mana addu'an samun nasara"

Umma tayi murmushi kafin tace ai addu'a kullun a cikin sa kuke kuje Allah ya karemun ku ya kuma dawo mun daku lafiya y'an Albarka"

Suka amsa da Ameen kafin suka futa, akan hanyansu na zuwa wajen Alh Sulaiman Fa'izu yace "zanyi farin ciki dan uwana idan akace yau kasamu aikinyi, kaga shikenan dukan mu zamu hadu mu taimaki rayuwar Umman mu, dan Umma ba karamun wahala tasha akan muba, yajima yana magana amma Faisal kwata² hankalinsa baya kan abunda yake fad'a masa, bin inda yake kallo yayi sai yaga wata yarinyace take tahowa sai kareraya takeyi ga kayan jikinta duk ya matseta gyalen data yafa dashi da babu duk d'aya suke, kallo d'aya yama yarinyan ya dauke kansa dan kwata² bata burgeshi ba asalima haka kawai yaji tsanan yarinyan a ransa.

Yarinyan tazo zata wuce ta gabansu Faisal ya kasa hakuri yace Assalamu Alaikum ya Sahibul kyau.

Saida tayi wani farrr da ido kafin tace "ya akayine d'an saurayi?

Ba karamun dad'i yajiba ganin ta tsaya ta sauraresa, kawai ya bude baki yana kwaranyo mata kalaman soyayyan dashi kansa baisan ya iyaba, yace kiyi hakuri na cikaki da surutu batare danasan sunanki ba!

Tace "Sunana Zainab amma anfi kirana da Zee big body!

Yayi murmushi kafin yace suna mai dadi to ni sunana Faisal wannan da kike gani k'anina ne shak'ik'ina sunan sa Fa'izu da fatan Hajiyah Zee zata karbi bakwancin mu.

Tace da guduma ai ba inda zakaje kace kana so ace a'a domin ka had'u ta ko ina, kanin namu baya magana ne.

Fa'izu yayi dariyan yak'e yace nabar masoya su gama tattaunawa ne kafin nasako bakina dan gudun karna yanke musu jin dad'i, sukayi dariya dukansu kafin tabawa Faisal number ta suka rabu suna tafiya suna waiwayan juna.

Akabar Fa'izu da memeta sunan data kira kanta a zuciyar sa Zee big body dagaji wannan sunan bana yar gidan mutunci bane, yace ya Allah ka dubemu karka bawa wanda zai ruguza mana zumincin mu k'ofa.

Faisal ya dubesa yace "Fa'izu sai naga kaman yarinyan nan bata maka ba, ka fadamun idan har baka santa ni kuma zan rabu da ita dan bazan tab'a auran mace da d'an uwana baya sonta ba.

Fa'izu yayi.......

INA MIK'A SAKON TA'AZIYATA WA ABOKINA  HAYATU BABA ZUBAIRU WANDA YA RASA KANINSA NURA BABA ZUBAIRU RANAN LAHADI 11-10-2020 INA MISHI ADDU'AN ALLAH YA GAFARTA MASA YASA ALJANNA CE MAKOMAN SA AMEEN, KU KUMA ALLAH YA BAKU HAKURI RASHIN SA.


🅿️11\12
Fa'izu yayi shuru yana nazarin sa, yasan tabbas dan uwansa ya fad'a son macen da kwata² bata dace da rayuwar sa ba, amma inyace baya sonta bema d'an uwansa adalci ba duba da yadda yake rawan kai a duk lamarin sa, amma zaiyita addu'an Allah ya musu zabin alkawai.


Ganin shurun san yayi yawa yasa Faisal cewa karka damu Fa'izu wlh inhar bata maka ba zan rabu da ita har abada.

Fa'izu ganin bazai iya k'untatawa d'an uwansa ba yasa yace "haba Faisal yanzu har kana tunanin akwai abunda zakaso nace a'a, karka manta farin cikin ka shine nawa duk abunda kakeso nima shi nakeso, nidai fatana Allah ya tabbatar da alkairi a tsakanin ku.

Faisal yaji dadin yadda kaninsa ya nuna yanason abunda yakeso cikin jin dad'i ya rungume shi yana cewa nagode sosai dan uwana, har suka isa kamfanin Alh Sulaiman yana mishi hiran Zainab da yadda yakejin ta a ranshi.

Alh Sulaiman yaji dadin ganinsu suka gaisa kafin yace Faisal tun ranan dana fara ganinka hankalina ya kwanta dakai, idan ba damuwa zaka iya zuwa kayi aiki dani?

Fa'izu ne yayi sauri bashi amsa da cewa "ba damuwan komai Alh aiko yanzu kace yafara aiki a shirye muke."

Alh ya kalli Faisal yace "wai hakane Faisal?

Faisal sai yanzu ya samu bakin yin magana yace "Alh banida bakin da zan maka godiya saidai nace Allah ya dad'a kareka daga dukkan mai nemanka da sharri ya d'auraka akan makiyan ka, Allah ya dad'a bud'ad'a ma arzikin ka.

Alh Sulaiman ya amsa da ameen cikin sakin fuska kafin yace "Faisal ranan ka nusar dani daga aikata abunda danayi da bazan taba yafewa kaina ba, ranan ashe d'aya daga cikin yarana ne ya tsallako zai shigo kamfani cikin rashin sa'a wani mai mota ya bigesa atake rai yayi halinsa dalilin dayasa suka tafi asibiti a kid'ime suka manta sukabar wajen a bude, kuma duk ma'aikata na nafijin dadin yaron dan yarone mai gaskiya da amana mutuwarsa ba karamun girgizani yayi ba ya karasa maganan tare da share guntun hawayen daya gangaro masa.

Suka masa ta'aziya tare da taya shi alhinin rasa yaron sa, kafin suka masa sallama tare da alkawarin idan Fa'izu ya gama hutun sa yafara zuwa aiki shima zaizo, Alh Sulaiman ya basu kyauta me nauyi suka futa sunata mishi godiya tareda addu'an kariya.

Suna isa gida suka tadda Goggo Larai a tsakar gida tana jiran shigowa su, aiko tana ganinsu ta mike da sauri ta nufosu tana cewa yawwa jinin k'aruna dama ku nake jira Ibrahim yamun bayanun duk abunda ya faru tsakanin ku, naso daya muku dukan dasai kun manta kamannin ku.

Umma ne ta futo da sauri ta musu halamun da duk abunda zatace musu suyi shuru karsu bata amsa, ba haka sukaso ba amma ba yadda suka iya domin farin cikin Umman su yafi musu komai a rayuwan su.

Fa'izu ya nuna kaman baigane abunda take fad'a ba shima ya karasa da sauri yana cewa oyoyo Goggo tunda na dawo naketa cikiyan ki ban gankiba ashe kin tafi anguwa ne, ya d'aga hanu ya sara mata yace yanzu kin dawo Maman dan sanda kiyi yadda kikeso danki na bayan ki.

Ta tab'e baki tace an gaya maka ina farin cikine da aikin ka? Kuma idan ka sake cewa ni uwar kane aradu hukuma ne kawai zasu rabamu, bari kaji indai ina raye saina raba wannan had'in kan naku sainasa kun dawo makiyan juna, kuma sai kun karbi abunda zakuci a hanun Ibrahim dina.

Faisal yana ganin Umma ta shige daki yaje kaman zai wuce ta kusa da ita a hankali inda ba wanda zai iyajin abunda zaice yace ki godewa Allah da kike tare da yan baiwa masu ilimi irinmu ba jahilai aka tara irinki bava gidan, dan da jirgi d'aya ne ya dauko mu da yanzu an kife gidan, yana kaiwa nan yaja hanun kaninsa suka shige d'aki Umma tana musu sannu da zuwa, suka bar Goggo Larai tanata bala'i ita kadai a tsakar gida.

Inda sabo sun saba da halinta shiyasa baya damunsu, nan sukaba Umma labarin duk yadda sukayi da Alh, Umma tace kai kunga mutumin arziki Allah ya saka masa da alkairi insha Allahu zanje da kaina nima namishi godiya, Faisal ya kurawa Fa'izu ido yanaso yaji Fa'izu yafara bawa Umma labarin Zainab da suka had'u yau.

Fa'izu ya nuna kaman baisan me yake nufiba saima dada kauda kansa da yayi dan har zuciyarsa bayaso suyi maganan Zainab, Faisal yace "anya Fa'izu kana son abunnan kuwa?

Ganin kaman dan uwansa zai gabe nufinsa yasa yace haba Faisal ai zanfi kowa farin ciki idan hakan ta kasance, kafin ya gyara zama yace " Umma mun girma fa yanzu dan daf kike da samun y'a mace a d'akin ki.

Ummah data fahinci inda zancen ya dosa tace to Alhamdulillah waye d'aya daga cikin ku? Faisal najin haka ya tashi ya futa yana sosa keya, Umma tayi murmushi irin tasu na manya tace Allah ya baku ta gari, washe gari suka fara zaga dangi domin musu godiyan d'awainiyar da sukayi, har dunduma saida sukaje.


Bayan dawowan su Faisal ya tasa Fa'izu a gaba sukaje gidan su Zainab, hankalin Fa'izu ba karamun tashi yayiba ganin shigan da tayi, tazo kaman zata shige jikin Faisal tana masa sannu da zuwa.

Tace "masoyina ban zaci zaka zoba harna fara marecin rashin ka"

Murmushi ya sake mata tare da cewa "Zainab kin dawo kaman bugun zuciya na bazan iya rayuwa babu keba, fatana ki rik'emun alkawari dan ina so nan bada jimawa ba na turo muyi aure"

Rungume shi tayi tana kai masa kiss ta ko ina a fuskan sa, shi kuma kunyane ya rufesa tunawa da yayi tare yake da kanin sa, a hankali ya zare jikinsa daga nata yana mata nuna da bashi kadai bane.

Murgud'a baki tayi tace to saimai tunda masoyina na runguma.

Shidai Fa'izu juya musu baya yayi yanajin kunan kasancewar ta da Yayansa, ta wani karyar da murya tana cewa k'aninmu barka da zuwa kayi hakuri da abunda kake gani, badda kai wayace maka ana rakiya zuwa wajen masoyo"

Yayi murmushi yace ai d'an bak'ara idan ma ina so nazo saidai nazo ni kadai, suka gaisa sama sama yaja gefe ya basu waje, ta dubi Faisal tace inason k'anin kan nan domin naga kaman yana da hankali"

Baki Faisal ya washe jin ta yabi jininsa, take yaji sonta ya kara shiga zuciyar sa, koda zasu tafi dakyar ya b'anbareta a jikin sa, duk yadda yaso ya gane yanayin Fa'izu a hanya amma ya kasa dan ba karamun kokari Fa'izu yayi ba wajen boye bacin ransa, har suka isa gida sunata hiransu suna dariya.

Tun daga ranan Fa'izu bai sake yarda yayi rakiya ba, duk da Faisal yana nuna masa b'acin ransa akan rashin binsa da yakek'i, shidai saidai ya bashi hakuri yace shima zaije watarana ya kwashi gaisuwa.


Posting letter su Fa'izu ya futo inda aka turasa Azare baiso haka ba amma ba yadda ya iya dole ya hakura, ya fara shirin tafiya wajen da aka turasa, ranan daya tafi Faisal shima ranan ya fara zuwa aiki, suka fara aikin su cikin kwarewa da gaskiya.

Bayan fara aikin Fa'izu da wata uku aka fara basu salary inda ya samu kudi mai dunbin yawa koda yazo bauchi yayiwasu Umma siyayyan kayan bukata sosai kafin ya zaga yaje ya siya fili a wani unguwa a cikin bauchi *gida dubu* ya nemi mai zane ya futar masa da flat biyu da single daki guda d'aya a tsakar gida kowa a wadace har gini yayi nisa ba wanda ya sani.

Saida yayi rufi akasa pop da interlocks a gidan sauran k'ananun abubuwane kawai suka rage, lokacin da aka kirasa aka sheda masa angama aikin da ya bayar yana samun off a wajen aiki ya taho bauchi, suna zaune sunata hira yace Umma ku shirya nakai ku gidan ogana ku gaisa, suka amsa masa da to kafin suka shirya suka tare keke napep yakaisu har kofan gidan, ya ciro ki a aljihun sa ya bude musu yace mu shiga yana ciki dama yace mun idan mukazo karna tsaya bugawa ya bani ki yace na bude mu shiga kawai.

Faisal yace ya kyauta wlh dan wani zubin mutum saiya fi awa yana jira ba'azo ba, da sallama suka shiga gidan, Umma tace anya da mutane a gidan nan kuwa Fa'izu naga duk yayi kura halamun ana aiki?


"Nima Umma ina tunanin hala sun futane amma mu shiga daga ciki mu jirasu"

Tace anya za'ayi haka, nikam nemo mun tsintsiya na gyara musu filin wajen nan kafin su dawo.

Faisal yace Umma mu shiga tukunna kinsan wasu basuson ana musu shishshigi a rayuwar su mu shiga kaman yadda Fa'izu yace, amma dan uwana inajin kirjina yanata bugawa kaman akwai abunda yake shirin faruwa ko?

Fa'izu yace bai kamata kayi wannan tunanin ba  bayan kana tare da jarumin kanin ka yanzu mubar wannan surutun mu shiga mugama abundan ya kawo mu, amma wani side kukeso mu fara shiga?

Umma tace duk wanda mai gidan ka yace"

Yayi murmushi a zuciyar sa yace to bansan wanne ne dan uwana zai fara zab'a ba dasai mu fara shiga nasa, kawai saiya bude kofan side din da suke shiya fara shiga kafin Umma suka rufa masa baya bakinsu dauke da sallamah.

Duka ido suka zaro waje ganin inda ya shigo dasu sabon ginine ba halamun an taba zama a wajen har suna rige rige tambayan sa gidan waye wannan kam?..



Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️13\14
Umma tace "Autana ban fahinci inda ka kawo muba, ka fad'amun gaskiya gidan waye wannan? Dan beyi kama da gidan da wani ya tab'a zama ba, ga dukkan halamu sabon ginine wannan, ta k'arasa maganan tana tsareshi da ido.

Yayi murmushi yace "da farko dai ina neman gafaran ku saboda nasan ban kyauta muku ba, ina neman gafaran ku dan Allah ku yafemun"

Faisal yace "Fa'izu nasan har na mutu bazaka tab'a mun lafin da har zan rik'esa a raina ba ballan tana har ka nemi gafara na, wlh duk abunda zakamun na yafe maka, ballan tanama bakamun komai ba.

Umma tace ba abunda kamun auta idan kamun ma abisa rashin sanine wlh na yafe maka har cikin raina, damuwata yanzu ka gaggauta fad'a mun gidan waye wannan, ko hankalina zaifi kwanciya.

Yace "nasan na muku laifi fatana Allah yasa idan na fad'a muku abunda nayi kar kuyi fushi dani"

Ya nunfasa kafin yaci gaba da cewa "Umma lokacin danayi wata uku dafara aiki suka fara bamu albashi, albashin mu na farko sun bamu kudi mai kauri shine nayi tunanin na mana gini nida d'an uwana, dan banaso Faisal yayi aure ya zauna a gidan da Goggo Larai take ko kuma gidan haya, wannan dalilin yasa na siya fili a b'oye nafara gini, dalilin b'oye muku kuma inaso na muku bazata nafison naga farin cikin ku lokaci dana bayyana muku gidan.

Umma ta dirk'usa tayi sujjadan shukur idonta na zubar da hawaye, Faisal kuma dawowa yayi kaman mutum mutumi saida Umma ta girgiza shi tace Faisal ka dawo hayyacinka, kamata yayi kama Allah godiya shine abunda ya kamata muyi.

Firgit ya dawo nutsuwar sa saiya fashe da kuka yace Fa'izu me kakeso na maka kamun abunda ko a mafarki banyi tunanin samun shi nan kusa ba, ya Allah kadubi zumincin mu! Allah kar kaba wasu daman ruguza mana zumincin mu! ya ubangiji kasan domin ka mukeyi zumunci! bamu son junan mu dan wata manufa, munayin zumuncin mu kaman yadda kace ayi, shima yayi sujjadar shukur ya jima yana addu'a kafin ya dago ya dubi Fa'izu yace "Dan uwana wannan ba laifi ka mana ba kamun abunda har na mutu bazan tab'a mantawa dakai ba, ya Allah kabamu abunda zamuwa zumincin mu, Fa'izu ka cika Babban masoyi a gareni kanuna mun soyayyan da ban isa na nuna maka irinsa ba, ina ma nima Allah zai bani abunda zan maka nima nasaka ka a cikin farin ciki fiye da yadda ka sakani...Fa'izu yayi saurin rufe masa baki yace " Faisal duk abunda nazama a duniyan nan kaine sila duk abunda zanyi kaine silan kaini matsayin danake yanzu, da yau kace tunda baka da lafiya ka hakura da aikin, ba wanda zaiyi tunanin ni natafi a matsayin ka saidai muce ba rabanka bane, amma aranan ka nunamun zallan soyayya kace natafi a madadin ka, na futo bayan samun warakan ka kaki karban aikin ka kace kabar mun, to me zakamun wanda ya wuce wannan, wlh nasan har na mutu bazan iya kwatan ta soyayya da kulawan daka nunamun ba, soyayyanka gaskiyace gareni bazan iya biyanka abunda kamun ba, kukane ya hanashi cigaba da maganan da yakeyi, Umma ta taso ta rungumesu duka tace Allah ya muku albarka y'ayana Allah yasa ku gama da duniya lafiya, ubangiji ya kurmartar da makantar da kunne da idanuwan makiyan ku, Ummah tayita musu addu'a kafin suka zaga gidan.

Fa'izu yace "Faisal ka zab'i inda kakeso ka zauna a ciki, amma ni inka yarda na zab'a maka?

Faisal yace na baka zab'i dan uwana"

Umma "a gida Babba ne yake zama a farko ko?

Umma tace " eh hakane kodan saboda kariya anaso Babba ya zauna a farkon gida, saidai tunda kaine jami'in tsaro kai ya kamata ka zauna a farkon gida, Faisal saiya shiga ciki.

Faisal yace "Umma dama taya me kaza zaici kai, flat din farko yafi yawan d'a kuna, kai ya kamata ka zauna anan, ni kuma saina zauna daga ciki ya isheni rayuwa nida ya'yana kai harma da jikokin mu" ya karasa maganan yana dariya.

Fa'izu saida ya gama jin bayanin su duka kafin yace "banji dadin abunda kace ba Faisal! Taya zakace gidana?

Nina d'auka duk abunda na mallaka a duniyan nan nakane kaida Umma, inhar abuna nakane toka dauki flat din farko, Umma duk da na kasance jami'in tsaro amma ai ba anan nakeda zama ba, idan na dauki wajen farko nasaka matana a wajen idan bana gari kuma wazai ba matana kariya tana farkon gida, tana mace taya zata zauna ita kad'e a farkon gida, Faisal kama magana na kyakykyawan fahinta dan Allah, karku dage saina zauna a farkon gida.

Umma tace "na fahinceka Autana Allah ya dad'a hadamun kanku har abada, Allah ya baku abunda zakuma al'umman annabi"

Suka amsa mata da ameen Umman mu!

Faisal yace "yanzu nine da farkon gida Allahu Akbar Allah ka k'arawa Annabi daraja, yau na mallaki wajen zama wanda masu kudi suke zama, yau da tsakar rana na dawo Alhaji kai jama'a, yazo ya rungume dan uwansa yace ina sonka Fa'izu inason farin cikin ka inason abunda kakeso Allah ya nuna mun ranan da zakayi aure ka aihu na d'auki danka a hanuna.

Fa'izu ya rungumesa yace inason na ganka cikin farin ciki dan uwana Allah ya jinkirta mana ranan da zamuyi kuka.

Suka amsa da ameen
Umma tace yanzu inaso kaman yadda kamana bazata muma haka zamuma mutane, bazan fad'a wa kowa ba sai ana gobe auran Faisal zanyi walima a gidan nan, suka amince da abunda tace daga nan suka koma gida, suna komawa Faisal yace yaukan karkace mun a'a dan Allah, ka taso muje gidansu Zainab.


Umma tace ku tashi ku tafi Allah ya muku albarka, Fa'izu ya tashi ya tafi badan ransa yaso ba, saidan kar d'an uwansa ya gane baya sonta.

 Sun foto zasu tafi Goggo Larai tace ayidai mugani, waiku masu had'in kai kun futa da uwarku, yanzu kuma zaku sake futa tare, toga sakona idan kunje kucewa bokanku wlh bokana yafi karfin sa, indai tsafine muzuba ni da ku zanga wanda zaiyi nasara.

Umma ce ta futo wuf tace y'an albarka kuje Allah ya tsaremun gaba da bayan ku a gaishe mun da surukata, suka amsa da Ameen suka futa ba wanda ya kulata, akabar Goggo Larai da cewa suruka kuma?


Suna zuwa gidan ya kirata a waya yace gamu a kofan gidan ku, yana kashe wayan sai gata ta futo tace yau kace tare kake da babban bak'o, sannu da zuwa k'anina ya hanya.

Ya sake fuska yace yawwa Aunty na hanya Alhamdulillah saidai yau naga kaman ba'a d'aukin mu?

Tace ba haka bane kasan mu mata idan munabin wata munyita fama da ciwon ciki kenan, yanzu ma abunda yake damuna kenan ta k'arasa maganan tana yamutsa fuska.

Faisal yace sannu masoyiya dama albishir nazo miki, yau Fa'izu ya mallakamun gida mai mungun kyau wanda attajirai ne kawai suke iya zama a cikin ta, kinga yanzu sai maganan auran mu kenan.

Wani tsalle tayi ta rungumesa tace yau nice zanyi aure zan auri mutum me mutunci da kamala irinka, kai dole yau na had'a Faty da daddare na gwada wa mutane nasamu mijin aure na nunawa wa sa'a.

Janyeta yayi a jikinsa yace karki damu mugodewa Allah, yanzu jeki kwanta zan dawo idan kin samu sauki"

Ko sallama bata musu ba ta kwasa gida da gudu tun daga bakin kofa take kwalawa Mamanta kira Baba Abu! Baba Abu! Baba Abu.


Fa'izu yayi murmushi yace ya kamata ace nanda wata daya kuyi aure kodan ganin irin d'aukin da takeyi, yanzu ana mana salary zan k'arasa k'ananun gyaran daya rage a gidan.

Faisal yace a'a kabari akwai kud'in aikina da nake tarawa dama inaso nasiya mana filine muyi gini to zan karasa dasu, nima wlh na matsu nayi auran nan, suka kwashe da dariya.

Suna zuwa gida suka tasa Umma a gaba akan dole yau saitaje ta samu Baffa Tukur akan magaban auran.

Umma tace "Faisal yau Ina kunyan ka ta tafine?"


Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️15\16
Ya sunkuyar da kansa k'asa yana dariya dan sai yanzu yaji kunyan abun yake fad'a.

Ummah tayi murmushi tace karka damu lokacin kane yayi saura na Autana.

Fa'izu ya marerece fuska kaman zaiyi kuka yace "Ummah nima kimun addu'a wlh naga wanda nakeso amma na kasa fad'a mata kullun cikamun ido takeyi.

Faisal ya kwashe da dariya yace ashe gwanda ni nayi namijin kokari, Umma ashe autanki matsoraci ne, Fa'izu ya turo baki yace Umma kinji shi ko.

Umma tayi dariya saboda tanajin dadin yadda y'ayanta suke kula da junansu, tace "kyalesa autana idan yasa tsiya sai a fasa nashi saiya jiraka.

Faisal ya zaro ido yace haba Umma ki rufamun asiri na daina, saiyaji kunyan abunda yace ya tashi ya futa da gudu yana dariya, suma suka kwashe da dariya.




Baba Abu ta futo da gudu tace lafiya takwara waya b'ata miki rai naje na zagi dangin uwarsa?

Zainab tace "yau ba fad'a na d'auko ba Baba Abu"

Ta cigaba da cewa ina saurayi na da nake fad'a miki?

Baba Abu tace kai k'arshe zancen dan na matsu naji me yake faruwa dan jikina har tsuma yakeyi"

Zainab tace dad'ina dake gajen hakuri,cemun yayi zai turo muyi aure kwanan nan kuma k'aton gida yake dashi wlh Baba Abu"

Baba Abu tayi gud'a tace wannan karan bazan tab'a bari batun auranki ya shirirince ba, saina ba makiya mamaki.

Zainab tace "wlh har jikina yayi sanyi, Faisal fa shine na goma sha uku a masuson aure na, sauran goma sha biyun har sai ansa mana rana suce sun fasa, wannan karan idan aka fasa kashe kaina zanyi.

Baba Abu tace wa'incan ma mu muka zuba ido kawai muka barsu suka tafi, yanzu tashi muje wajen boka yama tufkan hanci ta inda bazasu iya fasawa ba musuma dan nasan da zaran sunji labarin kina raba jan nama suma zasu fasa.

Bayan ta gama fadawa boka buk'atan ta, yayi dariya kafin yace wannan auran dole sai an daurasa in kunga ba'ayiba baku cika sharad'an da zan baku bane.

Cikin rawar murya sukace meye sharad'in?

Saida yayi dariyan mugunta kafin yace da farko zaki ajiyemun dubu d'ari da hamsin sannan zakibar mun Zainab na kwana da ita, indai akayi haka to nanda sati shida zatayi aure.

Zainab tace indai mu kwana tarene na baka jan nama wannan ai mai saukine zan maka duk yadda kakeso saidai boka dubu hamsin ne damu.

Yayi yarensu irin na bokaye yace indai nakarbi dubu Hamsin to zamuyi kwana uku dake anan sannan duk lokacin da nakeda bukata zan zurma ba gardama.

Sukace sun amince kafin Baba Abu ta bashi kudin ta tafi zuciyarta tas tana farin cikin y'arta zatayi aure.

Boka ya lashe baki yace tashi mu shiga mu fara aiki ko!

Tun a wajen ta tub'e zigidir shima haka suka wuce ciki.

Bayan sallan magriba Umma da Fa'izu suka tafi gidan Baffa Tukur, bayan sun gaisa Umma ta gyara zama tace Baffan yara nazo maka da maganan auran d'anka ne"

Baffa Tukur ya washe baki yana cewa masha Allah wanne daga cikin su?

Tace Faisal ne yaga mata yace yanaso kaje ka tambayo masa auran ta.

Baffa Tukur yace to Alhamdulillah saidai wani hanzari ba gudu ba, kafin muje ya kamata mu fara bincike akan yanayin tarbiyan yarinyan, a ina gidan nasu yake?

Fa'izu yace " gidansu yana Malam goje sunan ta Zainab"

"Kace kusa muke ma to insha Allahu bayan sallan isha zanje nayi bincike akanta, ko kasan sunan ma'aifinta?

Fa'izu yace bansan sunan kowa nata ba, nan ya mishi kwatancen gidan su.

Kaman yadda ya fad'a musu kuwa, ana idar da sallan isha ya kama hanyan anguwan su Zainab, gane gidan be masa wahala ba.

Yana tsaye a wajen wani dattijo da suke sallah tare yazo ya samesa yace Malam Tukur yau kaine a unguwar mu, da fatan dai lafiya?

Baffa Tukur yace lafiya qalau nazo dan bincikene a unguwarku, d'anane yake naman aure a nan gidan shine nazo bincike dangane  halin gidan da kuma yanayin tarbiyan su.

Yace innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'um Malam Tukur mu nemi wajen zama!

Suka koma gefen gidan kan dakalin mak'otansu suka zauna, kafin yaci gaba da cewa Malam Tukur duk garinnan ansan ku mutane ne masu daraja da daraja mutane, wani tsautsayine ya kawoku neman aure gidan nan, bari dai nabaku labarin ahalin gidan nan kad'an idan kacire mai gidan to dukkansu shed'anune har uwarsu dan sun addabi mutanen anguwa da kewayan ta.

*Zainab* y'ace ga Lauwani mai shayi asalinsu y'an cikin garin Bauchi ne iyaye da kakani, kowa yasan Lauwani me shayi mutumin kirkine bugu da kari kamiline mai riko da addini, saidai yayi rashin dace da abokiyar zama, inda soyayya yajashi ya auri Abu ba yadda ba'a fad'a masa ba macen kwarai bace amma fur yak'i amincewa.

Daga farko zaman auransu abun sha'awa dan lokacin shayi nakaiwa Lauwani ba karamun cikini yakeyi ba, komai Abu tace tanaso cikin b'arin jiki Lauwani yake siya mata, kwatsam wata rana Lauwani ya tashi da gobara shagonsa ya k'one kurumus, tun lokacin komai nasa ya koma baya, daga lokacin ya fara fuskantar matsala da Abu, wataran har rufe masa kofa takeyi idan bai kawo mata abunda take so ba, a wannan halin Allah ya basu rabo ta aifi y'a mace yama yarinya hud'uba da sunan ma'aifiyar sa.

Nan Abu ta buka kasa tace wlh bataga wanda ya isa yasama y'arta suna ba, ita dashan wahala shida rad'a mata suna, yanaji yana gani ta canjawa y'ar suna tama kanta takwara wato itace ZAINAB bayan aihuwan Zainab suka sake aihuwa  nanma ita tasakawa yaron suna Hamza a haka tayi ta aihuwa har tayi y'aya biyar duka sai sunan data gadama take sawa yaranta.

A wajen tarbiyan yaran ba karamun fama suka shaba ta inda be isa yama yaran fada ba saidai suyi tayin duk abunda sukaga dama, daga karshe dataga ya tubure mata saita sa bokaye suka rufe masa baki duk abunda yaran sukayi saidai ya bisu da ido, wannan dalilin ya aifar masa da hawan jini me tsanani yanzu haka yana kwance a gida bayida lafiya duk y'an uwansa sun fita harkansa saboda sunce ya rabu da ita yak'i, yana gani yaransa mazan biyu sun dawo y'an daba biyu kuma suna luwad'i, inda Zainab ta dawo rijiyar kowa.

Indai zaka kawo mata nama to zata bada kai bori yahau, wannan shine kad'an daga cikin tarihinsu.

Baffa Tukur yaja dogon nunfashi kanshi duk ya daure ya rasa wani irin hukunci zai yanke wa wannan al'amarin, yama mutumin godiya kafin ya tafi da zummar zai sake tambayan wasu yaji ko zaiji akasin labarin da aka basa, yakai sati yana bincike duk wanda ya tare da maganan saida a daura masa akan wanda yaji a baya.

Fa'izu harya tafi Baffa Tukur baizo ba, a zuciyar sa yace tabbas Baffan nasu yaji abunda aka sheda masa dangane da gidan su Zainab.

Bayan tafiyan Fa'izu! Baffa Tukur yazo ya samu Umma ya sheda mata abunda yaji, Umma ta sauke nunfashi tace Baffa Tukur kana ganin idan mun kyaleta munyi daidai? A nawa tunanin mubarshi ya aureta kawai maybe muyi silan shiryuwan ta, kaga idan mukayi haka zamu samu lada a gurin ubangiji, kuma kayi tunani ba wanda ya wuce irin wannan k'addaran idan y'armuce baza muso ace anzo neman auran ta an fasaba haka zamu turata ma duk wanda yazo mu b'oye aibun ta, idan anabin hali da y'an Larai bazasu auruba, amma duk da halin uwarsu bai hanasu auruwa ba.

Baffa Tukur yace kuma kema kin fada magana mekyau, muyita addu'a Allah ya tabbatar mana da alkairin da yake ciki, sukayi sallama ya tafi yana jinjina tawakkali irin na Umma Hajara.

Faisal koda yaji abunda akace a kanta yasan hakane amma soyayyanta ya rufe masa ido baya ganin aibunta kwata².

Fa'izu kuwa baiso ace Umma ta amince da auran nan ba, amma shima ba yadda ya iya kawai saiya bisu da addu'a.

Akakai sadaki daganan aka fara shirye² ta kowoni fanni musamman Zainab ta dage wajen durawa kanta maganin mata da matsi, danso take ta batar da hankalin mijinta idan ya shiga jikinta.


***********************
AZARE
Shakuwa ce mai karfi tsakanin Safiyyah da Fa'izu kullun inya tashi a aiki bayida abokiyar hiran daya wuce Safiyyah.

Safiyyah kanwan abokinsa ne,  tun a jami'a suke tare da Adam kuma suka sake had'uwa a wajen training, akayi posting dinsu waje daya, saidai shi Adam garinsu aka dawo dashi, tare suke tafiya aiki kullun, Fa'izu sun shaku da Adam sosai hakan yasa dayazo garin Adam yace yazo gidan su ya zauna daga farko Fa'izu yaso yaki saida Adam ya nuna masa bacin ransa sosai kafin ya hakura suke kwana tare.

Tun lokacin dayaga Safiyyah Allah ya jarabce shi da mungun sonta saida bai fad'awa kowa ba, yabar abun shida zuciyar sa saidai duk wani hanyan da zata gane yayi, itama da yake yarinyace mai kawaici da tarbiya yasa ta nuna masa batasan abunda yakeyi ba, saidai itama anata fannin ta kamu da sonsa ta inda bazata iya rayuwa babu shiba.

Kullun suka zauna bata da hiran daya wuce na k'awarta Sumayyah takance masa zata iya sadaukar da rayuwarta domin farin cikin Sumayyah, shima dalilin son da yake mata yasa yake tausayin halin da Sumayyah take ciki.

Yauma kaman yadda tasaba shiga gidan su Sumayyah, ta shiga tana  shiga tajiyo muryan Maman Sumayyah tana cewa "Sumayyah wai wa kika mayar bawanki ne a cikin gidan nan, kin kasa auruwa to ubanwa zai miki aikin gidan shegiyar yarinya me bak'in jinin tsiya, kowa ya rab'u dake saiyayi bak'in jini, shiyasa  bana barin kannan ki suzauna kusa dake dankar sukasa auruwa irinki, ni wlh danasan haka zaki zamomun dana zubar da cikinki tun yana k'arami.

Safiyyah cikin sanyi jiki ta karasa shigowa da sallama a bakinta, Mama ta amsa tana cewa y'ar albarka kin shigo, saidai ina miki gudun kar Sumayyah ta goga miki bak'in jini amma furkin ki janyewa a gareta ko.

Safiyyah tayi saurin kawar da zance da cewa ina wuni Mama!

Mama saida tatab'e baki kafin ta amsa da lafiya, ta mik'e ta karasa wajen Sumayyah da murmushi a fuskanta tace "k'awata zaki fara aiki ne"

Sumayyah cikin dauriya dason b'oye kukanta tace eh Safiyyah, tsintsiya Safiyyah ta dauka tafara shara tace maza jekiyi wanke wanke kafin na gama shara da mofin saimu daura girki ko"

Sumayya kullun ta kalli Safiyyah sai taji sanyi aranta dan duk kawayen ta Mama tayi nasaran rabasu, Safiyyah ce kawai taki rabuwa da ita, afili tace Safiyyah tabbas kinfi JININA.

Safiyyah ta d'ago daga sharan da takeyi tace hooo Sumayyah  nasaba dajin wannan kalman, ban miki komai ba a rayuwa amma insha Allahu nadauwa kaina alkawarin nan kusa  zan kawo karshe k'uncin ki hakika inajinki a raina fiye da JININA.

Sukaci gaba da aiki nan danan suka kammala komai, Mama tana ganin zasu shiga d'aki tace ke Sumayyah waye zaiyi wankin kuma kinsan dai kanneki da Allah ya auran mu dasu basu gaji bakin jinin kiba sun turo da wankinsu dana y'ayansu to waye zaiyi?

Safiyyah tace Mama bamusan sun turo bane kiyi hakuri, ta kama hanun Sumayyah tana mata murmushi tace zo muje muyi, kinga basuda yawa yanzu zamu gama.

Batace mata komai ba sukaje suka fara wankin sunayi Mama nacewa wlh idan akamun jik'a jik'a na jibgi mutum, basu kulata ba Safiyyah naganin yadda Sumayyah taketa share hawaye har suka gama suka shiga dakin Sumayyah.

Sumayyah ta dubeta tace ".....


Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️17\18

"Meye laifina?"

"Nina hana kaina mijin aure?"

"Daman idan mutum ya kasance dan fari a gidan su saiya dawo wulakantar ce, iyaye da dangi suk'isa"


Mama ta takurawa rayuwata gani take ni nake kin kula manema, batasan duk wanda sukazo wajena da kalaman fasukanci suke zuwa mun, dududu shekarata ishirin nefa a duniya amma take tsangwama ta, wataran idan nazauna ina tunanin anya Mama itace ta aifeni kuwa, abu d'ayane yakesa na yarda itace ma'aifiya ta, ganin kaman nin mu da ya b'aci da ita daidai da tafiyata irin nata ne, shi kadai yakesa na yarda itace ma'aifiya ta.

A halin yanzu duk dangi sun juyamun baya daga na Mama har Baffan mu, cewa sukeyi wai najawo musu abun kunya, tunda suke basu tab'a goya y'a takai shekara goma sha biyar ba a gida amma ni har shekara ishirin, kina gani duk hidiman da za'ayi ba'a zuwa dani idan kuma nayi gangancin zuwa ranan saina dawo da raunika a jikina, sunyita dukana kenan kaman jahilai, Safiyyah kibani shawara wlh harna fara tunanin na kashe kaina kawai na huta da wannan mungun rayuwar da nake ciki."

Shekara d'aya kawai na baki amma iyayanki basu tab'a takura miki akan maganan aure ba kuma dukansu Fulanine kaman nawa iyayen, ta kifa kanta akan cinyan Safiyyah ta saki kuka maicin rai.

Safiyyah batayi yunkurin hanata ba dan tasan in bata samu ta futar da abunda yake ranta ba kuma tayi kuka, shakka babu ciwon zuciya zai iya kamata, wannan dalilin yasa duk lokacin da suka keb'e in zatayi kuka bata hanata, ganin kukanta nayau yafi na kullun gashi har jikinta ya dau zafi halamun zazzab'i ya sauk'a mata yasa hankalin Safiyyah ya fara tashi ta mike da sauri ta samo mata ruwa mai sanyi ta bata, ba musu ta karba tasha, hijabi ta saka kafin ta mika mata nata tace saka muje zaga gari"

Murmushi hade da kuka  Sumayyah tayi tace kar muba kammu wahala nasan ko mun futa haka zamu dawo ba wanda zai biyoni, sai fasikai wanda suran jikina yake birgesu"

Safiyyah tace karki damu yanzu ba abunda zai futar damu ba kenan, suka futo

Safiyyah taje d'aki ta samu Mama tana kallo da sauran yaranta tace Mama zamu futa muje gidan Aunty na"

Mama saida ta tab'e baki kafin tace to saikun dawo, nidai nace kija baya da wannan me b'akin jinin, dan inta shafa miki iyayenki zasu shiga damuwa kaman mu, ace yarinya har shekara ishirin tana gida ba mashinshini sai kace wanda mukayi sunanta da bakin bunsuru, ko a dangi an rasa mai son ta, tashi tayi suka tafi tabar Mama tanata surutu da zage².


Suna futa sukaci karo da Fa'izu a kofan gidan yana kokarin kiranta awaya, yana ganinta ya sauk'e ajiyan zuciya yace Alhamdulillah hankalina harya kwanta, kinsan banaso nadawo daga aiki nasamu bakya gida shine kika futa ko?

Saidai Allah ya taimake ki gidan k'awarmu kikazo da yau me rabamu sai Allah.

Sai yanzu Sumayyah tayi dariya tace nikam kullun kuna samun kaina a duhu wai wa da kanwa ne ko kuma masoya ne?

Safiyyah tayi saurin kai mata duka tace lallai baki da hankali, Yaya yana iya shakuwa da kanwan sa fiye da namu ma, shiyasa idan naga wasu sai nace Yaya Fa'izu da Yaya Adam basu sona ta k'arasa maganan tana tura baki kaman zata kuka.

Idan tana shagwaba abun yana burgesa dan har lafiyan sa take tab'a masa shiyasa duk ranan Alhamis da Monday baya yarda su had'u gudun kar azumin shi ya samu rauni, dan ko yanzu da zata lura da wandan sa zataga yadan kumbura.

Yace to shikenan ni kuma namiki alk'awarin nuna miki kulawa da soyayyar da ba y'ar gatan da zata samu irinsa, dariya sukayi dukan su kafin suka koma kofar gidan su Safiyyah suka zauna hira suka fara abun sha'awa, a take Sumayyah ta manta da duk k'uncin da yake ranta, saidai abunda bata fahinta ba duk lokacin da Safiyyah zatayi kwatancen soyayyah saita had'a sunanta dana Fa'izu.



*BAUCHI*
Boka tunda ya d'an dana Zainab yaji tamishi yadda yakeso duk da bata da wani zak'i amma tana da juriya, dan d'aga kafa tayi tabarshi yayi tayin yadda yakeso, kwana ukun da sukayi kwata² bai bada magani ba, yunwa ne kawai yake d'agasu sunacin abinci zasuci gaba da aiki, dan sun manta da ana sallamah kwata², shi kuma tunda yake haye masu karban magani bai tab'a haduwa da kalan saba irin Zainab.

Shiyasa yau da zata tafi ya kanne ido d'aya ya fara sharara mata k'arya, yace Zainaba akwai babban masifan dazai tunk'aroki idan kikayi aure, amma bazan fad'a miki ba harsai lokacin yayi zaki kawo kanki, sannan idan auranki ya rage saura kwana goma sha bakwai zakizo muyi kwana uku akwai abunda zansa a alkalamina ina zuba miki a allonki.

Zainab tace indai ta wannan harkanne karka damu ko nayi auranma bazamu rabuba, dan Indai naji mijina bayida karfinka dole ina kawo maka ziyara, ya bata k'ullin magani ta tafi tana mishi godiya.

Shiko boka dariya yayi yace kinyi kuskure da kika fad'a mun sai mijinki bai miki ba zakizo wajena, niko bazan tab'a bari kiji yana gansar dake ba, ya sake kwashewa da dariya yace irinku mukeso halakakku.

Koda ta dawo gida tama Baba Abu bayanin yadda sukayi da boka, Baba Abu tace ko yau yace ki koma gidan harsai anyi biki zan koraki, ballan tana kwana uku kawai.

Lauwani mai shayi yana kwance yanajin duk abunda sukeyi yayi kuka da takaicin irin uwar daya zab'awa y'ayansa saidai koba komai yanawa y'arsa addu'an Allah yasa sanadiyar shiryuwanta kenan.

Ya lallab'a ya futo yace Zainab mijin auranki yazo ina tayaki murna Allah ya zaunar mun dake lafiya ya baki zuri'a d'ayyiba, saidai ina mai baki hakuri da banida halin da zan miki komai ki yafemun y'ata, amma namiki alkawarin duk lokacin da nasamu wadata zan miki"

Tace nagode Babana ashe kana sona ban sani ba, karka damu nima daga yau zan fara kula dakai har nabar gidan nan.

Faisal ya dage yanata shirin bikinsa, kuma da zuciya d'aya yake kallon Zee big body mutane da dama sukance masa ta gama rabawa maza jar namanta, amma fur zuciyarsa taki amincewa da hakan, kallan kamilan Mace yake mata kullun.

Kaman yadda boka ya bikaci tazo kafin bikin, yau ta shirya ta tafi, zuwan ta tasamu mutane a layi yana ganinta yace kowa ya tafi sainan da kwana uku su dawo, kafin suka wuce ciki da ita ba karamun gurzanta yayi ba, ranan data cika kwana uku a wajensa, ta shirya zata tafi ya bata wani k'ullin magani yace ta tabbatar ta saka a gabanta kafin mijinta ya kusan ceta kullun har maganin ya k'are, ta tafi tanata murna, a tunaninta ya bata abunda zata mallaki hankalin Faisal d'inta ne.

Daga nan asibiti ta wuce direct taje aka mata dinki ciki da waje, tana dawowa gida tafara gashi da shaye shayen maganin mata.

Umma da kanta taje k'auyen dunduma ta sanar da bikin sannan ta shedawa danginta da dangin Malam, Faisal sun gina gidan dazasu zauna a ciki shida dan uwansa, ba karamun farin ciki sukayi ba dajin zancen sukayita musu addu'a tare da fatan daurewan rayuwa mai albarka.

Yau biki ya rage saura kwana biyar Faisal ya gama gyara gidan fes har fenti ya musu.



*AZARE*
Fa'izu bayan sun gama hira suka raka Sumayyah har kofan gida, har zata shiga Safiyyah tace kai yau ina sha'awan daukan foto ki tsaya muyi ta ciro wayar ta ta musu foton dukansu uku kafin tace Sumayyah da Fa'izu su tsaya ta musu tare, ba wanda ya kawo komai a ransa suka tsaya tayita musu foto kuma sunyi mungun kyau, kafin ta turawa Sumayyah a wayan ta suka tafi gida.


Suna komawa gida ta shige kasancewar mangariba yayi shi ma Fa'izu ya wuce masallaci.


Bayan tayi sallan isha ta kwanta tanata tunani a zuciyar ta, a hankali ta furta Allah kasan inason Fa'izu fiye da rayuwata amma  zanyi wannan sadakarwan dan kawata ce.........

🅿️19\20
Tunda ta kwanta bacci yaki idonta tayita sakawa da warwarewa har k'arfe biyun dare bacci yaki zuwa idonta karshe ma ta tashi taje ta d'auro alola ta shin fida dadduma ta fara kaiwa Allah kukanta akan ya mata zab'in alkairi.

Bayan kwana biyu suna zaune da Fa'izu suna hira sai take ce masa "yau ko ka tambayeni aminiya ta?

Ya rasa meyasa take damunsa da hiranta yanzu, harya farajin haushin Sumayyah a ransa, saiya kawar da maganan da cewa " kina lissafin bikin Faisal dina kuwa?

Tace "ba dolena ba aiko dan murnan zanje Bauchi dole ina k'irga sauran kwanakin"

Yace indai kina lissafi yau saura kwana nawa?

Saida tayi dariya kafin tace "Yaya Fa'izu ta jini kawai kafini kusanci dashi amma inaji a jikina Yaya Faisal kaman shine Garkuwa wa rayuwata wlh ko sunan shi ka kira sai naji kaman tuntuni sunane mai kima da daraja a raina, kar kazaci ina fad'a ne dan kaji dad'i, kawai ina fad'a maka gaskiyan abunda yake zuciyata ne.

Wani girma da kima na musamman yau ta kara a idon sa, ya dubeta yace Safiyyah kin gama da rayuwata tunda kikaso dan uwana mafi soyuwa a gareni wlh duk wanda yaso Faisal dina nima zan sosa koda kuwa baya cikin tsarin rayuwata, Faisal shine duk wani farin cikina a duniya, yau soyayyanki ta nunku a zuciyata fiye da yadda nasan shi da.
Safiyyah? Ya kira sunan ta a hankali.

Maimakon ta amsa saita tashi da ganin bakinsa zai iya sub'ucewa yau yace yana sonta, tace zan tafi gidan su Sumayyah!

Yace wai meyasa kike kokarin nunamun kaman Sumayyah tafini muhimmanci ne a gurinki alhalin da ba haka kikemun ba"

Tace kayi hakuri Yaya Fa'izu ba ina nufin Sumayyah tafika bane, saidai dukan ku wani barine na rayuwata,  dama Sumayyah ce tace tanaso ta futar mana da ankon da zamusa a bikin Yaya Faisal yau, shine zanje kartaga kaman ban dauki abun da muhinmanci ba.

A take yaji haushi abunda ya furta akan Sumayyah saiya saka hanu a aljihun sa yace nima ga nawa gudumawar ba yawa ku dada a siyayyan da zakuyi.

Baki ta tura tace Yaya Fa'izu muna da kudin mu, kawai kabar abunka"

D'aure fuskan sa yayi sosai ya fara magana cikin b'acin rai yace "wato Safiyyah baki d'aukeni mutum mai muhimmanci a rayuwar kiba kaman yarda na dauke ki, wlh Safiyyah ko kasa kika iba kika bani zan karba kuma nayi farin ciki, amma ni bankai na miki kyauta ki karba ba, so tari abun na konamun rai ina shurune kawai amma nayau yafi na ko yaushe cimun rai, kije nima zan zauna a matsayin da kika ajiyeni ya juya zai shiga cikin gidan.

Da gudu taje tasha gaban sa, ganin kaman bazai saurareta ba yasa ta saka mishi kuka, tunda suke bai tab'a ganin kukanta ba sai yau abun saiya mungun d'aga masa hankali shima ya tsuguna ya kira sunanta da muryan da zuciya ta narke da soyayyan wanda yake sauraro yace "Safiyyah mai kikeso danine yau kin kona mun rai yanzu kuma so kike kisamun ciwon zuciya, ya karasa maganan yana tab'a saitin zuciyar sa.

Da sauri ta fara girgiza kanta kafin tace " Yayanah kayi hakuri wlh banyi da ninyan kuntata maka ba, naga kana da hidimane a gabanka shiyasa amma kayi hakuri ka yafemun bazan sake ba, yanzu kabani kud'in zan karba kuma namaka alkawarin bazan sake kin karban duk abunda ya futo daga hanunka ba.


Baice mata komai ba yasaka hanu a aljihu ya ciro kufi ya dad'a akan wanda ya fara bata kafin ya mika mata tareda tsareta da ido.

Da farko saida ta zaro idonta gudun karya sake fushi yasa ta sake masa murmushi ta karba tare da cewa nagode Allah ya jikam malam yakai haske kabarin sa" bata jira jin maize ceba ta shige cikin gida, bayan ta yabi da kallon yana sauke wani sihirtaccen murmushin da shi kadai yasan ma'anar sa.


Tana shiga gida direct dakin Innanta ta shige tana zuwa ta zuba mata kudin a cinyan ta.

Inna tace kudin waye wannan Safiyyah?

Tace Inna zan miki bayani daga baya, saidai inaso kimun wani taimako yanzu Inna"

Inna tace taimako kuma? Wani irin taimako kikeso na miki ke kuwa"

Ta gyara zaman ta tace Inna dan Allah kar kicemun a'a, inaso kije kisamu Maman Sumayyah ne ki rok'a mana abarta muje bikin Yayan Fa'izu a Bauchi"

Inna tace "kema kinsan iyayanta bazasu tab'a bari tayi tafiya ba"

Tace "Inna nasan indai kika shiga da kanki zasu barta ki taimakeni kije dan Allah Inna na"

Inna tace to zanje amma kimun bayanin inda kika samu kud'in nan!

Safiyyah ta gyara zaman ta dakyau dan so take Inna ta yarda da k'aryan da zata mata tace " Inna kinsan akwai soyayyah mai k'arfi tsakanin Yaya Fa'izu da Sumayyah, to shine ya bani wannan kudin muje muyi siyayya dan yace inshi ya bata bazata karb'a ba"

Inna wani abu taji ya tokare mata kirji, sai tayi saurinyin yin istingifari dan kar shedan ya kawo mata kishin abun a ranta, amma har zuciyar ta tayiwa y'arta sha'awan Fa'izu domin yarone mai hankali da tarbiyyah, afili kuma tace kai amma nayi farin ciki ko ba komai yanzu Sumayyah zata futa daga cikin k'uncin datake ciki, yanzu tashi ki rakani nasamu iyayenta.

Har sunje k'ofan gidan Safiyyah tace "Inna dan Allah karki fad'a musu Yaya Fa'izu yana sonta kibari sai nan gaba, dan yace sai ya gama shirin komai yakeso ya fad'a da bakin sa"

Inna tace to shikenan insha Allahu ba wanda zaiji daga bakina.

Suna shiga Safiyyah ta gaishe da Mama, tace Mama zamuje kasuwa da Sumayyah "

Mama tace ku dawo lafiya, amma kina addu'a kafin ki tab'a jikin ta dankar ta goga miki bakin jini, suka futa sukabar su da Inna.

Inna ta dubi Mama tace wani alfarma nazo nema a wajenki"

Mama tace fad'a kanki tsaye ai tsakanin mu ba alfarma.

Inna tace dama zamuje biki Bauchi ne shine Safiyyah tace bazataji dadin zuwa ita kad'e ba, shine nace to bazan zo na roka mata alfarman ko za'a bamu aron kawarta mutafi tare!

Mama tace aiba saikin shigo da kanki ba ko aikowa kikayi bazan iya hanawa ba saboda tsakanin mu mun wuce makota mun dawo y'an uwa, yaushe ne tafiyan tasu?

"Inna tace nan da kwana biyar ne "

Sukayi sallama har zaure ta rako Inna suka dan tab'a hira kafin ta wuce.


Suna zuwa kasuwa Safiyyah tace ki zab'a mana kayan da zamusa a Bauchi dan so nake mutane su dauka mu tagwaye ne, sannan mu zab'i atamfa da leshi da shadda kala biyu wanda zamuyi tsaraba dashi.

Sumayyah tace kedawa zakuje Bauchi?

Dake mana dan nasan Mama harta amince ma, ta bata amsa.

Sumayyah ta washe baki tace kai amma danaji dad'i wlh, saidai bani da kudin ko zaren dinki ballantana atamfa.

Safiyyah tayi dariya tace kina da Yaya Fa'izu kice baki da kudi, ya bani kudin dazai wada cemu siyan komai sannan nima na dauko kudin da yayuna suke bani da Baffanah na had'a duka muyi siyayyh.

Baki Sumayyah ta sake tanata maimaita kalman inada Yaya Fa'izu kuma a zuciyar ta.

Da ido tayita bin Safiyyah har suka gama siyayyan su, daganan suka wuce wajen tela suka rokesu su musu dinkin nanda kwana uku, telan yace na mazan kaman wa zanyi?

Tace kaman Yaya Adam dina d'aya less da atamfan kayi dinkin dattijiwa, har suka rabu bata samu tama Safiyyah tambayoyin da yake ranta ba, zata shiga gida ta kwalawa Safiyyah kira, tana juyowa tace k'awata kinfi JININA saita shige gida da sauri, Safiyyah tayi murmushi kawai kafin itama ta shiga gida, har Fa'izu ya tafi Bauchi tak'i bari su had'u koda ya shigi mawa Inna sallamah rufe idonta tayi ta fara baccin k'arya, saida ya futa kafin ta tashi.

Duk abunda takeyi Inna na lura da ita, duk sai tausayin y'arta ya kamata tace ya Allah karaba y'ata dason maso wani.

Yau ya kama daurin aure inda Gidan su Safiyyah sukayi sammakon tafiya kuma yau Umma Hajara zatayi waliman bud'e gidan Ya'yanta.

Goggo Larai tana zaune tana ta musu habaici a tsakar gida, Umma Hajara taje ta sameta tace Larai yau zanyi walima a gidan da Faisal zai zauna shine nace bazan fad'a miki ko zaki samu zuwa?

Goggo Larai tace "ai dole naje kodan ganin irin gidan da aka kama haya, dan saidai sukare da zaman gidan haya, gina gida sai d'ana Ibrahim.

Itadai Umma Hajara ta tashi ta bata waje tana murmushi kawai, suna cikin aiki sukaji sallaman baki Umma ta daga kai ta kallesu ganin Adam yasa ta gane da iyayensa yake tafe ta musu lalai da zuwa.

Daki suka shiga aka shinfida musu taburma, kafin aka cika musu gaban su da kayan abinci.

Fa'izu ne ya shigo fuskan shi na nuna tsantsan farin ciki, ya musu sannu da zuwa kafin suka gaisa Safiyyah da Sumayyah suka gaishe sa, bayan sun gamacin abinci  Safiyyah tama Sumayyah halamu da taba Umma tsaraban ta, ta tashi ta d'auko wani Leda ta mikawa Umma cikin ladabi tace Umma gashi kiyi futan biki dashi.

Ba Umma bama harsu Fa'izu da Adam sunji dad'in gudumawar data bayar, Umma tace to y'arnan Allah ya miki albarka yasa ki gama da duniya lafiya.


Safiyyah tace Ameen kafin ta dubi Fa'izu tace Yaya Fa'izu ina ango muje mu gaisa"

Yace to barin kirasa ma yazo ya gaishe dasu Inna ya tashi ya futa.

Safiyyah ta dauki leda tace Sumayyah muje naba Yaya Fa'izu aikansa, suna futa suka hadu a zaure suna k'okarin shigowa, Safiyyah tace ko ba'a fad'a ba nasan kaine ango, haka kawai yaga ta burgesa yace nima ko ba'a fad'a ba nasan kece Safiyyah kanwar Adam.

Tayi dariya tace ka fad'i bani bace gatanan kusa dakai ta nuna masa Sumayyah.

Fa'izu yace ashede Safiyyah ta ta iya k'arya.

Ta turo baki tace Yaya Faisal kaga yacemun wai na iya karya ko"

Faisal cikin dariya yace toyi hakuri mundaina kulasa"

Tadubi Fa'izu ta murguda masa baki tace daga yau Yaya Faisal kai kad'ene Yayanah, muje waje nabaka sakonka tama Fa'izu gwalo suka futa.

Suna futa ta daidaita nutsuwarta tace Yaya Faisal ga gudumawa ta dan Allah kasa daya yau in dakaje daurin aure dayan kuma gobe, ta karasa maganan kaman zatayi kuka"

Yace ko yanzu kikace nasa zan saka dan haka kawai nakejin ki kaman kanwata da muka futo ciki daya.

Ta mishi godiya kafin tace Yaya Faisal akwai maganan da zamuyi kafun mu koma Allah yasa zakamun kyakykyawan fahinta."

Yace insha Allahu, kafin suka koma ciki suna tafiya kaman wanda suka jima suna tare, a daki suka tadda su Fa'izu har sun shigi.


Fa'izu yace......



Mmn Ashfaq
