🌐 HAJOW 🌐
Hakuri da juriya online writer's



🅿️21\22
Fa'izu yace "har maganan taku ta k'are da wuri haka, ai nazaci saikun kai gobe kunayi"

Faisal ne ya cafke da cewa karki bashi amsa kanwata yana kishin ba'ayi dashi bane, kuma nagane wayon sa so yake nafad'a masa abunda mukace, kuma bazan fad'aba domin yau na yayeka ita kad'ece k'anwata yanzu.

Duka suka gwashe da dariya Inna taji dadin yadda y'an uwan Fa'izu suka karb'esu, itadai Safiyyah sai gwalo take masa, Balkisu ta saci idon Goggo Larai wuf ta shige d'akin Umma Hajara itama tama baki sannu da zuwa kafin ta zauna aka fara hira da ita,  Safiyyah tace Yaya Fa'izu bamusan sunan wannan y'ar uwarkun ba?

Fa'izu cikin tsokana yace niba Yayanki bane bazan fad'a ba, ya dube Balkisu yace yar uwata zomu zauna tare tunda nima sun wareni.


Faisal yace kin manta nine yayanki ni zaki tambaya,  ya rike amsan shi nizan fad'a miki, sunanta Zainab?

Balkisu ta kwashe da dariya tace lallai Zainab ta gama da zuciyar Yayana tunda harka kira kanwanka da sunanta, suka kwashe da dariya dukansu dakin kafin aka tab'a hira,  lokacin sallah nayi suka shirya tafiya masallaci.

Kaman yadda yama Safiyyah alkawarin zai saka kayanta a wajen dauriin aure, ya bude kayan sai yaga riga da wondo da malun malun sai wani jamfa dabam ko wanne da agogo da takalmi da hulansa, yayita juya kayan yana mamakin kyautan da Safiyyah ta masa, atake yaji yana mata son dazaima kanwan sa da suka futo ciki d'aya, yaji mamakin da ta dauko kayan duka best colour dinsa sky blue ne sai jamfan nervy blue, ya d'auki mai malum malun d'in ya saka shi ya saka farin takalmi da hula mai blue da ratsin fari hakama a gogon ya fesa turaran Dubai da body pray na presedent, kansa yasan kayan sun masa kyau gashi kaman tasan size dinsa yayi murmushi yace nasan ba kowa bane zai fad'a mata size d'ina ba sai Fa'izu.

Yana futowa sukaci karo da Fa'izu, baki ido da hanci Fa'izu ya bishi dashi, kafin yace Faisal yaushe muka fara haka dakai, kaifa da bakinka kacemun na d'inka mana farar shadda iri daya dan anko kakeso muyi ranan daurin aurenka, shine saida kabari nasaka nawa kai kuma ka zaga ka dinka wanda ranka yakeso, amma meyasa baka fad'amun dinkin dana mana be maka kyauba,  aida saina sake maka wani ya karasa maganan fuskansa na nuna rashin jin dad'in abunda Yayansa ya masa.

Ya ratsa ta gefensa ya wuce yana cewa inma kishi kakeyi don wani kad'e tayi bata makaba gonda ka dena, nasan dole kasan da kayan inba haka ba taya zatasan size din jikina da takalmi da hula na, ya tafi ya barsa da mamakin wato kayan Zainab har yafi nashi muhinmance tun kafin ta shigo gidan, kawai saiya juyo yabi bayan sa, suka nufi d'akin Umma.

Yana shiga dakin Umma Safiyyah na ganin sa batasan lokacin data furta masha Allah, Yayanah kafi kowa kyau kafito sak angon ka.

Yayi murmushi yace aikece sirrin kyaun domin ke kika futo da Yayanki, ya dubi Umma yace "Umma kinga kayan da k'anwata ta kawo mun nayi kyau ko?

Umma a ranta ta jinjina kara irinta dangin su Adam, tace masha Allah kace duk mun daura musu nauyi Allah ya saka da alkairi ya k'ara muku zumunci" Inna da bata fahinci wace kanwan sa ake nufi ba tace Ameen,  ai ba abunda yafi zuminci dad'i.

Fa'zu yace Umma zamu wuce masallaci bazamu dawo ba harsai an daura auren.

Suka musu addu'a da fatan alkairi, kafin suka futa, suna zuwa zaure Fa'izu yace Faisal kasamun kai a duhu, wata kanwanka ce d'aya ta maka kayan nan dahar tafini a wajenka?

Faisal ya dubesa dakyau kafin yace "dawa akakai dinkin kayan nan?

"Wlh ban saniba" ya bashi amsa.

Yace "kana nufin Safiyyah bata fad'a maka tamun dinkin nanba? To waya bata size dina?

Shuru Fa'izu yayi na wani lokaci,  wani farin cikine ya baebaye zuciyar sa, cikin tsantsan nuna farin cikinsa yace ba abunda na sani dangane da kayan nan kona Umma ma danaga sun mika mata a lokacin na sani kuma nasan wannan tsarin kyautan duka na Safiyyah ce.

Faisal yace to yanzu andaina fushin bansa wanda kamun ba?

Kafin ya bashi amsa Adam ya shigo yana cewa amma kun manta lokacin sallah yayi ko kuka tsaya anan, kuzo mu tafi inyaso idan muka dawo saiku k'arasa.

Sukayi dariya kafin suka futa gaba dayansu, suka shiga motan da Faisal ya karb'o musu agun Alh Sulaiman

Ko a wajen daurin aure su Fa'izu sunga bariki inda Baba Abu da kanta ta futo da goro kafin ta zab'i wanda zai daurawa y'arta aure, Faisal jikinsa yayi sanyi yace karde abunda mutane suke fad'a masa akan Zainab da iyayenta gaskiyane, gabaki d'aya ya nemi farin ciki ya rasa sai wani kunci da yakeji a zuciyarsa.

Bayan an d'aura aure abokansa sukazo sunata tatashi murna amma ko murmushin yake ya kasa musu  sai hanu kawai dayake ta mika musu, ubangidan sa Alh Sulaiman ya karaso inda yake fuskan shi kwata² ba walwala yace kazo ka sameni a gida anjima,  yana gama fad'an haka ya wuce abunsa.

Fa'izu ne tausayin d'an uwansa ya kamasa sai yanzu yake dana sani rashin shedawa d'an uwansa bayason Zainab, amma yanzu ba yadda suka iya da ikon Allah, daidai ya iso wajen sa da zumman yaja shi su tafi gida saiga Zainab ta futo da gudu ta ratsa tacikin mutane tazo ta rungumesa tace "yau ina cikin farin ciki kasancewa ta mata a gareka, ka shirya yau na maka tanadi mai d'umbin yawa dan ba k'awar da zata kwana mun a d'aki, nace musu yau mijina zai angwance dani ya ratsa big body na.

Zare ido kawai Faisal yakeyi yana kallon y'an uwansa da abokan sa, karshe dawowa yayi kaman mutum mutumi kowa a wajen ya kasa magana, sai Ibrahim ne ya kwashe da dariya yace kinmun daidai amarya zanji dadin yadda zaman ku zai kasance, yanzu sakesa ki koma gida karya rasa nutsuwar sa ya sake kwashewa da dariya har yana rike ciki.

Adam ne yazo ya fisgeta ya wurgata gefe yaja hanun Faisal sukabar wajen shima zuciyarsa na kuna, duka yan uwa suka kama hanyan gida jiki duk a sanyaye suna tafiya a mota sai yanzu Faisal yaji wasu hawaye med'umi suna karyo masa, Fa'izu ya kama hanunsa yace ba lokacin kuka bane yanzu ka sake ranka nasan tsantsan farin cikin samun abunda ranta yake sone ya sata aikata haka, kayi mata kyakykyawan zato haka yayita fad'a masa kalamai masu dad'i har suka koma gida, zuwa lokacin yaji sanyi a zuciyar sa, suna shiga y'an uwa suka fara tsokanan sa ango ango! Ango kasha kamshi, amma kwata² babu farin cikin da angwaye sukeyi na ranan auransu a fuskan sa, Umma ta lura da baya cikin farin ciki saita kirasa suka shiga kuryan d'aki tace Faisal yanaga akasin abunda nake fatan gani a fuskan ka?

Cikin karayan murya yace Umma ina zargin abunda mutane suke fad'amu akan Zainab gaskiya ne, Umma yau tun kafin muje wajen d'aurin auran nan nakejin kaman na fasa auranta, nan ya bata labarin duk abunda ya faru a wajen d'aurin aure be b'oye mata ba har runguman data futo cikin baiyanan jama'a ta masa da abunda Ibrahim yace duk ya fad'a mata,  ya daura da cewa Umma ki amince mun nasake ta yanzu dan inaji a jikina ita ba alkairi bace a gareni.

Umma taji ba dad'i a ranta amma dayake uwace tagari saitayi ta mishi nasiha mai shiga jiki,  ta d'aura da cewa koya ka sameta kayi hakuri ka karb'eta, haka taka kaddaran yake saikayi addu'an Allah ya baka ikon cin jarabawa, saida ta tabbatar hankalinsa ya kwanta kafin ya futa daga dakin.

Safiyyah tabi bayansa daya futa tace Yaya Faisal an d'aura aure lafiya?

Saida ya sauke ajiyan zuciya kafin yace lafiya qalau kanwata.

Suka zauna a dakalin k'ofan gidan sunata hira, har y'an tafiya walima suka futo, Umma tace Safiyyah dama kina waje muketa nemanki a ciki, to taso mutafi walima.


Tama Faisal sallama suka tafi, Goggo Larai da Hafsat sune kan gaba ana isa anguwan Goggo Larai ta sake baki tana ganin gidaje masu kyau bata sake tsurewa ba saida taga motoci suntsaya a kofan wani gida mai shegen kyau, kowa ya gama futowa aka tsaya ana jiran isowar Umma, Goggo Larai ta d'aga murya tana cewa jama'a tsayuwar me mukeyi anan, ko motan iya tsayawan ta kenan zamu k'arasa da k'afa"

Da yake duk wajen ba wanda baisan halinta ba, shiyasa ba wanda ya kulata, can Fa'izu suka iso da Umma,  ta futo itada malaman da zatayi wa'azi a wajen tace kuyi hakuri dan Allah mun biya dauko Malama ne"

Sukace ba komai ai muma yanzu muka iso, ta ciro key a jakarta tayi bissimillah ta bud'e gidan ta kama hanun Safiyyah suka fara shiga bakinsu dauke da sallamah, kafin Umma tace wa mutanen bissimillah ku shigo daga ciki.

Goggo Larai tace eye kin wani fara shigewa saikace wani gidanki,  dad'in abundai kowa yasan gidan hayane.

Kanwan Malam tace Larai duk wanda yace miki gidan hayane to karya ya miki wannan gidan y'ayana ne gidan da suka gina da guminsu, kinsan duk d'an daya gama da ubansa lafiya dole zaiga cigaba a rayuwansa.

Wani tsufane hade da jiri ya kwashewa Goggo Larai da gudu Hafsat taje ta tareta tana cewa ki nutsu Goggo karya suke miki.

Goggo Larai tace.....


Mmn Ashfaq

🌐 HAJOW 🌐
Hakuri da juriya online writer's.


🅿️23\24
Goggo Larai tace Hafsa kice mun gidan hayane wannan ba y'ay'an Hajara bane suka gina, indai su suka gina bazan tab'a yafewa kaina ba,  nama Ibrahim dina alk'awarin shizai farayin gini kafin su.

Hafsa tace laifin kine Goggo da kika zuba ido wa Umma Hajara da y'ayanta kika barsu sunayin abunda sukaga dama, gashi rana d'aya sunyi abunda zuciyarki ta kusan bugawa, wanda ko mutuwa kikayi yau ba fasa bikinsu zasuyi ba, dan haka tun wuri kisan inda dare ya miki.

Balkisu da take tsaye akansu tanajin duk abunda suke cewa tace "haba Hafsa keda zaki nuna mata abunda takeyi ba daidai bane,  amma sai kike sake hura mata wutan abun, baki suka sake suna kallonta da mamaki a idonsu, taci gaba da cewa wlh Goggo tun wuri gwanda ki tuba ki koma ga Allah domin yanzu rayuwar duniya bata da tabbas, idan ka shuka sharri wlh tun kafin ka mutu kake ganin abunda ka shuka, dan naga ishara akaina, tunda nayi aure k'annan miji da y'ay'an yayunsa suka sakani gaba ko tsakar gida ban isa na futo ba,  da zaran na futo masu zagina dabam masu mungorin halin uwata dabam, sannan k'ananan yaran da gangan zasu futo kaman zasu wuce saisu takani kuma su zageni suce nasaka musu k'afa a hanya.

Tun lokacin nagane muzgunawa Umma Hajara ne da mukayi yajawo mun haka, na lallab'a nazo na nemi gafaranta, tun lokacin nasamu sauki a gidan aurena, yanzu kinga da dangin mijina mukazo biki.

Goggo Larai ta mik'e tace haba konaji shiyasa naga yanzu kina binsu kaman ragumi da akala, to bari kiji wlh Hajara bata isa ta mallakemun y'aba bokanta yayi kad'an wlh, dole naje na nema miki maganin karya tambaya domin nasan Hajara ba banza tabarki ba.

Balkisu ganin bazasu fahinceta ba yasa taja k'afanta ta shige cikin gidan, bayan anyi addu'o'i kowa yama gidan fatan alkairi, mutane suka tashi suka fara zagaye gidan suna sanbarka gida yayi kyau Allah ya sanya alkairi, sai lokacin Goggo Larai suka shigo da Hafsa sunata raba ido kafin suma sukabi mutane, Goggo Larai ganin gidan saida ya kusan kunce mata kwakwalwa tafara sambatu tana cewa wlh mutane kufara tsoron Hajara da Y'ayanta domin sun shiga k'ungiyan mayu.

Malaman datayi wa'azi tace haba baiwar Allah kisaka albarka wa yara, idan bazaki iyaba kiyi shuru dan gudun tsayuwa a gaban Ubangiji, kince sun shiga meta kin tab'a ganin sun kashe wani ne?

Goggo Larai tace inba meta suka shigaba a ina suka samu kud'in da suka gina wannan k'aton gidan, kowa yasan duk wanda ya gina gida irin wannan to zan iya rantse miki da kud'in meta akayi sa.

Umma Hajara tazo taja hanun Malaman tace idan kika biyewa wannan yanzu saita saka miki haukan da take da ita"

Maganan ya mungun k'onawa Goggo Larai rai tace dole kicemun mahaukaciya mana tunda kinga zan tona miki asiri a gaban mutane susan halinda kuke ciki, haka taro ya watse kowa yana Allah wadar da hali irinna Goggo Larai.


Faisal yana ganin sun tafi shima ya yaje kiran da ubangidan sa yake masa, bayan sun gaisa, Alh Sulaiman ya dubesa yace "Faisal ina maka kallon yaro mai hankali da tunani, ashe ba haka bane, ina tunaninka ya tafi?  Meya rud'eka ajikin yarinyan nan? yanzu kama iyayenka adalci kenan? Karasa wani sakayya zakama ma'aifiyarka saika bata suruka mara tarbiya, yarinyan da uwarta bata da tarbiya ballan tana asa tsammanin za'a samu a wajen y'ar, yanzu idan kuka haifu me zakace wa y'ayanka, cemusu zakayi ka kasa sama musu uwa tagari kenan? taya zaka zamto mai adalci alhalin ka kasawa kanka adalci, Faisal ka kasance mai tunani da bincike kafin ka aiwatar da abu, wlh inhar bakayi kokarin gyara rayuwar kaba tabbas zakayi danasani babba a rayuwar ka, danasanin da bazaka iya yafewa kanka ba nan gaba, shikenan kiran da nake maka kenan tashi ka tafi abunka Allah ya baku  zaman lafiya.

Faisal jikin sane yayi sanyi ya kasa kwankwaran motsi a wajen,  saida Alh Sulaiman ya sake cewa " nace ka tashi ka tafi, amma ka rik'e a ranka duk abunda ya faru kaika jawo wa kanka.


Faisal na futowa daga gidan Alh Sulaiman ya kashe wayarsa ya tafi gida lokacin da iya isa gida ya samu basu dawo daga walimah ba direct dakinsu ya wuce ya k'ulle kansa a ciki, lokacin da Fa'izu suka dawo bai ganshi a gida ba, yayita kiran wayan sa amma a kashe har lokacin zuwa daukan amarya yayi suka tafi, kawayen amarya suka futo sukace dole sai angwaye sun basu kudin futa da amarya kafin ta futo, Adam bayason hayaniya dasu ya cire dubu biyar ya basu, Baba Abu itada kanta  ta futo kai Zainab gidan ta, bisa al'adar Bauchi ba'akai amarya direct dakinta, gidan iyayen miji ake fara kaita ida ta kwana akayi wuni, zuwa yamma ko dare sai akaita d'akinta, Baba Abu naganin sun d'auki hanyan kofan ram sai tace direba ina zakayi damune?

Yace zankai amarya gidan surukanta ne"

Baba Abu tace ba dani za'ayi wannan banzan al'adar ba gwanda ka mike ka kaimin y'ata gidanta tun kafin na tara muku mutane.

Dama abokin Faisal ne me tuka motan sai ya rage gudun motan ya kira number Faisal yaji a kashe, saiya kira number Fa'izu yana d'agawa yace "Fa'izu wata dattijuwa a cikin motan da amarya take tace wai baza akawo amarya gidan kuba" Baba Abu ta d'aga murya tace uwarka ce dattijuwa bani ba, kace musu uwar amarya tace baza'akai mata yarta ko inaba sai dakinta ehe.

Adam yana gefe yanajin duk abunda suke cewa ya karba wayan a hanun Fa'izu yace ko daji tace ka kaisu ka kaisu wawayen mutane jahilan banza kittt ya kashe wayan.

Daga nan aka wuce da amarya gidan ta, Baba Abu tace Zainab saidai kiyi hakuri nikam yau ga kafad'ata ga naki dan anan zan kwana sai gobe idan angama wuni sai na tafi gida.

Zainab ta zabura tace haba Baba Abu kema kinsa nasha maganin mata yamun yawa dan har tsaki nakeji anayi a kasata wlh inyayi tsaki wajen sai tsikan jikina duk ya tashi ji nake kaman naci babu, dan Allah kitafi nasamu yazo ya ragemun inyaso gobe saiki dawo da wuri, tunda ba budurcine daniba kinga ko ya kwana yana kusantata bazanji komai ba.

Baba Abu tace wlh koke kika kawo jaraba duniya yaukan anan zan kwana, inyaso gobe ku shekara kunacin junanku, ba yadda ta iya dolenta tabar Baba Abu ta kwana,  tayita neman number Faisal akan yazo ko a daya bangaran ya rage mata zafi amma wayan shi yana kashe dole ta hakura ta shiga bayan gida tayita shafa jikinta tana tura yatsunta cikin kasanta tayi released yakai sau biyar amma bataji koda d'igon sha'awan da takeyi ya raguba saima karuwan da yake tayi.


Ummah koda taji labarin cewa Zainab tace baza tazo nan gidan ba, tace ba komai ai duka d'ayane, Fa'izu ba irin neman da basuyiwa Faisal ba amma har shabiyun dare basu sameshi ba gashi bayida number Alh Sulaiman ballantana ya kirasa yaji ko yana wajen sane.

Adam yace muje mucire manyan kayan nan inyaso sai muje wajen aikinsu megadi ya mana kwatancen gidan Alh Sulaiman din sai muje mu dubasa acan,  Fa'izu yana bud'e dakin yaga Faisal yanata sharan baccinsa hankali kwance, Fa'izu yace Alhamdulillah kaganshi ya d'aga mana hankali ashe yananan yanata sharan baccin sa.

Adam yace banga lefinsa ba duk wanda ya auri irin matarsa dole shima haka zaiyi domin banga abun d'auki anan ba, Fa'izu yace hakane nasan shima d'aukinta ne ya futa a ransa.

Adam yace subhanallah bamu kaiwa su amarya komai ba, tashi muje Fa'izu Allah yasa musamu masu kayan ciye ciye.

Fa'izu ya cigaba da cire kayansa yana gamawa ya kwanta yace nikam ban manta ba ina sane,  kawai banyi niyan siya musu komai bane"

Adam yace nima wlh sunbani haushi,  ya kamata muma murama tanan, nasan sunacan zuciya fal kunci, shima ya kwanta abunsa sukayi baccin su.

Gidan amarya kuwa sunacan sunata jiran kayan motsa baki amma shuru, haka suka kwana da kuncin abunda angwaye suka musu, washe gari Umma tayi abun karyawa tasa Fa'izu yakaisu Balkisu da Safiyyah da Sumayyah suka kai musu, suna shiga Zainab idonta ya sauka akan Safiyyah sai taji ta tsaneta azuciyarta tace dubeta dakyau kaman aljana.

Suka shiga d'akin da sallaman su suka gaishesu, kafin suka ajiye musu abun karyawa Zainab tace suwaye ku?

Safiyyah ce ta basu amsa da cewa "sunana Safiyyah wannan Balkisu mu k'annen Yaya Faisal ne wannan kuma sunanta Sumayyah itace wanda Yaya Fa'izu zai aura"

Sumayyah da sauri ta juyo ta kalleta da tambaya a idonta, Safiyyah ta mata halamun da tayi shuru, a tunaninta Safiyyah batason ta nuna kantane shiyasa tace  itace, Zainab ta yamutsa fuska tace to naji zaku iya tafi.

Suka taso sunajin mamakin abunda amarya ta musu koda suka shiga mota suna hanyan dawowa sai Balkisu tace "d'an uwana naga amaryan mu lallai ka iya zab'e, kasamu kyakykyawan yarinya ga nutsuwa da sanin darajan babba.

Safiyyah tace muma ai munyi dace samun irin Yaya Fa'izu a yanzu saimai rabo.

Ya kalli Safiyyah ta madubin saman mota suna had'a ido saita kashe masa ido d'aya, yayi murmushi yace Balkisu shiyasa kullun nake dad'a godewa Allah dayaban mace irinta. Sukayita hira har suka isa gida itadai Sumayyah batace komai ba, suna isa bayan sun sauka a mota Sumayyah taja hanun Safiyyah sukayi gaba tace haba Safiyyah wannan ba halinki bane taya zakiyi ta sake baki kina magana a gaban dangin wanda zaki aura,  wlh zasuga kaman bakida tarbiya duk inda nayita hararanki a mota amma kikaki kulani"

Safiyyah tace karki damu lokacin da zaki fahinci yak'inda nakeyi ya kusan zuwa, nidai dan Allah ki tayani da addu'an samun nasara k'awata.

Sumayyah tace "nasan duk abunda kikeyi na neman farin cikin rayuwar kine, kuma insha Allahu zakiyi nasaran duk abunda kikasa gaba, inaji ajina zakiyi nasara kawata"

Da yamma Safiyyah da Sumayyah suka saka ankonsu ba k'aramun kyau sukayi ba, kowa yagansu saiyace sambarka, Fa'izu da Sumayyah ya faracin karo yace kaikai Sumy anya kece kuwa kinganki kaman wata sarauniya,  da badan nasanki ba sai nace munyi bakuwan sarauniyace a gidan mu,  Safiyyah naganin Umma na wajen saita sake komawa daki dankar ta futo ya fad'a wani abunda Umma zata gane ita yakeso, yayita baza ido yana duba ta ina Safiyyah zata futo dan ya matsu yaga wani irin kyau tayi, sai yaje zai dubata a d'aki tana ganin ya d'aga labule sai tayi saurin tasowa ta matso kusa dashi tace dan Allah kar kace nayi kyau a gaban Umma zanji kunya.

Duk da yaso ya yabeta sai yayi shuru tunda ta rokesa amma a zuciyarsa sai hamdala yakeyi ta futo Umma "tace zo y'ata ni na yabi kwalliyanki tunda naga abun nashi dason zuciya,  taje kusa da Umma tayi magana ta inda bazaiji ba tace Umma ai dole ya yabeta tunda ita budurwan sane, Umma tace haba konaji shiyasa abun yayi yawa, kema y'ata sha kuruminki kema naki nanan zuwa.

Ya matso yace Ummana dan fad'amun abunda tace miki"

Umma tace nak'idin ina ruwanka ina magana da y'ata kace sai kaji"

Ya kalli Safiyyah cikin tsokana yace zan rama ina kinmun wayo kin kwace Faisal da Umma to nima idan muka koma saina kwace Inna da Baffah.

Faisal ne ya futo yasha d'ayan shaddan da Safiyyah ta dinka masa yanata zuba kamshi yace kyalesa kanwata zo mufuta abunmu nakaiki mu zaga gari.

Fa'izu yace dad'in abun nima ga Sumayyah kema taso muje muzaga gari abummu, mukan a mashin zamu tafi ta inda zakifi ganin gari dakyau.

Safiyyah a zuciyarta tace Alhamdulillah second point Yaya Fa'izu ka sake kama kanka, a fili kuma tace eh mun yarda muje idan muka dawo kowa saiya fad'a inda sukaje.


Da daddare Umma dakyar tasaka Faisal ya tafi gidan sa, dazai tafi saida yayita kuka kaman mace Umma tayita masa nasiha mai ratsa zuciya.

Bangaran Zainab amarya kuwa tun mangariba ta kore kowa a gidanta, ta canja kaya inda ta saka wani rigan material shara² gashi ba shafi bra da fant kawai ta saka ta zauna tana jiran isowar angonta.

Karfe goma sha d'ayan dare Fa'izu suka sauk'esa a kofar gidan sa, yace kuzo mu shiga mana Adam yace suwa dan Allah kabarmu mu mutu maza sukaimu, wannan amaryan taka da take hanu inaga muna shiga da tab'arya zata biyo mu"


Yace abun......



Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️25\26
Faisal yace "abun sharrine, kar ku damu nima zan rama ba wanda zan rakasa ranan auransa"


Fa'izu yace mun yarda Allah ya kaimu lokacin, mudai bazu shiga a koromu ba.


Da sallama ya shiga gidan, Zainab najin shigowan sa ta tashi da gudu taje ta rungumesa tana cewa haba masoyi wani irin laifi nayi maka da zaka horani dajin d'umin jikinka, jiya dakyar na iya bacci zuciyata yanata muradin ka, banida burin daya wuce najika a jikina.

Faisal da mamaki ya cikasa ya rasa mezaice mata, yasan dai a kasar hausa amare sunajan aji a ranan darensu na farko, yau sai gashi labari yasha banbam a gidan sa, ya bambareta a jikinsa kafin ya rik'e hanunta yace muje muzauna amaryata.

Koda sukaje wajen zama Zainab tak'i zama a kan kujera ta haye cinyansa ta zauna tana jujjuya jikinta akan sandar girma sa, abunka da sabon ango jiyayi kaman ya kwantar da ita ya fara aiki,  kawai daurewa yakeyi yace tashi na dauko mana kazan muci.

Ta tashi tace amma tsaya na rage maka kayan jikinka kafun ka dauko, ya tashi ta cire masa riga da singilet ta tsuguna, a zatonsa zata zauna a kasane kawai ba zato ba tsammani yaji hanunta ya damk'i sandar girman sa, daya hanun kuma tana kunce zariyan wondon sa har tayi nasaran rabasa da komai na jikin sa, baiyi yunk'urin hanata abunda takeyi ba, saidai ya cika da mamakinta a zatonsa namijine yakeyin abunda take aikatawa ba mace ba.

Ita kuwa ganin abun nasa ya mata yadda takeso ta sauke ajiyan zuciya dan tajin dadin yadda tasamu Faisal dinta, ta jawo hanunsa ta fad'a jikinsa saita had'a bakinsu waje d'aya atake tayi nasaran birkitasa nan danan ya rasa control dinsa ya fara bata hot romance itama tana tayasa.

Cikin bukatuwa tace Faisal wasan ya isa haka,  mukai gaci namatsu najika a jikina,  shiko kokari yakeyi ya cire mata riga dan ba abunda yakeso a jikin mace sama da breast yana cirewa kuwa yakai hanunsa da niyya ya cafke kirjinta kawai saiyaji hanunsa ya cafki kashi, duk da yana cikin hali bukatuwa saida ya tsaya yana kallonta, itako takai makura turesa tayi ya fad'i tahau kansa yana shiga jikinta ta fara kurma ihun dad'i, kasan cewa Faisal sabon shigane shima ya fara zage karfinsa yana bada gudumawan sa sunkai awa biyu suna abu d'aya, shiya fara gajiya yaja jikinsa ya koma gefen kujera yana mayar da nunfashi,  ido ta zaro tana kallonsa tace badai ka gajiba?

Ya nunfasa kafin yace "Zainab dama ke bazaurace baki fad'amun ba"

Jin maganan tayi wani iri tace "bazaura kuma Faisal, ban fahinci abunda kake nufi ba"

Ya gyara zamansa yace "eh bazaura"

Ya cigaba da cewa na farko Zainab ke kika nemeni da kanki sai nayi zaton kodan kina sona ne shiyasa kikayi mun haka, sannan yadda na sameki a bude da kuma kirjinki su suka dad'a tabbatar mun da cewa keba budurwa bace.

Kuka ta saka masa tace haba Faisal irin sakamakon da zan samu kenan a daramun na farko, duk kokarin danayi wajen tanada budurcina,  naki barin kowani namiji yasanni a matsayin y'a mace, shine zakamun sakamako da tukuicin zargi,  ban taba tunanin son karya kakemu ba Faisal sai yau, meye laifina dan narage maka hanya wajen kusanta ta ban bari munyi kokuwan da kauyawan amare sukeyi ba, nidai nasan nayi tsalle tsallen haye ruwa da hawa bishi, kuma ina daukan abu mai nauyi,  inaga su suka bud'ani amma dan Allah mijina karka zargeni da gaske na killace maka kaina, na tsantse maka ba d'a namijin daya tab'a sanina mace.

Ganin zata ishesa da surutu yasa ya tashi yayi shigewar sa toilet, yana shiga ya sakewa kansa ruwa, yana wanka yana tunanin yanzu maza nawane sukasan matan sa, da sauri ya kawar da wannan tunanin gudun kar Allah ya tsayar dashi ranan sheda tunda tayi rantsu bata taba tarawa da kowani namiji ba sai shi, dole na yarda da hakan, wani zuciyar tace masa to ya akayi breast dinta suka yamutse, afili yayi saurinyin istingfari yace inaga ita haka halittanta yake, saiya tuna wani abokinsa yace masa idan kayi aure ka tara da matarka sai kaji takaicin meyasa tuntuni bakayi aure ba saboda dad'in mace ya wuce duk wani tunaninka a duniyan nan.

Da karfi ya furka makaryaci kawai meiye abun dad'i a jikin mace, inba ta tara maka gajiya ba niko a mafarki bani sake sha'awan yin sex, ya gama wankan sa ya futo dan ya farajin yunwa yace abu ko dadi babu sai kwashewa mutum abun cikinsa yakeyi.

Tanajin zai futo ta sake b'are baki tana kuka yazo ya tsuguna a gabanta yace haba Zee meye na kuka kuma bayan kinji dad'inki, yanzu ki tashi kije kiyi wanka kizo mu kwanta muyi baccin mu ko masoyiya, kuma kiyi hakuri yanzu na fahinceki kuma na yarda baki taba tarawa da kowani namiji ba.


Murmushin jin dad'i tayi a zuciyar ta tace dole naje nabaka tukuici boka, saurin zare ido tayi data tuna batayi anfani da maganin da boka ya bata ba, ta tashi tana cewa akwai gobe dole da safe mu kuma domin ban isaba na zauna inaji ina gani namiji ya zauna yana fad'amun abunda yaga dama ba.


Bata san a fili tayi maganan ba yace mata me kikace masoyiya naji kaman kina magana.

Da sauri ta tashe bakinta tace bance komai bafa, tayi sauri shigewa ciki, tana shiga toilet tace lallai ni wawiyace zan tonawa kaina asiri, Allah yasoni baiji abunda nace ba,  soso da sabulu ta dauka tayi wanka dan batasan ya akeyin wankan janaba ba,   danko na haila bata tab'ayi ba yiba a iya rayuwarta.


Tana futowa ta sameshi a kan gado nacin naman daya shigo dashi tace haba Faisal shine koka jirani"

Ya dubeta yace ke kuma haka kikayi kanki ko Jan ajinnan na mata baki iya ba, tayi dariya tace bani da ita indai akan abunda nakeso ne,  tazo tazauna suka faracin naman har tana kwace wanda yakeci,  taso su kuma dan ita inba kwana akayi akanta ba batajin dad'i.

Da safe dakanta ta nemishi haka ya biye mata, saida ta cika gabanta da maganin da boka ya bata, koda ya fara sai yaji duk abun ya ishesa atake abun ya hau masa kai har wani zafi zafi yakeji,  ba shiri ya sauk'a ya shiga wanka yana futowa ya shirya cikin kayan da yashigo dashi da daddare, ta dubesa tace Faisal badai futa zakayi kabarni da sha'awa a marata ba.

Yace "ya isheni ne tunda abun tuwo ne" 

Tace ina dakaje kuma"

"Gidan mu" ya fada lokacin da yake futowa, da ido ta bishi tanacewa dole naje wajen boka ya seseta mun kanka.

Yana futa direct gida ya wuce dasu Fa'izu ya faracin karo sun dawo daga sallan asuba,  Fa'izu yace wazan gani anan kuma"

Adam ya kwashe da dariya yace ango saikace wanda ta koroka?

Ganin abun nasu harda tsokana yasa ya shige ta gefensu ya wuce yace ni kubani waje banida lokacin ku, suma bayansa sukabi sunata tsokanan sa.

Da sallama ya shiga dakin Umma, jin muryan sa yasa Umma dagowa da sauri, a zuciyar ta tace duk yadda kayi yarinyan nan batakai darajan ta dakin taba, taya za'ace karfe shidan asuba ango ya futo daga gidan sa yazo gidan iyayensa.

Safiyyah tace "Yaya Faisal meya kawoka muda muka yayeka jiya maiya kawo ka gidanmu da sassafen nan.

Yace wato Safiyyah hardake ko, hardake a zolayata, kema baki bukatan ganina ko?

Tace bance hakaba Yaya Faisal ai jiya jinayi kaman na bika, amma naga kabar Aunty ka gudo gida da sassafen nan.

Yace wlh naji sha'awan ganin Umma nane, shiyasa naga bazan iya bari gari ya gama wayewa banzo naganta ba.

Ya gaishesu kafin yace Umma bazanje na kwanta kafin kugama abun karyawa.

Adam yace "a ina zaka kwanta badai a dakin muba"

Fa'izu ya cafke da cewa Allah Faisal ka wuce gidan ka, saida ka tafi nagane ashe a takure muke kwana da, amma jiya d'aya bakaga yadda muka bararrajeba.


Inna tace ka kyalesu Faisal karka cewa kowa komai akwai ranan da zaka rama, jeka kwanta abunka d'ana.

Safiyyah ma tace Yaya jeka kwanta dan kasamu ka tashi da wuri saboda inaso muyi magana kafin mu tafi"


Yace kar kicemun yau zaku tafi, amma banji dadiba, saida kikabari nasaba dake shine zaki gudu kibarni, ya dubi Umma yace Umma wlh bansan dad'in K'anwa ba sai akan Safiyyah danso abar mana ita.

Fa'izu yace karka damu tayi kusan dawowa kusa dakai kwanan nan.

Ganin zai b'ata mata shiri yasa tace Yaya Faisal dina muje na rakaka, saida ta rakasa har kofan dakin kafin ta juyo.

Karfe goma ya tashi a bacci, lokacin harsun gama shirinsu shi kadai suke jira ya tashi su masa sallamah su tafi, bayan ya karya lokacin Inna da Sumayyah Fa'izu ya tafi kaisu gidan amarya,  Safiyyah tace Umma inaso nayi wata magana daku"

Faisal yabar danna wayan da yakeyi yace munajin ki k'anwata.

Tace dama akan maganan Yaya Fa'izu ne da.....


Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️27\28
"Dama akan maganan Yaya Fa'izu ne da Sumayyah"

Umma tace "meya faru dasu?

Hawaye taji zai gangaro mata da sauri ta mayar dashi tayi kokarin gyara nutsuwarta da muryanta,  dan sai yanzu da takeson cimma burinta taji zuciyarta ta karaya, jitayi kaman ta fasa domin tana mungun son Fa'izu,  kawar da tunanin tayi ta fuskaci abunda takeson aiwatar wa.

Tace "Umma dan Allah kuma magana ta kyakykyawan fahinta! Taci gaba da cewa Yaya Fa'izu sun jima suna soyayya tsakanin su da Sumayyah har yakai da yamata alkawarin bayan auran Yaya Faisal shima zaiyi maganar aurensu, yaso a had'a bikin lokaci daya amma ganin be shirya ba yasa ya tsayar da nasa bikin sai bayan naka,  satin daya wuce suna zaune da Baffa na suna hira.

Sai Baffa na yace masa Fa'izu ya kamata ace kunyi aure kaida Adam domin girma yanata cin muku.

Cikin zolaya naji yana cewa Baffa na, "Baffa zamuyi saidai matsalan har yanzu bansamu mai sona bane"

Baffa na yace masa indai wannan ne ai me sauk'ine tunda naga akwai shak'uwa mai k'arfi tsakanin ka da Safiyyah kaje ka shirya zan baka ita.

Cikin tashi hankali nabar zauran, ban tsaya naji amsan daya bashi ba, tun lokacin naga yanata ja baya da Sumayyah, nasan Yaya Fa'izu yanamun son K'anwa ce bana matan da yakeso ya aura ba, kuma Umma Sumayyah kawata ce tunna wasan k'asa idan namata haka bansan ya duniya zata d'aukeni ba, gashi tanama Yaya Fa'izu sonda ko ita batasan iyakan saba,  idan zaku lura da yanayin ta tunda mukazo bata cikin walwala  kullun  bata da burin daya wuce taga Yaya Fa'izu yana mata magana.

Dan Allah Umma kushiga lamarin mu, kumun wannan alfarman, Umma Yaya Faisal ku nuna masa lallai kunason ya auri Sumayyah nasan yanajin maganan ku kuma yana gudun bacin ranku,  ta karasa maganan tana fashewa da kuka a zuciyarta tana cewa shikenan na raba kaina da farin cikin rayuwata Allah ka kawo mun d'auki, Allah karka jarabceni da karyan danayi nayine dan cire k'awata daga cikin kuncin danginta.

Umma da Faisal sun yaba da k'ok'arinta sai sukaji ta sake shiga ransu duk da sunso ace itace wanda Fa'izu yakeso na gaskiya, amma duk da haka sunji dad'i daya kasance Sumayyah ce ba wata ba dan itama Sumayyan ba laifi.

Umma ta rungumeta tace indai akan wannan karamun abun kike kuka to kishare hawayenki kinji y'ata dolensa ya hakura ya auri Sumayyah kema Allah zai baki mijinki nagari.


Tace "Umma karkice masa nina fad'a muku dan inyaji nice zaije ya samu Baffa na ya fad'a masa,  kawai inaso ku nuna masa kunason ya auri Sumayyah kawai, duk da nasan zaice muku ni yakeso amma ba haka bane a zuciyarsa,  yana kunyan Baffana ne kawai"

Faisal yace karki damu Safiyyah nasan ta inda zan b'ullo masa,  kiyi shuru abunki kinji k'anwata kwanan nan zaki dawo bikin Sumayyah Bauchi.

Sumayyah ta rasa wanne zatayi d'aya farin cikin samun nasara ko kuma bakin cikin rasa masoyi, Umma tace tashi kije ki wanke fuskanki karsu dawo su zargi wani abu, taje ta wanke fuskanta ta zauna tafara kwalliya.


Fa'izu sun jima suna buga k'ofa kafin Zainab tazo ta bud'e musu k'ofa tana ganin su tace k'anin masoyi dama kaine?

Yace eh mune kinje kinata baccin amarci kinbarmu munata buga k'ofa, kafin suka shiga ciki suna zama idonta ya sauk'a akan Adam tace "Fa'izu dama kasan wannan shashashan mutumin ne wanda besan me yakeyi ba"

Yace "wa kenan?

"Gashinan kun shigo dashi ta dubi Adam tace "dama ina neman ka naja maka kunne, ranan daurin aurena ubanka na runguma da zakazo kawani tureni, wlh kaji sa'an ranan ina cikin farin ciki ne dana yaga maka rigan mutunci a waje wawa kawai.



Adam binta yakeyi da ido kawai ranshi ya mungun baci, yasan idan yaci gaba da zama a wajen za'ayi abin kunya domin zai iya zaneta, saiya tashi ya futa a gidan yaje mota yana jiransu, a zuciyarsa yace dole Safiyyah tace bazata biyomu ba wayasan meta mata.


Fa'izu da idonsa ya kad'a yayi jajazir yace Zainab yanzu abunda kika mana kin kyauta,  kinsan suwaye wa'innan mutane a wajen mu?

Tace Fa'izu tun kafin raina ya b'aci ka tattara wa'innan tsummo karan matan ku futa inba hakaba zan muku abunda baku tab'a zata ba.

Inna tace tashi mu tafi Fa'izu"

Fa'izu kuwa idan ranshi yayi dubu  yau ya baci ya futo yanata huci, Inna ce tayita bashi hakuri har suka iso gida, Sumayyah tacewa Safiyyah to gashi harmun dawo da kikak'i binmu"

Safiyyah tayi murmushi tace "natsaya gyarawa Ummana dakine fa koso kuke mutafi mubarta da aiki.


Shidai Fa'izu sai binta da ido yakeyi yanajin sonta na k'ara shiga zuciyar sa, dama a shirye suke Umma ta cikasu da tsaraban cincin din biki, har tasha suka rakasu saida matarsu ta tashi  kafin suka juyo gida da kewan su,  ahanya Faisal yace "wlh har raina nakejin kewan rabuwa da Safiyyah dan kallonta nakeyi kaman k'anwata  wanda muka futo ciki d'aya.

Fa'izu yace damma bakuyi dogon zama da ita bane, Safiyyah yarinyace mai hankali da sanin darajan Babba, har suka isa gida hiran Safiyyah kawai sukeyi.


Safiyyah kuwa a hanya idan ta kalli Sumayyah sai tayi murmushi, Sumayyah tace k'awata lallai kina cikin farin ciki kodai Umma tace miki muna komawa Yaya Fa'izun ki zasuzo maganan aurene?

Safiyyah tayi murmushi kafin tace ba wannan bane kawai ina farin cikin kin kusa aurene Sumayyah"

Sumayyah ta zaro ido tace aurefa kikace Safiyyah? Toni wa nakeda shi dazai aureni, wlh magananki harya tunamun halin dazan koma ciki a gida,  idonta ya ciko da kwalla.

Safiyyah ta dafata tace karki damu da wannan kuncin domin saura k'iris ya wuce, Sumayyah harna farajin kewarki tun kafin kitafi kibarni, gaskiya zan rasa babban aminiyata kusa dani.

Sumayyah tace wlh harkin bani dariya, kina wani magana saikace a littafin hausa ko shirin film.

Safiyyah tace aiduk abunda kikaga anayi a littafin hausa ko hausa film toki tabbatar abunda ya faru suke fad'akar da mutane masu hali irin na wanda sukayi wasa akansu, kawai idan kina gani ko kina karantawa kisa aranki kaman abun yafaru a gabanki.

Sumayyah tace hakane, to k'awata Allah yasa magananki ya zamo gaskiya.

Safiyyah tace amin kawata ya kusan  zamowa ma insha Allahu, amma inaso kimun wani alkawari"

Sumayyah tace alkawari kuma ki fad'a koma meye ni kuma namiki alkawari yimiki shi koya yake kuwa indai zaisaki farin ciki a rayuwa.

Safiyyah tace inaso kimun alkawarin yarda da duk wanda yazo miki a matsayin mijin aure, banaso ki duba wani abu da yake tsakanin mu ki amince dashi kawai, indai kikamun haka kin gama sani acikin farin ciki a rayuwata.


Sumayyah tace "Safiyyah kullun ina ronkon Allah ya nunamun ranan dazan miki abunda zaisaki farin ciki a rayuwa, wlh Safiyyah ko kuturu ne yazomun a matsayin mijin aure indai kinason sa wlh namiki alk'awarin zan auresa kodan naga farin ciki a fuskanki kaman yadda kika sani, hira sukayi tayi har motarsu ta sauka, suna zuwa unguwan su Inna tace Sumayyah ki shiga gida idan muka huta zan shigo nama Mamanki godiya ko.


Cikin sanyin jiki tace to,  Inna ta wuce harta fara tafiya Safiyyah tace tsaya na rakaki, ta dubi Inna tace Inna bazan rakata gida.

Suna shiga suka tadda Mama ta saka wanke wanke a gabanta zatayi tanata mita, saitaji sallaman su, tace yauwa zokiyi aikin ki dan ba baiwar da kika tafi kika bari, tunda kika tafi ba kwanan da aka wanke a gidan nan yauma zanyine dan bamuda kwanan da zamuci abinci kuma banason k'azanta.


Dukansu ba wanda yayi magana suka tsuguna sukafara wanke wanken, Sumayyah tace Safiyyah kije ki huta kinga mun kwaso gajiyan hanya"

Ko kulata Safiyyah batayi ba har suka gama suna gamawa ta watso musu wanki, shima suka d'auka sukayi, saida ta tabbatar sun gama kowani aiki kafin ta tafi gida.




Y'an uwan Malam dana Umma Hajara suma duk ranan suka koma gida, ya  watse saura Fa'izu ne kawai.

Bayan tafiyan su Safiyyah da kwana biyu Fa'izu ya shirya zai dawo bakin aikinsa, yace Umma nikam nagama shirina sai tafiya idan Faisal ya shigo kice masa natafi.


Umma tace aiko dole ka jirasa domin akwai maganan da zamuyi dakai.



Yace.......





Insha Allahu daga yau sau biyu zakuna ganin posting dina a rana nagode da soyayyannu gareni.



Mmn Ashfaq

Hakuri da juriya onliine writer's



🅿️29\30
Yace to Allah yasa ba laifi nayi ba"

Umma tace bakayi laifin komai ba auta kawai inaso muyi magana mai muhinmanci ne dakai kafin ka tafi, suna cikin magana saiga Faisal ya shigo bayan sun gaisa yace kaima har zaka tafi?


Yace "eh"

Faisal yace aiko inason magana dakai"

Fa'izu yace "yanzu Umma take fad'amun"

Suka zauna kafin Faisal yace "dama ba wani magana bane, naga wata yarinyace tamun shine nayi sha'awan ka aureta,  danhar nace a sanar da iyayenta kwanan nan zamuzo neman auranta.

Umma ta d'aura da cewa nima kaina yarinyan ta kwanta mun arai shiyasa da Faisal yayi maganan ta na amince saboda nasan halin autana, ina alfahari daku a duk inda na zauna, tunda nake daku ban tab'a cewa inason abu kuce a'a ba, yanzu Fa'izu na maka umurnin kaje ka nemi soyayyan yarinyan idan ka koma.

Tunda suka fara magana Fa'izu yaji jiri na shirin zubar da shi, ga wani zufa 🐾 da yake tsatstsafo mishi tun daga tsakiyar kanshi har zuwa tafin k'afan shi, dakyar ya iya cewa a ina yarinyan take?

Faisal yace " a Azare take kuma nasan kunason junan ku sosai, halamun dana gani yasa nace zamuje ayi maganan auran ku"

A fili ya furta Alhamdulillah! Faisal nima nasan ina sonta saidai na kasa fad'a mata cewa inason ta, amma nayi muku alkawarin ina komawa zan shedawa Safiyyah inasonta so na gaskiya bana wasa ba.

Umma a zuciyarta tace tabbas maganan yarinyan nan gaskiya ne,  tace Fa'izu ba Safiyyah nakeso ka aura ba Sumayyah ce tamun a matsayin surukata idan kuma bakason zab'ina shikenan kaje kayi duk abunda kakeso.

Yace Faisal dan Allah kasa baki Umma ta fahinceni wlh najima inason Safiyyah nauyin baki nayi tuntuni,  dana fad'a mata nasan yanzu da tare za'a had'a auran mu.

Faisal ya mishi kallon karka rena mana hankali mana, kafin yace idan harka daukeni dan uwanka to Sumayyah mukeso ka aura idan kuma bamu isa dakaiba kaje ka auri duk wanda ranka yakeso, amma kasani bani a lamarin auranka, yana kaiwa nan ya tashi ya futa abunsa,  Umma ma ta tashi taje ta cigaba da aikin gabanta.

Fa'izu ya rik'e kansa yanata maimaita innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un Allahumma ajirni fi musibati! Yajima yanata  tasbihi a zuciyarsa kafin ya d'an samu nutsuwa idonsa yana zubar da hawaye, yace ya Allah idan laifi nayi Allah ka aikomun da wannan jarabawan Allah na tuba, ubangiji ka dubi lamarina tunda nake ban tab'ajin koda d'igon son Sumayyah a raina ba, Allah na rok'eka idan itace mafi alkairi wa rayuwata kasamun sonta a zuciyata ka ciremun son Safiyyah a raina, yanzu Safiyyah idan taji wannan labarin bansan yazata daukeni ba, dukda ban tab'a furta mata kalman soba amma tasan inasonta kuma nima na hango sona a cikin kwayan idonta, ya zamo dole nasanar mata da kaina bazan bari taji labari a bakin kowa ba.

Yana zaune har Umma ta shigo yaje gabanta ya tsuguna yace "Umma kiyi hakuri ki yafemun idan kikace zakiyi fushi dani wlh bansan ya rayuwata zata kasance ba, indai Sumayyah ce na amince zan aureta idan hakan shine farin cikin ku.

Umma tayi murmushi kafin tace "Allah ya maka albarka inaso idan kaje ka fad'awa yarinyan wani watan zamu turo maganan auran ku"

Yace to Umma insha Allahu zan sanar da ita, kimun addu'an Allah yasa itace mafi alkairi a wajena, sannan ki tayani ba Faisal hakuri akan wannan k'aramun abun bai kamata yayi fushi dani ba, kice masa ko wata ya gani yace na aura koda ban tab'a ganinta ba zan aura ballan tana Sumayyah danasan halinta nasan tarbiyanta da hakurinta ko gobe kukace na aureta indai iyayenta sun amince zan aureta.

Saidai Umma itama a bauchi zata zauna kusa daku tunda banida gida acan kuma banason zaman haya.

Umma tamishi addu'a sosai,  saida yaga ta sake sosai dashi kafin ya iya tafiya, bayan la'asar ya isa Azare tunda yaje yakebin Safiyyah da ido, sai taji ta tsargu tafara tunanin ko Umma sun fad'a masa yadda sukayine.

Bayan sallan isha ya kirata a waya yace tazo ta sameshi a d'akinsu, ta tafi zuciyarta yana d'ar d'ar, bata tab'ajin fad'uwar gaba ba idan ya kirata sai yau, ta shiga da sallaman ta tazauna d'an nesa dashi tace gani.

Cikin sanyin murya yace Safiyyah ina cikin tashin hankali narasa ta ina zan samu sauk'i shine na kiraki kizo ko akwai shawaran da zaki bani!

Tace "to Allah ya futar mana da mafuta"

Saida ya yajuya mata baya dan bayaso idan yana maganan yaga kwayan idonta dan yasan itama zata shiga tashin hankali kaman yadda ya tsinci kansa lokacin dayaji maganan, yaci gaba da cewa "iyayena sunce wai sunason na auri Sumayyah ni kuma ba ita bace a raina akwai wanda nakeso dan Allah ki tayani nemo mafuta"

Dukda ita ta tsara maganan amma daya fad'a mata saida taji wani mummunan tashin hankali da bata tab'a tunanin zataji shiba, hawayene taji ya b'alle mata ta rasa taya zata tsayar dashi Allah ya taimaketa ya juya mata baya dayaga halinda ta shiga, dakyar ta iya saisata muryanta duk da haka saida ya futo yana rawa tace ina zuwa bazan mikawa Baffa abincin sa zan dawo.


Bata jira jin mezaice ba ta tashi ta futa da sauri tana zuwa dakinta ta wuce ta fad'a kan gado ta saki kuka maicin rai, ganin kukan bazai mata ba yasa ta tashi ta dauro alola tayita sallan lafila tana rok'on Allah ya sanyaya mata zuciyarta, cikin ikon Allah kuwa taji sanyi a ranta, zuwa yanzu jitayi kaman ba komai a ranta


Bayan futanta shima ya saka kuka kaman mace yace nasan dole ki girgiza, mu kuma haka tamu k'addaran tazo mana, idan ke mata tace zan aureki ko bayan na auri Sumayyah, ya jima yana kuka kafin ya tashi yayi alola ya gabatar da nafila yana rokon Allah ya kawo musu mafita.


Tunda ta shiga d'aki bata futoba sai washe gari, kasancewar yau asabar bazaije aiki ba tana gama aikin gida taje tasamu Inna tace "Inna zanje gidansu Sumayyah" Inna tace kidawo lafiya.

Tana futowa ta ganshi akan dakali yana zaune yayi zurfi cikin tunani, ta k'arasa tace Yaya Fa'izu ta kirasa yakai sau biyar kafin ya amsa, yace Safiyyah kin futo ina zuwa haka.

Tayi murmushi kafin tace wajenka nazo muje wajen kawata naga kalan farin cikin da zatayi"

Yace wato kema baki fahinceni ba ko?

"Yaya Fa'izu na fahince ka sosai saidai inaso kazamo me ma iyayenka biyayya, bazan baka shawaran ka butulce musu ba musamman uwa, rayuwarka bazata taba albarka ba idan har kakison abunda takeso.


Yace nagode wa Allah daya banike a matsayin kanwa, wlh nasan dakinbi son zuciya da kin tayani butulce wa  iyayena, tabbas ba Sumayyah bace a raina hasalima ban taba sonta ba amma yanzu nasan da taimakonki zanso ta, kuma nasan itama bata sona zanyi kokari na koyawa zuciyata sonta sannan na koya mata sona, kema inaso ki tayani yaki wajen neman soyayyan ta.

Tace insha Allahu yauma zan fara yanzu tashi muje na rakaka, mufara baran soyayyan mu,  
ya tashi suka tafi inda take mamakin irin dauriyan da tayi ko dikon hawaye bataji zaizo mata ba saima farin cikin dataji tana ciki, suna zuwa kofan gidan tace masa ina zuwa bazan kira maka ita yau zanga wanda yafi iya soyayyah a cikin ku.

Bayanta yabi da kallo yana mamakin dama Safiyyah bata sonsa shi yaketa fama da sonta, tunda ko kishin sa bai gani a idonta ba, kodai tana dannewa ne.

Safiyyah tana shiga ta tadda Sumayyah tana karatun qur'ani tace kawata yi sauri kikai aya, tajeta d'auko kayan kwalliyanta ita da kanta ta mata kwalliya ta fesheta da turare.

Sumayyah tace JININA kinyi mafarkin kinmun kwalliyane yau amma daiko?

Safiyyah tace ba lokacin yin bayani ta dauko hijabinta har kasa tasaka mata, tace wow Alhamdulillah kinga yadda kikayi kyau kuwa Sumayyah, zo muje waje ki rakani tad'i, sun futo zasu futa Mama tace yanzu wato abun yaci gaba har za'a futa ba'a sanar daniba.


Safiyyah tace Mama ba anguwa zamuje ba Sumayyah ce tayi bako a waje, Mama ta tab'e baki tace to Allah yasa kar'a koresa ko kuma ya gudu inya fuskanci bakin jininta.


Suna Futa Fa'izu ya kura mata ido a zuciyarsa yana cewa masha Allah itama dai ba laifi tanada kyau saidai Safiyyah ta fita, suka k'araso Safiyyah tace......... 🐾 


Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️31\32
Tace Sumayyah karki manta da alkawarin da yake tsakanin mu, idan kika cikamun alkawarin da kika d'auka mun to zan yarda da kalman da kike kirana dashi na JININA,  kinsan a rayuwa bazan tab'a yarda kije inda zaki cutuba dan Allah karki bani kunya, kuma karkiyi zargin nice na had'a komai, hukuncin Allah ne kawai dan abunda muke zato a zuciyarmu ba lallai ya kasance haka ba, jiya na dad'a tabbatar da hakan bayan naga yadda Allah ya sauya lamarin sa."

Sumayyah ta k'ura mata ido tace "bansan meyasa yanzu kikeson sakani a duhu ba, wani lokacin kinamun maganan da kwakwalwata bazata iya d'auka ba ballan tana na gano bakin zaren, dan Allah kina fahintar dani ta yadda zan gane, bazan taba mantawa da alkawarin mu ba JININA,  wlh na maimata miki ko kuturu makaho gurgu kurma kika kawomun zan auresa, saboda na yarda dake a rayuwata, nasan bazaki tab'ayin abunda zai cutardani ba"

Safiyyah ta dafata tace zan sake maimatawa karki manta da alk'awarin da kikamun, idan kika yarda da abunda zai shiga kunnenki yanzu kin gamamun komai a duniya ta.
 
Sumayyah tayi shuru tanata maimaita abunda zai shigo kunnena yanzu kuma to meye zai shigo kunne na, tanaso ta tambaye ta ganin sun k'araso inda Fa'izu yake yasa sukayi shuru, Sumayyah tayi dariya tace kice tare kuke da mutumin naki, tabbas Iran akaga wata dole za'aga Zara,  ta kallesa tace Yaya Fa'izu ba'a tab'a ganin ka a kofar gidan nan saidai in JININA tana nan, idan baka fara zuwa muna gaisawa ba saina maka halin babbar kawa na ziga ta mujuya maka baya.

Safiyyah ta kwashe da dariya tace kisha kurumin ki Sumayyah yanzu kullun sai yazo, nicema zanna zuwa ina rok'onki kozan samu ganin sa, takai kallonta ga Fa'izu tace Yayana fatan nasara bata jira amsan suba ta kama hanyan komawa gida, idonta ya ciku da kwalla a zuciyarta tace insha Allahu daga yau nadena tunaninka kenan, Allah ya baku nasara akan soyayyar ku.

Yace kidawo ai komai a gabanki za'ayi kince kinaso kiga wayafi iya soyayyah tsakanin mu, amma ko juyowa batayi ba,  sai cigaba da tayi da tafiyan ta.

Sumayyah ta d'aga murya tace JININA wani magana ne zamuyi ba'a gaban kiba dan Allah ki dawo ayi dake" wuf ta shige gida dan inta juyo zasu gane halinda take ciki.


Sumayyah tace Yaya Fa'izu matsala kuka samu?

Yayi murmushin karfin hali yace nasan harna mutu bazamu tab'a samun matsala da itaba, kawai tabarmu mu tattauna ne kar ganin idonta yasa mu kasa magana.

Wani irin magana ne da zamuyi wanda bazata iya tsayawa ba? Ta jefo masa tambayan, sunkai minti uku ba wanda yayi magana, Fa'izu ya rasa ta ina zai fara mata bayani yayi karfin halin cewa.

"Sumayyah Allah yasa ki fahinci abunda nazo miki dashi, ya sake kiran sunan ta, "Sumayyah na jima ina nazari akan halayyanki da rayuwarki, ke macene tagari ba namijin dazai sanki baiji sha'awan zama dake ba saidai mara rabo, inaso dan Allah karki k'i karban uzurina dan bani kad'e nakeda bukatan hakan ba harta iyayena kinbar musu soyayyanki mai karfi a zuciyar su, maganan gaskiya Sumayyah inason ki kuma banida bukatan mutsaya wani bata lokaci tunda kinsan halina nasan naki, burina ki amince mun zuwa karshen watan nan iyayena suzo da maganan auran mu.

Kaman wanda aka razana a bacci haka take kallon sa, tayi murmushi tace Yaya Fa'izu nice fa, nice Sumayyah ba Safiyyah ba nasan ka zata da Safiyyah kake magana, ka bud'e idonka dakyau zakafi ganina, nasan shakuwan mu da Safiyyah yasa kakemun kallon itace ni.

Murmushi mai ciwo yayi a zuciyarsa yace ashe itama tasan Safiyyah nakeso saidai kaddara ta riga fata, yayi saurin danne damuwan sa dan yasamu ya fiskanci abunda yakeso ya cimma.

Yace "Sumayyah nasan kece kuma ke nakeso da muradin aure Safiyyah ma shedace kece burin raina abun kaunata karkice baki sona dan Allah,  kibani dama na koya miki yadda zaki soni, Sumayyah ki dubi idona zaki gane inason ki ki taimakeni ki taimaki rayuwata kice kina sona zaki aureni.

A take taji kuka yazo mata cikin muryan kuka tace maina maka Fa'izu? Maina maka mai muni haka da kakeso ka rabani da k'awata,  daka shiga tsakani na da ita gwanda kamun sharri a wajen aikin ku azo a kasheni, kasan yadda Safiyyah take a wajena kuwa kasan irin soyayyan da nake mata kuwa? Bari kaji wlh Safiyyah TAFEE JININA soyiwa a wajena, da wani ido kakeso na kalleta nace inason abunda takeso, danasan zanga wannan ranan danayita addu'an Allah ya dauki raina kafin wannan ranan yazo, dan Allah kacemun wasa kakemun kanaso kana gwadani ne, kace bada gaske bane ta tsuguna a wajen tasaki kuka maicin rai zuciyarta na kuna.

Shima tsugunawa yayi idonsa na zubar da hawaye yace Sumayyah ki yarda dani ba komai tsakanina da Safiyyah asalima Safiyyah bata tab'a sona ba, bakiga yadda tayi farin ciki ba a lokacin da nace ina sonki, ita da kanta ta dage sainazo na sanar dake"

Wani kallon karka mayar dani mahaukaciya take masa, kafin ta kwasa da gudu tayi gidansu Safiyyah takoyi sa'an ba kowa a tsakar gidan ta wuce dakin Safiyyah da gudu lokacin Safiyyah tana karatun qur'ani taji an fad'o mata a jiki, ganin Sumayyah ne yasa tayi saurin kai aya ta ajiye qur'anin tace k'awata lafiya kuwa?

Sumayyah cikin kuka tace "dama Fa'izu yana shaye'shaye ne?

Safiyyah tace ban fahince kiba, me kika gani?

Ta cigaba da cewa wlh yanashan k'waya,  kinji abunda yazo yana cemun yanzu kuwa, maganan dagaji zakisan bana wanda yake cikin tunanin sa bane, nasan idan ya dawo nutsuwarsa saiyaji kunyata.


Aranta tace nasan dole za'a samu wannan matsalan,  tace ki fahintar dani dan Allah kin barni nima a dubu, kuma zan iya miki rantsuwa da cewa Yaya Fa'izu ko taba bayasha ballan tana k'waya inkinga yasha k'waya tona ciwone, amma meya fad'a miki.


Sumayyah tace to tabbas Fa'izu soyake ya rabani dake, cewa yayifa wai yana sona" ga mamakinta sai taga Safiyyah ta kwashe da dariya har tana rik'e ciki, ido ta zaro tace meye abun dariya kuma anan?


Safiyyah tace kece kikaban dariya, badda ke sai yanzu kikasan Yaya Fa'izu yana sonki to bari kiji duk biyoni gidanku da yakeyi badan ni yake zuwa ba yana zuwane kawai dan ya ganki, dan haka kibada fuska kawai musha biki lokacinmu ne Allah ya kawo.

Sumayyah tajima tana juya maganan a kwakwalwanta amma yaki dauka, tace Safiyyah karkimun irin wannan sadakarwan, inbaki manta ba nakamu da ciwon hawan jini a sanadiyar iyayena kece kika zamanto samun warakata, ta hanyan rarrashina, taya nizan zamo sanadiyar sa miki hawan jini, Safiyyah koni kurma ce kuma makauniya nasan kunason juna da Fa'izu duka wannan shirin nakine kekika juya ya dawo kaina, meyasa zaki sadaukar da farin cikin ki dan nawa,  meyasa zakimun haka, dana auri wanda kikeso gwanda na mutu banyi aure ba.

Safiyyah ta tureta a jikinta tace karki manta kinmun alkawarin zaki auri duk wanda yazo miki a matsayin miji meyasa zaki karya alkawarin da kikamun, bazan taba kamuwa da hawan jiniba dan ban auri Fa'izu ba sabod bana sonsa, saidai na kamu da hawan jini dan kinki auran sa, idan kin yarda dani banason Fa'izu ban tab'a masa son aure ba saina dan uwa, Sumayyah idan kika auresa kingama mun komai a rayuwata.

Sumayyah ta fara jujjuya kai tana cewa karki mun haka karkimun  haka dan Allah bazan taba iya zama da abunda kikeso ba.


Safiyyah tace tabbas kin kusa rasani a duniyan gaba d'aya domin wannan abunda zakimun zai iya sanadin rasa raina, cikin tsawa tace kije kiyi duk abunda ranki yakeso daga yau nafuta daga rayuwar ki, yanzu nagane nikade ce nake sonki Sumayyah baki sona tunda bakison abunda nakeso, kije kiyi rayuwa da duk wanda kikeso badan sake yarda da k'awa ba a rayuwa ta, tunda kika yaudareni da kalman jinin ki kikasa na dauka ke maisona ne, ashe ba haka bane nagode duk cikin kuka take maganan ta kifa kanta ta cigaba da kukanta.

Cikin kidima Sumayyah tazo ta fad'i a jikinta nunfashinta yana sama² dakyar ta iya cewa karkimun mungun fahinta Safiyyah wlh tsakanina dake ba yaudara a ciki, tunda hakan shine farin cikinki na amince zan auresa.

Da sauri Safiyyah ta rungumeta tace Allah nagode maka Sumayyah koyau na mutu burina ya cika, Allah ka nunamun ranan dazan ga auran k'awata amin.

Saida ta tabbatar hankalinta ya kwanta kafin suka futo zata rakata gida, akan dakali suka tadda Fa'izu yana zaune yayi tagumi kana ganinsa kasan yana cikin damuwa, Safiyyah tace ki fad'a masa.


Sumayyah tace kayi hakuri Yaya Fa'izu na amince saidai dan Allah karkazo hira wajena na yafe, katuro ayi auran kawai, tana kaiwa nan tayi gida da sauri, yabi bayanta da kallo a zuciyarsa yace kin taimakeni Sumayyah dan nima banaso ina zuwa wajenki,  sainaga kaman cin fuska zamuyi wa Safiyyah, saida yaga shiganta gida kafin ya kalli Safiyyah yace to tunda batason naje kice ta yarda ina kiranta"

Safiyyah ta karb'a wayansa photon ta tagani akan fuskan wayan sai tayi kaman bata gani ba, tasa masa numberta tace zaka iya kiranta aduk lokacin dakaga dama.


Zainab abun Faisal yafara damunta kullun idan suka kwanta yana fara kusantan ta abunsa zata kwanta saiya sauka ya barta da sha'awa a mararta, yau yana futa ta shirya ta tafi wajen boka tana zuwa suka shige ciki saida taji bukatan ta yabiya kafin tace gaskiya boka bazan iya zama ina magana yana magana ba, kuma naga yanason uwarsa da dan uwansa da yawa inaso ka fara cire masa son uwansa aransa kafin azo kan shegen dan uwansa.


Boka yayi dariya yace........




Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️33\34
Boka yayi dariya yace "zan miki yadda kikeso,  zan toshe bakin mijinki sai yadda kikayi dashi amma da sharad'i.

"Ka fad'i kowani irin sharad'ine, kasan zanyi shi komai wahalan sa" ta bashi amsa.

"Yace zakina kwana uku a gidan mijinki kwana biyu anan, idan har kin amince maganinki zai fara aiki daga watan da zamu shiga, sanna yanzu ma dakina zuwa ina gwajin aiki dake duk bayan kwana biyu.

Tace na amince boko, duk yadda kace haka za'ayi umurnin ka kawai nake nema na tafi, sukayi sallamah tatafi gida zuciyarta fari kal, tana zuwa ta sameshi a falo yana zaune, taje ta zauna kusa dashi take masoyi harka dawo?

Ya dubeta da kyau yaji jikinta yana warin wani irin magani yace daga ina kike?

Tace na raka Asabe kawata ce shine muka tsaya hira a hanya kasanmu idan mun had'u kaman karmu rabu.

Ya watsa mata harara yace duk ranan da nasake dawowa bakinan saina mungun sab'a miki, yana gama fad'an haka ya tashi ya futa a gidan, dan yafara gajiya da halinta

Yana futa tayi tsaki tace kayi lokacinka ne saura kiris ka shigo hanuna, daga ranan kuwa zaka gane kuranka, dan dariya ma sainaga dama kayi yaro, buga kofan gidan taji anayi da k'arfi had'e da kwad'a sallama ta tashi taje bakin kofan tace wayene zai ciremun dodon kunne da Allah, tana budewa taga wata mata tayi tsaki tace mutane basu da hankali da kikazo kikaji shiru basai kiyi tafiyan kiba, tunda ba gidan kibane"


Matar tace kiyi hakuri y'arnan da alkairi nazo miki,  sunana Goggo Larai nice matan Baban Faisal wanda uwarsa take wulakanta ni shine nazo miki da shawaran hanyan da zakibi ki kwace kanki,  domin naji jiya tana cewa bata sonki zatasa araba auranku ta aura masa wanda sukeso"

Zainab tace kan uba lallai matan nan batasan waye Zee big body ba batasan kaidina nida uwata ba, amma mu zuba nida ita shege ka fasa.

Goggo Larai tace zan iya shigowa?

Tace ai dole nabaki wajen zama harda abinci Goggo Larai kike kowa.

Suka shiga daga ciki, Goggo Larai ta sake ido da baki tana k'arewa gidan kallo, azuciyarta tace lallai ranan banga gidan da kyauba, taya zan barsu su zauna lafiya a cikin wannan babban gidan, ya zamomun dole na tarwatsa zaman lafiya da kwanciyan hankalin ahalin nan.

Goggo Larai tace naji dad'in haduwa dake dan inason mutane wayayyu wanda sukasan idan ammusu su zage damtse su rama bawai su zuba ido ayita cutar dasu ba, kuma bari kiji idan kika bari kinaji kina gani wannan kanin nasa zaina juyaki yadda yaga dama, dan komai zaiyi zakiji yace bari Fa'izu na ya dawo, tona kare miki lokacin da zai aureki da Fa'izun da Uwarsu sunki waike karuwane saida yaje wajen dangin malam kafin suka tsaya akayi auren, shiyasa sukace tunda akayi basuso saisun futar dake ink....tace haba Goggo kiyi shuru haka mana, ke baki hutawane idan kina magana.

Goggo Larai tace kibarni na amayar da abunda yake cikina kawai, ta cigaba da cewa na takaice miki zance yanzu hakama nabarta zataje wani k'auye taji labarin wani shahararran boka shizai mata aiki akanki dan tace danta bazai zauna dake ba, kuma duk abunda tace zatayi saita aikata shi dan nima datace saita rabani da mijinmu saida ta aikata, tunda tasa mijin mu ya koreni ban sake zuwa gidan ba sai ranan daya mutu.

Zainab tace lallai wannan Hajaran sunana kawai takeji, ni Zee big body har akwai wata macen da zata nunamun wajen boka, to muzuba nida ita shege kafasa, sainayi abunda d'anta ya zaneta a tsakiyan titi batasan waye niba, da nayi niyan narabata dashine kawai to yanzu sainasa anmusu abunda ko inuwansu tabari ya had'u waje d'aya saiya karta manta rashin mutumci.

Goggo Larai tayita murmushin samun nasara, tace yau ina cikin farin ciki yau ni Larai nasamu wanda zai tayani yak'an Hajara da y'ayanta.


Faisal yana futa bai tsaya ko inaba sai wajen Umma yana shiga yace Umma dan Allah kimun izini yau dinnan na saki Zainab"

Umma tace haba Faisal yaushe kukayi auren dahar kake tunanin ku rabu, kamanta yanzu Kaninka yake shirin yin aure sakinta zai kashewa Fa'izu jiki yaji shima ya fasa auren, iyayen yarinyan ma suce bakuda rikon aure bazasu baku auran y'arsu ba, yanzu fa kukeda wata uku da aure, kayi koyi da halin ma'aifinka mana, da wayonka ba irin wulakancin da Larai bata masaba a gaban idonku amma ya hakura da ita, daga karshe dakanta tabar gidan tace ita bazatayi jinyan k'atoba idan ya mutu zatazo karban gadonta, duk da haka bai saketa ba, to kaima na horeka daka koyi hali irin nasa.

Yace Umma yarinyan nan kwata² bataso taganmu tare daku ko sunanku nakira ranan munyita fad'a kenan Umma ina tsoron kaidinta dan taci alwashin saita rabamu,  ki amincemun kawai na saketa.

Umma tace "karka damu kayita mata addu'a kawai nasan tana fadane kawai amma ba abunda zata iyayi, idan tana fad'a maka bakin magana ka tashi ka futa a gidan kafin ka dawo zataji kunyan abunda ta fad'a maka,  tashi kaje Allah ya muku albarka ya baku zaman lafiya kaida iyalinka.

Yace to Umma nagode Allah ya kara girma, wani satin zamukai kudin auren Fa'izu ko?

Umma tace eh insha Allahu, kaga sai kana hakuri tunda girma ya kamaka, suka dan taba hira kadan kafin ya futa yaje ya siya mata nama ya dawo yace "Umma tsaban raina yana b'ace harna manta ban siyo miki naman kiba sai yanzu.

Umma ta karba tanata saka mishi albarka tare da mishi addu'an Allah ya dad'a basu zaman lafiya shida iyalin sa.




Sumayyah tana shiga gida ta tadda Mama a tsakar gida tana jiran dawowan ta, Mama tace zoman mai k'ashin tsiya ya kukayi da saurayin dayazo miki koyace baya sonki ya tafi abunsa?

Sumayyah taje ta tsuguna a gaban Mama tace "yace yanaso kubashi izini ya kawo kud'in auran mu"


Mama tayi shewa tace "ashe dama kinada rabon aure a duniyan nan,  ai da wuri za'ayi a gama dan kar shima ya gane bakin jini gareki ya gudu, bari Baban ki ya shigo na sheda masa, koda Baban ta ya shigo Mama ta fada masa, yace kice mata na bata nanda sati d'aya ya futo dan nagaji da ganinta a cikin gidana.

Sumayyah tana jinsu hankalinta ya mungun tashi hanunta yana bari ta dauki wayan ta takira Safiyyah, tanaji ta d'aga saita saka mata kuka tace.........

🅿️35\36
Cikin kid'ima Safiyyah tace lafiya! Meya faru kike kuka haka?

Still ba bata amsa ba, Safiyyah tace kiyi hakuri kimun magana dan Allah Sumayyah ki fad'a mun abunda ya faru dake, ganin bazata bata amsa ba yasa ta kashe wayan, Fa'izu da tunda yaji ta kira sunan Sumayyah ya kafeta da ido tana kashe wayan yace meya samu Sumayyan?

Safiyyah cikin nuna damuwa a fuskanta tace nima ban saniba kuka kawai take tayi ta kasa mun magana, amma bazanje naji meyake damunta, tana shiga tun daga tsakar gida takejin sheshshekan kukanta ta k'arasa da gudu taje ta tarar da ita idonta sun fara kafewa nunfashin ta yana futa sama sama ta karasa da gudu tana girgizata amma ina tayi nisa dan a hanunta ta sume mata, da gudu ta futa tayi dakin Mama tana kuka tace Mama kuzo kuga Sumayyah bata nunfashi, ta juyo da gudu ta koma dakin wayanta tasamu yana ringing ganin Fa'izu ne yasa ta daga cikin kuka ta mishi bayanin halin da ake ciki, ba bata lokaci yaje ya taro keke napep yace Safiyyah ta futo da ita sukaita asibiti.

Janta Safiyyah tafarayi suka futo tsakar gida sai yanzu Mama suka futo Baba yace subbahanallahi mezan gani yaje ya tab'ata yace bazan nemo abun hawa mukaita asibiti.

Safiyyah tace mun samo Baba yana waje, Mama ta tab'e baki tace indai akan maganan auren dana mikine kika suma to tabbas saidai ki mutu dan bazan janye ba, Fa'izu ganin shurun yayi yawa yasa ya shigo cikin gidan ya tarar Baba da Safiyyah sunata kokuwan d'aukan ta, hanu yasa ya d'agata cak batare da tunanin komai ba ya rungumeta a jikinsa ya futa da gudu yasata a nafe, Baba yabi bayansu shima hankalin sa ya tashi da ganin halin da y'arsa take ciki, ko'a cikin nafe Fa'izu bai ajiyeta ba tana rungume a jikinsa har suka isa asibiti.

Suna zuwa aka fara bata temakon gaggawa har akayi nasaran dawo da nunfashin ta daidai kafin suka mata alluran bacci, likita ya futo ya samesu yace kune kuka kawo Sumayyah Haruna?

Sukace "eh" yace to inason ganin d'aya daga cikin ku a office, dukansu sukabi bayansa, ya dubesu yace tana da aurene?

Baba yace "a'a"

Ya sake cewa "ita mareniya ce"

Baba yace "a'a ninema ma'aifinta Maman ta kuma tana gida"

Likatan ya dubesa dakyau yace "halamu sun nuna tana cikin matsin rayuwa wanda hakan yayi sabadiyar kamuwan ta da hawan jini mai tsanani, gaskiya kuna lura da walwalanta inba haka ba wata rana zata iya had'iyan zuciya ta mutu ko kuma takamu da ciwon barin jiki,  shawarwari ya basu sosai kafin suka futo Baba yana matsan kwalla sai yau yake dana sanin takura mata da sukeyi yayiwa kansa alkawarin gyara tsaka ninsa da yarsa.


Suka koma wajenta sai wajen shidan mangriba ta farfad'o lokacin Baba ya tafi gida kawo mata abinci kafin ta farka, sauran Fa'izu da Safiyyah tana bude ido ta saukesu aka  Safiyyah saita saka kuka tace " JININA Mama zasu kashe ni saboda basu sona, nan ta kwashe komai ta fad'a mata, Fa'izu da yake gefe yana jinsu yace karki sake saka damuwa a ranki, indai akan wannan ne a satin nan za'a kawo kudi auren mu da komai zuwa karshen wa muyi auran mu

Batasan yana wajen ba ta dago kanta a hankali tace Yaya Fa'izu karkayi gaggawa kabari ka fara sona aranka kafin ka aureni dan Allah"

Atake yaji tausayinta ya cika masa zuciya yaji tabbas zata iya maye masa gurbin Safiyyah, ya matso kusa da ita ya rik'e hanunta yace burina a yanzu kisoni kawai a zuciyar ki idan kika soni kingama mun komai, karki damu da zuciyata nasan nakine, kuma ina hango mana rayuwar mu a gaba, nasan zamu shin fid'ata akan soyayyah da kaunan junan mu, sannan namiki alkawarin wannan karan shine nakarshe da jininki zai hau.

Murmushine ya subce a fuskanta ta rike hanun Safiyyah da daya hanuntan tace har na mutu bazan daina alfahari da keba, nasan kozan samu masoyi mai sona tsakani da Allah a bayan kine, tabbas ke JININA ne Safiyyah,  yadda nakeson ki wlh ko iyayen da suka kawoni duniya bana sonsu haka, fatana Allah ya nunamun ranan dazan ganki cikin farin ciki a rayuwarki kaman yadda kika sakani, Safiyyah inasonki fiye da yadda nakeson rayuwata Allah ya dubi gabanki da bayanki ya karemun ke, tana magana ne hawaye na zuba a idonta duka suna rike da hanun juna.


Safiyyah tayi murmushi tace "Sumayyah kar kisa Yaya Fa'izu ya fara kishi dani mana, shi ya kamata kina yabo, kina fad'a masa kalamun soyayyah bani ba, kuma banaso naji kinamun godiya irin wannan saboda ban miki abunda zaki gode munba, burina kullun nagan ki cikin farin ciki da murmushi a fuskanki kaman yadda kike yanzu, koma nace fiye da yadda kike yanzu.

Sumayyah tace "koda zanso Yaya Fa'izu a raina bazai tab'a kai yadda zan soki ba JININA, nasan shima zai tayani yabonki a duk lokacin dana fara, Safiyyah wlh duk wanda naga d'igon kinki a idonsa koda mijin aure nane wlh bazan zauna dashi ba"

Fa'izu yace nima idan kikafi sona fiye da Safiyyah bazan ga kiman kiba, saboda Safiyyah ta cancanci duk wani mai nunfashi ya sota, fatana Allah yasa wannan zumuntar taku ya daure ya zamo silan shigan ku gidan Aljanna" suka amsa da Ameen, Safiyyah atake ta nemi kishin Fa'izu ta rasa a zuciyarta sai takejin kaman tun farko da Sumayyah suke soyayyah bada itaba, Mama da Baba suka shigo suka samesu sunata hira suna dariya, Baba ya k'araso da sauri bakinsa yana furta Alhamdulillah.

Mama tace dama na fad'a maka ciwon munafinci ne, so take muce mun fasa maganan auran ta, to bari kiji indai akan aurene yanzu mukasa k'afan wando d'aya dake.


Safiyyah bata tab'a mayar ma Mama da magana ba sai yau ta dubeta tace "haba Mama tana gadon asibitin ma bata tsira ba, kisha kurumin ki indai aurene insha Allahu bazata kai muku wata d'aya a gidan kuba.

Fa'izu ganin cikin b'acin rai take maganan yaja hanunta suka futa yana bata hakuri.

Baba ya kalli Mama yace tabbas bakida digon imani a zuciyar ki, amma kije tunda duk abunda na fada miki bazakiji ba nasan lokacin da zakiyi dana sani yananan tafe,  lokacin da zakiso yarki ta mannu dake ita kuma ta kasa saboda rashin shak'uwa yana nan zuwa, ya dube Sumayyah da take kwance yace kiyi hakuri kinji  Allah ya miki albarka ya baki lafiya.

Sumayyah wani farin ciki taji wannan ne karonta na farko dataji kalma mai dadi a bakin ma'aifinta, ta taso ta rungumesa cikin kuka tace nagode Baba.

Mama tayi tsaki tace dadin abun sunada yawa ba ita kad'ai na aifa ba tayi fucewar ta, Baba yace karki damu kinji duk lokacin da Allah ya baki miji zakiyi auran ki ai.

Sumayyah ta sunkuyar da kanta tana murmushi tace "Baba yace a satin nan zai turo da kud'in auran da komai zuwa k'arshen wata yakeso ayi auran"

Baba yace Alhamdulillah lokaci yazo to Allah ya kawo su lafiya, a ina yake manemin kin?

Tace "dan Bauchi ne iyayensa da kowa nasa suna Bauchi shi kuma yana aikin dan sanda a nan, abokin Yaya Adam dinsu Safiyyah ne"

Baba yace to to to wanda nake ganinsa a gidan sun nan?

"Eh shine Baba" ta bashi amsa kanta yana sunkuye dan ta kasa sakewa dashi.

Yace masha Allah, naga yaro  yanada hankali Allah ya sanya albarka, Safiyyah suka shigo sukaga suna hira abun saiya mata dadi tace koba komai yanzu Kawata zata dawo cikin nutsuwan ta, Safiyyah tace Baba dare nayi kozaka tafi gida.

Yace ai bazan iya bacci ba kona koma gida, ke kije kisamu kiyi bacci inyaso gobe da safe idan ba'a bamu sallamah ba saiki dawo ki karb'eni, ba yadda Safiyyah batayi da Baba ba amma yaki tafiya, karshe suka musu sallama suka tafi ita da Fa'izu, suna shiga gida Inna tace ya jikin kawan taki dazu Fa'izu yakira yake fadamun, kuma naga dare yayi shiyasa banzo ba,  nazaci acan zaki kwana"

Safiyyah ta kwashe duk yadda akayi a asibiti ta fad'a mata, Inna tace kowa da yadda Allah yake aiko masa da jarabawa ita kuma nata jarabawan na iyaye ne, Allah ya bata ikonci.

Washe gari da rana aka sallame su, asatin Fa'izu suka kawo kayan aurensu da sadaki, akasa aure nan da sati biyu, Safiyyah ta zage sai zirga zirgan auren kawarta takeyi, ta cire komai a ranta.


Zainab dataji labarin Fa'izu zaiyi aure kuma a gidanta za'a saka matan hankalinta ba karamun tashi yayiba, taje tasamu boka ta masa bayani.

Yace dama abunda yake jira kenan sai bayan auren zata fara ganin  aikin ta, yanzu ta hade kai da Goggo Larai da Hafsa, sai zugata sukeyi ita kuma tana hawa, ba abunda yake k'ona mata rai kaman yadda taga Faisal duk hankalinsa ya koma kan auran k'aninsa, sai kud'i yake kashewa dan shiya biya sadaki ya had'a masa kayan aure.


Ranan d'aurin aure Mama tana cikin mutane anata aiki,  duk k'annan  Sumayyah suma sunzo, wata mak'ociyar su tace Maman Sumayyah bazan iya shuru inajin mutanen anguwa sunayi dake ban fad'a miki ba"

Mama tace.......

🅿️37\38
Mama tabar abunda takeyi tace niko menayi da akeyi dani a unguwa?

Mata taci gaba da cewa, naji mutane na cewa keda Sumayyah kun had'u kumma Safiyyah kwacen saurayi, dan duk unguwan nan kowa ya san Fa'izu saurayin Safiyyah ne, sai mukaji labari yasha bambam,  maimakon ya auri Safiyyah sai mukaji wai da Sumayyah za'ayi auran.

Mama tace to inkun yarda ban tab'a sani ba, ninasan y'ata tanada bakin jini bansan ya akayi ba yaron nan yazo yace yana sonta ba, amma banga halamun hakan agun Safiyyah ba yanzu hakama suna d'aki a tare suna zaune.

Surayyah kanwan Safiyyah tace Mama kema kinsan Safiyyah tana da hakuri kota cuceta bazata tab'a nunawa ba, kawai kisamu Baba kice masa afasa auran nan, nasan Sumayyah ta mata kwacene dan tana kishin mu k'annanta munyi aure ita batayi ba.

Mama tace  hakane kuma, tabbas yau indai nina aifeta baza'ayi auran nanba, mutane sun cika awaje har anfara addu'an daura aure, Baba ya shigo d'aukan tiren da za'a zuba goro, Mama tace yauwa Baban Sumayyah gwanda daka shigo.

Yace meya faru?

Tace dama auran nan nakeso a fasa d'aurawa, saboda bazan iya zuba ido ina gani Sumayyah dan taga Safiyyah nada hakuri ta aure mata saurayi ba.

Baba yace ban tab'a zaton bakida hankali ba sai yau, kuma aure idan ke zaki bayar to kifuta kice kin fasa, ya dauko tire zai futa ta sake shan gabansa tace wlh indai nina aifi Sumayyah to baza'a daura auren nanba.

Ran Baba ya mungun b'aci ya d'aga hanu ya tsinka mata mari yace tunda ubanki zata aura ai dole kice kin fasa, wlh kika sake shan gabana namiki rantsuwa saina tsinke igiyan auran da suke tsakanin mu, da sauri ta bashi hanya ta koma gefe tana huci, tana ganin futan shi tayi d'akin da Sumayyah take da k'awayen ta, tana shiga ta rufe Sumayyah da duka tana cewa nizaki jawa abun kunya a unguwa,  kin lik'ewa yarinya ashe illa zaki mata,  ta dawo da kallonta kan Safiyyah tace ke kuma dayake shashashace kina nan lik'e da ita duk da tamiki kwace.

Safiyyah tace kwace kuma?
Meta kwace mun?

Mama tace kowa yasan Fa'izu saurayinki ne duk unguwan nan, Sumayyah gwanda na kasheki da akaiki d'akin yaron nan, ta futa neman abunda zata daketa dashi, Surayyah tace maganin maci amana kenan gwanda ta illataki.

Safiyyah ta tashi rai b'ace ta tsinkawa Surayyah mari tace wawiya mai hassada wato kinga mijinta yafi naki komai shine kike bak'in ciki da ita ko,  to wlh dukanku ba abunda kuka isa ku mata kunaji kuna gani za'a fita da ita lafiya qalau zuwa d'akin mijinta, ta hankad'a ta waje ta rufe kofan ta saka sakata, Mama ta tado tayi ta bugawa ba wanda ya kulata, tace Sumayyah banason kukan kinnan haka ya isheni, ko sokike nima nafara kuka?

Tayi saurin girgiza mata kai tace Safiyyah ni kuma rayuwata kullun a juye take zuwa mun daga nafita a wannan saina shiga wannan, yanzu ace ranan aurena ma Mama bazata bari mu rabu lafiya ba, wannan wani irin rayuwane, buga k'ofa sukaji anayi Safiyyah tace wlh inkunga na bud'e muku kofa ku tabbatar anzo tafiya da amarya ne, Baba yace wani abu ya faru kenan, Safiyyah zoki bud'emun k'ofa nine.

Taje ta bud'e kofan Sumayyah na ganinsa taje da gudu ta rungumesa tana cewa Baba bansan wani irin laifi nama Mama ba ta tsaneni haka, yanzu da badan Safiyyah ta rufe k'ofa tace saita kasheni.

Baba ya bubbuga bayanta yace kiyi hakuri akwai ranan da zatayi dana sanin abunda take miki yana nan tafe, Mama ta shigo tana cewa ina take sai taga Baba, ya juyo rai b'ace yace wlh kika sake zagin yarinyan nan ko dukanta yau ko kuma akazo daukanta kikace musu wani abu abakin auranki ke ko a tsakar gida naji maganan ki akan auran y'ata wlh na warware duk igiya ukun da yake tsakanin mu, ya juya ya futa a d'akin, Mama itama ta futa tana hararan Sumayyah ba bakin magana, y'an daukan amarya dama suna gidansu Safiyyah ne suka shigo d'aukan amarya Baba ya shigo ya mata nasiha mai ratsa zuciya,  kafin aka futo da ita, ta tsuguna a gaban Mama tana kuka tace Mama dan Allah ki yafemun duk abunda na miki.

Mama jitayi zuciyarta ya d'an karaya, tace tashi kuje Allah ya miki albarka ya baku zaman lafiya.

Ko ba komai taji dad'in addu'an da Mama ta mata, y'an uwa suka rufa mata baya aka dauki hanyan bauchi.

Gida Umma aka ajiyeta sai washe garin wuni aka kaita d'akinta.

Zainab kuwa kotazo a d'akin Goggo Larai take wuni su had'u suyita zagin Umma d ita,  duk wanda yaga Fa'izu yasan yana cikin farin ciki sosai, suna zaune da Adam a k'ofar gida.

Adam yace "Fa'izu ya kamata idan mun koma kayi k'okari ka shigar da takaddan ka tunds kaima yanzu ka had'a degree d'inka, takaddan wani ba abun gadara bane musamman idan matansa ta gane da sunan mijinta kake amfani, kaga duk wajen mu bamai shiri da Oga kamanka kaje kamasa bayani tunda yana sonka zai iya gyara maka komai, kafin wasu suje su maka cune, tunda nakai katin auranka na fahinci anfara kuskus a wajen ganin sunan da yake jikin katin Fa'izu ne ba Faisal d'in da suka sanka dashi ba.


Fa'izu yace hakane zanyi k'ok'ari nafara amfani da sunana duk da nasan bazan tab'a samun matsala da Faisal ba saidai da abokan aiki,

Faisal yazo yace to ango atashi aje a sallami Umma mukaika gidanka dan dare nayi, Umma ta masa nasiha sosai kafin suka tafi, suna zuwa k'ofan gidan Faisal yace dad'ina da gobe saurin zuwa yau nima zan rama abunda kuka mun saura kai Adam.

Duka basu fahince shiba, sukace mai muka maka?

Yace idan kun manta niban manta ba ranan aurena guduwa kukayi kukak'i rakani d'aki dan haka nima kunga tafiyata bazan kira k'anwata na mayar da ita gida, ya ciro wayansa a aljihu ya kira Safiyyah.

Safiyyah suna zaune suna jiran angwaye taji kira a wayanta ta d'aga ganin Faisal ne yasa ta d'aga da sauri tace Yayana nayi fushi,  sai yanzu zakazo d'auka na"

Yace amun afuwa kanwata ina waje ina jiranki"

Ta tashi tace Sumayyah nikam sai gobe idan nadawo,  aci amarci lafiya, sai Sumayyah tasa kuka tace dan Allah JININA karki tafi kibarni wlh tsoro nakeji, 

Safiyyah itama idonta ya ciko da kwallah tasan yau k'awanta zatasha wahala tace kiyi hakuri tunda basuzo ba bazanje naba Yaya Faisal hakuri nace masa anjima saiya dawo mu tafi, da haka ta mata wayo ta futa itama tana matsan gwalla a tsakar gida suka had'u da Zainab ta bangajeta tace wlh na tsaneki yarinyan nan, da fuska kaman na aljanu, itadai Safiyyah bata kulata ba tayi wucewar ta, tana futa ta shiga mota Faisal yaja yacewa Adam saina dawo d'aukan ka.

Bai kaita gida direct ba saida suka biya ya mata siyesiye kafin ya ajiyeta a gida,  ya koma d'aukan Adam.


Fa'izu suka shiga dakin da sallaman su, Sumayyah najinsu ta sake k'udundune wa akan kujera dan bata zaci zuwansu yanzu ba

Adam yace amarsu ta ango, yaudai Allah ya nunamun ranan auran ku fatan mu Allah ya baku zaman lafiya da zuri'a d'ayyiba, nasan abokina yana da hak'uri kaman yadda kema nasanki da hakuri, Allah yasa kuyi amfani da hakurin ku wajen gina rayuwa mai inganci da tsafta, ya mik'e yace to ni zan tafi ina muku fatan alkairi da rayuwa mai kyau.


Fa'izu ya rakasa ya dawo yana masa godiya, inda ya barta a nan yazo ya sameta yace kitashi kiyi alola kizo muyi sallah.

"Tace inada alola"
