🅿️41\42
Zainab ta ciza yatsa tace dole na gyara wa guy d'innan zama, wato daya tsaya muma muji dad'in mu,  shine ya futa inyaso sha'awa ta kashe ni kenan, ba komai lokacin kane nawa nanan zuwa kwanan nan.

Ihun Sumayyah ta sakeji ta mik'e a razane tace kai yarinyan nan zata kasheni, dama Fa'izu ya iya aiki haka, shine ban tab'a kai masa hari ba, ya zamo dole naja ra'ayinsa nima ya jiyar dani dad'in da yake jiyar da matansa yanzu, dama ance duk wani mai aikin d'amara akwaisu da jaraba, gabin mata, koda kuwa matar ubansu ne in tamusu zasu danneta, Fa'izu zan baka sati biyu ka more amaryan ka kafin nima na moreka, da wannan gurb'ataccen tunanin ta bacci ya d'auketa a wajen.


Fa'izu saida yayi awa biyu kafin ya samu gamsuwa ya d'agata ya koma gefe yana mayar da nunfashi, ya kamo hanunta yafara zunduma mata albarka, kafin ya tashi cikin layi ya shiga toilet ya tsarkake jikin sa ya had'a mata ruwa mai zafi, ya futo ya yaye bargon da tarufu dashi.

 Kuka tasaka masa da muryanta da baya futowa sosai tace "dan Allah Yaya Fa'izu kabarni haka zan mutu"

Yace ba abunda zan miki, muje na miki wanka.

Tace "zan iya da kaina ka futa a d'akin"

Bai tsaya sauraran taba ya d'auketa cak bai direta ko inaba sai cikin ruwan zafin daya tara domin gasata, wani irin mungun razana tayi saka mokon azaban zafin dataji a k'asanta, ta bud'e baki zata kurma ihu yayi saurin had'e bakinsu waje d'aya duk inda taso ya barta tayi ihu ko zataji dad'i amma fur yak'i bada wannan k'ofan, saida ya gasata sau uku kafin ya barta tsarkake kanta,  yaje ya yaye zanin gadon dasuka b'ata fuskansa d'auke da murmushi, ya d'aga hanu yanata ma Allah godiya daya bashi kyautar kamilan mace, ya gyara musu gado tsaf kafin ya kwanta yana jiran ta futo su kwanta, ganin shuruntan yayi yawane yasa ya tashi ya shiga toilet d'in a tsaye ya ganta jikin kofa ta kasa futowa tana ganin shi tayi saurin juya masa baya, cak ya d'agata tanata wutsil² ya sauketa yaki, ya kwantar da ita kafin ya had'a mata tea mai kauri tasha.

A gefenta ya kwanta ya jawota jikinsa, luf ta nad'e a k'irjinsa nan danan bacci mai cike da annashuwa ya dauke su, shiya fara farka wa ya k'ura mata idon yanata saka mata albarka, yana tunanin taya zai zare jikinsa daga nata dan bayason ya tasheta gashi har an shiga sallah, baisan tun farkawan sa ta tashi ba kunyane ya hanata bud'e ido, a hankali yake jan jikinsa har ya gama ya d'auko filo yasaka a tsakanin su, kafin yaje yayi wanka ya d'auro alola, jin har an idar da sallah yasa ya shin fid'a dadduma ya fara salla a gida.

A hankali ta bud'e idonta ganin yana sallah yasa ta tashi da niyan zataje tayi alola mik'ewan da zatayi taji wani mungun azaban zafi a k'asanta, batasan lokacin data saki kukaba tana harfe hanu, komawa tayi ta zauna shima zaman ya dad'a fama mata ciwon taci gaba da kukanta cikin nutsuwa, yana sallah amma duk hankalinsa yana kanta, yana idarwa ko addu'a baisamu daman yiba ya taso da sauri yana cewa sannu Hubbi na, yaje da sauri ya had'a mata ruwa mai zafi kafin ya d'auketa,  saida ya tabbatar ta gasu sosai kafin ya mata wanka, ta d'aura alola taji dad'in kasanta sosai dan da kanta tafuto,  dogon riga da hijabi ya saka mata, ta tada sallah shi kuma yaje ya soya mata kwai ya had'a mata tea mai kauri kafin ya d'auko pen relief tana idarwa ya lallab'ata tasha tea da bread taci kwai kad'an kafin ya bata magani tasha, da kanshi ya shafa mata mae da powder simple makeup ya mata yasa mata dogon riga roba², ta futo sak amaryan ta, a kan gado ya shinfid'ata ya gyara musu d'akin duk da bawani datti yayi ba.

Yazo zai kwanta ta saka masa kuka akan sai yaje ya d'auko mata Safiyyah, ya duba agogo yaga k'arfe tara da rabi na safiya ya tashi ya shirya, kiss ya mata a goshi kafin ya futa.

A tsakar gida yaga Zainab tana ganin shi ta washe baki tana cewa ango kasha k'amshi irin wannan ihun da kuka cika mana gida dashi haka jiya, lallai ango ka kwashi gara, nacewa Faisal yaga wa'inda suka iya sex ba shiba da ba abunda ya iya tab'ukawa.

Da mamaki ya tsaya yana kallonta wato ko kunyan shi bataji, ta tsaya tana fad'a masa irin wa'innan dating tolk d'in, murmushi dole kawai ya mata yace Aunty Zee kintashi lafiya ya kwanan Yayana, bai jiran cewarta ba ya fuce daga gidan, yana zuwa gida ya tadda Faisal a d'akin Ummah sunata hira harda dariya, suna had'a ido dashi yaji wani kunya ya rufesa musamman daya tuna maganan da Zainab ta fad'a masa, wai sunji ihunsu jiya, a kunyace ya gaishesu.


Umma sai hamdala takeyi a zuciyarta tasan daga ganin Autan ta yasamu nutsuwa agun matan sa sab'anin washe garin daran farkon Faisal, kanshi yana sunkuye yace Safiyyah k'awarki bata da lafiya tacemun dole saina d'aukoki yanzu.

Ai tunkafin ya k'arasa Safiyyah ta m'ike ta d'auko gyalanta tace "Subbahanallahi meya sameta! Jiya lafiya k'alau na barta fa"

Umma tayi nurmushi irin tasu na manya ta tashi taje ta d'auko wani k'ullin magani guda hud'u da wani goran tsumi da zuma, tace Safiyyah idan kinje kice tasha wa'innan maganin wahalan zaizo mata da sauk'i, wannan kuma ki dafa mata tana shiga ciki tana zama zataji dad'i sosai, ta karb'a zasu futa, Faisal yace Safiyyah kisaka hijab ki ajiye gyalan nan.

Faisal duk kansa ya d'aure da yake ba wani b'oye b'oye tsakaninsu da ma'aifiyar su, yace Umma kaina ya kulle jiya Fa'izu da matarsa sun hanani bacci da ihunsu saida natafi masallacin unguwarmu nayi bacci, yau kuma dagace bata da lafiya kinbasu magani, dama kinsan kalan ciwon tane?

Umma tarasa taya zata basa amsa ta daure ta fuskance sa, tace "Faisal kowace mace da irin baiwar da Allah ya mata, wata farjinta a rufe yake wata kuma tayi wasa da damanta ya bud'e, shine tsakanin matanku, Sumayyah ta rik'e kanta shiyasa ta wahala amma dazaran tasha wa'innan maganin zataji dad'i sosai, bazan b'oye maka ba Faisal tun kafin ka auri Zainab nake maka sha'awan mata biyu, ina rok'on wannan alfarman dan Allah ka k'ara aure"

Ya nunfasa a zuciyar sa yace meye abun dad'i a aure da Umma zata masa sha'awan ya k'ara saidai bazan iya kaucewa buk'atan kiba dolena na k'ara, ya dubeta yace indai shine farin cikinki zan k'ara Umma ko yaushe kikeso zanyi.


Kafin tabasa amsa wayarshi tayi k'ara ya d'aga Ubangidan sa Alh Sulaiman yace kazo yanzu inason ganinka a gida! Cikin girmamawa yace ganinan zuwa Alh.

Yace Umma Alh yana nemana saina dawo, ta mishi addu'a ya futa.


Safiyyah suna isa da gudu ta shiga gidan, har lokacin Zainab tana zaune a tsakar gida, tana ganin shigowan Safiyyah ta tashi da sauri tasha gabanta tace ubanme kikazo ma mutane a gida?

Fa'izu ne ya shigo yace Safiyyah me yafaru naganku kaman masu shirin yin dambe, cikin k'asa da murya tace kibini a hankali ko kuma na a koreki a gidan nan jahilan banza a kawo mana mai ilimi, koda yake ya zamo dole Yaya Faisal ya k'ara aure dan daga ganinki ke dusa ne, tajuya ga Fa'izu tace muna tattaunawa ne kasan idan mata suka had'u da dangin mijin su sunyi ta binsu kenan, itama tana lallab'a nine dan karnasa Yaya Faisal d'ina ya mata kishiya.


Fa'izu yasan ba zancen yake ba yayi dariya yace hakane dangin miji saida lallab'awa musamman irinku Safiyyah dole mutum ya biku a hankali, kodan gudun abunda gobe zai aifar.

Suka shige suka barta tsaye baki a sake tace "kan uban wawa dole na bincika waye yarinyan nan a dangin su Faisal, nasan dai d'akin su babu mace to waye ita?......

🅿️43\44
Kasa bin bayan ta yayi dan haka kawai yakejin kunyanta musamman idan ta fahinci abunda sukayi baisan da wani ido zai kalleta ba, ya juya ya futa, gidan Umma ya koma wajen Adam.

Safiyyah kuwa tana shiga idonta sai Allah Allah yakeyi ya gane mata halin da k'awarta take ciki, a tunanin ta hawan jininta ne ya tashi, bata ganta a falo ba tawuce bedroom d'inta a k'udundune ta hangota a cikin bargo tana baccin wahala, ta k'arasa tana cewa Sumayyah maiya sameki ciwonki ne ya tashi?

Jin muryan Safiyyah takeyi kaman a mafarki,  ta bud'e idonta a hankali tana ganinta tayi saurin fad'awa jikinta tasaki kuka tace JININA Yaya Fa'izun ki baida imani soran kad'an ya kasheni jiya da daddare.

Safiyyah bata fahinci abunda take nufi ba tace maiya miki? Dukanki yayi?

Gyara kwanciyanta tayi a jikin Safiyyan kafin tace,  ba gwanda duka ba akan abunda yamun, yasan fa a rufe nake amma yayita mun abu cikin mugunta wlh kaman zan mutu.

Safiyyah dasai yanzu ta fahinci kan maganan ta fara mata dariya harda hawaye tace haba Sumayyah akan d'an wannan abun kike ciwo,  wlh bakiga yadda hankalina ya tashi ba da yacemun bakida lafiya.

Sumayyah tace dariya kikemun ko? Ki rik'e a ranki zan rama gwandani ma nafiki dauriyan komai kekam nasan sai an had'a da asibiti.

Safiyyah tace nikam d'agani Umma tabani maganin dazan gasaki yanzu, itadai Sumayyah da ido take binta harta tafasa maganin da Ummah ta bata, ta d'anji dad'in maganin sosai, kafin ta bata garirrikan maganin da tsumi da zuma tasha, tace wayace wa Ummah jiya wani abu ya shiga tsakanina da Fa'izu?

Safiyyah tace yanace bakida lafiya Ummah ta gane bakin zaren, kuma kaman tasan Mama ko ruwan bissimillah bata baki ba, tabani wannan na kawo miki.

Sumayyah tace Allah sarki Ummah Allah yasa ta zamomun uwa ba suruka ba gareni, hira sukayi tayi sai daf mangariba Faisal yazo ya mayar da Safiyyah gida, tama Sumayyah sallama akan gobe da wuri zasujuya ita da Yaya Adam, sunsha kukan rabu da juna, musamman Sumayyah da take ganin zatayi zaman kad'eci ba k'awanta kuma jininta kusa da ita, koda Fa'izu yazo sake sex da ita sai taji batasha wahalan datasha daga farko ba.

Ango yau satinsa biyu yanashan angonci yanzu ba laifi Sumayyah harta saba da yanayin mijinta, suna zaune a falo tana kwance a k'irjinsa tace Hubbi yanzu gobe zaka tafi kabarni ko?

Yace nafiki shiga damuwa akan tafiyata gobe, da ina zaune lafiya, ko shekara zanyi banzo bauchi ba baya damuna, saidai nayita tunanin Umma, amma yanzu na d'an d'ana abunda zai hanani zaman lafiya indai baki kusa dani, bazan iya tsallake sati banzo ba, anan zannayin weekend d'ina.

Zainab tana lab'e tanajin duk abunda suke fad'a, aranta tace yazamo dole nasan yadda zanyi na moreka kafin katafi gobe, daki ta koma tasaka d'amammen wando da riga, Zainab irin lutayan nanne masu shape d'in tire ta k'asa, d'uwawun ta shafeffene kaman ture, sai fad'i amma ba tudu ba shape ga fad'in kafad'a, yawan zubar da cikin datayi ya shafe mata nono kaman ba mace ba.

Wayanta ta d'auka takira number Fa'izu ringing biyu ya d'auka yace Aunty Zainab lafiya?

Tace dan Allah kazo d'akina akwai maganan da nakeso muyi dakai kafin ka tafi, ya tashi da mamaki dan tunda Faisal ya aureta sau d'aya ya tab'a shiga d'akinta, yacewa Sumayyah ina zuwa Zainab na kirana.

Yana shiga ya ganta ta baje akan kujera kaman me nak'uda baidamu da yanayin daya ganta ba yace gani Allah yasa lafiya kike nema na"

Tayi wani juyi irinna y'an bariki kafin tace "na kirakane dan na jinjina maka da irin k'ok'arin danaga kanayi wajen bawa gado hakkinsa, sannan kuma nima ina gayyatan ka zuwa shinfid'a na nasan bazaka k'iba, ta karasa maganan cikin kareraya, ta zauna a hanun kujeran da yake.

Wani takaicine da bak'in ciki suka tokaresa a zuciya, ya tashi da sauri yana mata kallan bakida hankali, yace dama najima da sanin ke fasik'ace k'addarace ya shigo dake rayuwar d'an uwana, bari kiji koda ni fasik'ine nafi karfin na had'a shinfid'a da k'azaman mace irinki mazinaciya.

Tasowa tayi tana nufosa tace tunda kasan ni fasik'ace ai saikazo muyi hutawan mu nasan zakafi jin dad'ina fiye da wancan kwailan matar taka.

Hanu ya daga ya tsinka mata mari yace karki kuskura nasakejin kin ambaci mata na, wlh nasan sunan unguwansu ma yafiki daraja ba itaba, yasa k'afa ya nausheta ya futa yana huci, harya nufi dakinsa saiya fasa dan bayaso Sumayyah tagane halin da yake ciki, gidan Umma ya wuce direct Umma naganin sa tace lafiya kuwa Autana maiya faru a gidan naku?

Idonsa yana zubar da kwalla ya fad'awa Umma duk abunda ya faru tsakaninsu da Zainab.

Umma ta nunfasa tace zatayi abunda yafi hakama, mudai muyita ma Faisal addu'a Allah ya rabasa da kaidinta.


Yana futa taja gyalenta tayi gidan boka, tace masa lallai ya jawo mata hankalin Fa'izu ya kusance ta kafin ya tafi.

Boka yayi surkullen sa kafin ya buga ruwa yace harki mutu bazaki tab'a samun yaron nan ba, saidai yanzu lokaci yayi dazan fara miki aikin ki, yabata wani ruwa a kwalba yace ki had'a da jinin al'adanki ki dafa masa abinci, wannan garin maganin kuma kijik'a da asuba kina tashi ki kurkure bakinki dashi ki tabbatar kin kurkure wannan warin bakin ki sai kiyi tsarki dashi ki dama masa kunu dashi kitabbatar kinja hankalinsa yaci abinci da kunun da kika dama, ihar kinyi abunda nace kingama da mijinki, ko uwarsa kikace karya kalla bazai sake kallonta ba, yanzu tashi muje kibani hakkina.

Bayan sun gama basik'ancin su yace karki manta da alk'awarin da kika d'auka, kwana biyu a gidan mijinki kwana uku anan"

Tace angama shugaban b'atattu, yace jeki b'acecciya saidai duk abunda gobe ya aifar ba ruwa, tace karka damu idan komai ya janza zamu nemi hanyan gyarawa.


Tanaji tana gani Faisal ya tafi bata samu abunda takeso ba, yana tafiya tace Sumayyah.....




Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️45\46
Taje har d'aki ta taso Sumayyah tace dole tafara aikin gida yau, kasancewar Sumayyah bamesan hayaniya bane yasa ta d'auki tsintsiya tashare gidan daga farko har k'arshe, kuma tace dole yau ita zatayi girki duka bata k'iba tayi duk abunda tace dan a zauna lafiya.

Fa'izu yana komawa yaje yasamu Ogansa ya masa bayanin yadda ya fara aiki, da dalilin dayasa bada sunansa yake amfani ba.

Ba k'aramun fad'a Ogansa ya masa ba, yace zan duba lamarin kane kawai dan kanada kwazo akan aikin ka idan mun rasaka zamu rasa jarumi babba, sunan wa kake hawa.

Cikin ladabi yace Oga da sunan d'an uwanane, sunan ma'aifinmu d'aya ni sunana Fa'izu shi kuma Faisal"

Yace yanzu Fa'izune kawai kake buk'ata a shigar dashi a kashe sunan Faisal?

Yace "eh sir"

Kafin ya karb'a takaddunsa yace yaje zai nemesa.

Faisal yaje kiran da uban gidansa ya masa ya risina ya gaishesa, Alh Sulaiman yace "Faisal ba komai yasa na kiraka ba saidan na dank'a maka amanar kamfanina yanzu girma ya kamani gashi banida magaji ni Allah bai tab'a bani aihuwa ba, kuma na yarda da gaskiya da amanarka shiyasa zan dank'a maka amanar dukiyata duk wata idan kacire riban da akasamu ka raba gida biyar,  d'aya na albashin ma aikata! d'aya naka! d'aya na gyaran kamfani! biyu kuma nawa, ni yanzu zan koma gida na zauna nayi jinyan jikina dan yanzu banida ishashshen lafiya Allah ya tayaka rik'o.

Faisal yace Alh kashi d'aya yamun yawa zan kula maka da kamfanin ka bisa amana, kana biyana albashi kaman yadda ka saba bani, ya isheni ma amma Alh idan ka bani kashi daya bisa biyar din kud'in da kamfani yake tarawa yamun yawa, mutane zasu fara zargina kuma zasuga kaman ina ha'intan kane"

Alh Sulaiman yace karka damu da abunda mutane zasuce, gaskiyar ka da amanar kane ya kaika zuwa wannan matsayin, Faisal nasha gwadaka akan dukiyana da kayan kamfani amma baka tab'acin amanata ba, sannan nakira meeting zuwa karf'e goma sha d'aya kowa zai hallara anan zan gabatar musu dakai a matsayin sabon shugabansu.

Kaman yadda Alh Sulaiman ya fad'a zai tara taron kaddamar da Faisal hakan yayi ya shedawa kowa Faisal a matsayin sabon shugaban su, wasu daga cikinsu sunyi farin ciki, wasu kuma sunyi bak'in, suka nuna masa hassadansu a fili, dan a ganinsu su sunfi shi jimawa a wajen amma Alh bai basuba sai yabawa Faisal dayazo a bayansu.

Faisal yana komawa gida ya shedawa Umma cigaban daya samu, yace nima zanma Fa'izu bazata bazan fad'a masa ba,  harsai na gina mana k'aton gida wanda dukanmu zamu iya zama koda kuwa mata hud'u zamuyi, sannan Umma zaki d'aga mun k'afa sainayi gini kafin na k'ara aure.

Umma tayi murna sosai tare da mishi fatan alkairi, ta sake masa nasiha sosai akan ya zamo mai gaskiya da rik'on amana.


Zainab yau ta tashi da  jinin al'ada, murna kaman zataci babu, taje tayi duk yadda boka yace tayi sannan ta dama masa kunu, tana gamawa ta d'auki wayanta takira Faisal.

Da mamaki yake ganin wayansa dan rabonsa da Zainab ta kirasa tun ranan d'aurin auransu da safe, ya d'aga bakinsa d'auke da sallamah.

Tace Masoyi yaushe zaka dawo gida?

Da mamaki yace mata "lafiya yau kike nemana?

Ta sake karyar da murya tace "Masoyi kayi hakuri da abubuwan da nake maka a baya, yanzu nagane kuskurena nasan ban kyauta maka a zaman mu na aure ba, shiyasa nakeso na fara gyara kurena tunyau wajen kyautata maka.

Haka kawai bai yarda da maganarta  ba, amma saiyace mata ba komai idan ya tashi a aiki zai dawo, ta kashe wayar tana murnan yau zata gama da rayuwarsa yau zai dawo tafin hanunta sai yadda tayi dashi.

Bayan ya tashi daga aiki ya tafi gidan Umma yakaima mata abubuwan daya saba kai mata kullun kafin yayi sallan magariba da isha, ya sallami Umma harya kama hanyan wajen dayake zuwa cin abinci saiya fasa yace yaudai bari yaje yaga kota ajiye masa abinci, yana zuwa gida tun kafin ya k'arasa bud'e kofa ta futo da gudu ta rungumesa tace barka da dawowa masoyi.

Tana rungumansa yaji wannan warin da jikinta yakeyi yayi sauri janyeta daga jikinsa dan har zuciyarsa tafara tashi, cikin dauriya da danne abunda ya taso masa yace barkanki masoyiya ya gida?

Tasake rungumarsa tace jikina yana kewarka masoyi, zomuje kayi wanka kaci abinci, yau inaso na nuna maka yadda nayi kewarka sosai, inaso mudawo da soyayyan mu kaman yadda muke kafin aure.

Dama ta had'a masa ruwan wanka ya shiga yayi wanka ta goge masa jiki, ta shafa masa mae, shi kansa yau yasan yana sha'awan mace sosai a kusa dashi, Allah yasa yau idan suka kwanta yaji dad'in abun kaman yadda akace yanada dad'i, yana wannan tunanin tazo ta jera masa abinci agaban sa, sanin indai tana zaune warin jikinta bazai tab'a barinsa ya iyacin abincin ba yace "yanzu kije kiyi wanka kafin ki futo nagama cin abinci, inyaso saiki nunamun kalan kewata da kikayi yau" ya k'arasa maganan yana kashe mata ido d'aya.

Tasan warin turaran bokane daya kama jikinta yake damunsa, ta tashi tace angama masoyi ta shige wanka, duk goge jikinta datayi da soso da sabulu amma bai daina warin jikin boka ba, ta futa tashafa mai da turare masu k'amshi dan itama yau tanaso tasakejin jarimin mijinta ajikinta kaman daransu na farko.

Faisal yanakai loman abincin bakinsa yaji abincin kaman dusa ba dad'i babu alaman dad'i yanacine kawai dan ya faranta mata kaman yadda ta shirya masa yau, haka yayita tura abincin yasha kunun.

Tana futowa tayi wani shu'umin murmushi ganin yaci duk abunda ta ajiye masa, tace saura kamu na biyu yau kana kusantana anwuce wajen yaro bakai ba sake aure,  ta k'arasa tahau cinyan sa dukda yanajin wani abu na hawa masa kai, amma ya daure dan yau yanaso dad'in da akeji, yakai kwaloluwa wajen bukata ya kwantar da ita kawai sai yaji.......

🅿️47\48
Yana kwantar da ita da niyan ya sauk'e bukatan sa kawai sai yaji jini ya biyo hanunsa, ya koma gefe yana mayar da nunfashi yace "haba Zainab kinsan bakida tsarki kika barni na saka raina akan zansamu"

Tace "wani irin tsarki kuma nida ba kashi nayiba ko fitsari"

Ya sake d'aga kafanta dan tabbatar da abunda ya gani, jinin ya sake gani yanzu kam harya b'ata wajen da take kwance, yace "wannan ba jinin al'ada bane?

Tace shine to dan ina jini saika kasa biyamun buk'ata ta, ko kuma kai haka taka salon yake, ko kuma so kake ka walak'antani dan kaga inason jijiyarka, to bari kaji akwai maza da yawa idan baka biyamun buk'ata taba wasu zasu biyamun ta juya masa baya danso take taga maganinta ya fara aiki kuwa.

Ga mamakinsa yau sai yaga bakinsa ya kasa bata amsa, saima rungumeta da yayi ya tsinci kansa dason rarrashin ta, yace "kiyi hakuri masoyiya bank'i kusantanki ba waidan banaso, inaga nafiki bukatan ki kusa dani ma, amma namiki alkawarin daga zaran jininki ya d'auke zan kusanceki bazan barkiba harsai kince kin gaji dan kanki.

Hanunsa ta buge tace da Allah kadaina tab'ani wawa kawai nusarin namiji wanda bai iya biyawa matansa bukatan taba, saidai kullun kayi hanyan zuwa gidan uwarka, waya sanima ko ita kake dannewa shiyasa bakajin sha'awata.

Muryansa yana b'ari yace wlh Zainab ba abunda ya tab'a shiga tsakanina da Umma,  kawai ina kai mata namane da drings dan tasamu tanajin dad'in bakinta, baddake taya ma zan kwanta da Umma kin manta ita uwatace?

Tace koma mainene daga yau na haramta maka zuwa wajenta sannan kuma ban yarda ka sake siya mata koda da omon wanki ba ko gishiri.

Ya sauk'o daga gadon ya tsuguna a gabanta idonsa yana zubar da hawaye yace " dan Allah kiyi hakuri kimun aikin gafara, Umma ni kad'ai nake kusa da ita kuma bazataji dad'iba idan taga bana zuwa.

"Wannan kuma kai ya shafa, wlh ko kai d'aya ta aifa naraba ku, idan kuma ban isa nasa ka maka doka kabiba katashi ka futamun a daki ka koma d'akin tsohuwar munafikan uwarka inyaso tabaka abunda nake baka.

Magananta ya b'ata masa rai sosai saidai bazai iya nuna mataba dolensa yayita lallab'inta, harya samu ta d'anyi masa murmushi.  

 Washe gari yaso yaje wajen Umma amma yana tsoron kar taga bai dawo da wuriba tayi fushi dashi, haka har sati ta shud'e baije wajen Umma ba,  a ransa yanaso yaje ya ganta a afili kuma koya dauki hanyan zuwa gidan sai zuciyarsa tace tome zakaje ka mata, gwanda ka d'auke kafanka yawan zuwa waje yanasa a tsaneka, yana komawa gida Zainab tace yaukan yazo ya danneta kaman doki dan jininta ya d'auke ko abinci bata barsa yaciba kaman abun kirki suka fara romance amma dayazo ga cimma gaci yana farawa sai abunsa ya kwanta, ba k'aramun zagi yashaba a wajenta, ba yadda ya iya dole yayita bata hakuri.

Umma tana zaune a d'aki takira wayan Faisal yakai sau goma bai d'aukaba saita kira layin Fa'izu yana d'agawa yace Umma na yanzu nakece zan kiraki namiki wani albishir!

Tace inajinka Auta nah"

Yace Umma wani k'aramun gida akamun tallansa kusa damu, shine nayita kiran wayan Faisal akan ya dubamun gidan yaga idan yayi nasiya miki,  bai d'aukaba kusan kwana hud'u kenan ina kiransa baya d'auka,  shine nakira mai unguwa yaje ya dubamu  na turo masa kud'in yanzu gida ya dawo mallakin ki.

Umma tayi kabbara tace "Allah ya muku albarka autana Allah yasa ku gama da duniya lafiya, nima tunjiya nake neman wayan Faisal amma bai d'auka ba, yanzu ma ina tunanin konaje gidansa naji ko lafiya ya b'uya haka.

Fa'izu yace bazan kira Zainab naji ko yana gida...

🌐 HAJOW 🌐
Hakuri da juriya online writer's.

Bissimillahin Rahmanin Rahim.

🅿️49\50
Ya kira wayanta yakai sau goma amma ba'a d'agaba, bai hak'ura da kiraba,  ya cigaba da kiran wayan yakira ya yanke, zuwa yanzu harya fara tsoron ko wani abune ya faru da d'an uwansa, ya kira number Sumayyah ringing biyu ta d'aga bai tsaya amsa gaisuwanta ba, yace mata kina ganin Faisal?

Tace mishi yana nan yanzu hakama yana k'ofan d'akinsa yana taya matarsa aiki.

Ya sake jefo mata tambaya "kina ganin wayansa a hanunsa?

Tace "eh yana hanunsa"

"Matar safa itama wayanta yana nan?

Tace eh itama wayanta yana nan, dan ina shara yanzu naji tana cewa wai wani shashasha ya dameta da waya, sainaji Yaya Faisal yanace mata nima yayita kirana banida lokacin sane, domin na tsanesa yanzu sai suka kwashe da dariya."

Kashe wayan yayi yana furta "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un me yake faruwa da d'an uwana? Mai Matarsa ta masa? Yaso ya kira Umma ya fad'a mata bayanin dayaji akan Faisal saiya fasa dan bayaso taji abunda zai d'aga mata hankali, kawai saiya fara rok'on abarsa yaje gida, yama Ogansu k'aryan ma'aifiyarsa bata da lafiyane.

Yaji sa'a kuwa ya barshi, uniform kawai yaje ya cire ya kama hanyan bauchi.

Umma kuwa suna gama waya da Fa'izu ta d'auki hijabinta zata futa Goggo Larai ta kwashe da dariya tace "Hajara karkiba kanki wahala wajen zuwa bikon d'anki domin yanzu mai abu ta kwace abunta, yanzu zanga yaran da za'a tara anamana dariya a cikin gida, muzuba nidake wlh saina fiki gadara da d'an cikin ki, idan kuma kina ganin k'aryane kizuba ido kigani.

Duk da maganan Goggo Larai ya tab'a mata zuciya amma sai tayi murmushi kawai ta futa batace mata komai ba, gidan su Faisal ta wuce direct ta jima tana buga k'ofa amma ba'azo an bud'eba saida ta kira wayan Sumayyah kafin tazo da sauri zata bud'e.

Zainab ta daka mata tsawa tace ubanwa ya baki izinin bud'emun gida?

Cikin sanyi murya tace "Umma ce tazo"

Murmushin mugunta tayi ta kalli Faisal da yake mata danna tace jeki bud'e mata k'ofan, kai kuma ka kuskura naga idonka ya kalleta namaka rantsuwa da Allah saina kwakwule idonka.

Sumayyah taje ta bud'e k'ofan tana cewa Umma yau kece a gidan namu, sannu da zuwa Umman mu.

Zainab tace da Allah ki b'acewa mutane anan kin wani damemu dawani sannu da zuwa sai kace kinga wata tsohuwar arziki.

Faisal tunda yaji muryan Umma jikinsa ya fara b'ari yanaso ya d'aga ido yaganta amma ba hali, Umma tace sannu Faisal kwana biyu kana nan kuwa, banganka bane shine nace bazanzo na duba lafiyanka!

Bayida bakin bata amsa sai dad'a sunkuyar da kansa da yayi, yanajin k'unan abun a ransa,  Zainab tace tunda kin gansa lafiya sai kiyi gaba ko dan natsani a tsaya mun akai.

Da mamaki take kallonta tace baki ganeni bane Zainab?

Tace ganewa na nawa kuma bakece  Hajara ba uwar Faisal da Fa'izu, ko akwai abunda kikeso na fad'a mikine bayan wannan ki tabbatar nagane ki?

Sumayyah taja hanun Umma tace Umma zomuje kisha ruwa naga kaman kingaji da tsayuwa.


Wani wawan tsawa Zainab ta daka musu tace ke har kin isa kice tashiga bada izinina ba, wlh ki kiyayeni inba hakaba kina daf da barin gidan nan, sake hanun Umma tayi tashiga d'aki da sauri ta d'auki wayanta da niyan kiran Fa'izu saita fasa dan gudun karta d'aga masa hankali yana wajen aiki saita kira Safiyyah tana d'agawa tasaka mata kuka tace JININA matan Yaya Faisal tasakani gaba, nan ta kwashe komai ta fad'a mata, tunda suke bata tab'ajin Safiyyah tayi zagiba sai yau.

Tace bansan ke wawiya bane sai yau Sumayyah, ashe zaki iya bari a zagi Umma a gabanki bakici ubanta ba, na rantse miki idan bakije kincimun mutuncin matarnan yanzu ba bazan sake d'aukan wayanki ba, nasan ta gama da Yaya Faisal ne bazai iya magana ba yanzu, kije nasan ko dukan matansa kikayi bazai miki magana ba, kinuna mata indai akan Umma zaki iya cin mutumcin ta, ta sake daka mata tsawa tace wai me kike jirane da bazaki kashe wayan kije ba?

Hanu na b'ari Sumayyah ta kashe wayan ta futa, taga Zainab natura Umma wai tafuta a gidan, batasan lokacin data dakawa Zainab tsawa ba wanda ita kanta saida ta razana dajin tsawan datayi, tasake tattaro jarunta tace nalura ke k'araman mahaukaciya ce, amma idan baki dawo nutsuwarki ba zan baki maganin mahaukata yanzu, wawiya wanda batasan darajan iyayen taba ballan tana kisan na wani, wlh wannan ya zamo na k'arshe dazaki sakema wannan uwa mai daraja da kima a duniya irin wannan haukan.

Tazo ta rusuna a gaban Umma tace "Umma kizo mu shiga ciki"

Umma ta dafata tace karki damu Sumayyah zan tafi gida, Allah ya miki albarka.

Zainab tace mu kuma ai iyayen mu bacewa sukayi muje zamu ganiba, kullun albarka suke samana, da d'in abun munada mutane masu saka mana albarka kullun, ke kuma Sumayyah zakisan nace ta futa kindawo da ita.

Ba wanda ya kulata Sumayyah cikin girmamawa tace Umma idan kikak'i shiga zanga kaman nima namiki laifine"

Umma ta bita suka shiga d'akin ta d'auko mata abinci da ruwa mai sanyi, ruwan kawai Umma ta iya sha, Umma tace "zan tafi gida, amma dan Allah karki fad'awa Fa'izu abunda ya faru, zanyi tayiwa Faisal addu'a har Allah ya futar dashi daga cikin kaidin matan nan.


Sumayyah tace to Umma insha Allahu bazan fad'a masa ba, saidai na fad'awa Safiyyah kuma nasan ita bazata fad'a wa kowa ba, sukayi sallamah da Ummah ta futa tana alhinin abunda ya faru, bayan futan Umma bada jimawa ba Zainab tacewa Faisal ya kaimata ruwan wanka, ta shirya tace "yau ba kwanan ka bane inada inda zanje sai bayan kwana uku zan dawo, yace saikin dawo masoyiya Allah ya tsaremun ke.

Tace mun shuru bana buk'atan addu'an ka wanda uwata takemun ya isheni, saidai idan nadawo naji labarin kaje gidan wannan munafukan tsohuwar uwar taka zakasha mamakin abunda zan maka"

Yace "wlh Zainab tunda kika hanani zuwa gidan ban sake bin kota anguwan ba, kema kinsan bazan karya umurnin kiba"

Tace wannan kuma ya rage naka,  tafuta taje k'ofan d'akin Sumayyah tace ke bak'ar muna fuka wlh ki kiyyayi dawowata zakisha mamakin hukuncin dazan d'auka akanki ta juya ta futa abunta.

Itako Sumayyah tana d'aki suna waya da Safiyyah batasan me take cewa ba, Safiyyah tace "Allah sarki Ummanah wannan tsinanniyan matan ta sakata a damuwa amma insha Allahu komai zaizo da sauki zamuyita addu'a har Allah ya warware mana komai, sannan ko mijinki ya dawo kibari ya gani da idonsa ko kuma ita Umma ta fad'a masa da bakinta, dan labari mai dad'i ake saurin isarwa"

Sumayyah tace haka Umma tace mun karna fad'a masa dan gudun kar hankalinsa ya tashi, suka tab'a hira kad'an kafin sukayi sallamah.

Bayan gama wayansu Sumayyah taji zaman kad'eci ya dameta takira Mama ta rok'eta kozata bata aron Kanwanta Zahra tazo ta mata kwana biyu, kicin sa'a kuwa Mama ta amince amma da sharad'in kartasa mata y'a aikin domin ba baiwa zata kawo mata ba.


Zainab tana isa wajen boka ya daka mata tsawa yace ke harkinkai nace miki tunsafe zakina zuwa wajena ranan zuwanki, shine zaki kwaso k'afa kizo mun da yamma.

Tasan idan bata bishi a hankali ba zai iya b'ata mata aikinta, yasa ta fara masa kukan k'arya kafin tace mushiga ciki na fad'a maka dalilina nasan zaka fahince ni.

Ya tashi suka shiga ciki yana huci, tasan lagon boka shiyasa suna shiga ciki tayi saurin kama liliyansa tasaka a bakinta nan da nan ya sauk'a a layi daganan suka fad'a duniyan shed'anu ba kunya ba tsoron Allah, bayan sun dayo hayyacin su tace boka kayi hakuri uwarsa ce tace ita dole saitayi magana da d'anta shine na tsaya saida nakoreta a gidan kafin na taho.

Boka yace tunda dulmiyewa kikeso zan k'ara miki wani maganin dazai dena kallan uwarsa da k'aninsa da mutumci, saidai da sharad'i!

Tace meye sharad'in boka?

Yace sharad'in yana da wahala sosai, idan kuma kikayi wasa dashi zakisha wahala a rayuwarki"

"Zan kiye komai wahalan sa"

Boka yace sharad'n shine........

🅿️51\52
Sharad'in shine idan kikayi amfani da maganin nan to kitabbatar mijinki bazai k'ara aure ba, inhar ya k'ara aure daga ranan dayayi kwanciyan aure da yarinyan tabbas zaki wulak'anta a duniyan ma baki d'aya.

Dariya tayi tace nasan koka gwada ni kad'e zaka gani,  mucigaba daga yadda muka tsaya kawai wannan ai mai sauk'i ne, Faisal yasha fad'amun tunkafin muyi aure, bayida ra'ayin mata biyu, sannan yacemun sonda yakemun bazai iya barinshi yaga wata y'a mace a matsayin mace ba, kullun nikad'e yake gani da fuskan mata, sauran duk maza yake ganinsu.


Boka yace karkiyi saurin yanke wannan hukuncin, maza dubu nawane sukema matansu irin wannan alk'awarin daga baya kuma su karya, zan bincika miki kwaryan ruwana kafin kitafi mugani ko zai iya k'ara aure.

Tace Boka nasan Faisal harya mutu bazai sake k'ara aure ba, kawai mucigaba da abunda ya kawoni dan dama yunwansa nakeji aradu, suka cigaba da fasik'ancin su.

Faisal saida ya tabbatar Zainab tayi nisa kafin shima ya shirya ya tafi Azare wajen Fa'izu, danso yake ya fad'a masa halinda yake ciki, sai kuma sukayi sab'ani yana zuwa suka had'u da Safiyyah.

Safiyyah tace yau wazan gani a garinmu kaman Yaya Faisal d'ina?

Yayi murmushi yace tabbas abunda idonki ya nuna miki daidaine, nine a garinku yau "tace mushiga daga ciki, yaje suka gaisa da Inna, kafin ya futo Safiyyah tace Yaya Faisal me kazoyi a garinmu?

Ya rasa wani irin k'arya zai mata kawai ya tsinci bakinsa yana cewa munzo wani aikine da maigida na shine nace bazanma Fa'izu bazata"

Tace lallai kun tafka sab'ani, dan Yaya Fa'izu bai jima da tafiya Bauchi ba"

Yayi shuru kaman meson tuna wani abu can saiyace mata ba komai nasan zai dawo ya sameni anan, dama akwai wani magana mai muhinmanci dana keso mu tattauna dashi ne, dan abun yanacina arai narasa taya zanyi maganin abun, amma nasan shi zai iya samo mun mafita.

Tace Allah ya kawo mana mafuta gaba d'ayan mu,  harnaji dad'i dakace zaka jirasa, tayita masa hira daganan tajashi yawo dasunan wai ya zubar da gajiya, atake ya manta damuwan da yake ciki, ya saki jiki da ita sosai.


Fa'izu da mangariba ya iso garin bauchi gidan Umma ya fara zuwa, a d'aki ya sameta yana ganinta yasan tabbas tana cikin damuwa dan idonta duk sun kumbura halamun taci kuka.

Tana ganinsa tahau dariya tana cewa maraba da mutanen Azare, dan bataso yagane tana cikin damuwa, batasan tun shugowan sa ya fahinci halinda take ciki ba, suka gaisa ta bambayesa ya yabaro mutanen Azare.

Yace lafiyansu qalau sunce na gaisheki.

Tace Allah sarki mutanen kirki, ya Sumayyah?

Yace Umma ai banje gida ba nan nafara zuwa, yanzu tunda naganki lafiyan ki qalau bazanje gida, daga nan inaso kafin natafi ki koma sabon gidan ki.

Umma tace to masha Allah, Allah ya sanya albarka, aranta kuwa taji dad'in tashinta a gidan kodan habaicin da Goggo Larai take mata.

Fa'izu yana zuwa gidan sa ya kira Sumayyah yace kizo ki bud'emun k'ofa gani a waje.

Tayi dariya tace tsokanana zakayi ko dan kaga inason ka dawo"

Yace to kizo ki bud'e idan baki ganni ba saikici tarana, ta taso badan ta yarda ba tun kafin ta bud'e k'ofan k'anshin turaran sane ya fara mata sallama da sauri ta bud'e masa kofan, bai k'arasa shigowa ba tajawosa ta rungumesa tana murnan dawowan sa.

Shima yaji dad'i da yadda ta nuna farin cikin ganin sa ya dad'a rungumeta a jikinsa, yace mata ina mutanen gidan?

"Inaga sun futa dan d'azu naga Yaya Faisal ya futa da jaka a hanunsa halamun zaiyi tafiya, ita kuma Zainab inaga ta futa dan datana nan da yanzu ta futo maka sannu dazuwa.

Yace hakane to mushiga daga ciki nagaji gashi gobe inaso muje mugyarawa Umma gidanta dan a goben nakeso ta dawo.

Ba k'aramun murna Sumayyah tayi ba jin Umma zata dawo kusa da ita, washe gari kaman yadda ya fad'a sukaje suka gyarawa Umma gidanta, gidane d'an k'arami mai d'akuna uku da bayan gida da kitchen.

Umma kayan sawanta kawai ta d'auko ta rufo k'ofanta y'an unguwa suka rakota sabon gidanta, Umma tayita zuba ido kozataga Faisal ya shigo amma shuru da daddare suna zaune itada Fa'izu da Sumayyah ta dubi Fa'izu tace banga Faisal ba ko lafiya?

Sumayyah najin haka ta tashi tace Umma ina zuwa bazanje bayan gida, tana futa ta wuce gidan ta.

Fa'izu yace Umma nima tunda nazo bangansu ba dagashi har matarsa hala sunyi tafiyane, Umma tace Allah ya kyauta, Fa'izu yayita k'ok'arin mata hira har saida ya tabbatar damuwan ya d'an  ragu a zuciyarta, yaga bacci ya fara d'aukanta kafin ya mata sallamah ya tafi.

Yana zuwa yaga Sumayyah harta fara bacci akan kujera, ya fara mata tafiyan tsutsa a jiki ta bud'e idonta a hankali tace harka dawo?

Yace ai dole kicemun har nadawo tunda kin gudu kinbarni, tayi murmushi ta kawar da tambayan daya mata da cewe Hubbi namaka laifi, na rok'i Mama ta turomun Zahra ta kwanamun biyu batard dana nemi izinin kaba"

Yasa yatsan shi akan bakinta halamun tayi shuru, yace Sumayyah nan gidanki ne kinada ikon ki gayyaci duk wanda kikeso, tunda kince gobe zatazo da safe zamuje nasiya mata katifa sainasa mata a d'akin kusa damu kafin na tafi, taji dad'i sosai da yadda ya nuna kulawarsa akan y'ar uwanta, tace amma zaka kaimun sati kafin ka tafi ko?

Yace da kwana d'aya zanyi, rashin dawowan Faisal ya sakani dad'a kwana inaso muyi magana mai muhinmanci da shine, tace to Allah yasa ya dawo da wuri, daganan suka fara farantawa juna, washe gari suka siyo katifa da ledan d'aki, zuwa karfe d'ayan rana  Zahra ta iso murna a wajen Sumayyah sai wanda ya gani.


Faisal ya sake jiki da Safiyyah sosai, d'an zamansu na kwana biyu da sukayi ya aifar da shak'uwa mai k'arfi a tsakanin su, kullun saita futa dashi tayita zaga gari da shi yau kwanansa uku suna zaune a k'ofan gidan su da safe yace mata Safiyyah inada wani tambayan da nakeso na miki amma inajin tsoro, dan bansan taya zaki fahinci maganata ba.

Fuskanta d'auke da murmushi tace Yaya Faisal nasan idan zakamun bayani da yaran da nakeji dole zan fahinci abunda kake nufi.

Ya nunfasa kafin yace najima ina raya wani abu a raina amma nakasa furtasa dan bansan ta ina zan fara furtawa ba.

Safiyyah tace kodai tunanin Aunty Zee big body ne yasa ka kasa furtawa, naga k'ok'arinta ma data barka har kwana uku batare data kiraka a wayaba"

Gaban shine ya bada wani rass rass ya zaro ido waje yace harnayi kwana uku a garin nan ya zamo dole yanzu na tafi.

Safiyyah tace ka nutsu mana Yaya Faisal daga cewa kwananka uku shine zaka wani kid'ime, kuma ai magana mukeyi saika jira idan mun gama saika tafi.

Yace kiyi hakuri Safiyyah idan na nakoma zan kiraki a waya mu k'arasa, ya d'auko kayansa da sauri ya tafi kosu Inna baisamu daman musu sallama ba damuwan sa yagansa a gida kar Zainab ta dawo bata sameshi ba, dan inbai mantaba kwana uku tace masa zatayi.


Fa'izu yau ya ciki kwana uku a bauchi yaje yama Umma sallamah yace Umma dama Faisal nayita jira kozai dawo to har yau shuru ina gudun karnayi laifi a wajen aiki shiyasa zan tafi, Umma ta mishi addu'a sosai ya futa.


Sumayyah kaman tasaka kuka da zai tafi, saida tarakasa har tasha motansu ya tashi kafin tadawo gida.



Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️53\54
Faisal tun amota gabansa yaketa fad'uwa hankalinsa bai kwanta ba  saida yagansa a gida ya kuma ga Zainab har lokacin bata dawo ba, cikin sauri ya fara gyaran d'akin ya kad'e duk k'uran kafin ya goge ko ina, ya saka musu turaran wuta, ya d'aura girki cikin k'ank'anin lokaci ya gama komai yayi wanka, sai yanzu Safiyyah ta fad'o masa yayi murmushin dashi kad'e yasan ma'anarsa, ya d'auki wayarsa ya kirata ringing d'aya ta d'auka tace "amincin Allah ya tabbata a gareka Yayanah"

Saida ya gyara kwanciyan sa kafin yace "Amin tare dake"

Tace "ka'isa lafiya Yayanah?

"Na iso lafiya qalau Alhamdulillah saidai zuciyata tak'i bani lafiya saboda d'inbin kewarki, dazaki ganni a halin yanzu saiki d'auka banida lafiyane.

"Nima ji nake duk garin ba dad'i saboda rashinka, zuciyata sai k'unci nakeji kaman nayi kuka" tabashi amsa.

Yace "Safiyyah bazan b'oye miki ba wlh tun zuwanki bikina Allah ya jarabeni da sonki, saidai koda yaushe nayi yunk'urin sanar dake sai tsoro da fargaba su hanani, dan gani nake kaman kinfi k'arfina, shine maganan danace ina tsoron sanar dake a koda yaushe, nasan inhar kikace baki sona shakka babu zuciyata zata iya fashewa na mutu a take" ya k'arasa maganan yana jan nunfashi.

Shuru tayi takasa basa amsa, dan tarasa mema zatace masa, a zuciyarta tace idan nace bana sonka nama kaina k'arya, amma taya zance mishi na amince, sannan idan nace masa banason sa banma Yaya Fa'izu da kaina adalci ba, nasan Yaya Fa'izu zaiyi murna idan yaji muna soyayya da d'an uwansa, kuma ta wannan daman ne kawai zansamu daman dawowa Umma da d'anta.

Jin shurunta yayi yawa yace Safiyyah ki taimakeni ki fad'amun abunda yake zuciyarki, saidai nasan zanyi bak'in ciki harna iya kamuwa da ciwon zuciya idan kikace baki sona, saidai kuma idan hakan ya kasance ba yadda na iya zanyi takwakkali na rungumi kaddarata, shurune ya sake biyo baya kaman zaiyi kuka yace kimun magana dan Allah!

Muryan ta a sanyaye ta kira sunan sa "Yaya Faisal?

Saida tsikan jikinsa ya tashi saboda yadda yaji taja sunan sa, sai yaji kaman duk duniyan nan ba wanda ya kaita iya kiran sunan sa, da sauri ya amsa muryan shi har yana sark'ewa da yawu yace " na'am Safiyyah"

Ajiyan zuciya taketa sauk'ewa kaman wanda tayi gudu, tace Yaya Faisal bazan iya baka amsa da bakina ba, saboda kalman tamun nauyi a bakina bansan taya zan furta taba, kayi hakuri zan turo maka da sak'o.

Yace yasalam shikenan rayuwata tazo k'arshe muryanshi na rawa kaman me shirin kuka ya cigaba da cewa Safiyyah duk kalman da kikaji ancewa mutum baza'a iya fad'a masa da bakiba,  ba kalma mai dad'i bane, ba komai Safiyyah na hakura amma kisani sanki shine ajalina kitt ya kashe wayan, ya kifa kansa akan kujera ya saki kuka maicin rai.

Safiyyah wayan tayita kallo tana murmushi tace najima inajin wani abu akanka bansan so bane saida ka furta inasonka nima Yaya  Faisal d'ina,  bazan iya furta maka da bakina ba, saboda inajin kunyan ka bansan ta inda zan fara fad'a maka bane.

Zainab bayan sun gama shed'anan cinsu da boka,  ta shirya zata tafi, suka koma wajen tsafin Boka,  dan idan tazo suka shiga ciki basa futowa sai zata tafi, duk wanda yazo wajensa saida ya gaji ya tafi, yana zama ya d'auko k'ok'on ruwan tsafin sa, yayi yarensu na bokoye kafin yakira sunan Faisal ya buga ruwan da sanda, saiga fuskan Faisal da wata mace, saidai ita mace haske ya rufe fuskanta duk yadda ya buga tsafinsa ya kasa ganin fuskan yarinyan, kuma dodon tsafinsa ya gaya masa yarinyan itace waraka wa Faisal, saidai bazai iya fad'a mata ba, dankar tace zata canja boka, yarufe ba tare daya nuna mata ba yace tabbas mijinki zai k'ara aure.

A razane ta mik'e tace Boka duk yadda za'ayi ka kashe yarinyan tun kafin ya aureta ta k'arasa maganan ta huci, yace mata kizauna na fad'a miki mafuta, ta koma ta zauna, tace kanuna mun yarinyan naganta?

K'ok'on ya d'auko ya ajiye a gabanta yace fuskan yarinyan yak'i bud'ewa, saidai daga ganin halamun ba zaman dad'i sukeyi ba, kingansa a kwance ya rik'e kansa itama gata a yashe kaman gawa.

Boka ya fuskanceta dakyau yace "Zee big body dole mijinki zai k'ara aure, kuma auran hutune a gareki domin tamkar baiwa za'a kawo miki, kuma ita zata ta taimaka miki wajen mallakar komai da kike buk'ata, inyaso saina baki abunda zaki zuba mata tasha ta mutu.

Baki tasake tana dariya tace shikenan angama   Boka idan dahali ka matso da auran ayishi nan kusa, dan nasan bayida jijiyan dazai jiyar da ita dad'i.

Ya d'auko wani garin magani ya bata yace wannan ki shafa a fuska ki, kafin ki shiga gida,  indai kina waje duk abunda kikace ya aikata zaiyi cikin gaggawa, kuma bazai tab'a ganin kyan kowace maceba saike, sannan idan yayi aure karki kuskura kibari ya kusanci yarinya nan, domin suna kwanciyan aure komai namu zai ruguje.

Tace "Boka ai jijiyarsa baya tashi kwata²"

Yace dukda haka kiyi iya k'ok'rin ki wajen hana komai, idan anyi karki yarda su kwana d'aki d'aya.

Tace shikenan Boka, kodai za'a fasa auran ne?

Yace daga farko naduba hakan sai naga bazamu iya hanawa ba, idan kuma mun dage to komai naki zai iya b'aci, dole mubarshi yayi aure.

Sukayi sallamah ta tafi jikinta a sanyaye, tana isa gida lolacin ana sallan isha, bata same shi a d'akiba ya tafi masallaci, taga komai a gyare ga abinci ya girka mata, ta d'auko maganin ta shafa a fuskan ta, ko kunya babu ta zauna tafaracin abincin daya girka mata.

Faisal ana idar da sallah yayi addu'a ya futo wayarshi ya d'auka ya fara rubutawa Safiyyah sak'o kaman haka-:

*Inason kasan cewa tare dake har abada, aduk lolacin dana samu soyayyarki zanji tamkar ni ne na fi kowa sa’a a duniya, a dukkan sanda na rintse idanuwa na sai na hango ni tare da ke muna zaune waje d'aya kina fad'amun kalamai masu dad'i, idan na kwanta bacci sai na yi mafarkin mun zama mata da miji,  ina sonki, tare da son kasancewa tare da ke har abada, ki huta Lafiya sahibul k'albina, a cikin idanuwan ki akwai wani sirri  da yake haska mun duniyata a cikin kowanne dare, amma haske mafi haskaka fuska da yaye duhun zuciyana na gan shi ne a cikin idanuwanki. Ki kular mun da kanki ina son ki.*

Yana gama rubutawa ya tura mata, kafin ya goge a wayansa ya shiga cikin gida, yana shiga ya ganta tanacin abinci ta d'ago fuska tana kallonsa wani muni fuskanta ya masa yau, saidai kuma tsoranta ya d'ada shiga zuciyar sa, tace daga ina kake?

Cikin in'ina yace na.je..masallacine, ta kawar da fuskanta tana gamacin abincin tace mushiga kamun danna, ya bita shiya cire mata kaya, yayita mata danna hartayi bacci, yana ganin tayi bacci ya tashi ya shiga toilet yayita amae dan warin jikinta nayau yafi na kullun, ya futa ya koma falo ya duba wayansa ko Safiyyah ta turo masa da amsa.

Yana dubawa yaga shuru ya kifa wayan a k'irjinsa yanajin k'una a ransa,  yace ya Allah karka jarabeni dason wanda bata sona, anan bacci ya d'aukesa bai farkaba sai asuba, yayi alola yatafi sallah, harya dawo Zainab bata motsaba tanata baccinta harda minshari.

Safiyyah tana ganin sak'onsa a wayan ta taji wani sanyin dad'i ya mamaye zuciyarta, Inna tace Safiyyah yau tunda Faisal ya tafi kike cikin farin ciki, zoki fad'amun abunda yasaki farin ciki, Fa'izu da shigowan sa kenan yaji an ambaci sunan Faisal ya k'araso ya gaishe da Inna.

Safiyyyah tace kukan akwaiku da sab'ani kaman maciji, ranan daka tafiya Yaya Faisal yazo nemanka yaukuma ya tafiya kai kuma kadawo"

Yace "Faisal...

🅿️55\56
Yace "Faisal d'ina kike nufi"

Tace "ko kunya wai Faisal d'inka, Yaya Faisal d'ina na bauchi nake fad'a maka"

Yayi shuru yana mamakin yadda abubuwa suke tafiya, yace Allah sarki munyi sab'ani, Yayanki baice ki fad'amun komai ba?

Tace " yace akwai magana mai muhinmanci da yakeso kuyi dashi, wai abun yana cimasa rai yarasa taya zaiyi maganin abun, yanaso ka nemo masa mafuta.

Kansa ya rik'e yace innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un mai yake faruwa da d'an uwana ne, idan nakirasa baya d'agawa, sannan kuma yazo nemana amma ya kasa kirana yace nadawo ko kuma shiya juya muhad'u acan, sannan kona kirasa awaya baya d'auka, ya Allah ka kawo mana agaji.

Inna tace nima na fiskanci yana cikin damuwa, amma mu had'u muna yawan masa addu'a shine kawai mafuta, ya tashi jiki a sanyaye ya tafi dakinsu.


Karfe shida Faisal ya had'a musu abun karyawa, harya shirya ya futa bata tashi a bacci ba, ya tafi wajen aikin sa, kwata² ba walwala a tare dashi a wajen aiki sukayita tambayan sa mai yake damun sa, yace musu ba komai yanayin rayuwane kawai, duk bayan minti d'aya saiya duba wayansa yaga ko Safiyyah ta turo masa sak'o, dayaga bazai iya jure rashin jinta ba ya kira number ta yana fara ringing ta d'auka tayita sallamah baiyi maganaba jin muryanta kawai dayayi ya sauk'ar masa da nutsuwa a zuciyar sa, saita kashe wayan ta zaci network ne baida kyau, ya sake kiranta shima tayita sallamah baiyi magana ba saita sake kashe wayan, sak'o ya tura mata da cewa.

Idan nasake kira kika kashe mun waya ban yafe ba, Safiyyah kibarni naji da abu d'aya mana, baki sona bakison naji muryanki! Maina miki da kika tsaneni haka, idan har bakiso zuciyata ta fashe idan nakira ko zagina kiyi tayi zanji dad'i. Ya tura mata.

Tana karantawa takira shi bai d'auka ba harya yanke, ya kira ta d'aga da sauri tace "Yayanah baka mun komai ba wlh, kuma ban tsaneka ba, kabari watarana zan baka labari mai dad'i, wanda zai sakaka nishad'i.

Duk maganan da takeyi baice mata komai ba, cikin shagwab'a tace Yayanah nima kamun magana naji dad'i dan Allah.


Ajiyan zuciya ya sauk'e kafin ya kashe wayansa, ya tura mata da sak'on

Safiyyah baza kisake jin muryata ba harsai ranan da kika bani amsa, abu d'aya zaki fad'amun kawai ya gagareki kice Faisal bana sonka mu cigaba da zama a matsayin wa da k'anwan sa, dazaran kin fad'amun haka shikenan zuciyata zata zauna lafiya nikuma zan daina damunki. Yana tura mata ya tashi ya cigaba da aikin sa koba komai jin muryanta ya dad'a masa kuzari.

Zainab bata farka ba sai k'arfe goma sha d'ayan rana, tajeta wanke bakinta ta kafin ta duba kayan karyawan da Faisal ya had'a mata tana cikin karyawa taji kaman muryan mutane biyu a tsakar gida, tafuto danta tabbatar Sumayyah tagani da wata yarinyan dabasai ta tambaya ba tasan k'anwan tace, suna d'akin da Faisal ya gyara, zuwa tayi tana duba d'akin dakyau kafin ta koma d'akinta ta d'auki wayanta takira Baba Abu,  suna gama gaisawa tace Baba Abu ki turomun Kubra da kayanta yanzu.

Baba Abu bata tambayeta daliliba tace mata gatanan zuwa yanzu kuwa, kiran Goggo Laira ne ya shigo wayanta tana d'agawa Goggo Larai tace mata "Zee kitashi tsaye kisan inda darai ya miki, domin Umma Hajara tasa Faisal ya siya mata gida a kusa da ita, ke kina zaune kin bud'e baki kinata munshari, kuma yau nakejin labarin ta k'arb'o magani agun Bokanta Faisal na dawowa zatasa sa yayi aure ya koreki, shiyasa nace barin fad'a miki kisan abunyi tunkafin lokaci ya k'ure miki.

Zainab ta k'unduma ashar tace lallai Umma Hajara batasan koni waye ba, amma tabbas zan futo mata da ainihin colour na.


Kubra nazuwa tace mata ta ajiye kayanta a d'akin da Zahra take anan zatana kwana, koda Sumayyah taganta batace mata komai ba, tacewa Zahra dan Allah kota nemeki da fad'a karki kulata, ko kuma kizo mu koma d'akina da kwana idan Fa'izu ya dawo saiki dawo d'akin kuna kwana tare.

Koda Fa'izu yazo weekend yaga Kubra baice mata komaiba, duk yadda yaso su had'u da Faisal amma abun ya gagara, kafin ya tashi Faisal ya futa, yana dawowa suke rufe k'ofa, abun ya fara d'aure masa kai sosai, kawai ya du'ufa da addu'a, harya tafi basu had'uba.

Bayan tafiyan sa Kubra ta kwashe kayanta tatafi gida tace zaman gidan ya isheta, Sumayyah dataji haka tacewa Zahra ta d'auko katifan da daddare suna kwana d'aki d'aya tunda Kubra ta tafi, ta d'auko zata kai d'akin Sumayyah, Zainab ta futo tace ke ubanwa ya baki izinin d'aukan katifan nan?

Zahra tace zankai d'akin Aunty Sumayyah ne muna kwana tare"

Tace dake Ubankine ya siya shiyasa zakice dakikai d'akin y'ar uwarki, to tun wuri ki mayar dashi tun kafin raina ya b'aci.

Sumayyah tace eh ba Ubanta bane ya siya kaman yadda kema ba Ubanki bane ya siya, Zainab bansan ke wata irin jahila bane kwata² bakison zaman lafiya a rayuwarki, muzuba nidake shege ka fasa.

Zainab tayo kanta tana cewa dan abu kazan ubanki har kinkai ina magana kinayi wayeke a garinnan waye ubanki.

Sumayyah itama idonta ya bushe yau ko d'igon tsoron Zainab babu a idonta tace kije azare ki tambayi waye Ubana za'a fad'a miki, kowa yaganni yasan nasamu cikikken tarbiya daga wajen uwa da Uba, ba irinki ba gantalalliya wanda batasan darajan Ubanta ba.

Ji kake tasss Zainab ta d'auketa da mari, kafin ta mayar da hanu Safiyyah ta kifa mata mari da duka hanunta biyu, nan dambe ya kaure a tsakaninsu,  Zahra ta rasa me zatayi, tabasu hakuri amma ba wanda ya saurareta, ta futa da gudu taje ta fad'awa Umma, suka dawo tare.

Umma tashiga tsakiyansu tana basu hakuri Zainab ta d'aga hanu ta tsinkawa Umma mari a firgicce Umma ta d'ago tana kallonta "tace Zainab ni kika mara.

Tace idan kika sake magana anan da bulala zan zaneki, jin haka yasa Sumayyah nemo bulala ta fara zane Zainab tana cewa idan bakisan darajan manyaba yau bazan koya miki yadda ake girmama su.

Mutanen layin  sunyi iya bakin k'okarinsu waje rabasu amma abu yak'i, Zahra ta sake d'aukan waya ta kira Fa'izu ta sheda masa abunda yake faruwa, ko izini bai nema ba ya shiga mota ya taho bauchi, har ya iso basu daina fad'a ba, anrabasu amma Zainab taki bari Sumayyah kuma tace tagaji da cin kashin data keyi akanta, Fa'izu yana shigowa daidai Zainab ta sake d'aga hanu ta tsinkawa Umma mari.

Baisan lokacin daya k'araso ba ya fara ball da ita yana kifa mata mari, dakyar tasamu ta gudu d'aki ta rufe kanta, wayanta ta d'auka takira Faisal tace masa ga Fa'izu ya dawo sai dukanta yakeyi kaman zai kasheta bata jira jin mezaice ba ta kashe wayanta, ta kira k'annanta maza sukazo, da gora da adduna akan yau saisun kashe Fa'izu da y'an uwansa, dakyar mutanen anguwa suka rok'esu suka tafi,

Faisal yayita maimaita kalman Fa'izu yana dukan Zainab kuma to meta masa, shida baya gari meya dawo dashi?

Ganin bame nashi amsa yasa ya cigaba da aikinsa hankali kwance, wayar shine ya sake ringing ya duba yaga Zainab ce take kiransa again, ya d'aga tace Faisal wato ban isa nace kazo ba kenan.

Cikin rawan murya yace mata dama ina wani aikine amm...tsawa ta daka masa tace "duk abunda kakeyi kabari kaje polise station kazomun da y'an sanda suzo su kwashemun tsinanniyar uwarka da k'aninka.

Yace to Masoyiya yanzu zan taho dasu, suna gama wayan ya tafi police station yayi report din gawasu sunzo gida sunata dukan matansa, ba b'ata lokaci y'an sanda sukazo suka tattara Umma da Faisal, al'amarin daya firgita y'an unguwan gaba d'aya kowa ka ganshi yana cikin al'ajabin wai yau d'ane yasa akazo aka kama uwarsa, Umma kuwa kuka take sosai kaman zata shid'e.


Fa'izu yayita bata hakuri, koda aka tambayesu meyasa yaje gidan ya daki matar aure yace shima gidan sane, kuma yaga tana dukan uwarsane shiyas raina ya b'aci, a matsayina na hukuma banbi dokaba na yanke hukunci da hanuna.

Sukace wani irin aiki kakeyi?

Yace nima police ne kaman ku, ya nuna musu ID card dinsa suna karantawa suka sara masa, kafin sukace ita matar da aka mara maiye matsayinta a wajen wanda ta mareta.

Yace ma'aifiyar mijinta ne, dukansu sunji mamakin Faisal da shida kansa yazo yasa aka kama uwarsa, D.P.O dakansa yasa aka kore shari'an suna komawa gida washe gari tasaka Faisal a gaba sukakai k'aran Umma da Faisal kotu.

Lokacin da sammaci yazo musu, basuyi wani mamaki ba, ranan da za'ayi shari'an suka halacci wajen, Alk'ali yasa aka kira k'aransu Umma da Faisal suka tsaya a wajen da ake tsayar da wanda ake karansu..


Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️57\58
Maga takadda ya karanta karansu, kafin alkali ya dubi Fa'izu da Umma yace kunji abunda ake tuhuman ku dashi, kuna da lauyane?

Faisal yace bamu da lauya"

Alk'ali yayi rubutu kafin ya d'ago idonsa ya kalli Fa'izu yace ko maizaku iyacewa akan tuhuman da ake muku na shiga gidan matan aure ku daketa?

Fa'izu ya gyara tsayuwa yace bamu shiga gidanta ba, ina wajen aiki aka kirani aka sanar dani matan Yayana tana dukan ma'aifiyar mu,  koda na iso agaban idona tayita tsinkawa Umman mu mari, shine abun yaban haushi nima na mareta"

Alkali yace kuma kace baka shiga gidanta ba!

Fa'izu yace eh gidan da take gidanmu ne nida Yayanah kuma duka iyalanmu a waje d'aya suke.

Alk'ali yayi rubutu kafin ya dubi Umma yace kozaki iya fad'a mana sunanki da kuma matsayinki awajen matar da akama sharri ta mareki, sannan kuma ki fad'a mana abunda ya faru hartakai hanu jikinki.

Umma da hawaye yaketa malala a fuskanta kaman anbud'e bakin bohol, tace  "sunana Hajara, matar kuma matar d'anane, ina zaune a gidana yarinya tazo da gudu tacemun matan Faisal da Fa'izu sunata fad'a, shine nashiga na basu hakuri akan su danena fad'an shine...kukane yaci k'arfinta takasa k'arasa maganan, wajen yayi shiru sai kukan Ummane kawai yake tashi Fa'izu shima yasaka kuka yanama Umma halamun tayi hakuri tayi shuru.

Umma tayi k'okarin danne kukanta tace shine dannace suyi hakuri sai Zainab ta fara marina tanata zagina, wajen ya kaure da salati kowa yanata fad'an albarkacin bakinsa, saida alk'ali ya buga tebur kafin akayi shuru.

Alkali ya dauk'i hankicif ya goge zufan fuskansa kafin ya dubi Umma yace "idan na fahinceki ke ma'aifiyar wanda yayi k'aranne?

Umma tace "eh shine babban d'ana"

Waje ya sake kaurewa da surutu, alkali ya buka tebur akayi shuru, yace yana buk'atar Faisal yafuto gaban filin ya tsaya.

Faisal ya futo yayi rantsuwa akan zai fad'a gaskiya.

Alkali ya dubesa yace kaji abunda wannan baiwar Allah ta fad'a, ko akwai abunda zaka iya fad'a.

Faisal yace naji kuma da gaske ni d'anta ne,  saidai dan ina d'anta ba'ace su taru suyita dukanmu mataba, saboda bancewa iyayenta idan tazo gidana dukanta y'an uwana zasunayi ba.

Alkali ya dubesa dakyau yace yanzu kanaso abima matarka hakkinta kenan.

Yace eh inaso wannan kotun mai adalci tabima matata hakkinta.

Alkali yayi rubutu kafin yace zaka iya komawa ka zauna.

Alkali yace ko matar da sukayi fad'a da Zainab tana nan, Sumayyah ta futo tayi rantsuwa akan zata fad'a gaskiya.

Alkali yace ko zaki iya fad'a mana sunanki da abunda ya had'aku da Zainab harya kaiku da kokuwa.

Tace sunana Sumayyah, sannan ta musu bayani daga farko har k'arshe.

Kafin Alkali yace ina buk'atan ganinku a chamba bayan angama shari'a.

Maga takadda ya tashi ya karonta k'aran gaba na na matar data ciki mijinta a jijiyarsa akan zai mata kishiya🤣.

Su Faisal suka futa suka jira alk'ali, bayan Alkali ya futo suka zauna duka a gabansa, Alkali yace yanzu kai Faisal kanaga rayuwa zata maka kyau kana shari'a da ma'aifiyar ka? Uwafa ba abun wasa bane.

Faisal saida ya kalli inda Zainab take kafin yace niba shari'a nakeyi da itaba, nakawo k'aransune akan sun dakamun mata na.

Alkali yayi murmushi yasan bawani abubane yake yawo dashi illa kaidin mata,

Yace yanzu nabaku nanda mako d'aya kuje ku sulhunta kanku a gida, duk yadda kukayi sai kuzo ku sanar dani, Zainab tace "Alkali ba maganan sulhu tsakanin mu kawai ayanke musu hukuncin akan abunda suka aikata kawai.

Alkali ya dubeta fuskansa ba walwala yace ba umurnin mutane ko shawaran su nake nemaba kafin na yanke hukunci, kuje na gama magana na.

Suka futa ran Zainab a b'ace dan ba haka tasoba taso a tozarta Umma da Faisal akaisu gidan yari, ita kuma Sumayyah taje ta gasata da kanta.

Bayan sun koma gida Zainab ta tirke Faisal a d'aki tace wlh bazan hakura ba niba jaka bane da uwarka zata dakeni,  dan haka ka tarasu kashe dawa Umman ka da shashashan k'aninka zasu bani diyyan dubu d'ari uku da hamsin, sannan dukansu zasu tsuguna a gabana su bani hakuri, hakanne kawai zaisa na hakura"

Yace duk yadda kikace haka za'ayi Masoyiya.

Bayan kwana biyu Baffa Tukur da mai unguwa suka tarasu a gidan Umma, Baffa Tukur ya musu nasiha sosai kafin yace inaso ranan da za'a koma kuce kun janye k'aran.

Faisal yace zamu iya janyewa amma sai sunyarda da sharad'a biyu!

Mai unguwa yace maiye sharad'in?

Zainab ta karkatar da d'an kwalinta gefe ta kafe Faisal da ido.

Faisal yace na farko Umma zata bayar da dubu dari da hamsin wa Zainab kud'in diyyan marin data mata,  Fa'izu kuma zai bata dubu d'ari biyu kud'in diyyan dukan daya mata sannan zaku tsuguna a gabanta kuce tayafe muku abunda kuka mata bazaku k'araba.

Fa'izu yace Faisal kanada hankali kuwa, kasan abunda kake fad'a Ummace zata tsuguna a gaban matanka tana neman gafaranta, Faisal ka dawo hayyacinka mana katuna Umman mune fa, wanda tasha wahala da mu ta renemu ba irin gatan daba ta nuna mana ba.

 Faisal yace danta renemu tasha wahala damu sai kuma na bari ta daka mun mata na kyaleta, meye ban mataba aduniyan nan ba, duk wani hakkinta na sauk'e mata, nasan nabata abunda yafi wanda ta kashemu idan naga dama sai muyi lissafi nabiyata kud'in data kashemun ni kuma zan lissafa abunda na kashe mata inyaso sai muyi lissafi wanda ciko ya biyosa saiya biya.

Fa'izu ya tashi ransa a mungun b'ace yace Faisal yaushe ka dawo mahaukaci ban saniba d...Baffa Tukur da mai Unguwa suka tashi suka tare Fa'izu Baffa Tukur yace Faisal ka d'auki matarka ku tafi Allah ya kaimu ranan da za'a koma shari'an.

Zainab ta rungume shi ta gefe tace masoyi tashi mu tafi, idan kunne yaji jiki ya tsira, subi umurni su tsira, suk'i sutafi gidan yari, kafin ya tashi rungume da ita suka futa daga gidan Umma.

Mai unguwa ya bisu da ido kafin ya mayar da kallonsa kan Umma yace "Hajara dan Allah karkisa abunda ya faru a zuciyarki, nasan da ciwo ace d'anka yana fad'a maka maganan dayaga dama akan matarsa, ki tuna baya kituna lokacin da yake miki biyayyah lokacin ya yakeji kaman kece rayuwarsa, Dan Allah na rok'eki karki masa baki, nasan yana cikin kaidin macene mu had'u muyita masa addu'a har Allah ya nuna mana lokacin da zamuga k'arshen matarsa.

Umma ta share hawayenta tace " mai unguwa ban tab'a ganin laifin Faisal ba nasan ba acikin hayyacinsa yakemun komaiba, kowa yasan Faisal yarone me biyayya baya k'aunan b'acin raina kwata² insha Allahu zan cigaba da masa addu'a har Allah ya warware mana komai.

Fa'izu ya saka kuka yace tabbas nasan ba halin d'an uwana bane sharrin macene kawai amma nasan Allah bazai bartaba tunda ta tab'a zumunci.

Baffa Tukur yace komai muk'addarine daga ubangiji nasan wannan abun yana d'aya daga cikin jarabawan Faisal ne Allah ya yaye masa, shiyasa tun farko banso wannan auren ba saboda duk binciken danayi ba wanda ya fad'a alkairi ko guda d'aya akansu, amma ba yadda muka iya haka Allah ya tsara abunsa, saidai muce Allah ya kawo mana agaji, mu had'u muyita ma yaronnan addu'a.

Fa'izu ya kira Ogansa ya sheda masa halinda suke ciki, ba k'aramun fad'a ya masaba na rashin sheda masa da beyiba tunda wuri, shidai Fa'izu hakuri yayita basa, Ogansa yace ka basu kud'in da suka nema a wajenka ita kuma ma'aifiyarku kartayi kod'aya daga cikin abunda suka nema, zamuyi waya da alkalin, ya mishi godiya kafin sukayi sallamah.


Yau suka koma kotu, Faisal yacewa Alkali nagama yanke shawara nida matana indai anaso mu janye k'aran nan dole sai sunyi abunda mukace musu, yama alkaki bayanin yadda sukayi.

Alkali shi kansa ya girgiza saiya kalli Fa'izu yace kun amince da abunda suka fad'a ko kuma kuna da ja?

Fa'izu yazo gaban Zainab ya tsuguna yace kiyi hakuri ki yafemun abunda na miki insha Allahu hakan bazai sake faruwa ba, yasa hanu a aljihu ya ciro dubu d'ari biyu ya mik'awa Alkali yace ga nawa kud'in kaman yadda suka buk'ata, amma mai girma mai shari'a Ummana bazata tab'a tsugunawa a gabansu ta basu hakuri ba sannan bazata basu kosisi ba,  inya dage saita bayar shiya biya mata, ai uwarsa ne, ya koma ya zauna, kafin alkali yayi magana Zainab ta tashi tace....

🅿️59\60
Zainab tace lallai baku nemi zaman lafiya ba, dayake ke munguwane,  ki dakeni kizo kinama mutane kukan munafinci, to wlh baki isa kici bilis ba, dolenki ki biyani diyyan marin da kikamun ko kuma na jibgeki na jibgi banza.

Fa'izu zaiyi magana Alkali ya mishi halamun yayi shuru.

Umma da hawaye ya wanke mata fuska tace "Zainab karki manta nina aifi Faisal kobaki darajani a matsayina na uwar mijin kiba ya kamata ki darajani a matsayina na dattijuwa, kituna watarana zaki aihu, zaki aifi d'a namiji kema surukarki zata miki haka, sannan duk wanda ya zagi uwar wani shima za'ama nashi, Zainab kin zageni kin mareni! Sannan yanzu kinamun ikirarin zaki dakeni idan ban tsuguna na baki hakuri ba?


Abunku da jahila, wuf takai hanu ta bige bakin Umma tace idan baki manta ba akan shiga abunda ba ruwanki yasa ranan nayita tsinka miki mari, naga yanzu ma kin shirya k'arb'an hukuncina wawiya sokuwar mata.


Alkali ya tafa mata kafin yace "kin fad'a abunda nakeso naji, yanzu hukunci ya koma kanki"

Ido ta zaro tace wlh ba haka bane alkali ban mata komai ba sune suka had'u sukayita dukana, saita saka kukan kirsa tace dazan bud'e maka jikina da kaga shatan dukan da sukamun.

Alkali yace kima mutane shuru Allah ne ya tona miki asiri da yake Hajara bata da hakkinki shiyasa Allah ya matsi bakinki kika fad'a gaskiya.

Yanzu naji kinci taran kud'in da Fa'izu ya baki a dalilin ya daki mace kuma matar aure, sannan kema zaki biya dubu d'ari biyar na taran cin zarafin da marin dattijuwa irin Hajara, sannan za'a kaiki gidan tsaro na mata harsai kinbiya kud'in taranki kafin nabada belinki.

Faisal yace baza'a rufemun mata ba akan laifin dabata aikataba, koda kuwa ta mari Umman ma ai ita ta jawo maiya kawota gidanta, sannan a jirani yanzu zan kawo kudin belinta, ya tashi ya futa banki ya wuce direct ya ciro kud'in ya dawo ya mik'a musu, yace Fa'izu ka tabbatar zuwa gobe ka dawomun da aikina nafasa baka, sannan ka lissafa kud'in da ka karb'a tun daga fara aikin ka har izuwa yanzu kabani kud'ina, kuma zanje ma'aikatarku na fad'a musu gaskiya, sai kuma gidanka da nake ciki ka biyani duk kud'in dana kashe wa gidan zan futa nabar maka gidanka danni ba matsiyaci bane yanzu, ya kama hanin matarsa suka futa fuuu.

Alkali ya girgiza kai yace Hajara shawaran da zanbaki ki tashi tsaye da addu'a wa d'anki dan yana cikin kaidin mace.

Umma ta masa godiya suka tafi gida, suna zuwa gida Mai unguwa yace kaman nasan hakan zai kasance naje nasa aka canja passport din Faisal akan file akasa na Fa'izu, tun lokacin da ya kwanta ciwo, saidai matsalan yanzu sunan Faisal da yake kan file d'in, saidai shima insha Allahu zaizo mana da sauki, zamma yaron wajena magana zamusan yadda za'ayi ka kwantar da hankalinka Fa'izu.

Fa'izu yayi murmushi yace Baba mai unguwa ba komai koda Faisal yamun asaran aikina bazai dameni ba, idan rabona ne zan nema gaba nasamu.

Umma dai bata da bakin magana saidai tayita binsu da ido, dan yanzu abun ya wuce tunaninta, aranta tace nasan duk abunda yake faruwa dani jarabawa ne domin na rabu da iyayena lafiya, mijina ma munrabu yana samun albarka.

Washe gari Faisal yaje ya shigar da report din Fa'izu a headquater, akan da takaddunsa yake aiki, daga headquarter aka turowa Fa'izu takardan dakatar dashi daga aiki sai bayan an gama bincike, koda sak'on ya taddashi hankalinsa bai wani tashi ba

Bayida damuwar daya wuce yaga Faisal ya dawo tunanin sa, ya koma mawa Umma biyayyah kaman yadda yake mata da, dan yanzu Umma kwata² bata cikin walwala kullun ka kalli idonta zaka ganshi a kumbure halamun tasha kuka.

Zainab ta dubi Faisal tace masoyi kasan me nakeso kamun?

Yace "a'a masoyiya saikin fad'amun masoyiyi"

Ta cigaba da cewa inaso kacewa Fa'izu yau dinna   kakeso ya biyaka kud'in kuma kud'in million gama ne.

Yace to shikenan tashi muje mu sameshi naga ya futa d'azu kuma bazai wuce gidan Umma ba.

Fa'izu yana zaune a d'akin Umma suna hira, Faisal da Zainab suka shigo gidan ba sallama yace ina kuke ku futo kubani hakkina domin nagaji da zama daku, Fa'izu yace mai kakeso na maka yanzu nidai banida kud'i a hanuna yanzu.

Zainab tace saiya siyar da gidan nan ya biyaka kud'inka dan dama gulmane ya dawo da ita kusa damu.

Umma tace idan aka siyar da gidannan kud'in zai isa kud'in da kake binsa?

Zainab take eh to kobai kai bama zansa ya yafe masa soran, sannan kuma ba wani bane zai siya gidan sai mu, koba haka ba masoyi, da sauri yace mata hakane masoyiya.

Fa'izu yace ko gidan da muke ciki kace nabar maka zan baka shi ni naje na nemi haya, tunda Umma ta yarda nima na amince, amma kayi hakuri zuwa gobe Umma zata koma d'akinta na gidan mu, inyaso gobe saimu baka kin gidan nan, yace kunnema wa kanku mafuta suka juya suka futa.

Fa"izu ya tsuguna yana furta innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un Allah ka kawo mana mafuta, Umma tace karka damu Fa'izu komai yana da lokaci insha Allahu komai zai wuce kamar ba'ayi ba.

Yace Umma kullun ana cewa zai wuce amma har yanzu komai dad'uwa yakeyi kullun addu'a mukeyi amma ba canji sai cigaba.

Umma tace kadaina fad'an haka Fa'izu Allah bai manta damuba yana ganinmu kuma zai dubemu.

Zainab ta shirya ta tafi wajen Goggo Larai ta sanar da ita duk abunda yake faruwa, Goggo Larai tayi shewa tace kinmun daidai, gwanda da kuka kad'omun ita nan nasan gorin dazan mata ma kawai zai iya saka mata ciwon zuciya ta mutu muhuta, sannan Zainab ina rok'on alfarman kuna sakamun abinci saboda yanamun wahala.

Zainab tace aiko dan Umma taji haushi zan saka miki karki damu, ko data fad'awa Faisal zai dunga kaiwa Goggo Larai abinci bai damuba.

Matan anguwa da sukaga Umma ta dawo sukayita shugowa suna tanbayanta lafiya ta dawo nan kuma, saidai ta musu k'aryan d'akin da take cikine aka ginasa akan shadda shine yake burmawa shine yaran sukace ta dawo gida.

Tana zaune a tsakar gida taga shugowan Faisal da kulan abinci cikin farin ciki da murna tace sannu da zuwa Faisal yau kaine a gidan mu.

Ko kulata baiyi ba ya wuce d'akin Goggo Larai yayi sallama ta futo tana cewa d'an albarka kaine shigo daga ciki mana ya shiga tayita jansa da hira tun baya sakewa harya sake da ita kullun idan yazo sai suyita hira suna kwashewa da dariya

Duk abunda sukeyi Umma bata tab'a ce musu komai ba, har k'ofan gida Goggo Larai take rakasa, mutanen unguwa tun basu mayar da hankali akan suba, har kowa hankalinsu ya dawo kansu, tuni labari ya karad'a unguwa kowa da abunda yake fad'a.

Safiyyah tana kwance duk abun duniya ya dameta yau sati uku kenan rabon da taga Faisal ya kirata, ta d'auki waya takira sa, takira yakai sau goma baya d'agawa.

Faisal yana zaune yanama Zainab danna kiran Safiyyah ya shigo wayan sa, kurawa wayan ido yayi kaman mai tuno wani abu kawai saiyayi murmushi aranshi yace ina sonki Safiyyah nabari naga ko kema kina sona ne, zanso naji muryanki amma ba halin d'agawa wannan matar bazata barni ba saiya saka wayan a silent ya tura aljihun sa, yana ganin tayi bacci ya koma gefe ya ciro wayan sa yafara rubuta mata sako....


Next page yana zuwa nanda minti kad'an insha Allahu


Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️61\62
Sonki shine abokin rayuwata, da akwai wasu lokuta da nake zubar da hawaye saboda ke, na san za ki ce saboda me, kuma nasan kinsan amsan, da akwai wani lokaci da yake zuwa na ji tamkar na yi fikafikai na tashi sama naganni a gabanki, kin kuwa san duk saboda mene ne? Saboda motsawar sonki a cikin zuciya ta da tsintar kaina a cikin matsanancin shau’ki a dukkan lokacin da na ji muryarki, ko na tina da ke, ina fatan za ki amince mu rayu a tare har abada. Ina son ki! HMM!! Nasan zakice na dameki da yawa, dukkan lokacin da dare ya yi, banida burin daya wuce naji d'iminki a jikina, ki d’aga kanki sama ki kalli sararin samaniya, za ki ga wani tauraro yana saukowa izuwa gare ki, karda ki yi mamakin me ya sa haka ya faru, ki yarda da ni, hakan zai iya faruwa a zahiri domin kuwa ni ma na yi hakan har ta kai ga na same ki, Safiyyah kitai makeni kisoni nasan zansamu farin cikin dana rasa a rayuwata, ki huta lfy.


Ya tura mata tana gama karantawa taga ya kamata yau d'aya ta mayar masa da amsa kozai daina zarginta akan cewa bata sonsa, saida ta kulle k'ofan d'akinta kafin tafara rubuta mishi sak'o dan so take itama yau d'aya ta faranta masa kaman yadda kullun yake rok'a.


Honey nah!!! Murmushin ka shine mafi girman kyauta a gareni ka kasance wani bangare daga cikin jikina, babban farin cikina inganka cikin farin ciki, shine mafi samuwar nutsuwa agareni, lafazin ka shine mafi tasiri a zuciyata kamar yadda kalamanka ke tasiri a cikin zuciyata ka kula min da kanka abar k'auna ta ina sonka Honey ina k'aunar ka K'albina.

Tafi minti biyar tana tunanin tura masa, saida ta rufe idonta kafin ta tura masa, tana turawa ta kashe wayanta dan tasan tabbas saiya kirata da zaran ya gani.

Lokacin da sak'on ya shigo wayan sa bai dubaba dan a zatonsa alert ne na kud'in kamfani, ya kwanta a hanun kuje yafara tunanin daya saba, kullun tunanin sa d'aya meya canjasa haka? Meyasa yanzu baya iya control din kansa? Meyasa yanzu yake tsoron Zainab da yawa haka? Abunda yafi d'aure masa kai shine yarasa me Umma da Fa'izu suka masa yakejin tsanansu a ransa? Yanzu bayida wanda ya tsana sama da Umma da Fa'izu a duniyan nan, ganin bayida me bashi amsa yasa ya kawar da tunanin a zuciyar sa ya fara tunanin abunda yakeso ya cimma a rayuwar sa, yanzu ginina yayi nisa soran kad'an na kammala, ada burina mu zauna da Fa'izu har Umma ma da side dinta amma yanzu tunda summun laifi kona gamama rufe gidan zanyi ba wanda zai zauna aciki, ga Zainab ta dagemun saimun bar gidan nan, yanzu inaga gobe da safe zanje na nema mana haya kawai mu koma.

Sai kuma ya juya tunanin sa zuwaga Safiyyah, saida ya tashi ya lek'a Zainab ya tabbatar tana bacci kafin ya koma ya zauna a falo ya d'auki wayansa yakira layin Safiyyah dan yasan ita kad'e yake kira idan yaji muryanta yake jin dad'i a duniyan nan, yana kira yaji wayan a kashe tsaki yayi ya gyara kwanciyan sa zuciyarsa yana k'una na rashin jin muryanta,  har zuwa Safiyyah da k'uncin ya tashi, ya gama musu abun karyawa ya shiga wanka.


Safiyyah tunda asuba data farka ta bud'e wayanta a tunininta zataga sak'on Faisal sunata shigowa amma shuru,  har zuwa takwas din safiya bataga komai nasaba ta sake rubuta masa sak'o.

Yaya Faisal nazaci idan kayi karo da sak'ona zaka kwana kana kirana saboda kaji abunda kakeso, dan Allah idan gwadani kakeso kayi ka daina saboda kariga daka koyawa zuciyata sonka dan Allah karka gujeni a lokacin da komai nawa na mallaka maka, ina sonka fiye da nunfashina. Kahuta lfy.


Lokacin da sak'on ya shigo wayansa yana wanka Zainab tashinta daga bacci kenan ta d'auki wayansa saboda taga wayan tayi sake tazaci budurwan sane take kiransa ta d'auka ta zageta kawai sai taga sak'o ta bud'e ta fara karantawa ta jima tanabin sak'on wayan tana karantawa sai tayi tsaki, daidai ya futo daga wanka kenan yaji tana tsaki, yace Masoyiya waya tab'amun ke kike tsaki daga tashinki daga bacci?

Tace wlh MTN suna bani haushi da yawan tura sak'onsun nan,  naga wayanka yayi haske shine na duba nazaci kiranka akeyi sai naga ashe MTN ne suka turo sak'o kaima kana gogewa karsu maka yawa a waya mana.

Yace to Masoyiya barin gama shiryawa saina goge, amma yau naga kin tashi da wuri ko zakije unguwa ne?

Tace "eh ai yau ba kwanan ka bane, zan shirya na tafine, ta tashi taje ta kunce towel din jikinsa ta fara shafasa tana cewa gaka namiji har namiji maikyaun sura amma fanko ne ko ankwanta ba abunda zaka iyayi, wani irin wari jikinta yakeyi ta rungume sa ta kawo bakinta me wari zata masa kiss, yace laaaa namanta dankali a kan wuta ina zuwa barin duba mana ya janye jikinsa anata ya futa falo da gudu.

Tayi tsaki tace ko baka guduba nasan ba abunda zaka iyamun kaidai tafi da lagwaninka, yanzu zanje a d'aurani akan network.

Yana zuwa falo yayita mayar da nunfashi kaman wanda yayi gudun famfalaki, zuciyar sa yanata tashi saida ya nemi gauta kafin yafarajin ya dawo daidai, ya shirya tsaf yace mata mosoyiya nizan futa nasan bazan dawo na sameki ba, sai bayan kwana uku kenan ko?
.
Tace banason tambaya akan abunda kasani ni wuce kabani waje, ya futa yana zuwa k'ofan gida yaji kira ta shigo wayansa bai d'agaba har sau uku ana kira harta yanke baya d'agawa, yaje ya shiga motarsa yabar layin yana zuwa wajen aikinsa saida ya gama duba duk abunda ya kamata ya duba, kafin ya ciro wayar sa yabi kiran ringing d'aya ta d'auka, tana dagawa tasaka masa kuka, jin kukar yakeyi kaman ana buga masa guduma a zuciyar sa.

Yace "ya salam meya faru Safiyyah kike kuka haka, koso kike nima kisakani kuka, bakisan kece rayuwata ba farin cikinki shine nawa,  bak'in cikinki na kwana d'aya shine nawa na shekara dan Allah ki daure ki fad'amun abunda yake faruwa  karkisa zuciyata ta fashe.

Wani hawayen bak'in ciki ta goge tace Yaya Faisal ada daka cemu  kana sona nazaci da gaskene, kayi nasaran koyamun sonka, saida na fallasa maka zuciyata kafin ka gujeni kamun adalci kenan?

Kanshine ya d'aure dan be fahinci inda maganan ya dosa ba yace "Safiyyah dan Allah ki nutsu ki fad'amun abunda yake faruwa."

Tace "kana nufin bakaga sak'on dana tura maka bane jiya da daddare da wanda na tura maka da safe?

Yace wlh Safiyyah ban ganiba d'azu Zainab tace mun MTN ne suka turomun sak'o a wayata, jiya kuma harnayi bacci banga wani sms ya shigo wayata ba amma barin duba dakyau kittt ya kashe wayan.

Safiyyah tace hakan yana nufin Zainab ta goge mun duk sak'on dana tura kenan, tabbas ta makara da tabari soyayyata ya yahiga zuciyar mijin ta, barin bari zuwa anjima idan ya kammala abunda yakeyi saina k'ara kiransa danna tabbatar masa inason sa da bakina.

Faisal hanunsa har b'ari yakeyi wajen shiga sms d'in yana shiga kuwa yaga sunan daya saving dashi " Sweet Honey" ya shiga ya fara karanta sak'on, ya karantasu yafi sau goma, baisan lokacin daya sauk'a ya fara sujjadan shukur ba, yace Allah nagode maka daka nunamun wannan ranan da Safiyyah ta furtamun kalman so da bakinta, gani yayi kiran waya bazata wadace shiba, yaje ya shiga motar sa ya d'auki hanyan Azare yanajin shi sakayau kaman sabon jinjiri, yana tafiya na tunanin to me hakan yake nufi Zainab bata iya karatuba kenan, nasan data iya karatu yau dana shiga uku, dan duk sak'on da nake turawa Safiyyah suna wayan bana gogewa.

Safiyyah tayita kiran wayansa amma yak'i shiga ta d'aura hanu akanta tace wayyo Allah na, nikuma kalan tawa jarabawan kenan, na sadaukar da Fa'izu yanzu kuma saida nafara son Faisal shi kuma ya gujeni, Allah ka yayemun son wannan bawan naka, rufe idonta tayi hawaye na zuba a idonta duk abunda take fad'a a filine batasan Inna tana jinta ba.

Inna ta k'arasa shigowa tace "ansaka ihu Mahaukaci ya fad'a a rijiya,  yace ba fad'awa nayi ba ni wanka na nakeyi, wlh tun lokacin da kikace mun Fa'izu yana son Sumayyah zuciyata tak'i amincewa da maganan, na fahinci ke kika canja soyayyan ku da Fa'izu izuwa Sumayyah, to yanzu wa gari ya waya, ance miki ana sadaukar da soyayya zuciya ta zauna lafiya ne, dama kullun ina jiran wannan ranan dazan ganki kina zubar da hawayan dana sani.

Ta tashi da gudu taje ta arungume Inna tana kuka tace Inna kiyi hakuri ki yafemun b'oye miki danayi, Inna kinmanta halinda Safiyyah ta shigane akan rashin aure, nanta kwashe komai ta fad'a mata har yadda sukayi da Umma da Faisal, ta k'ara da cewa " Inna dan Allah kitayani da addu'a Allah ya ciremun son Faisal a zuciya na, ji nake kaman zuciyata zata fashe idan Faisal ya gujeni.

Inna tsayawa tayi tana kallonta da mamaki a idonta tace......
