🅿️63\64
Yanzu kuma son wanda baya sonki kika koma Safiyyah, ina kika bar kunyanmu na fulani? Safiyyah banaso ki kasance kalan macen da takebin namiji tana cewa tana sonsa alhalin shi bai tab'a sonta ba, kiyi hakuri da jarabawan ki Safiyyah karki jefa rayuwarki wajen dayafi k'arfinki, Safiyyah ke kad'ai na aifa y'a mace karkisa nayi kukan rashinki, kibari kema Allah zai dubi sadakarwan da kikayi domin shi, ya baki mijinki nagari wanda zakuso junanku ku zauna lafiya.

Safiyyah tayita juyawa Inna kai idonta yana zubar da hawaye tace Inna bani nace inason saba, hasalima da ban tab'a hango soyayyah tsakanin muba sai shak'uwa, nan ta cire kunya na fulani taba ma'aifiyarta labari tun farkon da yace yana sonta harda jan ran datayi ta masa.

Inna tace "duk da kowa yasan fulani akwaimu da kunya da kawaici, amma ba'ama soyayyah kunya musamman kalan soyayyan zamanin yanzu, gashi kinga abunda kunya ya jawo miki, karki damu idan bezoba da kaina zan kirasa naji ta bakinsa, zan tambayesa har yanzu yana sonkine koya hakura.

Tace to Inna na nagode Allah ya saka da alkairi, shiyasa kullun nake ganin kaman ba wanda ya kaini dace da uwa tagari a duniyan nan, Inna duk lokacin da nake cikin damuwa nake kuka, kina saurin sharemun hawaye na, Allah ya bani ikon faranta miki har k'arshen rayuwata.

Inna tace " idan ban share miki hawaye ba wazai share miki Safiyyah, nice uwarki nice k'awarki dolena na kula da damuwanki, yanzu kije ki kwanta Allah ya miki albarka.


Wayan tane yayi k'ara da kaman bazata jeba Inna tace jekiga wa yake kiranki, da gudu ta kawowa Inna wayan tana dariya Inna tace shine?

Tace shine Inna wlh shine har wayan ta yanke wani ya sake shigowa, Inna tace ba kuka zaki tsaya yiba ki d'aga wayan mana, ta d'auka amma ta kasa magana, ji tayi yace ki futo ina waje sai ya kashe wayan.

Inna tace meyace miki?

"Wai nafuto yana waje" tabawa Inna amsa.

Inna tace d'auko hijabinki saiki wanke fuskanki kije kiji mezaice miki, tasaka hijabi tace Inna zan goge fuskata kawai karyaga jima wata, ta futa Inna tabi bayan y'arta da tausayi tace Allah ya baki sa'a a rayuwarki ya dubi sadakarwan da kikayi ya mallaka miki abinda kikeso, Allah yasa Faisal shine alkairi wa rayuwarki.

Tana futowa yaji wani sanyi a zuciyarsa ya tsinci kansa cikin farin cikin da bai tab'a tsintar kansa ba a soyayyah, 'tunda ta futo ya k'ura mata ido ko k'iftawa babu.

Safiyyah ta futo ta duba ko ina bata ganshi ba cikin sanyi jiki ta koma gida, Inna tace mata yanaga kin dawo?

Cikin sanyin murya tace "baizo ba Inna naduba ko ina bangansa ba, Inna ta tausayawa y'arta, tasan Safiyyah yarinyace mai kunya da kawaici, amma wannan karan harta iya fad'a mata abunda yake ranta, ta tabbata ba k'aramun so takewa Faisal ba.

Faisal yana ganin ta juya yayi saurin tura mata sak'o, " meyasa kika juyamun baya, bayan kinsan ganin fuskanki ma kawai yana sauk'armun da farin ciki, kiyi hakuri kidawo koda bazaki mun magana ba, kitsaya nayita kallonki ya wadace ni, ya tura mata.

Taji shigowan sak'on amma yanayin datake ciki baisa ta bud'e ba, ta shiga d'aki ta rufe kanta kuka tasa maicin zuciya, wayanta yayita ringing bata d'aga ba, tasake jin shigowan sak'o wayarta shima bata duba ba.


Zainab yau tun k'arfe tara ta isa wajen Boka, ta samu mata alayi taje zata shiga, wata mata tace mata haba baiwar Allah ananfa kikazo kika samemu, ai saiki tambayi waye k'arshe ko?

Ta juyo tama matan kallon banza tace idan kika sake magana baza'a sake ganinki anan ba mtss, ta shige ciki wata farar mata tagani a gaban Boka yana kallonta yana sid'e baki, tayi tsaki ta wuce ciki.

Matar ta bita da kallo tana mamakin meya shigar da ita cikin d'akin Boka kuma, kodai shima yana danne mata ne ko kuma matar shine, ta dubi shi tace Boka inaso kadawo da wanda zan aura tafin hanuna sai abunda nace masa, saidai idan bayida jijiya nasan auranmu bazai tab'a yin k'arko ba, danni Harijin miji nake nema.

Boka yace karki damu kije idan bai miki yadda kikeso ba, kizo zanna sammiki jijiyata, ta kashe masa ido d'aya kafin ya sallameta ta tafi, a gurguje ya sallami matan wajen kafin ya rufe k'ofansa ya shiga daga ciki, 
Zainab tace waye matan da naga kana lashe baki kaman tsohon maye?

Boka yace itama inaso na dawo da ita y'ar hanuna ne kaman ke, dan daga gani nasan zata fiki ruwa.

Zainab ta zaro ido waje tace haba Boka duk irin d'aga maka k'afan da nakeyi bai isheka ba, harsai ka nemi wata.

Yace kafinke kinsan yawan matan dana danne? Kowa lokacinsa yakeci kema kiyi amfani da lokacinki kawai nasan nan gaba kad'an bake bane a shinfid'a na.

Zainab tasan idan tacika magana zai iya koranta ya daina mata aikima kwata² yasa ta had'e bakinsu wake  suka fara aikata masha ansu, amma a zuciyarta ta d'au alwashin gyara kanta ta inda boka bazai sake sha'awan ko wace irin mace ba

Faisal yakai awa biyu a tsaye a wajen har Adam yazo zai shiga gida ya futo da sauri yana kiran sunan sa, Adam saida ya k'ura masa ido dakyau kafin ya ganesa, dan ya rame yayi bak'i sosai, ya mik'a masa hanu suka gaisa kafin ya masa iso zuwa d'akinsa, sai yanzu ya yarda da maganan Fa'izu dayake cewa duk abunda Faisal yakeyi ba'a hayyacinsa yake ba, dan duk yanayinsa ya nuna masa, hira Adam yaketa masa dan yanaso ya karanci wani abu daga wajensa, ya shiga cikin gida ya shedawa Inna Faisal yazo, Inna tayi murmushi mai bayyana farin ciki a zuciyarta tace Alhandulillah, Safiyyah cikan burinki yazo, amma bazan fad'a miki ba saidai kugyara kanku da kanku, ruwa da abinci ta bashi yakai masa, kafin ya juya wajen aikinsa dama yazo d'aukan abune a gida.

Faisal ko ruwa ya kasa sha yanata jiran yaga ta ina zata bullo.

Safiyyah ta tashi domin gabatar da sallan azahar zuwa yanzu har wani dusu dusa take gani a idonta tsaban kukan data sha, ta dauki wayanta dan duba waye ya dameta da kira missed call din Faisal ta gani rututu a wayanta ta dafe k'irjinta hanunta yana b'ari ta shiga sak'on daya turo mata tafara karanta na farko kafin taje nabiyu.

"Safiyyah kin lasamun zuma a bakina yanzu kuma kinaso ki lashe kayanki, shiya na rok'eki ki fad'amun gaskiya abunda yake zuciyarki kikak'i fad'amun amma kisani indai baki sake futowa nagankiba, haka zanyita zama a garinnan har mutuwata kuma bazanci komai ba.

Da gudu ta futo tayi d'akin Inna tace " Inna kinga ya turomun wai yana garinnan tuntuni kuma wai yaga futowata d'azu"

Inna tayi murmushi tace karb'a kunun ayan nan kikaiwa Yayanki a d'akinsa, inyaso idan kin dawo saina rakaki dubasa, da gudu tatafi d'akin Adam danso takeyi su futa dubasa, tana shiga d'akin tayi karo da mutum  ta ajiya kunun ayan akasa tace gashi inji Inna ta juya zata futa da gudu.

Faisal ya tashi zai futa yayi sallah kenan yaga shigowan ta, yana ganin zata juya yayi saurin.....

🅿️65\66
Kamo hanuta yace ina zaki tafi kibarni kuma? Safiyyah horan ya isa haka ko sai bayan bana raye zaki fara sona?

A razane ta juyo tana kallonsa muryan ta yana rawa tace Yaya Faisal"

Sai kuka ya kubce mata baisan lokacin daya rugumeta a jikinsa ba, a tare sukaji wani shork din dabasu tab'a jiba, kanshi ya kwantar a wuyanta yana busa mata nunfashin bakinsa, had'uwan nunfashin su ba k'aramun tasiri yayiba waje canja masa yanayi, jiyayi kaman ana hura masa iska ta cikin wandonsa, k'ok'arin janyeta yakeyi daga jikinsa amma tak'i basa dama, gani takeyi kaman inta sakesa zai b'ace a wajen, yanaso yayi magana amma yasan muryansa zai iya fallasa yanayin da yake ciki, a hankali yayi ta raba jikinsu har yayi nasaran rabata da jikinsa gaba d'aya, ya zame ya zauna a wajen kaman wanda aka cire masa laka.

Itama zama tayi a gabansa ganin irin kallon da yake mata yasa tayi saurin sunkuyar da kanta k'asa tana wasa da zoben hanunta wanda Fa'izu ya tab'a bata.


Saida ya tattaro nutsuwan sa kafin ya fara magana kaman yaron da yafara koyan magana ya kira sunanta, ta d'ago idonta suka sake had'a ido da sauri ta sake sunkuyar da kanta, dan ba k'aramun tsoro idonsa ya bata ba, idon yayi jajazur ya k'ank'ance.

A hankali tace Yaya Faisal ka rame baka da lafiyane?

Saida ya sauk'e ajiyan zuciya kafin yace ke kikasa na rane ai"

"Ni kuma"

"Eh ke, da kinsan irin ciwon da kika samun a zuciyata bazaki tambayi na rameba, kullun sai nayi kuka saboda tsananin sonki da nakeji a raina, ina kukan takaicin son wanda bata sona bata damu da halinda zan shigaba"

Cikin shagwab'a tace "wlh ina sonka dakasan halinda nashiga bazakace bana sonka ba" ganin kallon da yake mata yasa tayi saurin rufe fuskanta sai yanzu takejin kunyan maganan da tayi.
 
Ya janye hijabin data rufe fuskanta dashi yace banason wannan kunyan Safiyyah domin yanamun illah, wannan kunyan soran kad'an ya kasheni.

Yace ki fad'amun yaushe kika fara sona?

Taso ta yakice kunyan amma ta kasa, kanta yana k'asa tace "tun ranan dakace kana sona"

Dariyan farin ciki yayi kafin yace "Safiyyah da bakijin kunyata, zuwanki bauchi a kwarya d'aya mukecin abinci, wai meya kawo wannan kunyan ne.

Tace Yaya kaje kayi sallah nima banyi ba, atare suka futa, sai yanzu ya farajin yunwa ana idar da sallah ya dawo yaci abincin kafin yasha kunun ayan data kawo masa, ya kirata yace tacewa Inna zai shigo ya gaisheta.

Bayan ta fad'awa Inna ya shigo suka gaisa, Inna dataga yadda ya dawo bayan futansa saida ta zubar da hawaye.

Suna futa taga ya nufi motar data ganshi d'azu a wajen, tace dama kaikazo da motar nan?

Yace "eh taciki nayita kallonki kinata dube dube, ta rufe idonta da tafun hanunta.

Yace Safiyyah yau zanyi bacci mai dad'i wanda inaga ban tab'ayin irinsa ba, nagode wa Allah nagode miki da kika karb'a tayin soyayyata, ba k'aramun ceton raina kikayi ba, yanzu abu d'aya nakeso kibani.

Tace " kafad'a abunda kakeso ni kuma idan baifi k'arfina ba zan baka"

Murmushi da yake d'ada bayyana kyausa yayi, yace ba wani abu me wuya bane, kuma kina dashi inhar kina sona kibani shi, zoben hanunki nakeso ki bani"

Saida taji gabanta ya bada bugun rass rassa gashi bazata iya ce masa Fa'izu bane ya bata, kawai saita cire zoben a hanunta ta kama hanunshi tasaka mishi zoben.

Tunda ta kama hanunshi yakejin feelings yana taso masa tun daga tafin k'afansa har zuwa tsakiyan kansa, yanajin mamakin idan jikinsu ya had'u waje d'aya feelings yake sauk'a masa, wanda Zainab kozata tub'e tsirara tana shafasa bayajin komai.

Harya tafi yana tamata kalamun soyayyah, sukayi sallama kowa ya tafi da kewar d'an uwansa a zuciyarsa.

Yana komawa Bauchi washe gari ya fara cikiyar gidan haya, ya samu a madina quarters flat din gida maikyau, ya biya kud'in hayan yayi fenti, kafin ya kwashe musu kayansu zuwa gidan katifa kawai ya bari, shima idan Zainab ta dawo saisu d'auka.

Duk abunda yakeyi Fa'izu da ido kawai yake binsa, Sumayyah tace "kaman tashi zasuyi fa"

Yace eh dama Zainab tace bazata zauna a gidan nanba saidai ya kama musu haya "tace Allah ya sauwak'e"

Zainab ranan da zata dawo bayan sun gama abunda zasuyi ta shirya zata tafi tace "Boka har yanzu Faisal bayajin tsorona yadda ya kamata dan sainace yaci mutuncin su kafin yakeyi, amma konace yaje ya samu uwarsa ya fad'a mata maganan dayaga dama baya iyawa.

Boka ya dubu kwaryan tsafinsa yace kece bakiyi abunda nace miki daidai ba, saida nace ki shafa maganin nan kafin ki shiga gida, amma baki shafaba harsai daya dawo.

Tace nashiga uku yanzu boka yazanyi kenan kayi hakuri ka gyaramun.

Yace kije zan duba idan akwai yadda za'ayi idan kin dawo saina gyara miki, ta tafi gida zuciyar ta ba dad'i.

Tana zuwa ta samu d'akinta ba komai sai katifa ta fara kwalawa Faisal kira, da gudu ya futo yana cemata sannu dawowa  masoyiya"

Tace.......

Saida ta yamutsa fuska kafin tace yawwa, inaka kai mun kayana?

Yace "dama na sama mana gidan hayane shine nafara kai kayan kafin ki dawo, dan banason abunda zai wahalar mun dake"


Tsaki tayi kafin tace ka taimaki kanka, yanzu wuce muje naga gidan idan bemun ba kuma wlh zakaga abunda zan maka, jiki na b'ari ya d'auko katifan daya rage ya d'aura a saman mota suka tafi.

Tun daga k'ofan gidan taga tsarin wajen ya mata yadda takeso, dama a gajiye take dan intaje wajen boka kaman inji suke dawowa, gashi basu bacci ko kad'an, ya shinfid'a mata katifa tahau ta kwanta bata jima da kwanciya ba bacci ya d'auketa, ba batun sallah dan dama sallah baya daga cikin tsarinta, zama yayi ya k'ura mata ido yana mamakin yadda akayi ya aureta da wannan fuska mai kama dana mutanen farko.

Daya tuna da Safiyyah saiyayi murmushi afili yace Allah ka nunamun ranan dazan mallaketa a matsayin matana, tunawa yayi da movement dinsu saida tsikan jikinsa ya tashi yace komai naki dabam ne, kinfi kowace mace, d'umin jikinki da laushin jikinki kawai naji amma ya canja mun yanayi na, inaga wani abu ya shiga tsakanin mu, nasan sainaji dad'in da ake cewa anaji a rayuwar aure, Ummana ta tab'a..sai kuma yayi shuru saboda tunawa da Umma da yayi sai yaji ransa ya b'aci, yace na kasa tuna laifin da Umma da Fa'izu suka mun, amma nasan sunmun laifi mai girma wanda bazan iya yafe musu ba, hakura yayi da tunanin komai ya kwanta, amma bacci yak'i idon sa, tashi yayi ya koma falo number Safiyyah ya kira ringing d'aya ta d'auka tace "Yayanah inason naji muryanka kozan samu nayi bacci mai dad'i amma ina tsoron kiranka.

Ajiyan zuciya ya sauk'e kafin yace " tsoron mai kikeji?

Tace "tsoron Aunty na, kuma kasan kaman cin fuskane na kiraka kana gida a d'akin matan ka"

Kawar da maganan yayi da cewa inaso kiyita bani labarin soyayyar mu har nayi bacci, dan nasan idan na kwanta da muryanki a kunnena zanyi bacci mai dad'i wanda ban tab'a yiba"

Tace yau kai nakeso ka koyar dani kalamai masu dad'i, nikuma saina maka bitan abunda na koya daga gareka gobe"

Yayi dariya yace wato ke mai wayo ko? safiyyah soyayya ta gare ki gaskiya ce, kuma ta ginu ne daga ranar da na fara ganinki, ina k'aunarki fiye da yadda zan fada miki, ina son yadda kike so na, karki yasar da ni,  dan Allah karki juya min baya, sonki tamkar nunfashi na yake in kikaga na daina sonki to tabbas bana raye ne, surely i realy love you, and i want be your Husband forever, be with me please.

Kalaman soyayyah masu karyar da zuciya yayita mata batasan lokacin da bacci ya d'auketa ba, jin tayi shuru sai sauk'an nunfashi da yake tashi a kunnen sa, yagane bacci tayi ta barshi, shima kashe wayan yayi ya kwanta da d'inbin kewarta

Washe gari   Fa'i'zu ya tashi yaga ba motan Faisal a gidan ya lek'a d'akin yaga ba kowa a saiya girgiza kai hawaye na zuba a idonsa  yace ya ubangiji ka dubemu ka gyara mana abunda ya b'aci a zumuntar mu da d'an uwana, ka dawo mana da hankalinsa da tunaninsa kammu.

Bayan wata uku Sumayyah zaman kad'eci ya isheta gashi Mama tasa dole Zahra ta koma, ta dauki wayarta ta kira Safiyyah bayan sun gaisa tace "JININA har yanzu banji kina cewa zakiyi aure ba, har abun ya fara damuna, kuma nasan kinada manema birjik, me yake faruwa ne JININA!

Safiyyah tayi murmushi tace "karki damu Sumayyah aure lokacine idan lokacina yayi zanyi, wlh duk garin Azare banga wanda yamun ba, na k'are miki zance ko saurayi bani dashi yanzu, dan bani da meso na" ta k'arasa maganan cikin dariya.


Sumayyah tace wannan maganan ba abun dariya bane, wlh rashin auranki ya fara damuna, amma insha Allahu kin kusanyi dan amintan mu zaifi d'aurewa idan muka auri miji d'aya,  ki shirya karb'an Fa'izu a matsayin angonki.

Dariya ma abun yabata tayita kyalkyakewa da dariya kuwa, tace sai yanzu nasan kwakwalwanki baya aiki yadda ya kamata, haba Sumayyah kozan mutu banyi  Aure ba wlh bazan tab'a auran mijin kiba, karma ki wahalal da kanki wajen had'a abunda bazai tab'a yiyuwa ba, kisha kuruminki nima soran kad'an nayi aure.

Tace "Safiyyah karki zaci har yanzu bansan abunda kikayi bane, ranan Umma taban labarin duk yadda kukayi lokacin da mukazo bikin Yaya Faisal, wasan ya isa haka ya kamata kiba zuciyarki abunda takeso, nasan kina son Fa'izu koda Umma bata fad'a munba najima da fahintar aurena da Fa'izu shirinki ne, wlh zanfi kowa farin ciki idan muka kasance a inuwan miji d'aya kitaimake n....sauri dakatar da ita Safiyyah tayi da cewa surutun ya isa haka mana, kina labarin abunda ya wuce ne, nasan da naso Fa'izu sosai, sai yanzu dana gamu da wanda nakeji idan bashi bazan iya rayuwa ba, na fahinci ashe da shakuwace tsakanina da mijinki ba soyayyah ba, na rok'eki karki sakemun irin wannan maganan kitt ta kashe wayarta, tanajin haushin maganan da Sumayyah ta fad'a mata.

Sumayyah tayi murmushi tace zaki gama d'an waken zagayan ki kidawo ma, domin indai ina raye saikin auri Fa'izu, shima na fahinci har yanzu yana sonki, sotari idan muka zauna muna hira kashi casa'in da tara 99℅ maganar Safiyyah ne.


Da daddare suna kwance ta dubi Fa'izu tace "K'albi"

Ya kama hanunta yana matsawa kafin yace "na'am"

Ta cigaba da cewa dan Allah zan nemi wani alfarma kar kace mun 'a'a, kuma nasan zaka iya mun duk abunda na rok'a"

Yace ki fad'a maganan ki kai tsaye komai girman sa indai baifi k'arfina ba kuma bai sab'awa addinin musulunci ba zan miki!

Tace baifi k'arfin kaba kuma addinin muma yanasan hakan ta gyara kwanciyan ta a jikinsa tace "inaso ka k'ara aurene K'albi"

Abun ma dariya ya bashi yace wato gwadani kikeso kiyi kiji zan iya miki kishiya ko bazan iyaba, to bari kiji harna mutu bana sha'awan na k'ara aure saboda banaso kan y'ayana ya rabu.

Tace wlh da gaske nakeyi, kuma ba wata nakeso ka aura ba illa Safiyyah tawa"

Da mamaki yake kallonta a zuciyarsa yace wato yaran nan so suke su dawo dani ball Safiyyah ta bugani kan Sumayyah ita kuma yanzu tanaso ta bugani ragan Safiyyah, da kinsan yadda nakeson Safiyyah baza kice na aureta ba.

Tace K'albi bakaji abunda nace bane!

Yace najiki maganan ki bayida amsa ne saboda kema kinsan kin fad'a abunda bazai tab'a yiyuwa ba"

Tace maizai hanasa yiyuwa? Ta had'a hanunta waje d'aya tace dan Allah k'albi ka taimakeni ka amince wlh har raina inason zama inuwan aure d'aya da Safiyyah, kuma nasan bazaka tab'a dana sanin kasancewa tare damu ba, kamun alk'awarin yarda da duk abunda na rok'eka, saita saka masa kuka mai tsuma zuciya.

Ya rungumeta a jikinsa yace kiyi shuru, zanyi tunanin yuyuwar hakan, idan na amince kina ganin Safiyyah zata amince ne?

Tayi dariya tace banida matsala da ita nasan hanyan da zanbi ta amince mun, nidai amincewar ka kawai nake nema.

Yace zanyi tunanin yuyur hakan kibani lokaci zan sanar dake hukuncin dana yanke.

Godiya tayita mishi kafin ta d'aura masa da hot romance.

Yau Zainab tana gama abinci ta d'auki na Goggo Larai tace Faisal zoka kaini yau da kaina nakeso nakaiwa Goggo Larai abincin ta, a tsakar gida suka tadda Umma ba wanda ya kulata a cikin su, tayi murmushi ta kawar da kanta dan zuwa yanzu abun ya daina damunta, suka shige d'akin Goggo suka sameta suna zaune ita da Hafsa, Hafsat tayi kicin kicin dare, Faisal ya Gaishe da Goggo Larai ya tafi, Zainab tace meya farune k'anwata naganki ba walwala?

Tace wlh wannan d'an iskan mijina ne ya dage da maganan aure, so tara yana neman Aure muna zuwa wajen boka ana fasawa to wannan karan har ankai kaya ansaka rana a b'oye ban saniba sai yau yake cemun wai auransa saura sati biyu, gashi banida kudin zuwa wajen boka.

Zainab tace indai wannan ne karki damu share hawayen ki, yanzu taso muje wajen bokana dan ya iya aiki kaman yankan wuk'a cikin farin ciki ta tashi suka tafi, Goggo Larai tace kunga yarinyan kirki da bakizo ba bansan ya zanyi da Hafsa ba, idan kinje ki sake karb'an maganin da zaki mallaki Faisal dan muma mu cigaba da moransa, burina naga ciwon zuciya ya bugar da uwarsa ta mutu kowa ya huta.

Ibrahim da shigo wansa kenan ya tari maganan a sama yace maiya ragema yanzu nasan ko yau akaje asibiti likita zai tabbatar mana da tana d'auke da ciwon zuciya, sukasa dariya dukamsu kafin suka futa Ibrahim shima ya bisu yana cewa ya kamata wannan tafiyan ayishi dani.


Boka yana ganin Hafsa yace ta masa indai zata biya masa buk'atansa, shima zai biya mata bukatan, atake tace ta amince su shiga ya dubi Zainab datayi kicin kicin dare yace kiyi hakuri itafa nad'an awanni ne keda muke morewar mu kullun.

Zainab tana zaune a wajen tanajin yadda boka yaketa ihun dad'i sai datayi dana sanin kawo Hafsan sunkai awa uku suna abun d'aya kafin suka futo yanata dariya, bayan sun zauna yace amma zakina kawo mun ziyara lokaci zuwa lokaci ko?

Tace sosaima dan nayaba da jarumtar ka, kullin magunguna ya bata ya fad'a mata yadda zatayi dashi, tace Boka nawa zan baka? Yayi dariya irin tasu na bokaye kafin yace aikin gama biyana da dad'inki, kuma duk lokacin da wani damuwar ya taso miki kizo zan miki maganin sa ni kuma kibani kayan dad'i.

Ya dubi Zainab yace karki damu kema ai gobe kene dani harna kwana uku kizo muji dad'inmu, suka futa Hafsa tanata mata godiya, koda ta koma gidanta tayi yadda boka yace mata tayi, Jibrin mijinta yana shigowa ta d'aure fuskanta tamau tace Jibrin zonan!

Jiki na b'ari yazo  ya tsuguna yace....

🅿️69\70
Yace "gani ranki shi dad'e"

Da isa da gadara take masa magana tamkar uwarsa,cikin bada umurni tace "inaso kaje ka karb'o duk abunda ka kai gidansu watsatstsiyan yarinyan da kake cewa zaka aure yanzu nan"

Yace angama ranki shi dad'e duk abunda kikeso haka za'ayi, ya tashi ya futa yana shu'umin murmushi, motar shi ya shiga ya zauna, yace ya akayi nabari na kamu da cutar k'anjamo ne! Waye ya sakamun acikin y'an matan da nake nema? Gashi yanzu shegen text dinnan da akeyi kafin aure yasa anfasa aurena, Hafsa duk ke kika cuceni kika sani bin matan banza, nasha fad'a miki ke kad'ai baki isana amma kika k'iji, da zaran kinji zanyi aure saikin san inda kikabi aka fasa auran, yanzu wa gari ya waya! Muna zaman mu kin jawo mana ciwon da yake karya garkuwan jiki, dama tunda sukaje sukayi text akace yana da HIV iyayen yarinyan dazai aura suka bashi sadaki da kayan aurensa, yace wake zan bawa kayan Hafsa, inaso ki zata bokanki yayi nasarane a kaina, zan baki daman da zakiyita juyani kafin labari yazo kunnenki.

Kwasan akwatinan yayi yashiga dasu cikin gida, tana kwance akan kujera taga shigowan sa ta sake d'aure fuskanta tamau a zuciyarta tana cewa Aunty Zainab kin taimaki rayuwata da kika had'ani da Bokanki da yanzu duk uban kayan nan watace zatayi amfani dashi.

Cikin tsawa tace kallon me katsayayi ka tattara kayan ka kaisu ciki, shidai sai murmushi yakeyi duk abunda tace yayi da sauri yakeyi, azaton ta aikin Bokanta ne.

Zainab ba k'aramun gyara tayiba dan bataso Boka yana sha'awan kowace mace sai ita kad'e, batasan ya raba musu kwanane ita da Hafsa ba yanzu, saidai Hafsa bata kwana takanzo ta wunine ta tafi.

Faisal yanzu ya mayar da hankalinsa kan gininsa dan soyake yaga ya mallaki Safiyyah, kuma ita kad'e yakeso ya saka a gidan.

Yaje ya samu Baffa Tukur yace masa "Baffa nazone kaje ka nema mun aure"

Baffa Tukur ba k'aramun dad'i yajiba da Faisal yace zai k'ara aure, yace gaskiya nayi farin ciki da maganan da kazomun dashi, yaushe kakeso naje?

Yace zan sanar dasu zuwanka inyaso wani sati saikaje.

Yace to masha Allah awani unguwa take yarinyan.

Ya mik'e kafin yace ba'a garin nan takeba, yana gama fad'an haka yayi fucewar sa, da ido Baffa Tukur ya bishi yace "ikon Allah gaskiyan shed'anne da yake cewa shi kansa yana tsoron kaidin mace, ga matar Faisal tanaso ta kaisa wuta da k'afansa, Allah ka kub'utar dashi.

Gidan Umma yaje ya mata albishir din cewa Faisal zai k'ara aure, ba k'aramun farin ciki tayi ba, tun batasan wanda zai auraba taji tanason yarinyan, addu'an samun nasara ta masa.

Fa'izu ma dayaji ba k'aramun farin ciki yayi ba, suna zaune shida Sumayyah yace Sumy kinji Yaya Faisal zai k'ara aure ko?

Tace Alhamdulillah fad'uwa yazo daidai da zama, sai a had'a da naka kawai, dan d'azu muka gama waya da Safiyyah tace mun ta amince.

Baisan lokacin da murmushi ya sub'uce masa a fuskan saba, yace kina ganin zan iya muku adalci?

Tace ban damu daka mana adalci ba, nidai burina kawai ka aureta, koda kuwa zan dawo bora a gidan nan, hankalina zaifi kwanciya idan ina ganinta kusa dani,  dukda tayi maganan da zuciya d'aya ne amma taji kishin murnansa daya nuna lokaci guda.

Yace na amince idan akaje tambayan auran Yaya Faisal sai aje nawa daga baya.

Faisal yaje gidan Balkisu yace mata inaso kimun lissafin kudin dazan kashe a kayan akwati, Balkisu saida tayi sujjada tsaban farin ciki tace Allah nagode ma daka kusan nunamun k'arshen wannan tsinanniyar matar.

Ido ya kura mata yace waye tsinanniyan?

Kanta tsaye tace "Zainab mana"

Kafin ta rufe baki taji sauk'an mari yace kinsan waye Zainab kuwa a wajena? To bari kiji itace farin cikina, ke tafi Ummana ma soyuwa a zuciyata dan zan iya rabuwa da kowa nawa akanta, yana gama fad'ar haka ya fuce fuuuuu ranshi b'ace.

Balkisu data kasa motsi tsaban mamaki ta d'auki wayanta takira Umma ta fad'a mata duk abunda ya faru.

Umma tace kayya Balkisu dabaki zagi Zainab a gabansa ba, yanzu yi sauri kibisa kibashi hakuri kice ba Zainab matar shi kike nufiba watace dabam, inba hakaba wa ita zaikai kud'in ta had'a kinsan kuma baza tayi ba.

Da sauri ta kashe wayan ta d'auki hijabinta ta futa, a motarsa ta samesa ya kifa kansa akan sterring motan yanajin k'unan abunda Balkisu ta masa, jin an bud'e motarne yasa ya d'ago idonsa da sukayi jajazir kaman jan gauta ya zuba mata tare da cewa futamun a mota.

Tace Yaya "Faisal kamun mungun fahinta ne dakasan Yadda nakeson Aunty Zee bazakace dan aibata taba, akwai wata yarinyan mak'otan mu sunanta Zainab yarinyan batada kamun kai gabin maza da malame shine tacemun kota halinyaya saita sameka, dalilin dayasa nace Allah zai nunamun k'arshenta kenan" ta k'arasa maganan tare da kafesa da ido aikuwa taga karyanta ya shigesa.

Yace to ai laifin kine da bakimun bayani ba, har kikasa na mareki kiyafen kanwata"

Tace baka bani daman yi maka bayani bane shiyasa, amma tunda yanzu ka fahince ni naji dad'i, ciki suka koma ya bata kud'in dazata had'a kayan auren amma yace karta kuskura tacewa Umma zaiyi aure.

Tace ai bazan tab'ayin abunda bakaso ba Yayana" sukayi sallama ya tafi, saida ta tabbatar yabar layin kafin ta kira Mijinta ta rok'esa akan zataje duba Umma batajin dad'i, tasan intace Goggo ne bazai barta ba, yace taje amma kartayi dare.

Tana shiga gidan ta shige d'akin Umma bayan sun gaisa tace Umma ga kud'in siyayyan da Yaya Faisal ya bani, ki siya duk kayan da kikaga ya dace, dan nima bansan mai da mai ake siya ba.

Umma tace Balkisu nasan kina tunanin kaman zan zargekine akan kudin, wlh jinki nake tamkar y'ar dana aifa a cikina, kema nasan haka kika d'aukeni kinamun biyayyah tamkar ma'aifiyarki, dan haka nabaki wuk'a da nama kije kiyi duk abunda ya dace.

Balkisu idonta ya ciko da kwalla tace inama ke kika aifeni Umma, da bamemun gori a duniya, Goggo ta zubar mana da mutumci da kima a idon duniya, kowa yasan da hanunta a rabaki da Yaya Faisal amma insha Allahu zasuga saka mako.
    
Umma tace karna sakejin irin wannan maganan a bakinki, koya Larai take ma'aifiyarki ce, shiriya na Allah ne idan tana da rabon rahman Allah rana d'aya zata daina duk abunda takeyi, kuma banaso kisa a ranki asiri akama Faisal, danni ban yarda da wannan ba jarabawa Allah ya aikomun, inata addu'an Allah ya bani ikonci.

Balkisu tanajin dad'in yadda Umma take da tawakkali, shiyasa yanzu tafi mannewa da ita dan rayuwarta yafi kyau, tace Umma cefane mutum d'aya bazaiyi kyauba, ya kamata na d'auki Sumayyah na tafi da ita.

"Umma tace duk yadda kukayi daidaine Allah ya muku albarka ya hanaku lalacewa"

Goggo Larai tun d'azu takejin murya na tashi kaman na Balkisu, ta futo tsakar gida dan ta tabbatar, sai taga ta futo daga d'akin Umma tace "kan abun kazan bayan uba Balkisu mai kikeyi a d'akin mak'iyata, ina uwarki ki shigo gidan ban saniba, to wlh Hajara kinyi kad'an daga ke har bokanki, kunyi kad'an ku mallake mun y'a muzuba nidake naga wanda zaiyi nasara.

Balkisu ta tsuguna tace sannu Goggo nasa meku lafiya?

Tace rufamun baki munafukan banza
sai yanzu kikasan ina gidan, na fuskanci da dahalin canja uwa dakin canjani, ganin Goggo zata tara mata mutane yasa ta fuce daga gidan tabarta tanata zage², Umma ma d'aki ta koma suka barta ita kad'e a tsakar gida tanata haushi.

Washe gari tayi sammako taje ta d'auki Sumayyah suka shiga kasuwa, Sumayyah tun tana tayata harta gaji tace Balkisu wai kayan mutane nawa muke had'awa ne?

Balkisu tayi dariya tace "da gangan nasa Yaya Faisal yabani kud'i da yawa dan kawai na k'untatawa Zainab sonake mu had'a kayan da kafin asamu wanda akamata irinsa sai an wahala, Sumayyah tace ainima zan tayaki kuma saina nayi yadda Zainab tagani, sunkai sati kullun sai sun shiga kasuwa.

Faisal yana kwance a d'akinsa da daddare shi kad'e Zainab ta tafi wajen Boka, ya d'aga waya ya kira Safiyyah tana d'awa cikin shagwab'a tace honey nayi fushi sai yanzu ka tuna dani"

Kunneshi ya kama kaman yana gabanta yace.....


Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️71\72
Yace afuwan Sweet honey ni kaina nama kaina laifi, na azabtar dashi danayi narashin jin muryanki, tun d'azu nakejin kaina kaman mara lafiya, narasa wani irin magani zansha naji dad'i, ashe kece maganin nawa, yanzu najini garau nasamu lafiya sai kasalan da muryanki ya sauk'armun.

Ajiyan zuciya ta sauk'e tace Honey nakan rasa me yake damuna idan naji muryanka, jikina yakanyi sanyi tsikar jikina ya tashi, sainaji kaman anamun tafiyan tsutsa a cikin jikina narasa wani irin yanayi nake shiga, ina sonka Honey, Tsuntsaye na bu’katar fukafukai domin tashi, kifi na bu’katar ruwa domin ya yi rayuwa, ni kuma ina buk'atar ka domin na yi rayuwar farin-ciki, burina mu kasance a tare, ina son jinka a kusa da ni, ta yadda numfashi na da naka zasu gauraya, ta inda zan ke juyo sautin bugun zuciyoyinmu na fita a tare! Mu kasance a tare har abada! Ina son ka! Ina fatan mafarkina ya zama gaskiya, a kullum na kwanta bacci na kan yi mafarkin ka zama uban ‘ya’ya na, ni kuma na zama mata a gare ka. Sai dai na san iya mafarki ba zai wadatar da ni ba a bisa kulawar da nake tinanin samu daga wajenka! Saboda haka nake fatan mafarki na ya zama gaskiya watarana naganni a kwace a k'irjinka.

Wani irin sanyi yaji a zuciyar sa wanda shikansa bazai iya musulta yanayin da yake cikiba, ya lumshe ido kalamanta masu dad'i suna masa yawo a kwalwalwa yace "Sweet honey furanni na furta kalmomin yabo a kanki a dukkan lokacin da na shiga lambu mai d’auke da ‘korama gami da korayen ciyayi da bishiyoyi, masu d’auke da tsintsaye! Bakina yakan rera baitukan yabo a gare ki, cikin harshen da zuciya ce kawai ke iya fahimtarsa! " ina son ki" bazan iya rayuwa idan ba tare dake ba. A mafarkina da kuma a cikin soyayya babu abun da ba zai iya faruwa ba. Ina son ki sosai hakan ya sa nake ji tamkar ba zan iya rayuwa idan ba tare da ke ba bana fatan rasa ki a rayuwa ta, domin kuwa a dukkan lokacin da na rasa ki, na tabbatar da rayuwa ta za ta shiga garari, ina son ki kuma ba zan ta6a canjawa a kan hakan ba, Sweet Honey dan Allah kibani izinin azo nema mun auranki a gidan ku, zuwa yanzu nasan gangan jikina bazai rik'e niba idan muka had'u, kuma banaso jikina da hannayena su aikata abunda Allah ya haramta, karkice sha'awanki yafi yawa a gareni, sonkine yajawo wannan yanayin da nake ciki.

Tsiyaya taji tanayi ta k'asanta kaman mai futsari tace nabaka izini Honey, dan nima nasan nakai stage din da rik'e kaina zaimun wahala, a halin yanzu zanso mu kasance inuwa d'aya ta yadda zamuna raya sunnan Annabi ( s.a.w) batare da tsoron zamu tarawa kammu zunubi ba.

Dad'ine ya maimaye zuciyarsa yace ki gafarce ni, zan kashe wayana yanzu domin na sanar da wanda zaizo nemamun auranki gobe.

Tace "haba honey hanzarin me kakeyi haka, ka duba lokaci kusan sha biyun dare, kabari sai dasafe sai kafad'a musu"

"Da kinsan yadda nakeji a zuciyata bazaki ce nabari ba, gaskiya safiya yamun nisa bazan iya hakuri ba, ki kwanta dani a zuciyarki dan kisamu daman yin mafarkina, mukwana lafiya ina son ki" ya kashe wayan da sauri dan gudun kar tasake ce mishi ya bari saida safe.

Wayan Baffa Tukur ya kira,  yana d'agawa yace kayi hakuri Baffa, na tasheka kana bacci, dama inaso gobe idan Allah ya kaimu zakaje nemamun aure ne.

Baffa Tukur yayi murmushi kaman yana kallonsa yace ba komai Faisal Allah ya kaimu safiyan, saika shigo, ya masa sallama.

Da safe yazo ya samu Baffa Tukur ya bashi dubu d'ari uku yace ka kai musu wannan kud'in sadaki ne da duk wani abunda sukeyi na al'adansu,  idan sunce ya kasa saimu k'ara, kasan fulanine ita akwai kud'in al'adansu da ake biya, wannan kuma dubu ishirin ne kayi kud'in mota, ya tashi yana cewa saika dawo Allah ya kiyaye hanya.

Abun dariya yaba Baffa Tukur yace "kabani kud'i kana cemun Allah ya kiyaye hanya kuma baka fad'amun inda zankai kud'in ba"

Ya sosa k'eya yace "ai nazaci kasani ne Baffa, a Azare ne gidan da muka zauna da mukaje auran Fa'izu"


Baffa Tukur yace to to masha Allah gaskiya nayi farin cikin samun gidan tarbiya dakayi Allah yasa matar arzikinka ne, insha Allahu yanzu zanyi wanka natafi, ya mishi godiya ya futa.

Safiyyah tunda ta tashi takeso ta fad'awa Inna yau Faisal zai turo neman aurenta, amma ta kasa ta rasa ta inda zata fad'a mata, Inna duk tana lura da yanayin ta yau, takirara tazo ta zauna kusa da ita.

Tace Safiyyah bakida wanda zaki fad'awa damuwar ki daya wuceni a duniyan nan, na fuskanci tun tashinki kaman akwai abunda yake damunki, ki daure ki fad'amun ni ma'aifiyar kice kuma abokiyar shawaranki.

Safiyyah ta sunkuyar da kanta ji takeyi kaman ta tsala ihu tsaban kunya, ta daure tace Inna dama__dama..Inna tace dama me? Ta kafeta da ido don ta matsu taji me yake damun gudaliyan y'ar tata.

Tace dama munyi waya da Yaya Faisal ne yake cemun wai yau iyayan sa zasuzo neman...ganin inuwar mutum a bayan Inna yasa ta kwasa da gudu tashiga d'aki ta rufe kanta dan bazata iya k'arasawa ba.

Inna ta d'aga hanu sama  Allah nagode maka daka nunamun cikan burin y'ata Allah kasanya albarka a rayuwarsu, Bappa yana gefe yana kallonsu yace nima nagodewa Allah daya nunamun farin ciki a fuskan y'ata, Inna tace "wato lab'e kake mana nida y'ata ko?

Yayi dariya yace aini tagani ta kwasa da gudu takasa k'arasa miki maganan, yanzu bazanje nafad'awa y'an uwana daganan sai nayi cefane ki d'aura abincin taran bak'inmu.

K'arfe goma daidai Baffa Tukur da Mai Unguwa suka iso garin Azare, basusha wahalan gane gidan ba, suka musu sallama Bappa ya mishi iso, yaji dad'in irin tarb'a na mutumci da Bappa da y'an uwansa suka musu, nan ya gabatar musu da abunda ya kawo su, yace yanaso su yanke musu sadaki su biya yanzu.

Yayan Bappa yace tsakanin mu daku Allah ne ya had'a dan haka kubamu abunda kukazo dashi kawai, dan hidiman suna da yawa bahaushe bazai iyaba"

Baffa Tukur yace to madallah mungode da wannan k'aramcin da kuka nuna mana, mai Unguwa ya ciro kud'in yace kuyi hakuri nasan wannan kud'in bazai kai kud'in da kukeso ba, dubu d'ari ukune kawai ya mik'a musu.

Bappa yace aiko Baffulatani d'an uwanta zata aura kud'in bazaifi haka ba, mungode Allah yasa alkairi zamu had'a.

Yayan Bappa yace kaman zuwa yaushe kukeson auran?

Baffa Tukur yace "to yanzu dai bansan yanayin shirin yaron ba, saidai kuyi hakuri bazanyi waya da gida yanzu naji,  ya tashi a wajen dan bayaso susan da Faisal zaiyi waya suce  basu da dattaku.

Number Faisal ya kira yana d'agawa wayan cikin zumud'i Faisal yace Baffa sun karb'a?

"Eh sun karb'a amma sunce yaushe mukeson auran" ya bashi amsa.

Faisal yace nikam Baffa ko yanzu sukace a shirye nake, yace to madalla duk yadda mukayi zan sanar dakai, ya kashe wayan ya koma wajensu, yace musu nayi magana da gida sunce mun yaron ya gama komai, duk lokacin da kuka sa mana muna shirye.

Suka juya harshe zuwa fulatanci kafin sukace ba damuwa muma a shirye muke zuwa nanda sati hud'u ya muku?

Baffa Tukur yace masha Allah yayi sosaima Allah ya nuna mana lokaci, suka d'an tab'a hira kafin suka musu sallama suka tafi, suna isowa gari ya sanarda Faisal yadda akayi, Faisal yayita masa godiya dukda baiso bikin yakai haka ba yaso ace wani sati an d'aura musu aure.


Safiyyah tanajin Bappa yana ma Inna bayanin komai, saida tayi kuka tsaban farin ciki ta d'auki wayanta takira Sumayyah dan ta sanar da ita.

Sumayyah na d'agawa tace y'ar halak maganan ki mukeyi da Fa'izu yanzu kika kira, inaso nabaki labari Yaya Faisal d'inki zaiyi aure.

Safiyyah tace "haba yaushe kuma wazai aura?

Sumayyah tace ban saniba dai amma yau aka kai kud'in auransa, ranan mukaje kasuwa muka had'o kayan auren sa, wlh JININA saida na tsorata danaga yawan kayan, sai kace wanda besan zafin kud'insa ba.

Safiyyah tayi dariya tace maybe yanasan yarinyan da yawane, Allah yasa matar arzikin sane.

Tace amin, yanzu muke maganan naki muda Fa'izu inaga wani sati zasuzo neman auranki, Safiyyah tace dad'ina dake rashin hankali, nifa nakiraki ne na fad'a miki ankawo sadakina kidaina mun maganan mijinki.

Sumayyah tace wani irin sadaki yaushe kukayi neman ban saniba, dan Allah muyi maganan gaskiya.

Safiyyah tace dama muna irin wannan wasan dake ne? Banaso kiji labarin agarine shiyasa na fad'a miki, ankawo sadakina yau kuma a Bauchi zanyi aure maybe ma mu zauna gida d'aya, sannan kuma Yaya Faisal d'ina zan aura, kitt takashe wayanta.

Sumayyah wayan tayita bi da kallo tana maimaita Yaya Faisal zata aura wani Yaya Faisal din.....

🅿️73\74
Faisal ya dubeta yace "maita ce miki"

Kasa magana tayi saida ya maimaita mata tambayan itama tambayan ta wurgo masa "wa Yaya Faisal zai aura?

Yace kema da wani irin tambaya kike taya zansan wanda zai aura,  kinfi kowa sanin ko magana baya mun,  hasalima ya tsani ya hango koda inuwata amma zaki tambayeni wazai aura! take annurin da yake kan fuskan sa ya d'auke.

Tace "kayi hakuri bamma wannan tambayan da ganganba kodan na b'ata maka rai, kawai Safiyyah ce ta fad'amun abunda ya k'ullemun kai, amma ka yafemun, sannan ka taimakeni kakira Yaya Adam ka tambaya mana da gaske wai ankawo sadakin Safiyyah kuma wazata aura?

Baisan lokacin daya tashi daga kwancen da yakeba ya zaro idonsa yace ita tace miki ankawo sadakinta? Wa kuma tace miki zata aura?

Tambaya biyu ya mata a lokaci d'aya amma bazata iya bashi amsan duka ba saidai tabashi na d'aya, tace "eh haka tacemun wai takusan aure amma bansan kota fad'a bane kawai dan nadaina damunta da maganan ka"

Wayan shi yaciro yakira wayan Adam yakira yakai sau goma Adam bai d'agaba yayi tsaki, yace yak'i d'agamun waya, amma naji Umma tacemun Baffa Tukur ne yakai masa sadaki, bazan kirasa naji ko a ina yakai, ringu biyu Baffa Tukur ya d'auka bayan sun gaisa yace mishi Baffa kundawo lafiya, dama nakira na maka bangajiya ne.

Baffa Tukur yace "Alhamdulillah nagode sosai, gaskiya naji dad'in gidan da Faisal yaje neman aure, iyayan yarinyan sunsan darajan mutum, banzaci zasu yarda suba bahaushe y'arsu ba, har ina tunanin zasu koremu, ashe yanzu Fulani suna auran hausawa.

Fa'izu yace wazai aura ne?

Baffa Tukur yace yarinyan daka zauna a gidansu a Azare Surayyah ce ko Salima ne namanta sunanta amma nasan yarinyan duk hidiman gidanku da ita akeyi yanzu kam yayi sa'an mata saidai muyita musu addu'an samun nasara a rayuwarsu.

Zufane yafara malala a jikinsa kaman ruwa take idonsa ya kad'a ya dawo jajazur makan jan gauta, zuciyarsa ta masa nauyi, nunfashinsa yaji yana sama² dakyar ya iya cewa Allah ya sanya alkairi nima naji dad'i domin nafi kowa sanin waye su, a gurguje yamasa sallama dan gudun karya fahinci halin daya shiga, yana kashe wayan ya dafe kansa yana furta innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un, Safiyyah meyasa kikamun haka kinsan ina sonki fiye da rayuwata amma shine zaki auri wanda bazan iyaja dashiba, duk maganan da yakeyi baisan a fili yakeyinsa ba, saida Sumayyah ta dafasa tana girgiza masa kai.

Ido kawai ya zuba mata dan ahalin da yake bazai iya mata pretending ba, tace nasan kanason Safiyyah so mai tsanani amma kayi hakuri ka karb'a kaddaran ka dan inaga Allah yayi bata d'aya daga cikin wanda Allah ya zab'a maka zakuyi rayuwar aure da ita, zan tayaka da addu'an Allah ya baka wacce zaka sota kaman yadda kakeson Safiyyah, ta k'arasa maganan idonta na zubar da kwallah, dan sai yanzu take tausayin kanta, da danasanin shigowa rayuwar wanda bai tab'a sonta ba.

Shima sai yanzu yaji kunya da danasanin abunda ya fad'a, hanunta ya rik'e duka biyu yace "Sumayyah wlh ina sonki kiyi hakuri da abunda kikaji na fad'a bakina ne kawai ya subce, kuma bana fatan Allah ya had'ani da wata mace ke kad'ai kawai kin isheni rayuwa, naso na auri Safiyyah kaman yadda kikaso amma namiki alk'awarin cireta a raina har abada, ke kad'ai zan sadaukarwa soyayyata ki yarda dani, ya fad'a yana kwanciya a kafad'anta.


Dukda taji sanyin maganan sa a ranta, tace Fa'izu idan zaka shekara kana yabon Safiyyah a gabana nasan bazan tab'a jin haushi ba, wlh ina mata sonda ko uwar data aifeni bana sonta haka, domin nafi sonta a kanka, Safiyyah tamun abunda harna mutu bazan manta da itaba tamun abunda uwar cikina bata munba.

Tasowa yayi daga jikinta zuciyarsa na k'unan abunda ta fad'a, ya mata tambaya danya tabbatar da abunda tace "kikace kinfi son Safiyyah akaina?

Kanta tsaye ta bashi amsa da "sosaima dan bazan iya had'a soyayyanta da kowa ba, kaima ina sonka amma baikai kwatan wanda nake mata ba, kayi hakuri iya gaskiyata nake fad'a maka"

Shima ya tabbatar da gaske take fad'a, cikin b'acin rai yace har waye Safiyyah da zakice ni mijinki kinfi sonta fiye dani, tana mace ina namiji, tunda kinfi sonta sai kije ki zauna da ita, itadin banza dahar zaki had'ani da ita.

Tunda suke bata tab'ajin ya b'ata mata rai irin na yauba, itama cikin b'acin rai tace "Fa'izu ina ganin mutumcinka amma wlh yau ya zube, Safiyyah tafi k'arfin banza a wajena, kuma tafika mutunci da daraja a guna, angaya maka bazan iya rayuwa da ita bane, ganin fuskanta yafiye mun farin ciki fiye da zama dakai, kuma yanzun nan basai anjima ba zan tafi wajenta, nazauna da ita kozanfi samun farin cikin rayuwata.

Tsawa ya daka mata kafin yace nabaki minti 10 ki tabbatar kin barmun gidana yanzun nan.

Dariya tayi kafin tace basai kasha wahalan bani mintuna ba yanzunan zan bar maka gidanka bari nahar abada ma kuwa, nasan bakasan darajata ba saboda lik'a makani akayi, karka manta kai kana k'inane wani kuma yanzu zaifara sona, wlh Fa'izu bazan sake zama da wanda baya sona ba, yanzu nima nasamu y'anci zanyi rayuwata yadda nakeso, ta sake dariya tace ohh namanta karna cinye lokacina fa, cikin d'akin ta shige ta had'a duk wani abunda tasan zata buk'ata kafin tazo ta wucesa da akwatinan ta biyu, ji yake kaman yace karta tafi tazo yana sonta, amma yafison taje gida idan Mamanta ta wahalar da ita,  yasan da k'afanta zata dawo.

Har taje tasha sai taga kaman inta tafi batama Umma sallama ba bata kyauta ba, tabada ajiyan kayanta a tasha ta hau abun hawa tayi gidan.


Umma naganin fuskanta tasan akwai matsala taja hanunta suka shige cikin d'aki, Umma tace kifad'amun abunda yake damunki, dan Allah ki d'aukeni uwa ma'aifiya a gareki karki b'oyemun komai"

Maganan Umma ya raunana mata zuciya amma saita dake tace "Umma zan fad'a miki amma dan Allah karki hanani kwatan y'ancina, duk da nasan bazan iya rayuwa babu Fa'izu ba, ina masa son da bansan yaushe nafara ba, nanta kwashe komai ta fad'a mata, kafin ta d'aura da cewa inaso sai yafara sona zan dawo gare shi"

Umma tace maiyasa Fa'izu yamu  haka  kosai da suka girma sukeso bak'in cikinsu ya kasheni, amma bazan hanaki abunda kikeso kiyiba, saidai banaso iyayanki susan abunda yake faruwa, zan b'oyeki a inda bazai ganki ba harsai yayi dana sani kimun wannan alfarman.

Sumayyah ta rumgume Umma a jikinta, tace Umma naji ance bikin Safiyyah da Yaya Faisal saura sati hud'u shine nakeso kibarni naje idan anyi biki saimu dawo tare, amma idan kin yarda a gidansu ma zan sauk'a.

Umma tace wata Safiyyah kike nufi Faisal zai aura? Dan ban fahinci wata Safiyyah kike nufi Fa'izu yake kishi akanta ba!

Sumayyah tace "

🅿️75\76
Tace "Umma Safiyyah tawa ta Azare ita Yaya Faisal zai aura"

Umm batasan lokacin da tasauka k'asa tayi sujjada ba tayita jera kirari wa mahaliccinmu ta nagode masa, ta taso ta rungume Sumayyah tace nagodewa Allah daya baniku a matsayin y'ay'ana mata daban samuba, Sumayyah kun isheni komai a duniya domin samun ku a matsayin surukai wani haskene a rayuwata, Allah ya muku albarka tashi kije kar dare ya miki, tamata sallama ta futa, a zaure suka had'u da Fa'izu ya mata wani mungun kallo yace wato kinzo ki had'ani da uwata ko kuma kinzo danta hanaki tafiya ki koma gidana ko? To kisani wlh nafi k'arfinki.

Dariyama ya bata, tace Umma uwace a gareni nazo namata sallamane, domin ni nasan darajanta a wajena, burina naganta cikin farin ciki, b nayi abunda nasan zai k'unta mata ba, wlh ina taya Umma takaicin rashin sa'an y'ay'an datayi,  Fa'izu wanda ake sone ake bibiya, to kasani kai baka kai wanda zanso ba ballan tana nazo narok'i alfarman zama dakai, Fa'izu ba sonka natsane ka, tana gama fad'an haka tayi tsaki ta wucesa.

Da mamaki yabi bayanta da kallo yace tabbas ance ka kiyayi fushi mai hak'uri, ya bud'a kafad'a yace nasan da kanki zaki kirani kibani hak'uri ke zakiyi bikon kanki da kanki, ya shege cikin gidan suka gaisa da Umma, shuru yayi yana jiran yaji mai Umma zatace masa, ga mamakinsa sai yaji tace kace tare kuke da Sumayyah dan yanzu ta shigo take cemun wai sun shiga kasuwa ne da Balkisu shine ta shigo ta gaisheni.

Mamakin abunda Umma tace masa Sumayyah tace yakeyi azuciyarsa yace kardai da gaske yarinyan nan take nufi bata sona, amma meyasa ta b'oyewa Umma abunda ya faru tsakanin mu?

 Umma tace yanaji kayi shuru, ko akwai wani matsala ne?

Yayi saurin cewa a'a bakomai bazanje tana jirana a waje kar taga jimawa na, ya tashi ya futa Umma tabi bayansa da murmushi tace zaka gane kuranka yaro.


Tana isa Azare gidansu Safiyyah ta wuce direct Safiyyah taje da gudu ta rungumeta tace Hajiyata lafiya naganki da akwatuna har biyu?

Dayake Inna na wajen tace duk shirin bikinki ne a wajen, danni kam nazo biki kenan, kuma Fa'izu yace mun a gidan nan zan sauk'a har agama biki"

Inna tace  kai masha Allah, Allah yabar muku zumuncin ku har gaban abidina, suka amsa da amin, Safiyyah haka kawai bata yarda da maganan ba, tace to tashi muje kihuta saikici abinci, bayan tayi wanka taci abinci tayi sallah, tace JININA tashi ki rakani naje nagaishe da Baba dan nasan yana gida yanzu"

Safiyyah tace amma kina ganin Mama baza taji haushi ba, idan kikace mata a gidanmu kika sauk'a?

Sumayyah tayi dariya tace kanajin haushin wanda kakeso ne, kema kinsan Mama bata sona ballan tana taji haushi dan ban sauk'a a gidanta ba.

Safiyyah tace hakane to muje, a falon Mama ta samesu dukansu harda Baba,  taje da gudu ta rungume Baba tace "Baba nayi kewarka da yawa, tunda natafi bamu sake had'uwa ba, kuma kaima bakaje ka dubani ba"

Baba yace nasan kina lafiya shiyasa banzo ba, kuma gashi yana yinki ya dad'a tambatarmun da hakan, kin dad'a fari da k'iba, ya fad'a yaba shafa fuskanta, ta gaishe shi kafin ta gaishe da Mama.

Da mamaki take kallon Mama da take amsa mata gaisuwan ta cikin sakin fuska, Mama tace "haba Sumayyah laifin me muka miki dazakifi shekara da aure bakizo kin dubamu ba?

Sumayyah tace wlh Mama banajin sha'awan garinne kawai yanzu ma bikin Safiyyah nazo ana gamawa zamu tafi tare, kuma a gidan su zan zauna yanzu ma nashigo na gaishe da Babana ne dan nasan shikad'e yake buk'atan ganina"

Mama tace "Sumayyah ninefa uwarki kokin manta ne?

Saida ta tashi kafin tace ta ina zan manta dake Mama! Aiko zan manta kowa a rayuwata bazan manta dake ba, laida ta mik'awa Baba cike da tsaraba tace Baba najima ina tara maka wannan tsaraban.

Ya karb'a yana sa mata albarka, tace Baba zamu tafi saida safe ta juya da ganinta wajen Mama tace saida safenku, kafin ta futa a d'akin Baba yanata saka mata albarka, dama Safiyyah tak'i shiga a zaure ta jirara, tana futowa suka tafi.

Mama ta saka salati tace Baban Sumayyah kaga abunda nagani kuwa,  yarinyan nan kaman wanda aka bata manta uwa tasha, kullun bani da maganan daya wuce nata amma tazo tak'i zama a wajena.

Baba yayi dariya kafin yace banga laifinta ba, konine uwata ta walak'antani kaman yadda kika mata wlh iya kacina dake gaisuwa natafi abuna, bazan tashi naje na nema mata gashashshiyan kaza taci kafin ta kwanta dan nayi kewar y'ata, nagodewa Allah dayasa nadawo daga rakiyanki kafin yarinyan nan tayi aure, da yanzu nima haka zatamun, idan baki manta ba dama nace miki ki kiyayi rana irinta yau, gashi kuwa yazo, kinaso kiji d'umin y'arki amma kin jima da cire mata k'awa zucinki a ranta, ni kinga futana bazanje wajen y'ata muyi hira.

Dana sanine mara adadi ya shiga zuciyar Mama, amma tasha alwashin saitaja y'arta ta nuna mata soyayyan dabata nuna mata ba kafin ta tafi.

Sumayyah tana zaune a tsakar gida suna hira dasu Yaya Adam a tsakar gida sukaji sallaman Baba ta tashi taje, ta sake jiki sukayita hira dashi kafin ya bata gashashshan kaza da drings ya tafi, ta shigo lokacin dare yayi kowa ya tafi d'akinsa itama ta shige d'akin Safiyyah ta sameta tana shirin kwanciya itama kayan bacci tasaka kafin ta yanki kazan ta d'auki goran maltina guda d'aya takaiwa Inna, tadawo suka zauna sunaci suna hira,  bayan sunyi brush sun kwanta, Safiyyah tace Sumayyah ki fad'amun gaskiya Fa'izune ya barki kizo kiyi sati hud'u kafin ki koma?

Sumayyah ta sauk'e ajiyan zuciya idonta ya fara tsiyayan hawaye, Safiyyah tace subbahanallahi meya faru Sumayyah?

Sumayyah saida tayi kukanta mai isarta kafin taba Safiyyah labarin duk abunda ya faru tsakaninta da Fa'izu har yadda sukayi da Umma ta fad'a mata.

Safiyyah tayi dariyan takaici tace lallai Fa'izu ya tafka babban kuskuran dasai yayi mungun nadama, karki damu Sumayyah zamu gasasa cikin ruwan sanyi, ya zaci na had'a auranku dashi dan bana sonki ne ko kuma dan bazaki samu mijin aure ba, kuma ba Umma bane zata b'oyeki nine zan b'oyeki da kaina, nizan k'arba miki y'ancin ki da hanuna, ta had'a hanunta waje d'aya tace kiyi hakuri ki yafemun K'awata nasan duk nina jawo miki wannan abunda ya faru dake"

Sumayyah tayi saurin sauk'e mata hanunta tace "karkisa nafara tuhuman kaina mana, wlh Safiyyah baki tab'amun komai ba taya zan daura miki laifi, nasan zafin rasakine yasa yamun haka...Safiyyah tace da sannu zaiyi dana sani kuwa tayita bata baki har taji zuciyarta yayi sanyi, wayar tane ya fara ringing tana dubawa taga Honey nah sunan datasa wa Faisal kenan tace Malama sakeni nayi waya da mijina.

Sumayyah tayi dariya tace masu miji manya dad'in abun nima inada miji, keda bakisan dad'in auran ba, Safiyyah ta kwalkwale da dariya tace rik'e gorinki kwana nawane maye yayi amarya"

Sumayyah tace ki d'auki wayanki kin tsaya surutu harya yanke, kuma kigama na miki wani albishir.

Safiyyah tace idan zai kira sau dari ban d'aukaba bazai hak'ura ba harsa na d'auka, wani kiranne ya sake shigowa ta d'auka tayi shuru, ta d'aya bangaren yace "Sweet honey a zabtarwan ya isa haka, me namiki yau kikak'i d'aukan wayata, kinkosan halinda nashiga kuwa?

Safiyyah cikin shagwab'an da yake dad'a dilmiyar dashi tace ai kunyan ka nakeji yau shiyasa ban d'aga wayan ba"

Yayi murmushi mai sauti kafin yace in Allah ya yarda nakusan cire miki kunyan nan, banso asa mana sati hud'uba domin yamun yawa, amma ba komai kaman yaune ranan zatazo, namiki tanadi mai yawa dazan tarb'eki ranan dazamu kasance d'aki d'aya ta inda nunfashin mu zasu gauraya waje d'aya, ranan dazan tsotsi yawun bakinki kema kisha nawa, ranan da zufanmu zasu gauraya su dawo iri d'aya Safiyyah bazan b'oye miki ba ina d'aukin wannan ranan.

Nunfashi tayita sauk'e masa a kunne a take tayi nasaran sauk'esa daga kan network, wani irin gurnani yayita sauk'e mata a kunne, sunkai minti talatin a haka, kafin suka samu nutsuwa, ita tafara magana da cewa "Honey ina neman alfarma, Allah yasa abun bazai b'ata maka raiba?

Cikin kasalan data sauk'e masa da sanyin murya yace "ki fad'a koma meye zan miki shi sweet honey"

Tace dama inaso ka yarda mun mu zauna tare da Sumayyah matar Fa'izu gida d'aya ne, amma ita kad'e ba tare da Fa'izun ba, kasan inason ta sosai banaso nayi nesa da ita, kuma nasan kanason duk abunda nakeso zaka yarda da buk'atata.

Shuru yayi na wasu d'an lokuta kafin yace tunda kince ita kad'ene ba komai zaki iya zama da duk wanda kikaga dama ai gidan kine kinfini iko dashi"

Taji dad'in amincewar da yayi batare datasha wahala ba, tace nagode Honey Allah ya karemun kai ya dauraka akan mak'iyanka ya tsareka da duk abunda ya halitta mutum da aljan, honey inasonka ina rok'on ubangiji yasa nayi rayuwata dakai har abada,  sunjuma suna musayan kalamai kafin sukayi sallamah.

Sumayyah tace JININA yaushe kika dawo y'ar iska haka ban sani ba, kinsan wanka yahau kanki"

Safiyyah tayi dariya tace nasan muddin mukayi waya da daddare sai nayi wanka shiyasa kikaga na shigo da wancan k'atan roban da buta ga kuma ruwan zafi a plast d'ina"

Sumayyah tace gwanda da akasa bikin nan kusa kafin kusa a kifewa su Inna gida suna zaman su, JININA naji kaman kina maganan zamu zauna gida  d'aya, a d'akin da Zainab ta zauna kema zaki zauna?

Safiyyah har tana shak'ewa da yawu wajen cewa Allah ya sauwak'e, haba Sumayyah kibarni na mutu maza sukaini mana, a sabon gidan da honey nah yake ginawa zamu zauna, kuma Fa'izu bazai tab'a zaton kina can ba, ke har sabon kaya zansa ya zuba miki inyaso kibarsa yayita juyi shi kad'ai acan.

Sumayyah tace nima ai nikad'e zanyita juyi akan gadon ai, ke kuma inda naga dukanku jarababbune ku cikamun kunne da ihun iskancin ku......




Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️77\78
Safiyyah tayita dariya harda rike ciki, tace ke kuma bakisan haka, ni fad'amun albishir din da kikace zakimun"

Ta shafa cikin ta kafin tace "JININA kinyi kusan samun 'ya ko d'a..cikin zumud'i Safiyyah tace kina nufin kinada ciki?

Sumayyah tace harna tsawan wata biyu da kwanaki ma kuwa, yau naso nafad'awa Fa'izu amma yanzu kam saidai ya ganni da abuna.

Safiyyah ta rungumeta tace Alhamdulillah gaskiya naji dad'in wannan labarin bazan fad'awa honey na nasan shima zaiyi farin ciki sosai, sms ta tura masa na albishir d'in Sumayyah tana da ciki.

Sakon baifi second goma da shigaba taga kiransa yace kin tabbata tana da ciki?

Safiyyah tace "yanzun nan take fad'a mun"

Ya sake jefo mata tambayan, cikin daren nan ta kiraki kenan?

"A'a muna tare yanzu haka, dan ina fad'a mata ansa bikinmu ta taho tace bazata koma ba harsai anyi biki" 

Yace what kina nufin harta taho bikin mu?

"Eh ta taho, kuma nice ta farko danasan tana da ciki saikai kabiyo baya, ko Fa'izu bai saniba"

Yace bata wayan na mata murnan jin wannan labari, Sumayyah da kunya ta karb'a wayan ta masa sallama.

 Yace "sannu Sumayyah lallai kin cika masoyiyar asali, inason Safiyyah kuma tabbas duk meson Safiyyah masoyinane, naji dad'in yadda kika girmama hidiman matana Allah ya rabaki da cikin jikinki lafiya, sannan nayi alk'awarin d'aukan nauyin cikin nan tun daga gobe har iya k'arshen rayuwar abunda zaki aifa, abun cikinki d'anane bana Fa'izu ba, nasan koshi bazai kaini farin cikin samun cikin nan ba, bansan me mijinki yamun ba na tsanesa, amma zanyi tunani nagane laifin dayamun, koda nagane ma bazai shafeki ba, dan kina da daraja da kima a idona, gobe insha Allahu zanzo naganki a Azaren"

Zuciyarta ne ya karye take tausayinsa ya kamata tasan shi kansa bayajin dad'in abunda yakema d'an uwansa, tace nagode sosai Yaya Faisal Allah ya k'ara girma da d'aukaka, kafin ta mik'awa Safiyyah wayan.

Yace sweet honey wani suna kikeso musawa Babyn mu?

Safiyyah ta kwashe da dariya tace sannu Daddy zumud'i ai kabari abun cikin yazo duniya kafin ayi tunanin sunan daza'a saka masa."

Yayi murmushi kafin yace bakisan yadda nakeji bane wlh Safiyyah ji nake kaman nadawo da aihuwan gobe, kullun ina addu'an Allah ya nunamun jininmu sai gashi Allah ya karb'a addu'ata, dan Allah kikula da ita sosai dan bana son abunda zai tab'ata.

Safiyyah tace aiko tunda tazo ita zata mun wanki da shara dasu wanke²"

Yayi dariya yace ai nasan Safiyyah ta tana da imani da tausayi baza kiyi abunda zai wahalar da itaba, kuma nasan son da kike mata bazai bari kibarta ta d'aga koda tsintsiya ba. Sukayi sallama ta kashe wayan, ta dubi Sumayyah da taketa hawayen farin ciki.

Tace waike Sumayyah abun kuka baya miki wahala ne?

Sumayyah tace kukan farin ciki nakeyi, sannan ina tausayin halinda Yaya Faisal yake ciki, Zainab ta rikirkita masa kwakwalwa tasa baya ganin uwarsa da d'an uwansa da mutumci, nan  ta kwashe labarin komai ta fad'a mata.

Safiyyah ta dafe k'irjinta tanata furta "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un, Sumayyah so baimun adalci ba dayake so ya wurgani wajen dazan wahala, amma da safe zan fad'awa Inna da Bappa halin da ake ciki inaga zan fasa auran nan"

Sumayyah tace dan Allah karkiyi haka ki taimakesa ki ceto rayuwarsa daga halakan daya fad'a, kinga yanzu son da yake miki yasa yake farin ciki da d'an da Fa'izu zai aifa, kuma nasan sanadin ki tsakanin sa da Umma da Fa'izu zai gyaru ki daure dan Allah.

Kusan kwana sukayi suna kuka daga k'arshe suka d'auro alola sukayi addu'an zab'in alkairi, da safe Inna taga idonsu duk a kumbure bayan sun karya tasakasu a gaba tana tambayansu meyake damunsu.

Safiyyah tace "Inna nafasa auran Faisal" daidai Bappa ya shigo yaji abunda take fad'a, ya nemi waje ya zauna yace meya faru kika fasa auran sa? kuka ya hanata yin magana tana kuka Sumayyah na tayata tana cewa wlh kika fasa auran nan nima sai Fa'izu ya sakeni inyaso muyita zama tare.

Bappa ya fara musu magana cikin lallab'i yace kuyi shuru ku fad'a mana mai yake faruwa dan duk kunsamu a zullumi.

Dakyar Sumayyah ta iya tsayar da kukanta ta musu bayanin komai, da mamakinsu sai sukaga Bappa da Inna suna dariya, Safiyyah cikin kuka tace maiya baku dariya Bappa?

Bappa ya gyara zama kafin yace kun bani kunya da kuka tsaya wannan d'an k'aramun abun yasaka ku kuka, cikekken baffulatani ba'a sanshi da tsoro ko karaya ba, kun manta Allah ya mana baiwan karya duk wani muguntan da muka gani, dukan mu munsan da wannan damuwan, kuma munyi masa shiri mai girma domin b'ata lamarin.

Inna tace karkisa wannan abun a ranki,  darajan Annabi da alqur'ani Bappan ki zai karya komai, akwai shirin da mukeyi na musamman domin wannan abun, kunya ce ta rufesu suka kwasa da gudu sukayi d'akinsu Inna tayi salati tace Sumayyah kidaina biyewa Safiyyah kina gudu hakafa dan naga kaman kinada juna, kafin suka cigaba da tattauna yadda abun zai kasance.

Suna shiga d'aki Safiyyah tace nagodewa Allah daya kawo mun mafita Sumayyah da bansan yadda rayuwata zata kasance ba idan akace babu Faisal a cikinta.

Sumayyah tace nasan Inna bazasu yarda afasa auran nan ba.

Tace hakane amma labarin danaji ya tsorata ni, saidai yanzu naji k'arfin gwiwan tun k'aran komai, tashi muje gidan ku Sumayyah ki gaishe da Mama.

Sumayyah tace kyaleta nikam bacci nakeji yanzu wlh, bayan jiya duk kin hanani bacci, bari Yaya Faisal yazo saina fad'a masa sukayi dariya dukan su kafin Safiyyah ta futa tabarta domin tasamu tayi bacci.


Duk hidiman bikin nan da akeyi Zainab batasan me ake ciki ba Faisal yanata hidiman sa hankali kwance ya gama ginin gidansa ya zuba musu duk wani kayan buk'ata dan yace matarsa yakeso ba kayanta ba, wajen da Sumayyah zata zauna ma ya zuba mata komai na buk'ata dama yayi wajen domin Fa'izu ne.

Yanzu Zainab ta mayar da lokacin ta duka na Boka kullun acan take kwana inta tafi da yamma da safe saita dawo, Hafsa kuwa itama kusan kullun saitaje ta wuni yanzu boka baicika aiki ba sosai kullun saiya kwashi gara dama gashi irin harijan nan masu zobe ne.

Yau auran Faisal saura sati d'aya kuma yau za'akai akwati da kayan sakun lallai, Faisal yanata sauri ya shirya yafuta domin shizai kaisu da kan saba.

Zainab ta shigo ta samesa yanata sauri tace ina zakaje naga kanata sauri?

Cikin tsoro yace dama abokina ne zaiyi aure shine zankai masu kai kayan sa, tayi tsaki tashige ciki shima cikin sauri yabar gidan, gidan Baffa Tukur ya nufa yasamu suma duk sun had'u shi kawai ake jira, ya gaishe su kafin suka futo yaji mamakin ganin y'an uwan Umma akai kayan auran amma baiyi magana ba har suka isa adaidai k'ofan gidan ya sauk'esu suka nemi yara suka shigar musu da kayan, shi kuma yace Safiyyah ta futo tasamesa a mota.

Tare suka futo da Sumayyah dama suna d'akin Yaya Adam, tunda tafuto ya kafeta da ido ta cikin glass din motan dan wani kyau da fari ta k'ara masa take sandar girmansa ta mik'e tana neman abokin rayuwa saurin gyarata yayi acikin wando gudun kar Sumayyah taga halin daya shiga, amma yasan tabbas yau da ita kad'e tazo sai Allah yasan abunda zai aikata.

Dukansu bayan motan suka shiga yace "wato kun mayar dani driver ko?

Safiyyah ta sunkuyar da kanta dan talura da shu'umin kallon da yake mata tace "sannu da zuwa honey"

Saida ya furzar da iskan bakinsa kafin yace nak'i wayon, haka ake gaishe da miji? Kidawo kusa dani kizauna saina amsa sallaman ki.

Sumayyah ganin kaman ya manta tana wajen tayi gyaran murya kafin tace sannu dazuwa Yaya Faisal ya hanya!

Sai yanzu ya kalleta domin idan Safiyyah tana kusa dashi baya iya kallon kowace mace a matsayin mace yace "yawwa Maman Baby nasameki lafiya ya unborn baby nah?

Kunya tambayan sa ta bashi ta sunkuyar da kanta kasa ta kasa bashi amsa.

Wayan Sumayyah yayi ringing ta d'aga Inna tace ina kuke game dilke tazo tanata jiranku.....

🅿️79\80
Saida ta kalli inda Safiyyah take zaune kafin tace mun shiga gidan mune Inna, tazo ta samemu anan"

Inna tace to gatanan zuwa kafin ta kashe wayanta, ta dubi Safiyyah tace JININA wai mai dilke ne tazo ya za'ayi kenan?

Kafin ta bata amsa Faisal yace meye dilke kuma?

Sumayyah kanta tsaye ta bashi amsa da cewa a jiki ake shafe mutum dashi.

Yace kina nufin sai mutum ya tub'e kenan? Tace "eh" yace haba karkusa zuciyata ta fashe mana, yanzu wata k'atuwan ne take ganemun jikin matana? Gaskiya ban yarda ba, kar'asake yin dilken nan, akawo mun kayana haka"

Itadai Safiyyah kanta yana sunkuye batace musu komai ba, a hankali ta bud'e motan tace ina zuwa Sumayyah, ta nufi gidan su Sumayyah tana shiga mai dilken tafara mata, tunda Mama taga Safiyyah ta shigo tasan dole Sumayyah ta biyota, sai tahau murnan yau 'yarta zata shigo, bayan tafiyanta Faisal yaga shurun yayi yawa bata futoba, gashi zaman kurame sukeyi da Sumayyah, ita tana kunyan shi wan mijinta ne, shima yana jin kunyan matan k'anin sane.

Ya dubeta yace dan Allah shiga ki duba mana mai ya tsayar da ita! Tace "to"

Tana shiga Mama tace oyoyo Sumayyah kin shigo? Yaudai ta d'an daure ta sake mata fuska tace eh, munshigo yin dilkene, saboda ana karb'ar kayan auren Safiyyah ne acan, ta gaisheta kafin ta shige cikin d'akinta nada, tasamu ana mata dilke tace haba kekuwa kin barmu a mota munata jiranki!

Safiyyah tace kyalesa inya gaji zasu tafi, dan wlh yanzu wani kunyansa nakeji, muda sake had'uwa kuma sai bayan biki, dan wlh ko wayarsa bazan sake d'agawa ba"

Sumayyah tace sweet honey d'in?

Koma waye kuwa ni kibani waje da Allah, ta bata amsa.

Sumayyah tayi dariya tace nan kike sakani agaba kuyita iskancin ku a gabana, sai yanzu zakice kinajin kunyansa, wlh Safiyyah Allah Allah nakeyi a d'aura auran nan, domin kunnena ya k'agu da son jin ihunki ran daran farkon ku.

Safiyyah tace kanki akeji yarinya, ance miki ni raguwa ce irinki, ko baizo ba zan jawo kayana nad'aga k'afa natura, kizuba ido zakisha kallo.

Tace aiba iya idoba harda gangan jikin na zuba.

Mama nan danan ta had'a musu girki tayita jan 'yarta da hira amma rashin sabo ya hanata sakewa.

Tajima tana nadaman abunda tayi tama Sumayyah a baya, gani take kaman rana irinta yau bazaizo ba.


Faisal gajiya yayi da jiranta suka tafi gashi yayita neman wayanta akashe har suka isa Bauchi bai samu ba.


Zainab tana kwance tana ramukon bacci, k'anwanta ta shigo ta d'aka mata duka abaya, a razane ta farka, ganin kanwanta ne tayi tsaki tace amama ke 'yar iskane ina cikin baccina mai dad'i zaki tasheni.

Kubra tace dama akwai wani baccin dazai miki dad'i yanzu, kinga kayan da mijinki yayi kuwa, wani irin sake kikayi har mijinki zaiyi aure?

Wani ashar Zainab ta k'unduma kafin tace Faisal d'inne zaiyi aure ban saniba? A ina kika samo wannan labari?

Kubra tace a ina nagani dai, jiya kanwansa Balkisu ta kirani naje naga kayan auransa, kina nufin bai fad'a mikiba, lallai zama be samemu ba, tashi muje wajen Bokan Baba Abu musa ya wargaza auran nan tun kafin a d'aura ta zamo mana k'arfen k'afa.
 

Suka tafi wajen Boka sun isa ana kiran sallan magriba lokacin Hafsa ta tafi kenan, tace Boka ya akayi Faisal zaiyi aure baka fad'amun ba, ya duba k'okon ruwansa yace saidai kiyi hakuri dan tabbas sai yayi auran nan, saidai karki kuskura kibari ya kusance ta, kinga nan ya nuna mata kwaryan yace kinga idan sunyi auran ma wani nasara zamu samu, dan gasunan dukansu a yashe da halama mutuwa zatayi, ke kuma zaki sake samun nasara akanta.

Zainab tace nagansu a cikin mawuyacin hali, amma banga fuskan yarinyan ba, Boka yayi dariya yace nima kaina banga fuskan ta ba ballan tana ke, kibarshi yayi auransa kawai amma ki tabbatar idan anyi auran karya kusance ta.

Tace bazan bariba boka, bayida k'arfin kusantar mace ma dan rabona da wani abu yashiga tsakanin mu tun ina amarya, ko wani irin wasa na masa abun a yamutse yake kaman tsohon tsumma.

Yace nidai na fad'a miki idan kika bari karkiyi kuka dani, ki kuka da kanki, kitashi ku tafi nabaki hutun yau d'aya gobe saiki dawo, suka masa godiya kafin suka, futa, Kubra tanata Allah Allah su futa ta tambayi Zainab hutun mai boka ya bata.

Akan hanyansu na komawa gida Kubra tace nikam hutun mai Boka ya bakine?

Zainab ta kwashe da dariya kafin tace lallai anbarki a baya Kubra, bakiga ban bashi kosisiba duk abunda yamun, kawai yamun aikine nikuma na basa munta, yaushe zanje wajen boka na biyasa ai gwanda na d'aga k'afa, shi kuma ya zuba mun aiki.

Kubra tace haba Zainab ai zunubin zai miki yawa gana zuwa wajen boka gana zina, ni kinganni a iya yawan wajen bokaye da shafe² da tsotse tsotse na tsaya, da zaran naga kakai hanu jikin pant dina zamu samu matsala dakai, ina sha'awan na karanta novel naji ance mijin ya samu matan sa cikakkiyar budurwa, shiyasa na tanada budurci na kodan nayi ihun daren farkon nan.

Zainab tace lallai yarinya baki more rayuwa ba, wayace miki ana wahalan kai budurci d'aki, zauna nan kiyita tanada budurcinki, shi kuma wanda zaki aura ya gama watsar da nasa.


Kubra tace koma ya watsar ni zan tanada nawa, dan inaso naji wannnan azaban da ake fad'a, sannan kema ina guje miki kamun Allah domin abunkin yayi yawa ba iya kan mijinki kika tsaya ba, harda dangin sa da uwarsa, ga kuma zina Zainab wlh ki tuba kikoma ga Allah tun kafin lokaci ya k'ure miki.

Wani wawan ashar Zainab ta d'urawa Kubra tace na rantse miki kika sake mun wa'azi saina tsinewa dangin ubanki, ina wai ke halin Baban mu kike dashi, wlh sainaci k'ashin bayan Ubanku, har suka isa gida sunata zage² a hanya.

Tana shiga gida ta tarar da Faisal yana kiran waya yanata zagaye d'akin, tace kai shahsasha wai harni zakamun kishiya ban sani ba, uban waye ya baka izinin yin aure? Kai waima waye ubanka a garinnan?

Ya tsuguna ya kama k'afanta yace dan Allah kimun rai wlh kyautan ta akabani a masallaci bani nace inasonta ba, idan kikace bakiso sai afasa, dama ina gudun abunda zai b'ata miki raine shiyasa ban fad'a miki ba.

A zuciyarta tace tunda kyautan masallaci ne tazo zanyi maninta, tace ka bud'e kunneka dakyau kaji sharad'in dazan lissafa maka inhar ka kiyaye to bazan hanaka aure ba"

Insha Allahu baza'a sake fashiba har agama, 



Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️81\82
Ya sake maida hankalinsa kanta cikin tsoro da fargaban abunda zatace yace "to ki fad'a ni kuma namiki alk'awarin kiyayewa"

Tayi murmushin mugunta kafin tace na yarda kayi aure kodan cimma wani burina, sannan kuma na haramta maka keb'ewa da matar da zaka aura koda na second d'aya ne, kuma a k'ask'antaccen gida haya nakeso ka kama mata haya domin bazan yarda ta zauna a gida maikyau ba, ko kuma ka sakata a gidan da Ummanku ta zauna wanda muka kwace a hanun Fa'izu, idan naji ka sab'awa umurni na kaima kasan baza kaji dad'iba a hanuna.

Wani dogon ajiyan zuciya ya sauk'e dayaji ba hanasa auran zatayi ba, yace "insha Allahu bazan sab'awa umurnin kiba, duk yadda kikace haka za'ayi.

Tayi tsaki tace wannan kuma ya rage naka,  tayi wucewar ta cikin d'aki, sabo da jijiyan boka yau ya hanata bacci, yau d'aya da basu tare duk hankalinta ya tashi, falo ta futa tasamu Faisal yana bacci, jikinta har b'ari yakeyi wajen zame mishi wandon sa, ganin shi a mike ba k'aramun dad'i tajiba, ta kama jijiyansa tana murzawa, a daidai lokacin shi kuma yana mafarkin yana sex da Safiyyah shiyasa tayi nasaran dad'a rura masa wutan sha'awa da zafi² yafara romancing dinta, ya tashi amma idonsa a rufe yake ya kwantar da ita dama ba komai a jikinta, warin dayaji ne yasa shi bud'e idonsa da sauri, kawai sai yaga da Zainab yake tare, gudun kar tace zatayi fushi dashi idan beyi sex da itaba, ya d'aga k'afanta zai shigeta kawai sai abunsa ya dawo kaman lagwani duk yadda tayi ya tashi amma abun yaci tura, tayi tsaki tace inaga aljanan darene ta aureka tunda idan kana bacci abun yana mik'ewa, dazaran zaka tara dani saita dawo lagwani, sai kasan yadda zakayi ka gansar dani, dan bazan iya bacci ba banjini a sama ba, ya rasa ya zaiyi yasa bakinsa da hanunsa a jikinta me warinnan, a hankali yajawo robon dayaci abinci a wajen, dama taliya da mae da yaji yaci, hanunsa yasa cikin roban yajin kafin ya ciro, ya mutsike shi da d'aya hanun, ya matso kusa da ita yana darinyan mugunta k'asa².

Ganin ta bararraje kaman sabuwar 'yar kaciya, ya d'aga k'afantan dakyau ya tura hanunsa k'asanta yana murzawa a hankali da farko har tafarajin dad'i, tana dad'a d'ago masa k'asanta, ya sake tura sauran yatsunsa guda hud'un duka, wani dungure tayi saida ta kifa bakinta a k'asa kafin ta kurma wani uban ihu tana cewa Faisal kai wani irin k'azamine da zakaci abinci baka wanke hanu ba, ta kwasa dagudu kaman wanda aka zabureta ta shiga toilet ruwan sanyi tayita kwarawa a wajen tana wankewa, kafin tazo ta kunna fanka a saitin k'asanta ta kure gudun fankan, haka ta kwana tana ihun zafi, shiko yana falo yanata dariyan mugunta, yace shikenan nasamu maganinki duk lokacin da kikazo mun da wannan warin jikinkin haka zanna miki.


Fa'izu rashin Sumayyah ya fara damunsa yau kusan sati hud'u kenan basu tare, wani irin kewanta yakeji, baisan yana mata so mai tsanani ba saida ta tafi tabar shi, idan sha'awanta ya tashi masa haka zaiyita kuka yana zagaye d'akin, yau dai yace ya zubar da makaman yak'in ya d'auki laifin, ya d'auki waya yakira number ta.

Sumayyah suna kwance duk sun gaji, har bacci ya fara d'aukanta taji wayanta yana ringing tace ho hoo hooo Mama bazata barni na huta ba, yanzu kuma kiran me takemun da daddaren nan, Safiyyah tace ki d'auka mana, tunda ta gane kuranta kuma ba'a wasa da iyayefa, ki godewa Allah tunda yanzu tana sonki abunda tuntuni muke mafarkin samu daga gareta kenan.

Sumayyah tace "wlh inason Mama sosai saidai narasa taya zan sake da itane, duk yadda naso nasaki jiki da ita abun ya gagara, amma zanyi k'ok'arin mannuwa da ita kaman yadda kowani d'a yake da uwarsa.

Safiyyah tace naji dad'in da kika gane darajan uwa da wuri, yanzu kinga sai kira takeyi baki d'agaba bazataji dad'iba.

Jin wayan ya k'ara ringing tasa hanu a k'asan fulonta ta zaro wayan bata duba sunan ba, dan tasan Mamace kawai zata iya kiranta a wannan lokacin dan taji kotayi bacci idan tace batayi ba tukunna, takance mata kiyi addu'a ki kwanta dan kusamu ku tashi da wuri, a kunne tasa wayan tace hello Mamana!

Jin an sauk'e mata ajiyan zuciya a kunne yasa tayi sauri duba sunan wanda ya kirata taga K'ALBI da sauri ta yanke kiran ta tashi ta zauna tace JININA! Fa'izune ya kirani komai zai cemun oho.

Safiyyah da take kwance tana kewan rashin jin muryan Faisal d'inta tace "ki saurare shi mana sai kiji abunda zaice"

Tsaki tayi tace ashe baki kishina, nafasa shawaran dake dasafe zan kira Ummana nafad'a mata intace na d'auka saina nad'aga, itadai Safiyyah lumshe idonta tayi kaman mai bacci, batace mata komai ba, komawa tayi tanata kallon kiransa da yake shigowa ya yanke, tanajin dad'in ganin sunanshi a wayanta, kusan kwana yayi yana kiranta tak'i d'agawa ji yake kaman zuciyarsa zata fashe tsaban k'uncin rashin d'aga masa wayan da batayi ba.

Washe gari da sassafe ya tafi gidan Umma bayan sun gaisa yayi shuru, Umma tayi murmushi tasan kewar matarsa ne yake damunsa ta lura dashi tun bayau ba, tace Autana ya Sumayyah?

Yace lafiyanta qalau jiya ta tafi Azare bikin Safiyyah wai tare zasu dawo.

Umma tace masha Allah hakan yayi kyau sosai, ta lura tunda Sumayyah ta tafi yanzu bayida walwala, yana zaune wayan shi yayi ringing da sauri ya ciro daga aljihunsa ya zaci Sumayyah ce, sai yaga sunan Ogan sane, yana d'agawa ya haushi da fad'a akan cewa maiyasa da aka turo masa da takardan dakatarwa bai kirasa ya sanar dashi ba.

Yace kayi hakuri sir lokacin baka k'asar ne shiyasa, yace kabar duk abunda kakeyi kazo kasameni a Abuja, kafin sukayi sallama, ya sanar da Umma duk yadda sukayi Umma tace "Alhamdulillah tashi katafi yanzu, ta d'auko dubu talatin tabasa tace gashi kozaka buk'aci wani abu acan"

Yace Umma keda baki sana'ar komai a ina kika samu kud'i?

Tace Safiyyah ce ta turomun da kud'i wai nayi hidiman biki dashi, sannan Balkisu ma takawo mun sauran kud'in daya rage na had'a akwatunan da sukayi, tashi kaje Allah ya bada sa'a.

Ya mata godiya kafin ya tafi gidansa ya shirya, har yaje tasha ya shiga mota sai yaga rashin dacewar tafiyan bai sanar da Sumayyan saba, yasan koya kirata bazata d'auka ba, kawai saiya tura mata sak'on zai tafi Abuja Ogansa yana kiransa, tana karantawa ta turo masa da amsan neman dacewa da Allah yakaishi lafiya ya dawo dashi lafiya, yaji dad'in ganin amsanta dan beyi zaton gani ba.

Yau ya rage saura kwana d'aya d'aurin aure, Safiyyah batayi wani event a auranta ba, sai walima kawai inda ta gayyato shahararriyan Malam nan, Malam Juwairiya tayi musu nasiha mai kyau da ratsa zuciya, ba k'aramun fad'akarwa kuwa tayi ba, dan kowa yasan Malama Juwairiya wa'azinta akwai fad'akarwa da k'aruwa sosai, da daddare harta kwanta Bappa ya turo a kirata, tasaka hijabi taje d'akinsa ta tsuguna kusa dashi tace gani Bappa.

Yayi gyaran murya kafin yace "Safiyyah na kiraki ne dan na dad'a miki nasiha akan wanda aka miki, nasan kinada hakuri da nutsuwa amma inaso ki k'ara akan wanda nasanki dashi, Safiyyah ke kad'e na aifa 'ya mace a cikin 'Yayana gata d'aya zaki iya nuna mun shine ki zauna da mijinki da danginsa lafiya, bance ki kawo mun komai ba, idan kika zauna lafiya a gidan ki shine zamu tabbatar tarbiyan da muka iba tsawon sheka ishirin da d'aya muna baki yayi amfani, ba ruwanki da kishiyanki, karki damu da duk abunda kika gani tanayi, dan tarbiyanku ba lallai ya zamo iri d'aya ba, ya mik'o mata wani kwarya yace shanye wannan kibani kwaryan.

Tana kuka ta k'arba tasha, ta mik'a masa kwaryan ya karb'a kafin ya d'auko wani k'aho da k'ullin magani yace wannan......
