Bissimillahi Rahmanin Rahim.

🅿️83\84
Na cikin k'ahon idan kikaje kwanciya ki shafe jikinki duka dashi ki kwanta, idan kin tashi da asuba karkima kowa magana harsai kinyi wanka kinyi sallah tukunna, wannan kuma ki rik'esa dakyau ki tabbar kafin mijinki ya kusan ceki kin shafa a dukkanin jikinki da k'asanki, ta nanne kawai zaki ceto rayuwar mijinki daga halakan daya fad'a.

Kuka ya hanata ma Bappan ta godiya yace ta tashi taje ta kwanta, ta futa tanata kukan kewan rabuwa da iyayenta, kodata shiga d'aki tasamu har Sumayyah tayi bacci ta shafa maganin a jikinta ta kwanta, ana kiran sallan asuba ta tashi taje tayi wanka, tayi sallah kafin ta tashi Sumayyah tayi sallah.

Inna ta shigo mata da ragowan naman da aka dafa mata da tsumi ta sakata gaba saida ta cinye naman kafin ta tsiyaya mata tsumin a kofi tasha, ta sake mik'o mata had'in dakan da aka damashi da kindurmon nono, kuka tasa tace Inna wlh nagaji dashan maganin nan"

Inna tayi dariya ta lallab'ata tasha, kafin tace zanba Sumayyah sauran maganin ta ajiye miki, duk lokacin da kikasan amfanin sa saiki karb'a.


Faisal yau jinsa yakeyi kaman bayida d'igon bak'in ciki a zuciyar sa, tun k'arfe goma suka kama hanyan Azare, Umma ma ba'a barta a baya ba, gayyata tayi sosai, Zainab tana dawowa daga gidan Boka tasamu Faisal baya nan, tace lallai Faisal yanaso raini ya shiga tsakanin mu dashi wato ya tafi aiki bai jira dawowa naba,  ta gyara ta kwanta tace zaka dawo ka sameni wato ko abincin karyawa baimun ba ya tafi, tanata mita taji anbanko k'ofa anshigo, muryan Goggo Larai taji tana cewa kina ina?

Ta futo tana cewa lafiya Goggo kika shigo a birkice haka, ko Umma Hajara ce ta mutu?

Tace yoo ba gwanda mutuwar Hajara ba da wannan mummunan labarin danaji, kina ina kikabar mijinki zai k'ara aure?

Tace wlh harna manta da wannan labarin, a ina kikaji zaiyi aure?

A ina nagani dai, yanzu haka Hajara ta cika gida da mutane tanata girke² wai angwaye sun tafi wani gari d'aurin aure.

Cikinta ne taji ya bada kukan kululuuuu tace kan Baban nan, me kike nufi, karkice mun yaune d'aurun auran dama munafikin nan bai sanar dani ba?

Goggo Larai tace ga zahiri kuwa tunda harya ba uwarsa kayan abincin da zata ciyar da mutane, har 'Yan uwan Malam na Dunduma sunzo, meyasa kika barsa  Yayi aure, kinsan illan yin kishiya kuwa, domin ya faru dani muna zaune da Malam lafiya, ina juyasa yadda raina yaso, kawai mungun Yayansa yasa shi yin aure dole, kuma k'arin abun takaici 'Yar garinsu yasa ya aura, tunda akayi auran nan komai ya jagule mun, Hajara ta kwace gidan da dangin Malam gaba d'aya, daga k'arshe saida takaici yasa nabar gidan da k'afana, gashi ke ko aihuwa bakiyi ba, kika bari zai miki kishiya.

Zainab tace baza kigane kalan nawa ba, Boka yace dole sai yayi aure ta nanne zan sake samun nasaran aikina, kuma kina maganan ciki ta ina zaimun cikin bayan ba abunda ya ke iya tab'ukawa, abunsa a muce take.

Goggo Larai tace kice lagwanine shi, amaryan ma zata gudu da kanta watarana, yanzu shirya muje mu kafa tukunya muma, mu nuna musu munfusu gadara da bikin, sannan ki kirasa kice masa idan an d'auko amarya a kawota d'akina kafin ki d'auketa ki kaita gidan daya kama mata haya da kanki, kinga sai muci ubanta dakyau, muzana mata dokokin mu, ta inda zatasan gidan wa ta shigo.

Zainab tace angama Goggo dad'ina dake kwakwalwanki yana ja ba irin nawa ba, ta shirya cikin laishi mai tsada, suka tafi a hanya Zainab tace nifa bazan kashe ko naira naba akan bikin wata banza wai kishiya ba, saidai muje dole a girka abamu muci.

Goggo Larai tace kuma magananki gaskiya ne, suna shiga gidan Zainab taga mutane a cike anata aiki, ta d'ura ashar tace wato kud'ima Faisal ya bayar a wadace ayi hidima, lallai zai dawo ya sameni dan kutumar abu kazan shi.

Mutanen Dunduma da yake ba wani saninta sukayi ba, suka cewa Umma Hajara wannan wacce yarinya ne mara tarbiyya haka!

Umma tayi murmushin takaici kafin tace itace uwar gidan Faisal, da mamaki suka kalle Zainab, sukace wani irin sake yayi wa matarsa har take iya fad'an maganan banza a gabanki?

Caraf maganan a kunnen Zainab tace munafukan banza, yanzu zan fara nuna muku irin saken da yayi,  takira wayansa ta dad'a muryan wayan ta inda kowa zai iyaji, yana d'agawa tace wato wuyanka yayi kauri dahar zaka tafi d'aurin aure baka sanar dani ba ko? Zaiyi magana ta daka masa tsawa tace bance kamun magana ba, kuma idan and'auko amaryan akawo mun ita wajena, d'akin Goggo Larai za'a kawomun ita ba d'akin shashashan uwarka ba, tana gama fad'an haka ta kashe wayanta tana kallonsu ta gefen ido, taga dukansu sun shiga shork, tace bakuga komai bama tukunna, sai yadawo kuga yadda zanyi ball dashi da amaryan a gaban idonku.

Balkisu ta taso tace kinyi kad'an Zainab saboda amaryan mu tafi k'arfin ki, dan ita ba jahila dabba  irinki bane, Goggo Larai tazo ta tsinka mata mari tace kinsan dawa kike magana kuwa?

Tace nasani Goggo ina magana da jahilan matan Yaya Faisal ne"

Ta d'aga hanu zata sake marinta Zainab tace k'aleta nasan ba yinta bane a asirce miki ita akayi, ki bari gobe zamuje wajen boka na ya warware miki komai 'yarki ta dawo hanunki, kafin suka shige d'aki suna tattauna yadda zasuci mutumcin Faisal da Amaryan sa a gaban mutane, 'yan uwan Umma Hajara dana Malam sukace "Hajara dama har yanzu kina cikin uk'uban matan nan baki sanar damuba!

Umma Hajara tayi dariya mai kama da kuka tace karku damu nasaba da irin wannan abun, ku cigaba da aiki karsu dawo bamu gama ba.

K'arfe hud'u dubbanin jama'a suka sheda auran Faisal da Safiyyah, kowa yaga Faisal yasan yana cikin farin ciki, Fa'izu shima saida yayi k'ok'ari yasamu zuwa auran d'an uwansa, shine mutum na farko daya fara mik'awa Faisal hanu yace natayaka murnan samun Safiyyah Allah ya sanya alkairi a rayuwar auranku.

Faisal ya mik'a masa hanu kafin ya kafesa da ido, ganin yadda d'an uwansa ya rame yasa yaji tausayin sa amma bazai iya tambayan sa me yake damunsa ba, sun jima rik'e da hanun juna kowa da irin tunanin da yakeyi, Faisal sai kokarin tuna laifin da d'an uwansa ya masa yakeyi amma ya kasa tuna komai, idonshi yayi jajazur.

Ganin haka yasa Fa'izu janye hanunsa yace zan tafi Abuja yanzu don mai gidana yanaso a mayar dani kan aikina ne, nazone kar ace ina jininka guda banzo d'aurin auranka ba, yana gama fad'a yayi saurin barin wajen dan bayaso farin cikin daya gani a fuskan d'an uwansa ya gushe, bayansa yayitabi da kallo harya b'ace masa, Alh Sulaiman ne ya yanke masa tunanin daya tafi ta hanyan mik'a masa hanu kafin ya rungumesa yana dukan bayansa yace namaka murnan samun mace tagari Allah ya baku zaman lafiya da zuri'a d'ayyiva, kafin 'yan uwa da abokan arziki sukayita tayashi murna.

K'arfe biyar aka futo da amarya tanata kukan rabuwa da iyayenta, Sumayyah ta futo da akwatinan ta tasaka abayan mota, har zata shiga mota ta hango Mama tanata kallonta idonta yayi ja, taje da gudu ta rungume Mama tana kuka tace "Mama zamu tafi saina sake zuwa dubaki"

Ba k'aramun dad'i Mama tajiba jin 'yarta a jikinta tace Allah ya muku albarka Sumayyah ya baku zaman lafiya a gidajen ku, nima inanan biyoku zuwa bikin,  ta saketa tana dariya tace saikun taho Mama, taje ta shiga mota, saida kowa ya shiga kafin aka d'auki hanyan Bauchi Inna tanata kukan rabuwa da 'yarta.

Suna isa Bauchi Faisal yace a nufi gidansu ta ita,  Zainab tanajin motoci sun tsaya taji gabanta ya fad'i, amma dayake ta shirya shuka tsiya yasa ta gyara zamanta tana girgiza k'afa, Goggo Larai da Hafsa sunata zugata, Ibrahim ma ya shigo yashiga layin 'yan zugi.

Masu kawo amarya suka shigo da Sallaman su Safiyyah tana tsakiyan dattije biyu Sumayyah na bayanta sai ayarin yan rakiyan amarya suna bayansu, sunzo daidai k'ofan d'akin Umma zasu shiga Faisal ya shigo da sauri yace "d'akin gaba zaku kaita ba nanba" yana gama fad'an haka ya futa da sauri.

Umma Hajara tazuba musu ido hankalinta tashe, Safiyyah ta jawo Sumayyah ta mata rad'a a kunne, kawai mutane ganin Sumayyah sukayi ta......





Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️85\86
Taja hanun Safiyyah da k'arfi sunkaje suka rungume Umma, suna murnan had'uwa da ita.

Umma kukan farin ciki tasaka kafin nasamun su a rayuwarta, taja hanun sirikanta suka shiga d'aki, sudai 'yan rakiyan amarya basusan me yake faruwa ba, suma sukabi bayansu zuwa d'akin.

Sauran 'yan tsakar gidan sukayi hamdala a zuciyar su, Balkisu ta saka gud'a kafin tace gaskiya Sumayyah kin birgeni da kikaja hanun amarya zuwa d'akin surukanta, kinmun daidai kin nunama wata ita k'aranar kwaroce, sai muga ta fad'i yanzu ko akwai wani gadaran daza'a nuna mana kuma yanzu.

Zainab tana zaune a d'akin Goggo Larai tana kad'a k'afa ta turo d'ankwali gaba tana jiran shugowan masu kawo mata amarya, kaman asa taji abunda Balkisu take fad'a suka futo da gudu, Zainab tace har akwai wanda ya isa nace ga yadda za'akai amarya a canjamun, to wlh Amarya gonda kifuto inba hakaba yanzu nasa asakeki.

Ba wanda ya kulata futa tayi kaman mahaukaciya tana neman Faisal a waje, amma bata gansaba, ta dawo gida tanata d'ura ashar tana kiran layinsa yana ringing ba'a d'agawa, tace lallai zan d'auki mummunan mataki yau d'innan.

Hafsa tace Goggo ina miki jajen asaran aihu da kikayi, dan daka aifi 'ya irin Balkisu gwanda ace kayita b'arinsu sau dubu.

Goggo Larai tace asirce mun ita akayi amma zan kwato kayana, nan bada jimawa ba, Ibrahim yace wlh Goggo da badon tanada tsohon ciki ba danaje na kararrayata a wajen amma ba komai zaki dawo hanu.

Zainab tanata cika ta batsewa ita kad'e, saiga Faisal ya shigo jikinsa yana b'ari yazo gabanta yace Masoyiya kiyi hakuri nabar wayan a mota ne sai yanzu nazo naga kiranki, ta daka masa tsawa tace da Allah kama mutane shuru, wato nice zan saka doka akarya mun ko?

Muryan shi har hard'ewa yakeyi wajen tambayanta meya faru, tace nace akawo mun amarya shine za'a kaita d'akin wancan shashashan, to ka gaggauta sakinta tun kafin na b'ata maka rai.

Safiyyah najin haka ta kalli Umma ta mata halamun su shiga cikin d'aki, ta fara shiga kafin Umma tabi bayanta, suna shiga tace Umma inaso kisaka kira'an qur'ani yadda kona futa 'yan uwana baza suji abunda zanceba a waje, sannan kibani kaya nacanja da hijabi da likaf wanda bazasu gane nina futaba, hakanko akayi tacanja kaya tazo ta wuce ta gaban su basu ganeta ba, Umma tace bazan sa mana karatu mudaina jin shirman da akeyi a tsakar gida sukace mata to, Sumayyah tanaganin futan Safiyyah tayi dariya tace Allah ya baki sa'a JININA, taje gabansu ta tsaya lokacin Faisal yana cewa saki nawa kikeso na mata? Safiyyah ta yaye likaf din fuskanta sukayi ido biyu da Zainab.tayi murmushi tace   Sannu uwar gida mai bada umurni, inaso ki nunamun ke cikekkiyar mace ne, wanda ta yarda da kanta bawai muna mace ba, inaso kibari muzuba kishi dake muga wanda zai kwace kansa agun mijinta.

Goggo Larai tace karki yarda da zak'in bakin ta, nasan yarinyan nan saita futar dake a d'akin ki in kikayi wasa.

Zainab tayi dariya duk da dafarko ta razana da ganin Safiyyah a matsayin kishiyarta, aranta tace dama wannan mai kama da aljanun ya auro, ta kalli Goggo Larai tace haba Goggo har kinmanta waye ni, kinmanta irin kaidina, nafasa baza'a saketa ba, zan gasata irin gashin dasai tayi dana sanin shigowa gonana.

Takalli Faisal tace inaso kafara hukunta ta na karyamun dokana da tayi"

Ya kalli Safiyyah k'irjinsa yana bugawa yace Safiyyah zaki zauna da Umma har abada tunda akanta kika zab'i ki karya dokana.

Zainab ta tafa tace yamun daidai, yarinya zaki gane kuranki bakiga komai bama tukunna.

Safiyyah tace kidaina saurin murna ki kiyayi kalan tawa hukuncin, ta juya ta koma d'akin Umma, taje ta canja kaya, Umma data lab'e tana sauraronsu tace Safiyyah kiyi hakuri, kalan taki jarabawan kenan ranan da kika shigo gidanki ranan kika fara gamuwa da matsala.

Safiyyah ta rungume Umma tace karki damu Umma insha Allahu watarana sai labari, kibarta tayi duk irin shukan da takeso a hankali zata girb'e abunda ta shuka.

Washe gari 'yan uwan Safiyyah sukayi jeren kayan kicin dukda sun samu komai a kicin d'in haka sukayita jona nasu sauran suka barsu a buhu,  kowa ya dawo yana yabon kyau gidan.

Zuwa yamma suka juya garinsu, sukabar amarya a d'akin Umma.

Wasa² saida Safiyyah tayi sati biyu a d'akin Umma,  Faisal baizo ya d'auketa ba, saidai taga shugowansa ya kawo wa Goggo Larai abinci ya tafi.

Sumayyah tace JININA haka zakiyi ta zuba ido Zainab tanayin abunda taga dama baki d'auki matakin komai ba?

Safiyyah "tace karki damu Sumayyah yanzu zan kira Bappa na fad'a masa halinda ake ciki, duk ansan daya bani dashi zanyi amfani, takira wayan Bappa cikin nutsuwa ta mishi bayanin komai.

Bappa yace kinyi sake da yawa Safiyyah meyasa tun tuni baki d'auki mataki ba, yau inaso kicire kunyan mu na fulani kibi mijinki, idan kunje gida ki nemeshi dakanki kobai nemeki ba, sannan ki tabbatar kinyi amfani da maganin dana baki.

Cikin kunya tace to Bappa sukayi sallama, ta dubi Sumayyah tace " yau d'innan zamu koma gidan mu, ta d'auko kin gidan ta mik'a mata tace kije ina zuwa, da daddare kiyi mana duk abunda ya dace.

Sumayyah da mamaki take kallonta tace taya kike tunanin zaki koma gidanki batare da yazoba, kuma kinsan ko kulaki bayayi"

Safiyyah tace "tunda nace miki kije ina zuwa kije kawai, Sumayyah ta tashi ta tafi jikinta a sanyaye.

Umma ta shigo d'akin tace ina abookiyar hirankin ta tafi? Safiyyah tace na turata gidan mu zuwa dare zanbiyo bayanta, Umma ta zauna kusa da ita tace "yace zaizo ku tafine?

Safiyyah tace "a'a dama Umma inaso namiki wani tambaya"

Umma tace fad'a tambayanki ina sauraronki.

Tayi shuru kafin tace "Umma kiyi hakuri da irin tambayan dazan miki, nasan bazai miki dad'i ba ko kuma kiga kaman naraina kine, kawai inaso nayi hisabin wani abune a zuciyata"

Taci gaba da cewa "Umma me kikama Goggo Larai ta tsaneki dayawa, kuma takeson rabaki da 'ya'yanki?



Umma tayi shuru kafin tace "ba komai tambayanki ba laifi bane, duk da banason tuna baya amma yau zan baki labarin irin zaman da mukayi da Larai a tak'aice.

"Malam mutumin kirkine wanda mutane suka sheda hakan saidai beyi sa'an mace tagari ba dan agaban kowa takanci mutumcin sa, duk da haka bai tab'a tunanin ya rabu da itaba, Bappa Tukur shine Babban Yayansu Uwa d'aya uba d'aya ganin da yayi d'an uwansa bayida kwanciyan hankali a gidan auransa yasa yace dole saiya k'ara aure, Bappa Tukur dakansa yaje Dunduma ya nema masa ni a matsayin matar dazai aura, kasan cewar Malam mutum ne mai biyayya yasa ya amince masa tun kafin ya ganni, nida Malam bamu tab'a ganin junaba harsai da aka kawoni gidan sa.

Aranan naga tashin hankali, domin Goggo Larai takafe ta tsare tace bai isa ya shiga d'akina ba, haka dole Malam ya hakura bai shigaba, kullun haka zasuyi tayi da Malam akan bazai shiga d'akina ba, kinsan me hakuri bai iya fushi ba, Malam yazo zai shiga d'akina tasha gabansa tace bazai shigaba, ranan Malam yayi abunda bai tab'a tunanin zaiyi ba, ya mata dukan dasai datayi laushi, kafin yasamu ya shiga d'akina, tunda mukayi ido biyu da juna muka fara son junan mu, haka mukayita zama yau da dad'i gobe ba d'adi har Allah ya bamu ciki akusan tare da ita, ta aihu da kwana hud'u nima na aihu dukanmu maza muka aifa...

🅿️87\88
Nanma tayita masifa wai ina kishi da ita shiyasa na aifi d'a namiji, duk abunda takeyi bamai kulata, danni nafi bada hankali akan kula da mijina.

Ahaka har na sake samun ciki na aifi Fa'izu, kafin daga baya itama tasamu ciki ta aifi Balkisu da Hafsa tun daga lokacin Malam ya fad'a jarabawan ciwo, Larai ta rufe idonta tace ita bata auresa dan tayi jinyan saba, nik'a nake jinyansa.


Danginsa na Dunduma sukazo sukace zasu d'akesa suje su masa jinya acan, nayita kuka ina rok'onsu akan subarshi zan iya jinyan sa, sukace bai kamata abarni nik'ade ina jinyan saba, saidai in zamuyi da kishiya ta.

Baffa Tukur yace Larai ya kamata kicire komai aranki kiyi jinyan mijinki, a gabansu ta tattara kayanta  da 'ya'yanta tabar gidan, tana ce musu ita ba'a halicceta dan tayi jinyan gardi ba, idan ya mutu ku turomun nazo na karb'a gadona dana 'ya'yana, ranan malam ba k'armun kuka yasha ba, yace dama k'iyayyan da Larai takemun har yakai haka, yanzu banga amfanin aurena akanta ba, zan sauwak'e mata taje tayita zamanta a gidansu.

Nace a'a Malam bazaka saketa ba kabarta akan 'ya'yanta kawai, dan yanzu kana sakinta za'ace ninasa ka saketa, kabarta da halinta duk wanda yayi daidai kansa, ai ita ba yarinya bane da za'ace ta gyara, muka had'u dukanmu mukayita bashi hakuri harya hakura da sakin da yace zaiyi.

Dagani sai Faisal muke jinyan sa, duk da lokacin ba wani girma Faisal yayi ba, kullun Malam bayida aikin daya wuce sakamun albarka dani da 'ya'yana, yakan cemun karna yarda d'ana ya auri yarinyan da gidan su ba tarbiya dan yaga illan hakan akan Larai, sai kuma kaddara ta hanani jin nasihan Malam nasan danabi abunda yake fad'amun, bazan bar Faisal ya auri Zainab ba, dan ba abunda da ba'a fad'amun ba dangane da gidansu narufe idona nace abarshi ya aureta koba komai yayi jahadi, lokacin da komai ya kwab'e mun, mutane da dama sunzo sunace mun sihiri akama Faisal dan arabani dashi dan haka natashi tsaye domin ceto rayuwar sa, kowa ya shigo da irin abunda zaice mun, wasu suce naje wajen malaman zaure zasu iya warware mun wasu suce mun ai abunda akayi ta hanyan sihiro, sai boka ne zai iya karyasa, nayi amfani da maganan da suke fad'amun na natashi tsaye, natashi nayita kai kukana wajen Allah kuma har yanzu banzauna ba, nasan Allah maji rok'on bawansa ne, kuma nasan yana jina lokacine kawai baiyi ba, ta goge hawayenta tace kinji tarihin zamana da Larai, bansan ko alabarin dana baki akwai wajen dana musguna mata bansani ba.

Safiyyah itama ta share hawayenta tace "baki mata komai ba Umma, zalincine da rashin wadatar zuciya yake d'awaniya da ita, bansan meyasa mutane yanzu basuda imani ba, taya zakace zaka raba d'a da uwarsa, tun ciki yana da kwanaki a jikin uwa take fara rasa walwalanta idan ya tsufa bata isa tayi bacci kaman yadda kowa yakeyi ba, watakam ma bata baccin kwata², bazata tab'a dawowa hayyacin taba harsai ta aifi cikin nan, tayi fama da renon d'a, idan ansamu mai kuka haka zai hanata bacci, ba wanda zai tayaka rainonsa da daddare, amma idan ya girma ya dawo mutum ace za'a rabaka dashi, wannan wani irin rashin imanine, muna ganin kaman ba hakkine, duk macen da take haka, tama kanta hisabi kafin Allah ya mata, ta duba itama zata aihu zata aifi d'a namiji watarana itama za'a rabata dashi, idan haka ya faru da ita zataji dad'i, Umma ki cigaba da addu'a, addu'an uwa baya fad'uwa kasa banza, kuma insha Allahu sun gama nasara akanki da 'ya'yanki har abada, Umma naji dad'i da baki d'auki hud'ubar mutane ba, da yanzu kema ansakaki a hanyan b'ata, anason akoda yaushe musulmin kware ya yarda da Allah ne kad'e yake iyayin mummuna da kyakykyawa, zan tafi yau Umma nima ki sakani a cikin addu'anki da kika saba, inaso na kwato rayuwan Yaya Faisal daya fad'a halaka, lokaci yayi da Goggo da Zainab zasu fara girb'an abunda suka shuka.

Umma ta rungume mata a jikinta tace "Allah ya miki albarka Safiyyah, tabbas kin wuce surika a wajena ke 'yatace, samun irinki a rayuwata babban nasarane Allah shiyasan abunda ya shirya tsakanin ku, shiyasa komai ya juya Sumayyah ta auri Fa'izu ke kuma kika auri Faisal, ina muku fatan alkairi a rayuwar ku gaba d'aya, Safiyyah ko bakice na miki addu'a ba yazamo dolena namiki, kuma nasan insha Allahu zakiyi nasara tunda da zuciya d'aya zakiyi komai, yanzu yaushe zaki tafi.

Tace inaso idan ya kawo wa Goggo abinci sai mutafi tare"

Umma ta tashi ta d'auko wani gora mai zuma tace inaso kinashan cokali uku kullun har ya k'are, kafin ta had'a mata magunguna tasha, itadai sha kawai takeyi amma bataso, domin maganin sun fara damunta.

Goggo Larai ta banko musu k'ofa ta shigo ko sallamah babu ta dubi Safiyyah tace "yarinya ina guje miki zama bazaura mai aure akanta, taurin kai daji dakai a wajen dabaki kaiba yasa kink'ima kishiyarki biyayya, to ina tabbatar miki haka zaki mutu da aure a kanki, nasan wannan munafukan sirikan taki ita take zugaki kike yin duk abunda kukaga dama, gwanda kisan inda dare ya miki dan anjima kad'an Faisal zaizo kuma yana shigowa zansa ya sakeki ya huta da bakin cikin zama da kikeyi da makiyiyarsa.

Safiyyah tace idan kin gama zaki iya tafiya, domin ban cika juran a tsayamun akai anamun haushi ba, idan kuma kina ganin wasane ki cigaba da tsayuwa, zakiga abunda zan miki, kinsan ba kowani dabba nake tausayin saba.

Goggo Larai tayi shewa tace kifad'a komai yanzu zuwa anjima ke zaki dawo karan dazansa a koreki, kifara had'a kayanki domin nasan baza kifi awa biyu ba a gidan nan, ta juya ta futa tana dariya.

Zainab ne tsugune a gaban Boka tace "Boka abun Faisal ya fara damuna kullun idan yana bacci zakaga abunsa ya tashi dazaran naje garesa saita kwanta kaman lagwani.

Boka yace bazan b'oye miki ba nine nan nadawo dashi haka a gareki, domin alokacin banida wanda take iyawa dani saike kad'e, shiyasa na hanaku jin dad'inku, amma yau zan baki makarin abun, ya d'auko wani kwab'abb'an magani kaman ruwa mai d'an karan wari yace kije kishafa wannan a gabanki, yau ko kwana kikeso yayi akanki zai iya batare daya gajiba.

Ta k'arba ta tafi tanata murna yau zata sakejin d'umin Faisal a maranta, tana zuwa gida ta d'aura girki ana sallan isha ta gama, Faisal yana kwance akan kujera ta kwala masa kira tace maza d'auki abincin nan kakaiwa Goggo kuma ka dawo yanzu.

Har zai futa ta kirasa tace na rantse maka idan naji ancemun ka kalli k'ofan d'akin munafukan uwarnan taka saina kwak'ule maka idonko.

Ya futa yana addu'an Allah yasa idan yaje yaga Safiyya ko a tsakar gidane, dan ganinta yana ragemasa rad'ad'in da yake zuciyar sa, yana isa yayi fakin din motansa a inda ya saba, ya kifa kansa a siterin motan yanajin k'unan hukuncin daya yankewa Safiyyah a zuciyar sa.

Safiyyah tanajin tsayawan motan sa dama tayi wanka ta shirya komai shi kad'e take jira tasaka hijabin ta tace Umma zan tafi kimun addu'a.

Umma ta tashi ta rungumeta tace ina miki fatan alkairi 'yata Allah yasa zaki shiga gidanki a sa'a,  suka sake juna Safiyyah ta futo tanajin kewan Umma a zuciyarta.

Faisal ganin tunanin bazai masa ba ya bud'e motar ya futo jikinsa a sanyaye ya kama hanyan shiga cikin gidan yana shiga zaure yaji an rungumesa, dukda besan wanda ya rungumesa ba amma yaji wani sanyi a zuciyarsa wanda ya hassada masa kasala tare da lumshe idonsa, sheshshek'an kukan dayaji ne yayi saurin dawo dashi daga duniyan tunanin daya tafi.

Fuskanta ya d'ago yana addu'an Allah yasa Safiyyan sane ta rungumesa, aiko suna had'a ido jikinsa ya fara b'ari yayi saurin had'a bakinsu wa.......



Kuyi hakuri da rashin ganin posting dina yadda ya kamata👏🏻👏🏻





Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️89\90
Waje d'aya yana bata hot kiss kaman maijin yunwa daya samu abinci duk ilahirin jikin sa b'ari yakeyi, dakyar ta iya kwace bakinta tana mayar da ninfashi a hankali, ganin ya fad'a tarkonta ta saka masa kuka kafin ta kwantar da kanta a k'irjinsa hanunta yana shafa fuskansa d'aya hanun nashafa bayan sa tafara magana cikin kuka.

"Yaya Faisal meyasa ka yaudareni da kalman so alhalin ba haka bane a zuciyarka! Meyasa ka koyar dani sonka kasan zakazo ka gujeni! Dama ka aureni dan ka kawoni Bauchi nama Matar ka bautane ko kuma danku had'u kuna gasani da azaban ku" Karfin kukanta ne ya k'aru.

Jin kukan yakeyi a duka ilahirin jikinsa, gashi feelings ya hanashi yin magana dan ba k'aramun wutan sha'awanta ta rura masa ba, kawai saiji tayi an d'agata sama bai direta ko inaba sai cikin mota, kafin ya rufe yaje ya shiga mazaunin driver ya zauna a 360 yaja motan yabar unguwan kaman wanda za'a kamashi saida suka hau kan titi kafin ya rage gudun motan, yana tuk'i da hanu d'aya, hanunshi d'ayan yana shafa duk inda haninshi yakai a jikinta, atake yayi nasaran sauk'eta a network magungunan da Inna da Umma suka bata ya fara aiki, tayita matse cinyanta tana lumshe ido, da badon tana tsoron karya zubar dasu a titiba data mayar masa da martani
Sumayyah tana zaune a tsakar gida tun zuwanta dan batasan sashinta ba, ganin har anyi magariba da isha basuzo ba saita fara tunanin hala bai yarda ya d'aukota bane gashi tayita kiran layinta a kashe, tana zaune shuru sai bishiyiyoyin gidan dasuke kad'awa, gidan yayi dubu tafara jin tsoro, ta tashi da niyan dawowa gida sai taji tsayawan mota a k'ofan gidan da sauri ta koma cikin gidan ta b'uya a bayan fulawowi, ta zuba ido taga waye zai shigo.

A waje Faisal yabar motar ya futo ya zaga ta wajen datake ya rungumota kaman jinjira, Sumayyah tana ganinsu suna shigowa tayita cewa Alhamdulillah Allah nagode ma daka nunamun wannan ranan.

Side d'in daya tanada dominsu ya shige da ita, akan kujera ya kwantar da ita ya juya zai futa ta rik'o hanunsa idonta yana zubar da hawaye, kai ya girkiza mata halamu ta daina kukan ya isa haka.

Tace "tafiya zakayi ka barni ni kad'e a gidan?

Nan ma kai ya girgiza mata cikin muryansa dayake futowa dakyar yace ba nisa zanyi ba yanzu zan dawo insha Allahu, kafin ya futa da sauri, Sumayyah tanajin yama motansa key ta futo daga b'uyan datayi ta shige d'akin da gudu, Safiyyah na ganinta ta taso da gudu suka rungume juna tace Sumayyah tab'a k'irjina kiji yadda zuciyata take bugawa wlh tsoro nakeji dan naga Faisal komai da zafi² yakeyin sa, anya zan iya cikawa Umma alk'awarin dana d'auka kuwa?

Sumayyah ta rik'e hanunta cikin k'arfafa mata gwuwa tace "haba Safiyyah maiyasa kike abu kaman ba baffulatana ba, kowa yasan fulani yasanmu da jajircewa akan abunda mukasa a gaban mu, koda kuwa za'a kashemu bamu sarewa, kituna hajadi zakiyi tanan ne kawai zaki dawowa Umma da d'anta hanunta, kituna irin sonda Umma take mana! Umma bata tab'a mana kallon sirikai ba sai 'yayan cikinta, ta nanne kawai zaki nunawa Umma muma munason ta zamu iyayin komai domin farin cikinta, koso kike Zainab tayita nasara akanki?

Tana ambatan sunan Zainab ta nemi tsoro ta rasa a zuciyarta tace Sumayyah koda zan rasa raina yau, zanba Faisal kaina kodan dawowan farin cikin Umma dana Fa'izu.

Sumayyah tayi murmushi tace wayace miki ana rasa rae a wajen yin sex da ana rasa rae da iyayenmu basu aifemu ba, kedai kizamo jaruma kawai, dannima jarumtarne yasa kika ganni da ciki.

Hira suka cigaba dayi Sumayyah tana ta dad'a k'arfafa mata gwuwa, kafin tace JININA tunda nazo a tsakar gida nake zaune bansan sashina ba, nan suka tashi zuwa neman side d'inta sun duba wajen Flat hud'u duka ba kaya, sai ana biyar d'inne suka samu da kaya a ciki kafin suka shiga Sumayyah tanata yaba tsaruwan wajen, tace JININA tashi ki koma wajenki karya dawo yasamu bakinan.

Safiyyah tayi kalan tausayi da fuskanta kafin tace anya zai dawo kuwa?

Sumayyah tace jikina yana bani zai dawo, ta tashi ta tafi jikinta a sanyaye, tana shiga ta duba inda toilet yake ta shiga ta d'auro alola tafara gabatar da nafila da neman albarkan ubangiji a cikin rayuwar aurensu.


Umma taji shuru bata dawo ba, saita futo ta lek'a waje plast din abinci taci karo dashi a zaure tayi murmushi tare da komawa ciki,  haka kawai takejin farin ciki a zuciyar ta, ta d'auro alola tazo tafara nafilfili takai kukanta wajen Rabbil samawati wal ard.

Zainab ta shirya tsaf ta shafa magani a gabanta tana jiran Faisal ya dawo sufara aiki domin in bata manta ba ranan daransu na farko ba k'aramun gurzanta yayi ba, tsikan jikinta ne ya tashi data tuna da wannan ranan.

Goggo Larai ta futo zataje shago siyan maganin sauro ta tsaya a k'ofan d'akin Umma tace Safiyyah kike kowa, ina sheda miki ki shirya nasan yanzu zaizo ya k'ara miki gudu, dan ko minti d'aya bazan bari ki k'ara a gidan nanba idan yazo, kafin tasa kai ta futa tana zuwa zaure taci karo da plast d'in abinci ta d'auka kenan saiga Ibrahim tace kaga yaron nan anan yau kuma yabar mun abincin, kuma akwai abunda nakeso yamun kafin ya koma, dan Allah Ibrahim yi sauri kaje gidan kace masa yazo yanzu ina nemansa.

Ibrahim yace kema kinsan bazan iya zuwa da k'afa ba ko?

Naira d'arin data futo dashi zata siya maganin sauro ta bashi tace maza ku taho tare, ya kama hanyan gidan yana zuwa yaga k'ofa a bud'e, d'akin ya shige direct abunda ya ganine yasashi kama wandon sa da sauri, muryan shi yana rawa yace Zainab zan iya shigowa.

Lokacin tafara futa hayyacinta ba abunda takeso taji kaman dumin namiji a jikinta tace kashigo inzaka iya biyamun buk'atata.

Ibrahim abun nema ya samu dama ya dad'e yana sha'awanta, yana shiga be tsaya tambayanta wani buk'ata take nufiba yahau cire kayansa ya haura kanta atake suka sake ihu,  ya fara sukuwa akanta kaman d'an marak'in doki.


Faisal yana futowa ya nufi wajen da ake gashin kaza a state locost ya siya kaji manya guda uku, kafin yaje ya had'a musu da drings da fresh milk, kafin ya kama hanyan komawa tunawa yayi da zainab saiya sauk'a gefen titi ya ciro wayansa ya rubuta mata sakon cewa "kiyi hakuri masoyiya bazan samu dawowa ba saboda wani uzuri mai k'arfi ya rik'eni" ya tura mata kafin ya cigaba da tafiyansa hankali kwance yanata Allah Allah yaga ya isa gidan zuwa wajen Sweet honey sa.



Safiyyah tanajin tsayawan motansa gabanta ya fara bugawa da k'arfi bata dad'e tsinkewa ba saida taga shugowan sa, yana shigowa ya ajiye ledodin hanunsa yaje ya d'auro alola yajasu sallah suna idarwa ya kama kanta ya mata addu'an da Annabin Rahma ( S.A.W) ya koyar damu kafin yayi mata tambayoyi  game da addinin mu ta amsa kaman yadda yayi tunani.

Ya kama hanunta yana murzawa a hankali kafin yace .......

Mmn Ashfaq

🅿️91\92
Safiyyah wlh ina sonki so mai tsanani ma kuwa, ban aureki da niyan na wulak'anta kiba, dan Allah kina mun uzuri aduk yanayin da kika ganni aciki duk abunda kikaga inayi basona bane, ni kaina narasa meyasa halina ya canja lokaci guda, kiyi hakuri kiyafe mun Sweet honey insha Allahu bazan sake miki abunda zai k'ona miki raiba, shiyasa na kawoki gidan ki danna nuna miki irin son da nake miki, kuma nasan yau zaki tabbatar nayi kewanki da yawa hakuri kawai nakeyi.

Ta fad'a jikinsa tana kuka tace na yafe maka honey dama ban rik'eka a zuciya taba, d'agota yayi yana share mata hawaye kafin yace ina buk'atan wani abu a wajenki zaki bani?

 Tace zan baka Yaya Faisal koda kuwa rae nane"

Murmushin jin dad'i yayi yace ubangijine kawai zai iya karb'an ran bawan sa aduk lokacin dayaso ba mutumba, yanzu tashi kije kiyi alola kafin nashigo.

Tace "to" kafin ta tashi ta shiga bedroom ta shiga wanka ta sakeyi kafin ta d'auro alola tana addu'an Allah yasa karya shigo saita gama, har tagama tafuto tashafa mae da turarruka kala² saida tabi duk lungu da sak'o na jikinta da humra mai d'an karan k'amshi, kafin ta d'auko kayan data cire ta mayar.  

     Ta  zauna a kan gadon ta cikin Lullub'i kaman yadda duk amare sukeyi a daresu na farko, gabanta sai fad'uwa yakeyi domin tasan yana gamawa zai shigo ya sameta, maganin da Bappa ya bata ta d'auko tayi duk yadda yace tayi.
   
Tunawa tayi da abinda k'awayen ta da sukayi aure suke fad'a mata, azuciyar ta tace Sumayyah ma tace mun lokacin da Fa'izu ya shigar da abunsa tasha wahala, domin kuwa danaje naga duk zanin gadon ta saida ta b'atashi da jini. Kusan satin ta daya gabanta yana mata ciwo.

   Sai Nally Abdul itama k'awata ce  tacemin ita har suma tayi.
   Kai wallahi duk a tsorace nake, domin kuwa bansan yadda nawa mijin zaizo mun ba.

   Ina cikin wannan tunanin ne naji yace mun bari ya shiga wanka ya futo.
    Gabana ya yanke ya fad'i naji kamar idan ya shiga na saka sakata na kulle shi a toilet d'in, amma kuma ina tsoron abunda zai b'ata masa rae, haka na zauna inata tunanin mafita, cikin wannan tunanin ne naji Faisal yana cewa Amarsu ta ango.

Nan ya kama min wasu zantuka marasa kan gado, kai dagaji kasan so yake kawai yamun wayoo, cikin raena  kawai sai salati nakeyi, ina ganinsa yaa dauko Ledar daya taho da ita, ya bud'e ya dauko kaza da lemo, ya tashi ya dauko kofi ya zuba mun fresh milk, sannan ya bud'e kazar ya d'auko cinyar kazar banso na ci ba, amma kuma saida ya shawo kaina na saki jiki harna ci kazar na k'oshi, daganan kuma sai ya tasheni yace muje muyi sallah bayan mun idar, ya kama goshina yayi addu'an da Annabin Rahma ya koyar damu ( S.A.W ) kafin mukayi godiya ga ubangijin mu daya had'amu a matsayin miji da mata, sannan muka rokeshi ya albarkace mu da iyalai na gari.
   Daga nan naga Faisal yatashi ya fara cire kayansa, ya rage dagashi sai gajeren wando da simgileti, duk abinnan da yake ina tsayee ina kallonsa, bayan ya gama ya kalleni sannan yace
"Nasan kin gajii dan haka ki shirya ki kwanta ki huta"

Wannan maganar daya fad'amin ba karamin tasiri tayi a zuciyata ba na saki jiki a ganina Faisal ya fuskanci na tsorata dashi ne sosai dan haka ba abinda zaimun yau, na cire kayana na saka kayan baccin dana taho dashi wanda marabansa da tsirara kad'anne, sannan na d'aga bargo na shiga.

Shima ya hau gadon yasa hannun ya kashe fitilar da take a kunne.
Inajin ya kashe wutan ya kwanta kusa dani nayi sauri  juya masa baya sannan na rufe idona da niyar nayi bacci, amma ina bacci yaki zuwa mun domin kuwa a hankali naji Faisal yana matsowa kusa dani ina matsawa wani abu naji mai tudu a kwankwaso, idan na zame sai naji ya kuma matsowa  a haka har mukazo karshen gado dole na tsaya anan.

   Faisal ya k'ara manna mun abunsa a mazaune na sannan yasa hannunsa ya rungume ni hanunsa duka biyu sunakan breast d'ina.
   Daganan naji ya zira hanun sa cikin riga ta ya kama kan ninple d'ina yana murzawa a hankali, bansan lokacin danace washhh ba, dan wani yanayi na tsinci kaina wanda ban tab'a jiba, ya tallafi breast d'in daga kasan su yana shafowa zuwa tsakiyar su.
    
Wani irin sambatu yakeyi yana sauk'e mun wani nishi a kunne na, duk ya rikita mun lissafi jikina ya fara tsima tsumin da nasha ya fara aiki, a take namanta da wani tsoro.
    Faisal ya juyo dani, sannan ya tubemin riga ta, nidai shiru nayi a zuciyata inaso a wuce wajen ya  cigaba da abunda yakeyi, ta wani bangaren kuma inajin tsoro.
   Ya sa breast d'ina a bakinsa ya fara musu wani salon tsotson daya haddasa mun kusan d'aukewan nunfashi, nishi kawai nake sakar masa dai-dai da hannuna nakasa motsawa, jikina ya mutu gaba daya, ina jinsa ya tub'emin ragowar kayan dake jikina, ya mayar dani nicket.

   Ganin halin danake ciki, nasa kafata na maste cinyata, amma Faisal baidamu ba sai yaci gaba da shan breast d'ina yana shafa jikina, a hankali ya gangaro har marata, ganin yadda yake shafaa marata a hankali cikin kwarewa yasa naji wani abu yana min yawo cikin k'asata.
    K'aik'ayi ya dameni ba shiri na bud'e k'afata sai jin hannun Faisal nayi a k'asata.

   Bansan lokacim dana furta wayyooo Allah na, duk na jike da ruwa, ganin haka da Faisal yayi yasa ya k'arasa cire boxers d'insa nan take naga wata mirtik'e k'iyar abu ta bayyana, a mulmule kamar an mulmula fulawa.

Gaba na ya yanke ya fad'i rasss atake tsoron da nakeji ya dawo, nasan yau tabbas ba makawa sai ya k'arb'a budurcina.
    Ban gama tunani ba naji Faisal  har ya hau kaina yana addu'an saduwa da iyali sai neman hanyar shiga yakeyi, duk ta inda ya tab'a zafi nakeji.
    yana cikin wannan laluben ne naji ya samu kofar shiga.
  wani zafi da rad'ad'i had'i da azaba suka had'u mun lokaci daya.
   A hankali ya danna mun harta kusan shigewa ciki, sannan ya faramun sama da k'asa.
   Wayyo Allah na rasa me nakejii a lokacin nan, zafine kaman wanda yake shirin rabani da duniyan gaba d'aya, duk iya k'ok'arina nakar nayi kuka amma abun ya gagara nasaki wani marayan kuka had'ida ihu.

  Kukan danake yi da ihuna na d'auka zaiji tausayina amma kuma ko kadan banga alamar tausayin ba kaman ihuna dad'a masa k'arfi yakeyi.
    Wasshhh a hankali nafara jin zafin yana dad'uwa nasaka hanuna na jawo shi da niyan ya kyaleni haka amma kaman hanun dana saka a jikinsa wani dad'i yake k'ara masa, nace wayyo Allah dama haka daran Amarci yake zuwa da azaba, inaga har yafi yadda k'awayena suke fad'a mun, ihu nakeyi kaman zan tsaga gidan amma Faisal besan inayiba.

Sumayyah da take d'akinta tafara bacci tajiyo ihun Safiyyah zumbur ta mik'e tun tanajin ihun da k'arfi har taji yaja baya sosai tasan kukane ya dusar da muryan, hanu ta d'aura akai tana kuka itama tana cewa Yaya Faisal dan Allah kabarta haka inba so kake ka kashemun itaba, wayyo Allah JININA yau kema kin d'and'ana kalan azaban danasha.

Faisal tun yanayi cikin hankalin sa har yadaina sanin abunda ya keyi saida ya kwashi kusan awa uku yana abu d'aya kafin ya barta ya koma gefe yana mayarda numfashi, yanaso ya jawota jikinsa yasaka mata albarka, amma sarawan da kansa yakeyi ya hanashi tab'uka komai dakyar ya iya tashi yana tafiya yana layi ya shiga toilet yaje ya tsarkake jikinsa, kafin ya d'ora towel a k'ugunsa ya futo yana zuwa tsakiyan falo ya fad'i ya suma a wajen, sanyin asubane ya farfad'o dashi, ya bud'e idonsa a hankali yana tunanin meyasa ya kwanta a k'asa, atake kwakwalwansa ta fara tuno masa abunda ya faru, yanaso ya tashi amma ya kasa dakyar yake iyajan jikinsa har yazo bakin gadon jallabiyan sa da gajeran wando ya saka daga kwancen da yake, yafara buga katakon gadon yana cewa Safiyyah tashi ki taimaka mun nayi sallah bazan iya tashi ba!

Jin dayayi bata bashi amsa ba yasa ya d'ago jikin sa a hankali ya duba idon ta hanu yasa da sauri akan hancinta yaji bata numbashi baisan lokacin daya iya tashi ba jiri na ibansa, sunan ta yake kira da k'arfi yana girgizata amma ba halamun nunfashi, ya ajiyeta ya futo yana dafa bango d'ayan hanun sa ya dafe kansa da yakeji kaman zata rabe biyu tsaban azaban ciwon da take masa a k'ofar d'akinsu yaga wata a tsugune yace baiwar Allah ki taimakeni kije kikiramun Ummana da Fa'izu yana gama fad'an haka ya fad'i a wajen ya suma.

Sumayyah da gudu ta futa a gidan......

🅿️93\94
Tanata tafiya a k'afa bata samu abun hawaba saida tayi nisa kafin wani mai Napep yazo zaiwuce ta tsaresa tace Malam bakin kura damuje mu d'auko wata mu dawo"

Yace d'ari shida zaki bayar, batace komai ba tashiga tace muje, gani take kaman basu tafiya tace dan Allah ka k'ara gudu marasa lafiya nake dasu a gida rae a hanun Allah.

Yanajin haka ya k'ara gudun keken, suna isa daidai Fa'izu shima ya iso k'ofan gidan kenan, yana ganinta ya fad'ad'a murmushinsa yana cewa kaman kinsan nadawo, yanzu ma nazo na fad'awa Umma zanje bikon matana Azarene, tace kajirani anan ina zuwa ta kwasa da gudu tayi cikin gidan takoyi sa'a tsakar gidan ba kowa, dama Goggo Larai da Ibrahim sai rana ta futo suke tashi sallan asuba, ta shige d'akin Umma ko sallamah ta kasayi Ummah tunda ta tashi takejin gabanta yana fad'uwa haka ta rarrafa tayi sallan asuba, bayan ta idar ta d'auki qur'ani tafara karantawa taji anshugo da gudu, ta tashi a falo sukayi karo da Sumayyah, tace lafiya na ganki da sassafen nan?

Bata iya bata amsa ba sai hanunta dataja tana cewa saka takalminki muje Umma, mai Keke Napep yana jiranmu a waje, cikin tashin hankali Umma take binta turus tayi da suka futo ganin Fa'izu a wajen tace wai meyake faruwa ne?

Fa'izu yace nima ina isowa naganta tacemun najirata anan" Sumayyah cewa take Umma muyi sauri karsu mutu" kafin tasaka kuka tanajan hanunsu.

Suna shiga mai Napep din yaja da gudu yazo daidai inda ya d'aukota yace Hajiyah ta ina zamu shiga? Takasa magana sai dahanu take nuna masa inda zasu har sukazo k'ofan gidan Fa'izu ya ciro gudan dubu ya bashi.

Umma da Fa'izu atare suka had'a baki wajen tambayan ta "gidan waye wannan Sumayyah?

Hanunsu ta sake ja tana gudu suma da gudu suka bita suka wuce first get sunzo second get Fa'izu ya tsayar da ita yace karkisa mushiga gidan mutane su tuhumemu, cikin kuka tace Umma wlh zasu mutu idan bamu k'arasa ba, Umma dayake tasan kwanan zance tace Fa'izu mushiga zaiyi magana Umma taja hanunsa Sumayyah ta dad'a kwasa da gudu suka rufa mata baya.

Suna zuwa k'ofan d'akin tace kungani har yanzu bai tashiba Umma ki shiga kiduba mun JININA inaga ta mutu.

Fa'izu a firgice ya d'aga d'an uwansa yana kiran sunansa ganin baya nunfashi ya saka kuka yace Umma baya nunfashi,  kiduba mun Umma wlh baya nunfashi.

Umma ji tayi kaman zata zube a wajen ta dubi Sumayyah data d'aura hanu akai tana kuka tace ina Safiyyan? Cikin d'akin ta nuna mata da hanu Umma ta shige da sauri, tana shiga taga Safiyyah kwance jikin jini da gudu ta k'arasa ta fara bata taimakon gaggawa toilet d'inta ta shiga taga ruwan zafi a tare a k'aton roba tadawo dakin tayi² ta d'aga ta takasa saida ta kira Sumayyah ta tayata suna sakata a cikin ruwan ta bud'e idonta a razane saboda wani azaban zafi dataji a k'asanta, tana ganin Umma tasaka kuka tana cewa Umma Yaya Faisal baida tausayi ya kusan kasheni"

Sumayyah itama cikin kuka tace JININA shima yana falo a sume baisan waye a kansa ba!

Atake kukanta ya d'auke tace Sumayyah ki d'auko waya kikira mun Bappa kice masa Yaya Faisal zai mutu yabarni, Sumayyah ta tafi falo dan anan taga jakanta saida ta zazzage jakan gaba d'aya kafin ta d'auko wayan ta koma ciki.

Umma ita kad'ai tasan abunda takeji a zuciyar ta, kawaici ne kawai irinna manyan mu yasa ta zauna jinyan Safiyyah amma hankalinta yanakan d'anta, Sumayyah tana shigowa ta kira number Bappa bayan sun gaisa da Sumayyah, Bappa baijira jin mai zatace ba yace mata wani hali Faisal d'in yake yanzu? Tace Bappa ya suma har yanzu bai farfad'o ba"

Yace ina Safiyyah? Tace gatanan tana jinka Bappa"

Yace ta d'auki sauran maganin data shafa a jikinta taje tashafa masa insha Allahu zai tashi, amma ta tabbar tayi alola kuma inzata shafa masa tayi bissimillah, sannan kukira ma'aifiyarsa ita zata shafa masa a fuskansa"

Sumayyah tace to Bappa mungode, zamu kiraka idan sunyi, sukayi sallamah kafin Umma ta tashi tace Safiyyah daure kiyi wanka sai kiyi alola kifuto ko, Sumayyah ita ta tayata tayi wanka kafin ta tsarkake jikinta tafuto Umma dakanta tasaka mata kaya tace tayi sallah bata musanta mata ba tayi sallah tana idarwa ta dubi Sumayyah tace rik'eni na tashi nad'auko maganin muje kafin ta k'araso Umma tazo ta d'agata ta d'auko maganin, dakyar take iya d'aga k'afanta har suka isa falon, ganinsa tayi kaman gawa akan kujera tuni ta manta da zafin data keji a k'asanta, ta k'arasa da gudu tana girgizasa, Umma tace kibar kukan haka karya dad'a miki zazzab'i.

Ta share hawayen ta kafin ta kunce ledan maganin ta kallin Fa'izu da Sumayyah kafin tayi magana Sumayyah ta gane mai take nufi, ta kama hanun Fa'izu tace muje waje za'a shafa masa magani ne.

Suna futa Umma ta tayata suka cire masa rigansa kafin ta shafa masa maganin a dukka jikinsa, tace Umma mumayar masa da rigan kafin kishafa masa, dan nasan yana farfad'owa Sumayyah zasu shigo, suka mayar masa kafin Umma ta karb'a maganin har zata shafa masa saita tashi taje tayi alola tayi addu'a kafin ta d'auki maganin hanunta yana b'ari tayi bissimillah tafara shafa masa idonta yana zubar da hawaye tana gamawa ta koma gefe tare da k'ura masa ido a hankali ya fara bud'e idonsa yana salati Umma ya fara gani da sauri ya tashi yana cewa Ummana Safiyyah bata nunfashi ki shiga kidubamun ita Umma ina sonta karta mutu tabarni.

Umma batasan lokacin data rungume d'anta ba tace Allah nagode ma daka nunamun wannan ranan, kallon dataga yana matane yasata yin shuru da sauri Fa'izu da Sumayyah suka shigo, yace Fa'izu kana garine inaso nakiraka nacema nagina mana gida kazo ka zab'a yadda zaka zauna amma bansan meya hanani kiranka ba, shima dagudu yaje ya rungume d'an uwansa yana kuka Faisal yace kukan mekukeyi ne kaida Umma naji sai godiya kukewa Allah daya nuna muku wannan ranan meya faru yau d'in?

Sumayyah itama taje ta zube ajikin Safiyyah tana kuka, wani k'ara Safiyyah ta sake wanda ya haddasa mata suma dan ba k'aramun kama mata likinta Sumayyah tayiba tsalle d'aya Faisal yayi ya ganshi a gabanta ya d'agata yana cewa Umma dama nace miki bata da lafiya muje nakaita asibiti, tana hanunsa yaje ya d'auko kin motansa da wayarsa suka futa dikansu, suna zuwa asibiti doctor ya fara bata taimakon gaggawa bewani sha wahala ba ta farfad'o kafin ya futo yace akwai wani ubun farin ciki ko akasin haka daya sata ta suma, amma yanzu ta dawo daidai zaku iya shiga kuganta, Faisal suka masa godiya kafin suka shiga, Umma kuma tabi bayan likitan ta zauna tace likita inaso adubamun lafiyan k'asanta domin jiya shine daranta na farko da mijinta"

Likitan yace ba komai yanzu zan turo nurse mace ta dubamun ita, koda aka dubata nurse tace gabanta robane irin wanda idan angama sex dasu suke komawa su rufe ba abunda ya sameta! Sai yanzu hankalin Umma ya kwanta.

Faisal yana zaune jikin matan sa Umma ta tafi gida dan ta dafa mata abunda zataci idan ta farka, Fa'izu ya kalli Sumayyah yayi kalan tausayi da fuskansa kafin yace kizo muje akwai maganan da nakeso muyi dake, da kaman bazata bishi ba ganin Yaya Faisal yana kallonsu yasa ta tashi ta futa tana turo baki.

Suna futa Faisal ya k'urawa Safiyyah ido yana murmushi dan yasan yayi sa'an mata, Safiyyah farace doguwa gata da diri,daga samanta ya tsuke gasan yayi fad'i, irin wanda ake cemusu coca cola shape, na shanunta a tsaye suke, bakinta d'an k'arami gata da dogon hanci, gashinta har gadon bayanta, gata da sexy eyes, duk lafiyayyan namijin daya ganta dole saiya sauk'a a layi, kana ganinta kaga bafulatanan asali, take yafara jin kishin likitan daya dubata, yace yanzu haka duk ya kallen mun suran mata yayi tsaki mtss, kafin ya gyara mata rufuwa yana shafa fuskanta a hankali yanama Allah godiya daya bata ikon kawo masa budurcin ta, ba kaman Zainab ba kaman dusa yana ambatan sunan Zainab yaji kaman akwai abunda yayita faruwa dashi a baya ya dafe kansa kwakwalwansa yana tariyo masa shekara d'aya data wuce.


Zainab suna tsaka da fasik'ancin su taji wayanta yayi k'ara ta dakatar da Ibrahim da sauri, taduba wayan sai tayi tsaki tace nasan wannan iskancin MTN ne suka turomun sak'o da number Faisal, tace cigaba da gashi Ibrahim.

Kwana sukayi suna abu d'aya saida asuba suka sarara wa junansu take bacci ya d'aukesu a wajen, basu farka ba sai k'arfe goma sha d'ayan safiya, suna farkawa ko wanke fuska basuyi ba tafara laluman Ibrahim tana cewa d'an hanuna mu sakeyi ko sau ukune saika tafi, nasan hala Faisal ya shigo yaganmu akan network ne ya futa ya kwanta a mota!

Ibrahim ya zaro ido yace kina ganin bazaice zai d'auki mataki ba? Tayi dariya kafin tace aiko da uwarsa nake kwance bai isa yace mun komaiba ballan tana dakai, nidai taso namatsu nasake jinka akaina, shima ya tashi yace kaman kinsa bata kwanta ba, sai wajen k'arfe d'aya Ibrahim yabar gidan akan zai dawo da daddare sukwana tare.

Wayarta ta d'auka takira number Faisal lokacin Safiyyah ta farka kenan yana lallab'ata tasha tea kafin Umma tadawo yaga kiran Zainab ya shigo wayansa, yayi tsaki ya kawar da wayan tasake kira cikin sanyin murya Safiyyah tace ka d'auka mana ko akwai abunda takeso ta fad'a maka.

Badan yaso ba ya d'aga, tanajin ya d'aga tace Faisal uwarka waye a garinnan dazan kiraka baka d'agamun  waya ba?

Idon ya zaro waje kafin ya kashe wayan yana kallon Safiyyah da itama shi take kallo yace......

🅿️95\96
Yace kinji abunda ta fad'a?

Ta girgiza masa kai kafin tace "ta ina zanji"

Yace Ummana fa ta zaga, dan Allah Safiyyah ki bani labarin abunda ya faru dani abaya, jiya nayi mummana mafarki, wanda bana fatan Allah yasa ya faru a rayuwata, nayi mafarkin na walak'anta Fa'izu da Ummana da gaskene.

Tayi shuru dan tarasa me zatace masa, bataso yaji komai daga bakinta tafison ya tuna da kansa, ya kama kafad'anta yace dan Allah kifad'a mun gaskiyan abunda ya faru dani!

Cikin kukan shagwab'a tace washhh honey ka kamamun ciwona tafara yarfe hanunta tana kuka, da sauri ya saketa yanata faman jera mata sannu, ita kuma sai kukan shagwaba take masa.

Umma tana futa a asibitin  saida tabiya gidan Faisal ta wanke musu zanin gadon da suka b'ata, kafin tabiya kasuwa tasiya kayan ciki mai yawa ta nufi gida tanajin farin ciki a zuciyar ta, a tsakar gida taci karo da Goggo Larai, tana ganinta ta taso da sauri tana cewa munafukan banza wato kin futa da yaran nan dan karnasa Faisal ya saketa to bari kiji duk inda kika kaita kinyi a banza domin yau d'innan zansa ya saketa! Umma tayi murmushi kawai ta wuce domin so take tayi tagama takaiwa Safiyyah farfesu kafin ta farka, a gurguje tayi tagama tayi wanka ta futo ta tafi tabar Goggo Larai tanata zage zagenta.

Fa'izu tunda suka futo yaketa lallab'a Sumayyah akan ta yafe masa yace "Sumayyah nagane kurena bayan kin tafi kinbarni nasha wahala sosai, wlh ina miki sonda bansan adadinsa ba,  kiyi hakuri kiyafe mun"

Zuciya da abunda takeso take ta rungume mijinta tace nima ka yafe mun mujina! Dad'a rungumeta yayi yace nagode matana Allah ya miki albarka, kuma akwai albishir d'in dazan miki idan mun koma guda.

Ta tallafe fuskansa da hanunta duka biyu tace nima inada albishirin dazan maka, nasan saikafi jin dad'insa fiye da wanda zaka bani" Umma a wannan yanayin tazo ta samesu taji dad'i sosai ganin komai ya gyaru mata a lokaci d'aya, suna ganinta sukayi saurin sake juna Sumayyah ta sinkuyar da kanta, shiko Fa'izu dayake namijine ko'a jikinsa, ya tashi ya karb'a kulolin hanunta, kafin suka nufi d'akin.

Safiyyah tanata zuba mishi shagwab'a sukaji an bud'e k'ofan d'akin suka juya a tare Umma ce dasu Fa'izu suka shigo a tare da sallamah a bakin su, Umma tana ganin Safiyyah a zaune taje da sauri ta rungumeta tanata faman mata sannu tare da saka mata albarka, Safiyyah ta sunkuyar da kanta k'asa dan sai yanzu takejin kunyan Umma.

Umma da kanta ta zuba mata farfesun kayan cikin tayita lallab'ata har tasha da yawa.

Kiran Zainab ne ya sake shigowa wayan ya d'aga tare da k'ara muryan wayan tace Faisal ni sa'ar uwarkace dazanyi ta kiranka bakazo ba, nabaka minti biyar kazo idan ka kuskura kawuce haka wlh sainayi mummunan sab'a maka kittt ta kashe wayanta.

Ido ya zubawa su Umma yana cewa dan Allah kucemun abunda ya faru mafarkine ban walak'anta kuba Ummana!

Umma da Fa'izu suka rungumesa idonsu na zubar da hawaye, Fa'izu yace koda ya faru da gaskema mun yafe maka domin munsan ba a hayyacinka ka aikata komai ba, yanzu inaso kamanta abunda ya faru a baya mu fuskanci gaba kawai.

Ya sakesu yace tabbas Zainab tayi babban kuskure data sakani na walak'anta Uwata da d'an uwana, bazan barta ba saina nuna mata kuskuranta ya kama hanya zai futa cikin fushi Umma ta rik'e hanunsa tace "Faisal karka yanke hukunci cikin fushi kabari idan ka tabbatar da abunda kake zargi saika yanke duk hukuncin dakaga ya dace, yanzu kayi jinyan Safiyyah bai kamata ka tafi kabarta a wannan halinba domin tafi buk'atanka fiye da kowa.

Ya juyo ya zauna a kujeran da yake jikin gadon,  ganin haka yasa Umma suka futa a d'akin, Safiyyah naganin futansu ta gaggaro kusa dashi ta d'aura kanta a kafad'ansa  tana shafa jikinsa da hanunta a hankali take sauk'e masa nunfashi a kunne hakan datayi kuwa tayi nasaran mantar dashi daga kuncin da zuciyarsa ta shiga ya tashi yahau kan gadon ya jawota jikinsa ya matseta saida ta saki k'ara ya dubeta da idonsa da yayi jajazur yace na fama miki ciwo ko?

Tace a'a naga kana fushi dani ne"

Ya rungumeta yace bana fatan Allah ya nunamun ranan da zanyi fushi dake a rayuwata, ya had'e bakinsu waje d'aya yana shafa jikinta ta ko ina nishi suke sauk'ewa  a tare, k'ok'arin rabata da rigan jikinta yakeyi tayi saurin janye jikinta tace Yaya Faisal asibiti ne fa!

Sake jawota yayi yace haramun ne idan natara da matana a asibiti ya cigaba da shafata yana wani irin gurnani, ya kwantar da ita kafin ya haye kanta yana k'ok'arin tura hanunsa k'asan ta, ganin inta barshi zai dad'a tab'o mata lik'in da yanzu take jinyansa kawai saita saka mishi kuka tana yarfe hanu, d'agata yayi ya koma kan kujeran yana mayar da nunfashi, ta cigaba da kukanta, hanunta ya kamo amma ya kasa magana sai juya mata kai yakeyi halamun tayi shuru.

Likita ya shugo ya dubata kafin yace zaku iya tafi domin naga ta samu sauk'i ya mik'awa Faisal hanu yace Alh ad'an d'aga mata k'afa na kwana biyu domin tasamu tayi recovering.

Faisal ya d'aure fuskansa kafin yace mungode zaka iya futa domin musamu mu shirya muma.

Bayan sun koma gida Umma ta sake gasata sosai, ya shigo ya sameta a kan gado yace Sweet honey zan futa amma bazan jima ba zan dawo, ta d'aga masakai dan batason zamansa kusa da ita.

Yana futa ya nufi gidansa da suke zaune da Zainab yana shiga ya samu Zainab tana zaune a falo tana ganinsa tafara girgiza k'afa tana cika tana batsewa, ko kulata baiyi ba yaje zai wuce ta daka masa tsawa kafin tace zonan d'an wawiya.

Mamakine ya dawo dashi ba tsawanta ba, tace waye kai waye uwarka a garin nan da harzan kiraka kak'i d'aga wayata?

Ya dubeta kafin yayi murmushi dan sai yanzu ya tabbatar da abunda yake tsargi yace "ina wajen matana bata da lafiya kinsan jiya tare muka kwana namanta da ita cikekkiyar budurwace ni kuma na mata na manya na shigeta da k'arfi, dalilin daya haddasa mata suma kenan har muka kwanta a asibiti Ummane da Fa'izu sukayita jinyan ta.

A razane ta tashi kafin ta shak'e wuyan sa tana cewa shike nan ka gama dani Faisal ta wanka masa mari  tace waya baka izinin shiga d'akin munafukan uwarka harka kwanta da aljanan yarinyan nan, kamanta mena cemaka ko, yau ba wanda ya isa ya hanani kwak'ule maka idonka, naga da idon dazaka ganta gaba.

Ranshi ne yaji yayi mungun b'aci daya tabbatar da gaske tasa ya walak'anta Umman sa da Fa'izu, cikin k'unan rai ya hankad'eta ta kifu a k'asa kafin yaje ya rufe gidan wayan wuta mai kauri ya d'auko ya fara zaneta dashi tana cewa dole na koma waje boka ya dad'a mallakemun kai,  wlh indan ka sake shigowa hanuna Faisal har uwarka saina zane, bai kulata taba saima dukanta daya cigaba dayi tun tana gane waye a kanta harta daina fahinta ta suma dukda haka baikyaleta ba saida yaga ko ina a jikinta yana zubar da jini kafin ya wurgar da bulalan,  yace bazan sakeki ba Zainab saina gama a zaftar dake a gidan nan tukunnan ya kulleta a d'akin kafin ya juya ya tafi

Yabarta kwance cikin jini bata nunfashi.


Yana futa gidan su ya nufa ya samu Goggo Larai a k'ofan d'akinta tana zaune tana jiran shugowan su Umma, tana ganinsa ta fad'ad'a fara'anta tana cewa sannu da zuwa d'an albarka tun jiya dama nake jiranka kazo ka yarda kwallon mangoro ka huta.

Gaisheta yayi kaman lokacin da asiri yake jikinsa yace waye gwallon mangoro kuma Goggo?

Goggo Larai tace waye kuwa in badda matar daka auro mai kama da aljanu, so nake ka rubuta mata saki uku tunda ta zab'i zama da Umma Hajara ta karya dokan Zainab, kowa yasan Umma Hajara ba k'aramar tantiriyar mak'iyiyarka bace, dan haka nabaka  nanda minti uku ka rubuta mata takaddan saki uku.

Tsayawa yayi yana kallonta ya rasa wani irin kalan hukunci zai mata ba, ya kalleta sosai kafin ya d'aga k'afansa ya.....

🅿️97\98
Ya d'aga k'afa zai nausheta saiya mayar dashi dan yaga hakan be dace ba koba komai matar ubansa ne, ya girgiza kai ya dubeta yace nabarki da Allah Goggo dan shikad'e zai sakamun abunda kikamun" ya juya yayi fucewar sa yana takaicin san zuciya irinta matar ubansu.

Goggo Larai baki ta sake tana mamakin meya canjashi daga jiya zuwa yau, afili tace koma meye dole musake d'aukan mataki dan bazan zuba ido naga ka koma wajen uwarka ba, ni kuma abarni da hamma waneni dole nakira Zainab tazo mukoma wajen Boka.

Hafsat tana shirya yaranta zasu tafi school, sauri² take aikinta dan tasamu ta tafi wajen Boka, bayan tagama shiryasu Baban su ya d'aukesu suka futa, tayi wanka a gurguje, Ta saka kaya kenan taji sallaman bak'uwa tsaki tayi kafin ta amsa ta futo, wata budurwa ta gani a tsaye a k'ofan d'akinta tace baiwar Allah lafiya?

Salma tayi murmushi tace "lafiyan ne ya kawo haka, mijinki yana yawan yabamun halinki dacewa ke matar kwarai ne, kin iya karb'an bak'i, sai kuma naga akasin haka"

Hafsa ta zaro ido waje tace "idan na fahinci abunda kike fad'a ke budurwan mijina ne?

Salma tace zanso kigyara fahintarki, niba budurwa mijinki bane a yanzu, amma nice matar dazai aura da, nice wanda aka fasa auranmu biki saura sati d'aya nasan kinsan da hakan.

Hafsa tace aiho kicemun kece wanda nayi nasaran ruguza auran ku, yanzu kuma dame kikazo mun?

Salma tace dazaki bani izinin shiga muyi maganan a zaune inaga da hakan zaifi dan yanzu munruga da mun zamo d'aya" saida ta k'are mata kallo tun daga sama har k'asa kafin tace zaki iya shiga na yau d'aya, amma batun mun zamo d'aya kam babu shi.

Salma tayi murmushi kafin tace hakamma nagode, kafin suka shiga ciki kowa ya nemi waje ya zauma, bayan sun zauna Salma ta dubeta tace awaje naji kince mun ke kikasa aka fasa auran mu, hakan yana nufin ke kikasa masa ciwo kenan?

Hafsa bata fahinci abunda take nufiba tace "karya ciwo ya muku kenan, to bari kiji bayida ciwon komai, nice dai ban shirya zama da kishiya ba, kin ganni nan wlh idan Jafar zaije neman aure sau dubu ni kuma nayita ruguzawa kenan.

Salma tace "amma kin cuci kanki Hafsa kinjawo wa kanki kishiryar waje, wannan karan malamin ki beyi nasaran wargaza auran mu ba, kinsan duk aure ana gwaji kafin a d'aura anmana gwajin komai bamuda matsala sai akace mana sauran na RVS dayake dukammu bamusan me hakan yake nufiba, mukace bamuso shiyasa muka ciresa a jerin gwajin mu, bayan mundawo gida da murnana na shiga nake nunawa Yayuna angama test bamu da matsalan komai.

Babban Yayanah ya k'arba ya duba yace aibaku gamaba tukunna ki kiramun shi muje DAMOP ayi d'ayan daya rage nakirasa yace mun ai yana waje be tafiba tukunna, muna zuwa akaja jininmu bayan result ya futo aka kiramu ciki Yayana yabimu sukace bazasu b'oye mana ba auran mu bazai yiyuba domin Jafar yana d'auke ciwo mai karya garkuwan jiki, shiyasa kika bani tausayi danaji kece silan fad'awansa halaka harya kamun da wannan ciwon.

Hafsa zufa taji yana gangarowa daga kowani k'ofan jikinta, tacire d'ankwalinta tace baiwar Allah idan kinzo ki tsorata ni danna barki ki auri mijina ne kifad'amun wlh zan yarda ya aureki, amma dan Allah kicemun wasa kikemun Jafar baya d'auke da ciwon komai.

Salma ta tashi tace kidubeni dakyau kema kinsan nafi k'arfin nabiyo kishi har gida dan tabari na auri mijinta, wlh Hafsa natsaneki domin kece kikayi silan rabani da farin cikin rayuwata, dabadan kin hanasa k'ara aureba nasan da bazai tab'abin matan banza ba dan kowa yama Jafar kyakykyawan sheda kaddarace da rashin sa'an mace tagari ya jefasa cikin wannan yanayin, Hafsa tazo zata rik'e k'afanta tana kuka, Salma tayi saurin bugeta ta futa a gidan tanajin tsanan Hafsa a ranta.

Bayan yakai yaran makaranta ya dawo gida domin ya karya ya tafi wajen aiki, ya samu Hafsa tanata kuka kaman wanda aka aiko mata da mutuwa yazo da sauri ya d'agata yace lafiya Hafsa waye ya mutu?

Ta kwace jikinta tace nika rabu dani, Jafar ka cuceni ka cuci rayuwata dama cin amanata kakeyi ban saniba, ta shak'e wuyansa tace mena maka Jafar ka sakamun ciwon k'anjamau?

Rabata yayi daga jikinsa ransa a b'ace yace Hafsa har kina da bakin dazaki kirani fasik'i, idan kin manta barin tuna miki, muna kwance da daddare kinzaci ina bacci naji kina waya Goggo akan tasaka a wargaza aurena da Jamila tace miki boka yace idan anfasa auran tozanbi matan banza kikace kinyarda, nayi shuru nabarki ne azatona ba abunda zai iyasa nafasa aurena, amma saida kikayi nasara tun lokacin natsinci kaina dason sex da matan banza cikin ikon Allah da k'arfin addu'a abun yabar jikina nafara neman auran Salma, anan nagane nakamu da ciwon HIV bisa k'azamun rayuwar danayi abaya, dukda nasan kece sila amma nazuba miki ido kina tacin amana ta dake da bokan matan Yayanki, yanzu zab'i ya rage naki kishiryu muzauna lafiya, idan kuma bazaki iyaba kiyi gaba zansamu mai ciwo daidaini na aureta, yana gama fad'an haka yayi fuce wansa a gidan gaba d'aya.

Hafsa ta kifa kanta a k'asa tana kukan dana sani, sai yanzu take takaicin samun uwa irin Goggo Larai, yanzu ta gane Balkisu tafita hankali da wayo dataje ta mannu da Umma gashi rayuwarta ya mata kyau tana cikin kwanciyan hankali a gidan auran ta.

Murmushi tayi data tuna Zainab ma yanzu haka tana d'auke da cutan k'anjamau tace ban tab'a zinaba ina budurwa amma da aurena Zainab kika sakani a hanyan b'ata gashi bani kad'e na cutuba kema kincuci kanki, Allah ka karya alkadarinta Yaya Faisal ya dawo hayyacinsa yafara lura da Umma.


Bayan futan Faisal, Umma tace ya kamata natafi gida tunda ta warware Sumayyah tunda gidanku d'aya sai kina lura da ita.

Tace to Umma mungode Allah ya k'ara girma da d'aukaka, ta rakata tahau abun hawa kafin ta dawo, Fa'izu yace muma saimu tafi gidanmu tunda mujinta zai dawo anjima, nima sai muje ki kula dani ko?

Tace ainan ne gidan mu yanzu"

Yace ban fahinci abunda kike nufiba, hanunsa ta kama takai shi har flat din dayake mallakinsu kafin tace ga d'akinka da Yaya Faisal ya gina maka, shiya zuba mana komai a ciki, baki ya bud'e amma ya kasa magana ta cigaba dacewa ba iya wannan bama nan bada dad'ewa ba zaka dawo Abba insha Allahu ta kama hanunsa tana shafawa a cikinta daya fara futowa.

Dawowa yayi kaman mutum mutumi ko motsawa ya kasayi saida ta girgizashi tace kana tare dani kuwa?

Kuka ya fashe dashi yace Sumayyah abun dad'in yamun yawa ga gida ga ciki gashi ammayar dani aiki harda k'arin girma tabbas Annabi yayi gaskiya dayace idan ka godewa Allah shikuma saiya k'ara maka duk wanda ya yarda da jarabawan ubangiji tabbas zaiga alheri me tarin yawa a rayuwarsa, ya rungumeta yana murna, zuwa wani lokaci kad'an kid'an ya canja rawa, wunin yau sun farantawa junansu sosai, Sumayyah cire kunya tayi ta nuna masa itama tayi kewansa, sai bayan isha suka futo direct shashen Safiyyah suka nufa, afalo suka samesu Faisal yanata lallab'ata taci ferfesu, yana ganinsu yace kujuya bama buk'atan ku, Sumayyah ashe bazan iya baki amanar sweet honey na ba, kuka tafi kukabar mun ita a daki ita kad'e bamai jinyanta kuje nayi fushi daku.

Fa'izu ya dubi fuskansa yaga da gaske yake maganan dan fuskansa a d'aure yake,  yazo ya zauna kusa dashi yace dakasan halinda nashiga bazakace mun maka laifiba.

Ya dubi Safiyyah yace kifad'a gaskiya Aunty na bake kika boyemun mata na tsawan wata biyuba sai yau naganta"

Safiyyah ta harare shi tace honey kaji zasumun sharri ko, shifa ya koreta shine tazo wajena tun ranan da aka yanke bikinmu shine tace mishi na b'oye ta, cikin shagwab'a take maganan.

Shagala yayi da kallonta harta gama maganan kafin yayi k'arfin halin cewa kutashi ku tafi karkusa mun sweet honey na kuka.

Sumayyah tace JININA bance masa ke kika b'oyeni ba kawai ya fad'ane dan kiyi fushi dani, aini tafiyan yamun rana tunda gashi dana dawo sai b'are² yakeyi yana fad'amun kalaman soyayyah suka saka dariya dukansu, daganan sai hira ya b'alle tsakanin su nan Fa'izu yake sanar dasu an mayar dashi aiki"

Faisal yace da basu mayar dakai ba bazan tab'a yafewa kaina ba, sai wajen sha d'ayan dare suka tafi suka kwanta, Faisal ma d'aukan ta yayi yaje ya gasata sosai kafin sukazo suka kwanta duk a tsorace take tazaci zai mata wani abune.

Ya fahinci a tsorace take dashi yace sweet honey kiyi baccin ki ba abunda zan miki, ya rungumeta man ajikinsa bacci mai dad'i ya d'aukesu.


Ibrahim yana ganin dare yayi ya futa yanufi gidan Zainab yana shiga yaga k'ofan d'akinta a rufe amma ambar ki a jiki ya murza ya shiga kwance yaganta acikin mawuyacin hali, ya taimaka mata ta gasa  jikinta tace nagode Ibrahim zaka iya tafiya!

Yace waye zai tafi haba Zainab ai zaki tausayamun kinsan kalan tanadin danayiwa yau kuwa, tunda na koma nakeshan maganin maza wanda zai k'aramun k'arfi, idan natafi ya kikeso nayi yau ko mutuwa kikeso nayi?

Tace Ibrahim bakaga halinda nake ciki bane kayi hakuri idan na warke sai mucigaba yanzukam ba hali"

Ke kike ganin ba hali nikam naga halin da idona haka ya gurjeta son ransa tana ihu amma ko tausayinta baijiba sai wajen asuba ya sarara mata ya tashi yasaka kayansa yace saina sake dawowa da daddare, yayi tafiyan shi yabarta ciki azaba, ga tsamin jiki ga turmusan Ibrahim.


Shurunsu ya fara damun G........


Mmn Ashfaq

DAGA ALKALAMIN
Nabeeha Aminu ( Mmn Ashfaq )





🌐 HAJOW 🌐
Hakuri da juriya online writer's.


*Albishirin ku ma'abota bibiyan littafin mu! Shahararrun marubutan nan wanda suka saba nishad'artar da fad'akar daku akoda yaushe NABEEHA AMINU ( Mmn Ashfaq ) da Aminiyan ta NABEELA AMINU ( 'Yar mutan Dikkawa ) sun tak'ark'are sun zak'ulo muku wani labari mai tab'a zuciyar mai karantawa had'i da tausayi da zallan soyayyah karku bari abaku labari kubibiye littafinmu BASU SAN WAYE NI BA dan kumore karatunku.*

Bissimillahi Rahmanin Rahim.

Yafara damun Goggo Larai gashi kota kira wayan Zainab bata samu inta kira Hafsa kuma bata d'auka, ta yanke shawaran zuwa da kanta ta dubasu kawai, gidan Hafsa ta fara zuwa Hafsa najin sallamanta ta rufe k'ofanta ba irin bugun dabatayi ba tak'i zuwa ta bud'e mata, ta futa tana mamakin meya yake faruwa da Hafsa ne kotana kwance da mijinta ne shiyasa data jita ta rufe k'ofan gudun kar ta shiga tagansu a wani hali.

Daga nan tawuce gidan Zainab shima a rufe tasamu k'ofan tace gantalalliya kedai Zainab kin shiga uku da yawon bin maza a haka zaki k'are dama barewa bayayi gudu d'ansa yayi rarrafe ba duk abunda kikeyi uwarki kika gada,  kuma ayawon zaku d'auko abunda zai kasheku, zanso kikaini wajen bokan ku wlh har ke saina mallaka naci ubanku dan kema ba sonki nakeyi ba inaso na mallakeku ne kawai, ta juya ta futa tanata fad'e².

Zainab da take zaune a falo taji duk abunda Goggo Larai take fad'a tace wato shigo² ba zurfi matan nan take mun     Lallai kin tafka Babban kuskure kema kin shiga kaidina Larai bari na daidaita tsakanina da Faisal, sainayi maganinku dukan ku, zan mallaki komai na Faisal kafin nasa boka ya kashe mun shi kawai na huta.

Safiyyah tuni ta cire fargaban Faisal zai mata wani abu,  tasake jikin ta tayi bacci cikin kwanciyan hankali, sai jitayi yana tashin ta yana cewa asuba tayi, tace gaskiya na gaji dan bansan bacci ya dauke ni ba, ashe har asuba tayi! Bayan yaje masallaci ya dawo, yazo ya tarar dani ina sallah sanye nake da zundumemen hijab, amma ciki pant ne kawai, bayan na idar da sallah na koma kan gado na kwanta na juya bayana amma saida Faisal ya matso kusa dani a hakankali najii yana jan hijabina kasa sai ji nayii ya daga kafata sama nace wayyoo Asshhhh Faisal zafi ban warkeba, domin kuwa wannan karon jinsa nayi har cikin mahaifa ta ihu kawai nakeyi, shikuma sakar min jikinsa yake gaskiya Faisal akwai zafi sosai
    Shiko ko kulani baiyi ba, duk cikin marata yadau ciwo nayi kuka harna gaji Faisal kuwa saida yagaji dan kansa yabarni.
   na kwanta kamar matacciya na bude kafata domin iska tana ratsa ni a haka bacci ya d'aukeni, ya rungumeta yanata saka mata albarka, yace sai yanzu nasan me ake kira aure, idan mutane nace mun sex yana da dad'i wlh nazaci k'arya sukeyi kawai suna fad'ane saiyau da Allah ya had'ani da mar'atus salihan kafin na yarda da zancen, ya shafa kanta yace kiyi hak'uri Safiyyah banaso ina takura miki amma ba yadda zan iyane ni'imarki ya hanani yanani hak'uri dake.

Faisal ya manta da wata halitta Zainab soyayyan su sukesha da Safiyyan sa kowa nunawa d'an uwansa kulawa yakeyi kaman zasu mayar da junansu ciki.

Yau suka shirya dukansu suka kaiwa Umma ziyara murna wajen Umma ba'acewa komai tarasa mezata basu suci duk abunda yake d'akin saita d'auko musu, nan Fa'izu yake sanar dasu andawo dashi Bauchi da aikinsa sukayita murna, a gidan suka wuni sallane kawai yake futar dasu, suna zaune dukansu suna hira a tsakar gida, saiga Goggo Larai ta shigo wani wawan ashar ta buga tace yau mezan gani Faisal kaine a gaba  uwarka? Wani irin sake Zainab tayi haka!

Fa'izu ya tashi yaje gabanta yace Goggo kinaja da ikon Allah kenan! To kisa a ranki bakiga komaiba nasan Allah bazai barki hakaba dole saikin girb'a abunda kika shuka"

Faisal yace "Fa'izu dawo ka zauna bai kamata kana kula kowace mahaukaciya ba zomu zauna da ma'aifiyarmu muji d'iminta"

Sumayyah ce ta kawar da maganan da basu labarin rigansu nan sukayita dariya sukabar Goggo Larai da sambatu a wajen, suna cikin hira kiran Bappa ya shigo wayan Safiyyah ta zaro ido waje tace nashiga uku mezance masa?

Umma tace waye?

"Bappa nane" ta bata amsa.

Ta mik'awa Sumayyah wayan tace dan Allah ki d'aga kice masa bana kusa, Sumayyah ta d'aga kafin ta gaishe shi, yace ina Safiyyan nasan tak'i d'aga wayana neko! Kice mata mijinta yace mun tayi ciwone shiyasa nakeso naji ya jikinta.

Sumayyah ta kasa bashi amsa ta sakawa Safiyyah wayan a kunnenta, cikin rawar murya ta gaishe da Bappanta, yace Safiyyah lokacin da kike nemawa mijinki maga bakijin kunyana sai yanzu, to kisake kinamun magana kaman yadda muke, ba kunya tsakanin mu kinji?

Tace eh Bappa nagode kafin suka tab'a hira yabawa Inna wayan suka gaisa ta sakewa Innan ta sosai suka jima suna hira kafin sukayi sallamah, duk cikin fulatanci suke maganan.

Tana ajiye wayan Faisal cikin wasa yace ban yarda ana fulatanci a kusa damu ba kawai saikina canja harce idan kuna waya, koda yake laifin Umma ne da bata koya mana ba.

Umma tace lokacin ina nake da nutsuwan koya muku yare, sukayi dariya kafin Umma tace Safiyyah kidaina kunyan ma'aifinki haka bazaiji dad'iba, duk da bamu fatan wani abu ya sake faruwa gaba, kinga idan mukace ya taimakemu bazai yarda ba.

Faisal yace Umma wani taimako ya mana wanda yasa Safiyyah takejin kunyan sa, bata b'oye masa komai ba ta masa bayani, yaji son Safiyyah da iyayenta ya sake shiga ransa, ya sake tsanan Zainab da duk wani abu daya dangance ta, yace bazai sake faruwa bama wannan d'inma yana cikin k'addarata ne.


Hafsa tayi kukan harta gaji yau tasamu mijinta ta nemi gafaransa, shima ya nemi gafaranta suka yafewa juna, tun ranan suka dawo da zaman lafiya gidansu dangin mijinta har mamakin sauya wanta sukeyi, ana haka Balkisu ta aihu ranan sunan ta shirya ta tafi gidan suna suka had'u dasu Safiyyah suka gaisa suma ta nemi gafaransu, Balkisu ta dubeta tace 'yar uwata meya sauyaki haka naga kinyi laushi ne!

Hafsa tayi shuru idonta ya fara kawo kwalla tace "Balkisu nacuci kaina, nakai kaina na baro, nan ta basu labarin komai, ta cigaba da cewa yanzu damuwata d'aya  Allah yasa Zainab batasawa Faisal k'anjamau ba, dan dukammu muke tarawa da Boka kinga kuwa inba sa'a akayi ba shima yana d'auke dashi"

Safiyyah ta zaro ido tace kina nufin Yaya Faisal d'ina?

"Hafsa tace eh shi"

Saurin mayar da tashin hankalinta tayi kafin tayi dariya tace nasan Yaya Faisal d'ina bazai tab'a kamuwa da wannan ciwonba, saboda be ha'inceta ba ballan tana Allah ya saka mata  tanan.

Balkisu tace duk da haka kuje kuyi text Safiyyah hankalinku zaifi kwanciya inba hakaba kullun zaku kasance cikin zargin kuna da ciwon.

Safiyyah tace share kawai dan banji a jikina muna d'auke da komai ba, Sumayyah sai kuka takeyi tanaja musu Allah ya isa, tace Hafsa wlh inhar JININA tana d'auke da wannan ciwon wlh sainayi shari'a daku dan bazan tab'a barinku ba.

Safiyyah tace karki d'aga hankalinki Sumayyah bamu da komai ma, dan bamu zalince suba, ballan tana muyi tsammanin ganin irin wannan saka makon.

Balkisu itama hankalinta ba k'aramun tashi yayiba dajin wannan zancen, shiyasa tanajin tsayuwar motar Faisal yazo d'aukansu ta futa da kanta ta mishi bayani duk abunda Hafsa ta fad'a musu.

Kai ya rik'e yana maimaita innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un! allah kasa ciwon ya sauk'a akaina ni kad'ai karya tab'a 'yar mutane dan sunmun halarci a rayuwa, jeki kiramun Safiyyan mu tafi tashiga jikinta a sanyaye tace Safiyyah wai kuje ku tafi.

Safiyyah tace yaushe yazo kuma bai kira wata ba Sumayyah tashi muje, tunda ta shiga motan ta fahinnci baya cikin walwalansa sai tayi shuru a zuciyarta tace idan munje gida zan lallab'a kayana naji komeye damuwansa, gudu yakeyi kaman zai tashi sama ikon Allah ne kawai yakaisu lab d'in.

Suna isa ya dubi Safiyyah cikin sanyin murya yace muje ciki amiki text!

Bayan shi tabi tana mamakin waya fad'a masa wannan labarin kuma da banso yaji ba, suna shiga ya fad'a musu text d'in da yakeso a mata, ba b'ata lokaci akaja jininta cikin mintunan da baifi arba'in ba result ya futo Faisal hanunsa har b'ari yakeyi wajen bud'e takaddan, ido ya zaro da yaga sakamakon ya dubeta da sauri yace yace......


Mmn Ashfaq

Safiyyah ta zaro ido tace kina nufin Yaya Faisal d'ina?

"Hafsa tace eh shi"

Saurin mayar da tashin hankalinta tayi kafin tayi dariya tace nasan Yaya Faisal d'ina bazai tab'a kamuwa da wannan ciwonba, saboda be ha'inceta ba ballan tana Allah ya saka mata  tanan.

Balkisu tace duk da haka kuje kuyi text Safiyyah hankalinku zaifi kwanciya inba hakaba kullun zaku kasance cikin zargin kuna da ciwon.

Safiyyah tace share kawai dan banji a jikina muna d'auke da komai ba, Sumayyah sai kuka takeyi tanaja musu Allah ya isa, tace Hafsa wlh inhar JININA tana d'auke da wannan ciwon wlh sainayi shari'a daku dan bazan tab'a barinku ba.

Safiyyah tace karki d'aga hankalinki Sumayyah bamu da komai ma, dan bamu zalince suba, ballan tana muyi tsammanin ganin irin wannan saka makon.

Balkisu itama hankalinta ba k'aramun tashi yayiba dajin wannan zancen, shiyasa tanajin tsayuwar motar Faisal yazo d'aukansu ta futa da kanta ta mishi bayani duk abunda Hafsa ta fad'a musu.

Kai ya rik'e yana maimaita innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un! allah kasa ciwon ya sauk'a akaina ni kad'ai karya tab'a 'yar mutane dan sunmun halarci a rayuwa, jeki kiramun Safiyyan mu tafi tashiga jikinta a sanyaye tace Safiyyah wai kuje ku tafi.

Safiyyah tace yaushe yazo kuma bai kira wata ba Sumayyah tashi muje, tunda ta shiga motan ta fahinnci baya cikin walwalansa sai tayi shuru a zuciyarta tace idan munje gida zan lallab'a kayana naji komeye damuwansa, gudu yakeyi kaman zai tashi sama ikon Allah ne kawai yakaisu lab d'in.

Suna isa ya dubi Safiyyah cikin sanyin murya yace muje ciki amiki text!

Bayan shi tabi tana mamakin waya fad'a masa wannan labarin kuma da banso yaji ba, suna shiga ya fad'a musu text d'in da yakeso a mata, ba b'ata lokaci akaja jininta cikin mintunan da baifi arba'in ba result ya futo Faisal hanunsa har b'ari yakeyi wajen bud'e takaddan, ido ya zaro da yaga sakamakon ya dubeta da sauri yace yace......


Mmn Ashfaq
