Shahararrun marubutan nan wanda suka saba nishad'artar da fad'akar daku akoda yaushe *NABEEHA AMINU ( Mmn Ashfaq ) da Aminiyan ta NABEELA AMINU ( 'Yar mutan Dikkawa ) sun tak'ark'are sun zak'ulo muku wani labari mai tab'a zuciyar mai karantawa had'i da tausayi da zallan soyayyah karku bari abaku labari kubibiye littafinmu dan kumore karatunku.*

Bissimillahi Rahmanin Rahim.

🅿️101\102
Yace "Alhamdulillah Safiyyah lafiyanki qalau baki d'auke da ciwon komai Allah nagode ma daka karemun honey na" ya rungumeta yana murna.

Tace "honey mutafi gida haka murnan ya isa" sun juya zasu tafi ma'aikacin wajen yace Alh kai bakayi ba, kuma idan za'ayi aure duka akeyi a tare!

Safiyyah tace wannan kuma ba huruminka bane! Yace Hajiyah ina guje miki abunda zai dawo miki danasani daga baya, kar azo sai bayan aure kigane yana d'auke da k'anjamau.

Tace bakaji abunda nace maka bako! Wani kad'e kawai muke da buk'ata baddashi, kai ko kuturta da hauka da kanjamau yake d'auke dashi inason kayana haka ya kamata kana tsayawa a huruminka taja hanunsa suka bar wajen.

Suna shiga mota Sumayyah tace JININA mai result ya bayar?

Taso ta tsokane ta saita tuna tana d'auke da ciki bai kamata ta zolayeta ba, tace "bamu dashi dukanmu Allah ya karemu"

Dogon ajiyan zuciya ta sauk'e kafin tace Alhamdulillah Allah ka dad'a karemu karabamu da sharrin mutane masu halin irinsu Zainab.

Bayan sun isa gida Faisal ya nemi waje ya zauna yayi zurfi a tunani wanda besan hawaye na zuba a idonsa ba, saida yaji ana goge masa, ya rik'e hanunta yace "Safiyyah kece farin cikin rayuwata  bansan ya zanyi rayuwa idan akace babu keba, rayuwata zata zamo kaman na marayan daya rasa gata tako ina idan akace babu ke a cikinta, dan Allah Safiyyah kizauna dani ko baza muyi sex ba, kizauna nayita kallonki kawai ya isheni."

Tace "ban fahinci inda maganan ka ya dosa ba, badda kai waya isa ya rabamu inba Allah ba, amma ka fahintar dani inda bakin zaran yake!

Saida ya sauk'e nunfashi ya share hawayen daya sake gangaro masa, yace duk wanda yaji abunda Zainab ta jawo mana zaice na rabu dake, dan innaci gaba da zama dake zan iya ha'intan ki, Safiyyah nasan ko ba'a gwada niba nasan ina d'auke da HIV ba yadda za'ayi ace tana dashi niban d'aukaba, ya had'a hanunsa yace kiyi hakuri ki zauna dani a haka dan Allah.

Abun ma dariya ya bata tace "haba honey ina iliminka da wayonka suka tafi, nima da nakasance mace anduba ance banida shi ballan tana ku maza da baku cika d'auka da wuriba, ka dawo hayyacinka kayi tunani mekyau, amma kafin nan tashi muje ayi maka test danka tabbatar da abunda nake nufi"

Baiso suje ba dan bayaso yaje a tabbatar masa da shima yana d'auke da ciwon, ya bitane kawai dan gudun kar tayi fushi, suna zuwa akajs jininsa shima result ya futo babu, bai yarda ba saida sukaje wajajen test yafi biyar duka babu.

Suna komawa gida Safiyyah tace yanzu hankalinka ya kwanta baka d'auke da ciwon komai!

Yace eh to amma ina mamakin ta yadda na tsallake ba tare da nakamu ba, Allah abun godiya ne Allah ka dad'a tsare mana gaba da bayan mu, kinga da inada hakkinta ba makawa saina kamu da ciwon nan!

Safiyyah tace hakane nima ina mamakin ta yadda ka tsallake ciwon nan, duk da son yawan sex dinka, amma yanzu na yarda da maza basu cika d'aukan ciwon nan da wuri ba mune mana bugu d'aya muke kamuwa.

Ido ya zuba mata yana tuno wasu abubuwa yace tabbas sai yanzu kwakwalwana ta fara aiki, ba ta yadda za'ayi nakamu da ciwon nan, tunda mukayi aure sau d'aya na tab'a sex da Zainab shima ranan first night dinmu ne, duk lokacin danayi yunkurin sakeyi dana nufota sainaji abuna ya kwanta kaman tsumma, harna fara tunanin ko bani da lafiyane, amma ina fara nemanki na tabbatar lafiyana qalau, domin duk ranan da mukayi waya saina jik'e, kinga hukunci na ubangiji ko, idan ya kareka ba wani bawan daya isa ya cutar dakai"

Safiyyah tace hakane Allah ya dad'a karemu da sharrin duk wani abunda ya halitta, yanzu ya zakayi da Zainab d'in?

Yace dole zan rabu da ita amma ba yanzu ba, sai tagane kuranta, saina azaftar da ita kaman yadda tamun.

Safiyyah tace "baka tsoran ta sake cutar dakai"

Murmushi yayi kafin yace "dama data cutar dani hakan yana cikin jarabawa nane, da kuma sakaci da addu'a danayi amma yanzu in Allah ya yarda nafi k'arfinta, idan kuma kina tsoron karta saka mun ciwone kicire wannan a ranki dama ba abunda ya had'amu ballan tana yanzu, addu'an kawai nake buk'ata a wajen ki.

Ta d'aga masa kai kawai batace komai ba, ganin hankalinta bai kwanta da maganan saba ya jawota jikin sa yace nasan bakison zamanta tare damu, ki kwantar da hankalinki a satin nan zan saketa hakan ya miki?

Murmushi tayi ta rungumesa a jikinta tana bashi hot romance take sak'onta ya isa dan tayi nasaran sauk'esa a layi suka fara farantawa juna.

Mama yau suka shirya ita da 'ya'yanta duka suka nufi Bauchi zuwan bazata suka musu, dama Mama da sukazo bikin Safiyyah sunzo gidan, shiyasa batasha wahalan gane gidan ba, Sumayyah tana wajen Safiyyah suna hira kawai sukaji sallaman su Mama sukayi tsalle dukansu wajen rungumesu suna murna, Mama ba k'aramun dad'i tajiba ganin yadda 'yarta ta sake da ita, bayan sun huta suka d'unguma zuwa gidan Umma, zuwa yamma suka koma sukabar su Sumayyah da kewan su.

Zainab ganin Faisal bai sake waiwayan taba, yasa suka bud'e rayuwarsu ita da Ibrahim yanzu ya dawo kaman mijinta daidai da abinci a gidan yakeci, ko gidan boka bata samun zuwa yanzu domin yana biya mata buk'atanta yadda takeso.

Lauwani mai shayi yanzu jikinsa Alhamdulillah dan ba inda baya zuwa, yanzu ya koma cin kasuwan k'auye kullun a hanyan awu yake, idan ya saro yazo ya siyar a cikin gari da sauk'i har Allah ya dafa masa ya bud'e shagonsa, ana haka ya samu wata bazaura mai suna Maryam a unguwan su ya aureta, saidai gidanta daban ya sakata, Baba Abu kaman zatayi hauka dataji zaiyi aure, saidai ba yadda ta iya dan duk zagin dazata masa baya kulata, dan ya riga daya saba da halinta.

Yayi sa'a kuwa ya samu mace tagari duk abunda tasan zai faranta masa rae shi takeyi, ladabi da biyayyah kan ba'a magana, yau ya kasa hakuri yace Maryam dama haka aure yake da dad'i! Tunda na aureki haryau bansan wani abu waishi b'acin raiba saidai inba a gidanki nakeba, dayawan mutane suna bani shawara ada akan na sake aure amma sai naga kaman mata dukanku halinku d'ayane, ashe nayi muku mungun fahinta ne, dan Allah Maryam ina rokon alfarman kijawo 'yata Kubra jikinki ko Allah zaisa ta futa zakka a 'yayana, duk nasan halinsu da banbanci da 'yan uwanta, ita bata kaisu rashin kunya ba.

Maryam tace haba Baban Zainab ai 'yarka 'yatace insha Allahu zanja ra'ayinta ta dawo nanda zama, aure ne kawai zai rabamu"

Yayi ta mata godiya tare da tsunduma mata albarka, ta karb'a number Kubra kullun saita kirata tace takirane kawai taji lafiyanta, yau tace mata tazo tana nemanta, batak'i ba tazo amma a tsakar gida ta zauna.

Maryam bata damuba tace dama wasu turare da atamfa nagani sunmun kyau shine na siya miki, bazan kawo kigani idan basu mikiba sai muje tare ki zab'a duk wanda kikeso, ta tab'e baki batace komai ba, ta shiga ta d'auko mata, karb'a Kubra tayi batare data mata godiya ba ta tashi zata futa.

Maryam tace harzaki tafi to Allah ya miki albarka ya tsaremun ke ya baki miji nagari nima nakai 'yata d'aki"

Tsayawa Kubra tayi ta kasa motsi saboda yau taji abunda ba'a tab'a saka mata ba, a zuciyarta tace dama idan akasa wa mutum albarka haka yakeji, mu munsaba kullun saidai Baba Abu ta zunduma mana ashar, tunda ta aifeni ban tab'ajin kalman sannu ba a bakinta ballan tana albarka, take taji son matar Babanta yashiga ranta taji inama tace ta dawo gidanta dazama ko dan tayita saka mata albarka,  ta juyo da idonta da yayi jajazur tace nagode Aunty Maryam zan tafi amma idan kin yarda zanna zuwa kullun ina gaisheki.

Maryam ta matso ta dafata tace Kubra ainan gidanku ne zaki iya zuwa duk lokacin da kikaga dama, kuma kinga yau Babanki ba anan yakeba dazaki taimakeni mukwana tare banason kwana ni kad'e a gida.

Kubra abun nema ya samu tace maizai hana, nafasa tafiyan ma sai jibi zan tafi! Maryam tace to ai Baba Abu batasan kina nanba kar tayita nemanki hankalinta ya tashi yanzu kije ki fad'a mata inyaso saiki dawo.

Kubra tace wad'in ance miki Baba Abu ta damu da d'anta yaje wani waje ya kwana ne, ai idan zan shekara bananan bazai dameta ba itadai burinta mukawo mata kud'i, yanzu ma fad'a mukeyi akan nadena kawo mata kud'i, ni kuma maza sungaji da wayona, sunce kullun sai shafe² na iya bazan basu dama su shigeni ba, suma sun rik'e kud'insu, nima na rik'e abuna, kuma Aunty Maryam wlh inaso nakai budurcina d'akina ne.

Maryam tace subbahanallahi Kubra dan Allah kidawo muzauna tare dukda nasan bazata yarda ba, kice mata  zaki tare d'akin saurayinki ne, inyaso sai Babanki yana bamu kud'i kina kaimata har Allah ya baki miji kiyi aure.

Kubra ta yarda da shawaranta, a hankali ta tattaro kayanta ta dawo gidan da zama suke gudanar da rayuwarsu tare....

Bissimillahi Rahmanin Rahim.

🅿️103\104

Suke gudanar da rayuwarsu Maryam ta zage tana koyar da ita abubuwa da dama, take rayuwar Kubra ya gyaru ta dawo mutumiyar kirki, ta shiga islamiyya a kusa dasu, Lauwani ba k'aramun dad'i yajiba yayita saka musu albarka, ana haka k'anin Maryam ya ganta yace yana sonta Baba Abu dataji tace Allah ya kasheta baza'ayi wannan auren ba, harta gama borinta ba wanda ya kulata, Maryam ita tamata komai na uwa akayi auran, Kubra takai budurcinta yadda taso, shikansa mijin yayi mamakin daya sameta da budurcin ta, ya rungumi matansa da mutunci suna zaman lafiya.

Goggo Larai ganin abincin da zataci yana mata wahala ta lallab'a tayi aurenta daga farko suna zaman lafiya ita da mijinta, saida tafiya tayi tafiya mijin ya fara gasata duk damuna saita mishi noma kafin tasamu abunda zataci ga kullen dayake mata, idan ta fad'a abunda bemasa ba ya mata dukan tsiya haka take rayuwa cikin k'unci da takuri.

Zainab suna tsaka dacin duniyansu tafara ciwo yau gudawa gobe ciwon kai ga wasu manyan k'uraje da suka futo mata a fuska, Ibrahim ya dubeta cikin tausayawa yace ya kamata muje asibiti kiga likita, tace to shiya taimaka mata ta shirya kafin suka tafi asibitin, suna zuwa tama likitan bayanin irin ciwon da takeji ya bata gwaje² taje tayi, sukaje akaja jininta sukace ta dawo bayan awa d'aya da rabi, suka nemi waje suka zauna bayan awa biyu sakamako suka futo dukansu, ta karb'a takaiwa likita.

Ya dubeta yace kinada miji?

Tace "eh ina dashi tare mukazo ma"

Ya buk'aci ganinsa taje ta kirasa suka shigo tare, likita ya dubi Ibrahim yace kaine mijinta?

Ibrahim da gadara yace eh nine lafiya?

Likitan ya dubesa aransa yace daga ganin wa'innan mata da mijin basuda tarbiya, yace inaso kaima kaje a maka test ne, saboda na tabbatar wa'innan k'urajen jikintan be shafeka ba.

Yace to ai normal ne likita saika rubuta mun naje nayi, ya karb'a form d'in sukaje yayi test d'in, kafin ya kawo result wa likitan, ya dubesu yace inaso nayi magana dakai kad'e ita ta tafi gida zakazo kasame ta, yace zaki iya tafiya ganinan dawowa, bayan futanta likita yace kasan ciwon da yake damunku ko?

Ibrahim yace yake damunmu ko yake damunta?

Likita ya nunfasa yace inaso ku d'auki komai a matsayin k'addara kuma kusan cuta ba mutuwa bane, mutane da dama daga zaran ance sunada ciwo saisu saka damuwa a ransu suzaci kaman rayuwarsu ta k'arene, kaga damuwa shi yake kashesu ba ciwon bane.

Ibrahim yace bawai munzo dan kabamu labarin ciwon wasu bane, ko kafad'a mana damuwan da sukesawa a zuciyarsu, na kawo matanane danka dubata kabata maganin dazata warke kawai dan kwana biyu bana samun yadda nakeso a wajenta.

Likita yace tunda kai baka buk'atan shawara ko lallab'i to kaje kuyi duk yadda kukeso, amma kasani kuna d'auke da ciwon da yake karya garkuwan jikin d'an adam wato HIV AIDS, akwai wajajen da gomnati take bada magani kyauta idan kun shirya zaku iya zuwa ayi muku register.

Ibrahim cikinsa ne yafara kid'an k'ululuuuuuu atake zufa ya wankesa ya jik'e kaman wanda yayi wanka a ruwan sama, dukda office d'in akwai sanyin AC amma be hanasa cigaba da zufaba, yace likita wlh ba matana bane matar d'an uwanane, ina zuwa na rage zafine kawai a wajenta, saboda banida kudin aure kuma inada yawan sha'awa shiyasa nake kwana da ita, likita ka taimakeni wlh ko d'a d'aya ban ajiyeba kasake duba saka makon kodai ita kad'ece mai d'auke da ciwon banda ni!

Likita yace yanzu ai kaga illan cin amana ko, dan ga saka makon daya futar maka maikyau, dan haka tashi kafutamun a office kafin  nasaka a futarmun dakai maci amana kawai! tashi yayi yana tafiya yanata surutai kaman sabon mahaukaci, yana isa gidan ya ganta kwance a falo, tana ganinsa ta tashi tace me likitan ya fad'a maka?

Naushi ya fara kaimata a baki kafin ya zare belt din jikinsa ya fara zaneta yana cewa k'araman azzaluma dama kinsan kinada ciwon kanjamau kika barni na rab'eki wlh yau saina kasheki, banga wanda ya isa ya kwaceki a hanuna ba, ta razana dataji yace suna d'auke da k'anjamau tsaban firgita batasan yana dukanta ba sai maimaita kalman kanjamau takeyi a bakinta, shi kuma ganin kaman batajin dukan da yake mata yasa ya d'auko tab'arya ya fara kwad'a mata ata ko ina ajikinta.

Faisal yana zaune a wajen aiki ya d'auko fefa da biro yace yaukan Zainab kwanan auren mu ya k'are, saida ya zayyana duk abunda ta masa a takaddan kafin ya rubuta ni Faisal na saki matana Zainab saki biyu, kafin ya ninke takaddan ya saka a aljihunsa ya futa ya shiga mota ya nufi gidan yanajinsa kaman ya sauk'e nauyin da yake kansa, yana shiga gidan daidai Ibrahim ya sake mata tab'aryan akan daidai gwuwan k'afanta wani wawan ihu tasake wanda yasa Faisal k'arasawa da gudu zuwa cikin gidan, Ibrahim yagani yanata buga mata tab'arya a k'afanta.

Ibrahim yana ganin mutum a tsaye ya d'ago ransa a b'ace yace uban me...maganan sa ne ya mak'ale saka makon ganin Faisal a tsaye a kansu, ya wurgar da tab'aryan yace Yaya Faisal kaga halinda matarnan ta saka mu aciki ko? Kaima nasan yanzu kana d'auke da cutan k'anjamau, tasan tana dashi tabari na rab'eta, kaima ka d'auki bulala mu had'u mu kasheta kawai tanan ne zamu huce takaicin halinda ta sakamu, cewa tayifa nazo na taimaketa kai yanzu baka bata cikekken lokaci tunda kayi aure,  daga taimako shine ta wurgeni da ciwo, yanzu dan Allah irin saka makon daya kamata tamun kenan.

Faisal takaicine da bak'in ciki suka turnik'e masa zuciya, najin irin abunda suke aikatawa yace ina zuwa bazanje mota na d'auko bulalan, yana futa ya kira Fa'izu ya sheda masa halinda ake ciki, Fa'izu ransa ba k'aramun b'aci yayiba yace karka barsu su futa ina zuwa yanzu, bayan minti goma sha biyar motan folisawa suka bayyana a wajen, Faisal yace yawwa Fa'izu ku tattara shegiya maciyiya amana kuje ku d'aureta harta mutu, Fa'izu ya bada umurnin a tafi dasu dukansu, ana zuwa police station bayan angama jin ta bakin Ibrahim, Fa'izu yace Faisal kaima kanada laifi ko kuma nace kaine me babban laifi taya zakabar matanka batare da kana bata kulawa ba, harta kawo wani gardi yana sauk'e mata hakkin daya kamata kaizaka sauk'e shi? Ya fad'a hakane domin so yake Zainab ta fad'a gaskiya da bakinta dan mutanen wajen karsuga kaman yayi sonkai wa shari'an

Faisal yace kafin nafad'a dalilina inaso na sallami Zainab tukunna yasa hanu a aljihu yaciro takarda saida ya gyara wajen saki biyun ya dawo saki uku, yace "Zainab gashi na sakeki saki uku.

Ihu tasaka tana cewa kayi hakuri kamun rae wlh Faisal ina sonka, bazan iya rayuwa babu kaiba, idan ka rabu dani mutuwa zanyi, sharrin shed'anne wlh, shiyayi ta rinjaya ta nayita aikata maka ba daidaiba amma namaka alkawarin gyara halina, tanaso ta rarrafo ta rik'e k'afansa amma ta kasa ko motsa k'afanta, saboda azaban zafin da yake mata.

Fa'izu yace zansa ya dawo dake amma saikin fad'a mana laifin da kika aikata masa dayasa ya sakeki!

Tace indai zai dawo dani wlh zan fad'a, cikin azaban ciwon da jikinta da k'afanta suke mata ta musu bayanin komai tun daga farkon had'uwansu da Faisal har zuwan Ibrahim da yadda sukayita lalata, duk ta zayyane musu.

Faisal yace a haka kike tunanin zan iya zama dake, aini ba k'aramun taimako na kikayi ba da kikaje boka ya baki maganin da bazan iya tarawa dakeba, da bakiyi hakaba danima ina cikin yanayin da kike yanzu, kije bazan ja miki Allah ya isaba amma nasan Allah zai isarmun tunda gashi kinfara gani.

Kowa awajen saida jikinsa yayi sanyi suna mamakin dama akwai mata irin wannan a duniya, Fa'izu ya rasa wani irin hukunci zai yanke musu, kawai saiya yace zai tura case din kotu.

Ibrahim yace yawwa d'an uwana ai abunda ya kamaceta kenan akaita kotu kawai, dan ta ha'ince mu taje alk'ali ya yanke mata hukuncin kisa kawai.

Faisal da Fa'izu atare suka mishi wani mungun kallon da bashiri yayi shuru ya koma gefe yana tsuru² da ido, ganin an tattara Zainab an wurgata sel yasa yace zan iya tafiya ko?

D'aya daga cikin police d'inne ya tusa k'eyansa zuwa ciki sel din shi ma yanaji yana gani aka rufesa gashi ba halin yin magana.....

Bissimillahi Rahmanin Rahim.


🅿️105\106
Fa'izu ya futo rakasa yace "narasa wani irin hukunci zan yanke musu na irin wannan ha'intan da suka mana, shiyasa na yanke hukuncin kaisu kotun musulunci su yanke musu hukunci daidai da laifinsu.

Faisal yace dama inaso mu futo na maka magana ne akan hukuncin daka yanke, anawa shawaran damun barsu da abunda ya samesu kawai, shi kad'ema hukunci ne, suje suji da ciwon da suka jawa kansu inaga hakan yafi ko, mubarsu da Allah shi zai saka mana.

Fa'izu yace ai sun fara ganin sakayya ma tun ba'aje ko'ina ba, cikin tsokana yace "Faisal soyayya ba k'arya bane katuna irin soyayyan da kukayi da Zainab alk'awari bece haka ba, yanzu ka d'auki matanka kayi jinyanta kafin ta koma gidan su, ni anawa ganin dama ka rik'eta haka kawai, idan tasaka maka ciwon ba komai bane duk acikin sone big body nefa taka"

Tunda ya fara magana Faisal ya k'ura masa ido ji yake kaman ya shak'e kanin nasa yayita dukansa amma bazai iya ba, ya bude baki zaiyi magana kenan yawu ya hard'esa ya juya yashiga mota Fa'izu mezaiyi badda dariya harda rike ciki.

Yana ganin Faisal ya shiga mota yasa aka futo masa da Zainab ya sakata a napep, ya biya kud'in, tace Fa'izu kace inna fad'a gaskiya zakasa ya mayar dani.

Wani kallo ya mata wanda saida hanjin cikinta ya kad'a, ba shiri tacewa mai napep din mutafi, har k'ofan gidansu ya kaita tace ya shiga ya kira mata Baba Abu, ya shiga ya kira mata ita, Baba Abu naganinta tasaka ihu tana cewa wani d'an abu kazan ubanne ya dakamun ke, wlh koma waye bazan barshi ba sainaga bayan sa.

Zainab tace Goggo kikaini asibiti karna mutu, keke napep d'in ta shiga aka wuce dasu asibiti, likitoti suna ganinta sukace sai anmata aiki a k'afanta kuma akwai abubuwan dazasu kawo saboda ciwonta.

Baba Abu tace wani irin ciwo kenan, suka bata amsa da kanjamau, saurin mik'ewa tayi a kusa da ita tace kanjamau fa kukace, ai Zainab ki nemi mai jinyanki badai niba, yanzu zanje nasiyar da kayan d'akinki nakawo kud'in aikinki amma nidake sai nesa² wane ni da jinyan mai k'anjamau aiko a lahira muka had'u zan canja hanya, ta futa taje ta d'auko dillale cikin awa uku tagama siyar da komai na Zainab suturanta kawai ta ibo mata, amma daidai da cokali ta siyar ta karb'a kud'in taje asibiti tabiya duk abunda ake buk'ata, akace saita kira namiji yazo ya saka hanu kafin a shiga da ita aikin, futa tayi tanata mita ta nufi shagon Lauwani, tana zuwa ko sallama babu tace kai mijin kantace idan matarka tabaka izini kazo muje kasa hanu ama 'yarka aiki.

Duk da maganan ta ya b'ata masa rae, saiya dake yace wata 'yata kenan nasan Kubrace kawai 'yata kuma yanzu na barota a wajen Mamanta tazo ta wuni mata bayan ita bansan inada wata 'ya mace ba, kuma ki gaggauta barin wajen nan tun kafin nasa mutane su miki ihun b'arauniya.

Ganin duk wajen bamai kallonta da mutumci yasa tajuya tana cewa idan kaga dama kasa amun ihun mayya ba b'arauniya ba kuma ko kak'i kokaso Zainab 'yarkace, tana babban asibiti rai a hanun Allah za'a mata aiki a k'afa idan kaga dama kaje danni ba abunda zaisa naje jinyanta tunda ita ta jawo wa kanta tana word3 inkaga daman zuwa dubata, tana gama fad'an haka tabar wajen gaba d'aya.

Lauwani baiso yajeba amma jin tace tana mawuyacin hali yasa yabada ajiyan shagonsa ya tafi asibitin yana zuwa aka bashi takadda yasa hanu, yace likita kozan iya sanin maiya sameta?

Likitan yace d'azu wata mata takawo mana ita cikin mawuyacin hali wai wanine yazo har gida ya mata duka, muka duba mukaga dole sai anmata aiki, kafin muka basu gwaje² da saka mako yazo muka gane tana d'auke da cuta mai kashe garkuwan jikin d'an Adam wato HIV AIDS kaji abunda yake damun 'yarku.

Lauwani ya mik'e yana furta innalillahi wa'inna ilaihin raju'un, zufa yanata karyo masa likitan ya dafasa yace karka damu insha Allahu za'a mata aiki cikin nasara.

Lauwani ya mishi godiya kafin ya futa a asibitin ko inda take bai duba ba, ya koma shago abunsa yana takaicin irin rayuwar da Baba Abu tasaka 'yayanta aciki, hawaye yaji suna gangaro masa na takaicin rashin tsayawa ya zab'awa 'yayansa uwa tagari.

Baba Abu bata koma asibiti ba sai bayan sallan mangariba tana zuwa tasamu ana turo Zainab daga d'akin aiki kaman mutacciya ihu tasaka tana cewa shikenan sun kashemun 'ya sun cuci rayuwata, wata daga cikin nurse d'in tace kiyi hakuri baiwar Allah 'yarki bata mutuba da ranta zuwa anjima zata farfad'o, da gudu tabi bayansu zuwa d'akin da aka ajiye Zainab d'in, bayan sun kwantar da ita suka futa Baba Abu taje zata tab'ata da sauri ta janye hanunta tana cewa tab'aki ya dawo haramun a wajena domin yanzu jininki gurb'atacce ne.

Saida Fa'izu yasa akama Ibrahim duka kafin ya sakesa ya dawo gida, yana shiga ya wuce d'akin Goggo Larai yaga d'akin wayam ba komai a ciki, ya futo yana mamakin ina taje to, yasan dai bazata koma gidansu da zama ba amma ina zataje?

Futa yayi ya tambayi mak'otansu ko sunsan inda Goggo Larai ta koma, suka bashi amsa da cewa tayi aure.

Yace tayi aure kuma? Wata aura kuma?

Sukace ba zaman gadin rayuwarku mukeyiba sai kaje ka tambayi wasu, ya futa yana mamakin auran Goggon, da kanshi ya had'a wani guntun icen daya gani a d'akinta  ya dafa ruwa mai zafi ya gasa jikinsa.

Koda Fa'izu yaba Umma labarin abunda Ibrahim yayi ba k'aramun mamaki tajiba tayi Allah wadar dame hali irin na Ibrahim d'in, Faisal yazo ya samesu suna hira ya nemi waje ya zauna yace Umma nazo mu tafine fa dan bazaki zauna da Ibrahim gida d'aya ba, nama 'yan uwan Malam na k'auye magana idan sunason zama a birni suzo munbasu wajen mu, sannan nakira yayanki shima nace masa ya dawo burni zamu bashi gidan da Fa'izu ya siya miki, dan haka tashi mu tafi bazaki d'auki komai ba kibar musu kayan ciki idan sunzo sai suyi amfani dashi.

Umma ba k'aramun dad'i tajiba da abunda yace, tace Faisal ai kabari na iba suturana ko da soran takaddun ku.

Fa'izu yace wlh Faisal kaman ka shiga raina dama inaso idan mun koma gida muyi wannan shawaran dakai, gaskiya naji dad'i da Umma zata koma gidan mu, suka tattara duk abunda takeso suka kulle d'akin Umma ta shiga mak'ota ta musu sallama duka eriyan wajen suka futo suna mata fatan alkairi, sannan suka mata alk'awari gobe zasuzo suga wajen data koma tunda sunsan gidan, suka rabu suna kewan juna.

Suna shiga gidan tun kafin suyi sallama Safiyyah ta futo da gudu kaiba d'an kwali tana ganin Umma ta rik'e hanunta tana cewa Umma bata da lafiya wani abu takeyi kaman zata mutu!

Faisal yace waye?
Ta bashi amsa da Sumayyah da gudu Fa'izu ya shige cikin gidan sai gashi ya futo da ita a hanu yana cewa Faisal juya mukaita asibiti suka shiga motan, Faisal dukda yana cikin rud'u amma yaji haushin futowan da Safiyyah tayi haka ya d'aure fuskan shi yace ance miki asibitin mahaukata zamuje da zaki bimu haka ki futa kije ki jira gida duk yadda ake ciki zan kiraki, ganin ba wasa a fuskansa yasa ta futa tana matsan kwalla kafin yaja motan suka tafi, suna isa asibiti akace musu aihuwane sukawo kayan aihuwan.

Faisal yace kuyi hakuri wlh bamusan aihuwa bane amma bazanje na d'auko yanzu, ya futa da sauri yana isowa gida yaga Safiyyah a inda suka barta tanata kuka tana ganinshi taje da gudu tana cewa ina take ta mutu ko?

Sai ta bashi tausayi yaji beji dad'in abunda ya fad'a mata ba ya rungumeta yace tana raye ai Sumayyah bazata iya mutuwa ta barki ba, na barta a asibiti ance aihuwa zatayi yan zuma nazo d'aukan kayan aihuwan ne"

Take ta nemi kukan ta rasa ta buga tsalle tace Sumayyah tace zata aihu Allah nagode ma yau zanga jinin Sumayyah na ta marerece fuska tace dan Allah honey ka tafi dani!

Yace jeki ibo kayan aihuwan ta mu tafi to"

Da gudu taje ta d'auko akwatin da suka had'a kayan aihuwan tazo ta bashi kafin ta shiga kitchen ta juyo egg meat din da sukayi kafin ta fara nak'udan da plast din ruwan zafi da kayan tea ta zuba a basket tasaka k'atan hijabi ta futo tace muje honey"

Yace meye a hanunki kuma?

Tace "abinci ne da kayan tea inaso tana aihuwa taci"  yayi murmushi a zuciyar sa yanajin dad'in yadda sukebawa junansu kulawa ta musamman.

Isansu asibiti yayi daidai da aihuwanta nurse ta futo da sauri tace ina 'yan uwan Sumayyah Yusuf?

Umma tace gamunan" tace kubani kayan aihuwan ta Safiyyah ta mik'a mata akwatin ta karb'a zata shiga Safiyyah tace nurse ta aihune? Bata juyoba amma tace mata eh ta aihu yanzu.

Dukansu sukace alhamdulillah.......

🅿️107
Safiyyah ta rungume Umma tana murna, ta d'auko wayanta ta kira Mama tana d'agawa tace Mama albishirinki Sumayyah na ta aihu yanzun nan bata jira jin mai Mama zatace ba ta kashe wayan duk wanda take da number sa saida ta kirasa ta fad'a masa Sumayyah ta aihu kuma tana fad'an haka take kashe wayan, bayan awa d'aya Sumayyah ta futo daga labour room da Baby a hanunta, da gudu Safiyyah taje ta rungume su dukansu tana cewa Alhamdulillah Allah nagode ma daka futomun da ita lafiya kafin ta saketa ta karb'a Babyn tana cewa Allah yasa namijine asa masa sunan Faisal d'ina!

Muryan nurse taji ta bayanta tana cewa to baki canka daidai ba Baby girl kuka samu, rungume yarinyan tasakeyi tace kice Umman mu kika aifa mana, Faisal yayi dariya yace to Maman Baby abamu aronta muma muganta, tayi dariya tace Umma ga takwaranki minti biyu nabaku kubani 'yata, nurse d'in haka kawai taji Safiyyah ta burgeta tace to mutafi d'akin da Maman zata huta kafin ku tafi gida, suka shiga Safiyyah hankalinta yanakan 'yarta Sumayyah tace JININA kinyi 'ya harkin manta dani ko?

Ta matso kusa da ita tace ashe zan iya mantawa da kaina indai har zan iya mantawa dake kinsan d'ane dad'i garesa shiyasa, taje mota ta d'auko basket din data zo dashi ta had'a mata tea mai kyauri ta zuba mata egg meat din tace maza cinye wannan kibani plate da kofin na ajiye!

Sumayyah ta turo baki tace JININA banajin yunwa fa? Safiyyah tace wannan ne kuma baki isaba dole kici domin 'yata tasamu ruwan nonon dazata sha, haka ta karb'a tana tura baki hartaci da yawa kafin ta shanye tean, zuwa mangariba suka taho gida, Safiyyah tsaban  d'auki a d'akin Sumayyah ta kwana ba yadda Faisal beyiba akan ta dawo d'aki amma fur tak'i, haka ya kwana shi kad'e, da safe suna zaune dukansu a d'akin Faisal yace kibani yarinyan na mata hud'uba kuma kije yanzu ki had'amun ruwan wanka saiki dawo kiyita ungozoman ki,tunda shi kika zab'a akaina, ta mik'a masa yarinyan ta futa, bayan futan su Umma ta dubesu tace nidai ba sunana nakeso asawa yarinyan nan ba, Fa'izu yace sunan wa kikeso to Umma?

Tace kusa duk sunan da kukeso dan bani na cancanci amun takwara ba, aka tambayi Sumayyah wani suna takeso ta fad'a musu ra'ayinsu yazo daidai da Fa'izu, Faisal ya mata hud'uba ya futa dama Allah Allah yakeyi yaje ya turke Safiyyah a d'aki yana shiga, ita kuma zata futo yace koma inason magana dake ta koma tana zunb'ura baki, ya zauna kusa da ita yace wlh kika kuskura yau kika kwana ad'akin Sumayyah saina miki yajin wata d'aya, kuma gidan zan bari bazaki ganni ba, saikiyi bikinki yadda kikeso ya tashi ya shiga toilet ya fara wanka, tasan tunda ya fad'a haka zai aikata, ta tashi ta shiga toilet d'in ta tub'e sukayi wankan tare, suka futo sai b'ata rai yakeyi tace honey kayi hakuri bazan sakeba, dan Allah kadena fushi dani.

Yace kinsan bazan iya fushi dakeba shiyasa kikemun abunda kikaga dama, jiya ko bacci ban iyaba sai juyi nake tayi a kan gado, kinga gwanda nabar miki gidanki kiyi duk yadda kikeso.

Raurau tayi da idonta tace honey nace kayi hakuri bazan sake ba ta fad'a idonta na cuko da hawaye.

Yace kinsan zuciyata mai raunine akanki komai kikamun bazan iya fushi ba, na yafe miki, ta runumesa tana masa godiya

washe gari zuwa k'arfe goma gidan ya cika da mutane 'yan zuwa barka wasu basusan da aihuwan bama saida sukazo ganin wajen da Umma ta dawo.

Yau suna Safiyyah tana ma jinjira kwalliya 'yan rad'in suna suka shigo sai matan Baffa Tukur tace har an rad'a Allah ya raya Safiyyah da imani yasa cikon addinine Allah yasa tayi halin mai sunanta.

Safiyyah ta kwashe da dariya tace wato danni ce a kusa dake shiyasa kika manta kika kira yarinyan da suna na, ba suna na bane akasa mata sunan Umma ce da itafa.

Umma tayi murmushi tace ba suna na bane da ita Safiyyah ke kika cancanci a miki Takwara bani ba dan haka sunanki ne da yarinyan.

Safiyyah kasa magana tayi sai kallon Umma takeyi da jinjiran, Umma tace sake nunfashinki Sumayyah ita tace sunanki takeso shiyasa nima nagoyi bayanta sannan Fa'izu ma yace shi ma sunan da yakeso kenan.

Safiyyah ta tashi rungume da little Safiyyah ta shiga cikin d'akin inda akema Sumayyah kwalliya tana zuwa ta fad'a jikinta tana kuka tace Sumayyah nayarda ke masoyiya tace ta gaskiya tunda kika iya tsallake sunan kowa kika zab'i kisa suna na, Safiyyah bayan iyayena duk wani maisona a bayanki yake nima Allah ya bani ikon miki abunda zakiji dad'i.

Sumayyah tace aini ba wani abu daya rage zakimun a duniya kinbani farin cikin daba wanda ya tab'a bani irinsa a duniya kinmun abunda uwata ma'aifiya ta kasamun a duniyan nan yanzu har akwai abunda zan miki ki godemun, mai makeup ne ta yanke musu godiyan da sukewa juna tace Maman Safiyyah karki b'ata kwalliyan da muka fara da hawaye kizo mu cigaba!

Safiyyah ta mata murmushi tace aminiyan Safiyyah Maman Safiyyah ban tab'a jin sunan da had'insa yamun dad'i irin wannan ba, sukayi dariya dukansu kafin ta d'auki takwaran ta suka futa, Umma sai tsokanan ta sukeyi da Safiyyah Momin Safiyyah, tayita musu dariya kafin tawuce d'akin ta wajen Faisal.
Yana ganin ta yace Safiyyah Momin Safiyyah tayi dariya tace honey dama kasani kak'i fad'a mun?

Yace eh nasani tunda nina mata hud'uba da sunan danafi k'auna a duniya shiyasa namiki tanadi na musamman domin wannan ranan, ya shiga ya futo da akwati guda biyu da keken zaman yarinyan yace kinga wannan guduma wanki ne na Takwara d'aya na little Safiyyah d'aya kuma na Sumayyah sannan wannan keken tun daga Sudan nasa aka kawo mun guda biyu d'aya na babyn mu da zamu aifa d'aya kuma na little Safiyyah.

Ta rasa da wani baki zata gode masa kawai idanta ya fara tsiyayan kwalla yace bance kimun kukaba, bani yarinyan kije kiyi wanka ki shirya saiki futa da naki gudumawan dan ke nakeso kifara bada guduma kafin kowa yabi bayanki.

Tace nayi wanka saidai na shirya kawai, shiya zab'a mata kayan da zata saka sukayi kwalliya atare kusan shiya mata kwalliyan ma, ba k'aramun kyau tayi ba yace wow sweet honey kinyi kyau koda yake ke me kyaune dama, yace jeki kai gudumawar ki sai kizo ki d'auki 'yarki kinga bacci ma takeyi da halamun ta gaji hakurin mesunan ta.

Taja akwatin takai d'akin Sumayyah ta ajiye a falo kafin taje ta d'auko d'ayan da keken takai tace Umma ga nawa kayan Takwara! Umma tace masha Allah! Allah ya dad'a had'amun kanku.

Sumayyah ta futo taga kayan tace Umma idan nasake aihuwa Safiyyah zanyita sawa domin tayita mun akwatina sukayi dariya, Sumayyah ta bud'e baki zata mata godiya kenan Safiyyah tace ba godiya tsakanin mu saidai addu'a.

Sumayyah tace hakane to kema Allah ya nunamun ranan da zaki aifamun 'yan bakwai na rungumesu a jikina.

Kowa ya amsa da ameen, zuwa k'arfe goma 'yan Azare suka iso harda Inna lokacin da Inna taji jinjiran sunan Safiyyan ta taci ba k'aramun dad'i tajiba ta jinjinawa amintansu, tace to yanzu  da wani suna zakuna kiran 'yarku?.........

🅿️108
Sumayyah tace da JININA zamuna kiranta dan Safiyyah TAFEE JININA ma, kowa ya yaba da jarumtar Sumayyah sannan suka musu addu'an d'aurewan zumuntan su, Inna tace Allah yasa wannan zumantar taku ta zamo silan shiganku Aljanna, Umma suka amsa da ameen, anci ansha anraba jaka mai hoton baby, zuwa yamma taron suna ya watse aka barsu sukad'e a gidan.

Zainab bayan ta farfad'o taga ba kowa akusa da ita ta juya kanta acan nesa ta hango Baba Abu tana zaune ta d'aga mata hanu halamun tazo! Baba Abu tace wa ni kike kira? Ta d'aga mata kai halamun eh"

Tace ina Zainab ai zama kusa da juna ya haramta tsakanin mu kifad'a abunda kike buk'ata zan turo miki da sanda.

Zainab hawaye ne yayita gangarowa a idonta tana mamakin wai yau ita Baba Abu take kyama, ta kawar da kanta tana tuna rayuwarsu ada, da sai abunda tace ake aiwatar wa, wai yau itace a kwance uwarda ta aifeta tana kyamanta, taja majina afili tace rayuwa kenan wannan ma kawai ya isheni ishara Allah kabani lafiya kodan nasamu daman tuba, har wayewan gari idonta be daina zubar da hawaye ba.

Baba Abu iya kacinta da Zainab sannu daga nesa ko kayanta ta b'ata saidai nurse d'in su cire mata kayan su canja mata wani su mik'o mata wanda ta b'ata, wajen wankewa ma saita saka laida yafi goma a hanunta kafin ta wanke kayan, haka sukayi ta rayuwa nesa nesa har tafara koyan tafi cikin ikon Allah aikinta yayi kyau saidai ta dawo kaman mai k'afa d'aya da rabi don cangalal k'afan takeyi, watansu hud'u a asibitin aka sallamesu, bayan sun dawo tace Baba Abu ina Baba Lauwani da Kubra ne?

Saida ta tab'e baki kafin tace a ina zaki gansa yanzu bayan matansa ta killace shi sai abunda tace dashi yakeyi, Kubra kuma ta dawo munafuka ta auri kanin matan Babanku, shiyasa nace bani ba ita.

Zainab tace ba matan shi bane ta killace shi sakayyan abunda muka aikata ne yake bibiyan mu nikam yanzu natuba na koma ga Allah kuma ina addu'an Allah yasa nadace ya karb'a tubana, idan ankwana biyu na huta zannemi gidan naje na nemi gafaran sa, Kubra kuma na mata farin ciki sosai data gane gaskiya tayi auranta, dama tasha fad'amun naguji zuwan wannan ranan nak'i, ina ganin kaman nafi k'arfin namiji ya gujeni nida nakeda boka, namanta akwai wanda yafi bokan komai wanda saida yardan sa komai yake faruwa wato Allah, ta share hawayenta tace Allah natuba ya ubangiji kabani ilimin dazan san hanyan tuba a gareka.

Baba Abu tace aida masallacin saman mu kikaje kike wa'azin dayafi miki kizauna kina mun irin wannan ta tsuniyan kinganni duniya yanzu mukasa k'afan wando da ita kuma duk abunda mukasa a gaban mu saimunyi nasara kije kita tubanki ke kika sani taja gyalenta ta futa tanata zage².

Zainab tabi bayanta da kallo tace naso kiyi nadama kafin nadaman tazo ta riskeki har inda kike, haka sukaci gaba da rayuwa kullun cikin zagin Zainab take, yau da sassafe Zainab ta nemi gidan Baba Lauwani yake ta shiga tana dogara sandan ta shiga, shi kansa daya ganta saida ya zubar da hawaye yace Zainab haka rayuwarki ta dawo meye riban da kika samu a cikinta yanzu.

Cikin kuka tace Baba mudaina tuna baya domin bayida wani amfani, masu hali irin nawa ina musu addu'an Allah ya ganan dasu sugane gaskiya gaskiyace, Baba nazo na nemi gafaran kane dan ka yafemun kuskuran danata aikata maka abaya duk da nasan ban cancanci haka a wajenka ba ta k'arasa maganan tana kuka maicin rai.

Shi kansa kukan yakeso yayi amma ya daure yace karki damu Zainab Allah ma muna masa laifi ya yafe mana ballan tana mu bayinsa, na yafe miki kije kiyita istingifari kina neman gafaran Allah, Maryam ce ta futo da tsohon cikinta tace Baban Kubra bak'uwa mukayine?

Yace eh Zainab ce Yayar Kubra ta dubeta tace Allah sarki bata da lafiya ne dan ba haka nake ganin hotonta a wayan Kubra ba.

Ya nunfasa kafin ya bata labarin da likitoti suka bashi, yace yanzu haka tana d'auke da k'anjamau.

Maryam ta tausaya mata sosai tace karki damu Allah zai saka miki yanzu shi mijin naki bayan ya saka miki ciwo shine ya rabu dake, ba komai ya manta akwai Allah shi zai saka mana.

Zainab bataga amfanin b'oye musuba tace bashi ya sakamun ba asalima shi bayida ciwon komai yanzu haka, nan tabasu labarin tun ranan da Baba Abu ta fara kaita gidan Boka da cigaba datayi da zuwa, duka sun tausaya mata Maryam tace karki damu akwai maganin da akesha mutum ya zauna kaman lafiyayye zan kaiki kifara karb'a dazaran na aihu, saidai kema kiji tsoron Allah karki cutar da wani da ciwon.

Tace insha Allahu Aunty Maryam nayi tuba tuban da bazan sake aikata sab'oba, ki taimake ni na zauna tare dake, Baba Abu ko Baba ya bata abinci siyarwa takeyi kota dafa ita kad'e bata bani.

Maryam tace karki damu ga d'akin da Kubra ta zauna can kema ki ibo kayanki kizauna a d'akin har Allah ya baki miji mai irin cutarki kiyi aure.

Zainab tace dama Kubra anan ta zauna haba shiyasa rayuwarta yayi kyau Aunty Maryam dan Allah kikira mun Kubra mugaisa itama na nemi gafaranta!

Maryam ta d'aga waya takira k'aninta tace dan Allah ya kawo mata Kubra yanzu akwai abunda zasuyi ne, yace to Aunty kinyi sa'a kuwa ina gida gamunan zuwa, suna kashe wayan tace tashi kije d'akinki kihuta kici abinci kafin tazo tace to nagode Aunty Maryam Allah ya rabaki da cikinki lafiya.

Ta tashi ta tafi d'akin da aka nuna mata tana shiga ta kai mata abinci da ruwa, tayita mata godiya a zuciyarta tana cewa inama nima haka na gudanar da rayuwata kaman yadda takeyi nasan da duk wannan abun be faru dani ba, da yanzu ina zaune da Faisal d'ina, Allah sarki Faisal kanuna mun so na gaskiya amma ni na butulce maka Allah yasa matarka ta zamto farin cikin rayuwarka, ta share hawayenta kafin tafara cin abincin dama yunwa takeji.

Lauwani wani irin k'una yakeji a zuciyarsa yace Abu wato saida ta gama tarwatsa mun rayuwar 'ya'ya kafin ta gujesu, ya zamo dole na d'auki mataki akanta, ya tashi yanata safa da marya shi kad'e a d'akin yana tunanin hukuncin dazai yanke mata, futowa yayi ya saka takalminsa ya futa bema kowa magana ba suma basusan futan saba. 

Kubra ta kalki mijinta tace dawa kake waya? Yace da Aunty Maryam ne wai nakawo mata ke yanzu tanaso kuyi wani magana ne.

Kubra ta tashi dakyar dan itama cikinta ya tsufa aihuwa yau ko gobe tace ta aihune ko? Yace wlh nima ban saniba hijabi ta saka tace rik'eni na tashi mu tafi.

Ya d'agota yana mata dariyan k'iban da cikin yasa kata tayi yace harfa hancinki da bakinki sunyi k'iba, ta turo baki tana cewa wlh idan mukaje saina had'aka da Aunty Maryam!

Yace yi hakuri nasan kad'an daga cikin aikinta tace bazata bani keba saikin aihu, suna isa gidan tun daga tsakar gida tace Assalamu Alaikum Aunty kuma Mamana ganinan nazo Allah yasa kin aihune.

Maryam tayi dariya tace na kiraki kizo ki karb'a aihuwan ne, ta nufi d'akin Maryam din, tace mata kizo gani a d'akin ki, ta juyo tana cewa kice yau kinyi kewata kenan tana d'aga labule idonta ya sauk'a akan Zainab gabanta yayi mummunan bugawa kanta taji ya sara mata maranta yayi wani irin murd'aya dakyar ta d'aga k'afanta ta shugo tace Zainab kece kika dawo wani irin halitta da sanda a gefenki, abunka da JININ ka saita fashe da kuka tana cewa Zainab meya faru dake ban saniba, kiyi hakuri nayi watsi dakene dan kink'i bin abunda nake fad'a miki tazo ta zauna kusa da ita ta rik'o hanunta tana wani irin kuka maiban tausayi atake kukan ya juye mata zuwa nak'uda ba shiri suka tafi Asibitin tun kafin su isa suka kira mijinta akan ya kawo musu kayan aihuwa, Zainab itama badda kuka ba abunda takeyi, takai awa hud'u tana nak'uda kafin ta aifo d'a namiji, kowa yana murnan aihuwanta amma ita halin dataga 'yar uwarta aciki yafi d'aga mata hankali, Allah Allah takeyi ta futo ta tambayi Zainab meya faru da ita.

Bayan sun koma gida Zainab tana rab'e a gefe Kubra tace Zainab taso mu shiga ciki suka shiga sukabar mutane a falon, suna shiga Kubra ta sake bud'e shafin wani kukan tana cewa Zainab meya faru dake haka ban saniba ina 'yar uwarki!

Bata b'oye mata komai ba ta sanar da ita, tace Kubra kisha mun fad'a akan nadaina abunda nakeyi natuba na koma ga Allah nak'i kinga rayuwata inda tadawo ko, yanzu banida wata amfani inama mutuwa zata d'aukeni nahuta.

Kubra ta toshe mata baki tana kuka itama tace kidaina fad'an haka Zainab idan kika mutu yanzu mai zakice wa ubangijinki, ki rok'esa ya baki nisan kwana dazakiyi ta bauta masa, suka rungume juna suna kuka Aunty Maryam ce ta shigo tagan su suta kuka tace dama tunda naji kunjima baku futo ba nasan kuna d'aki kunata kuka, kuyi hakuri kuna addu'a domin ba bawan daya isa ya tsallake kaddaran sa, tayita musu nasiha har sukayi shuru kowa ya wanke fuskan sa Zainab tace Kubra zan koma gida sai gobe zan dawo insha Allahu.

Kubra tace gidan Baba Abu zaki koma dukda kyamanki da kikace mun tanayi?

Zainab tace a'a gidan Aunty Maryam zanje danna rok'eta akan nadawo gidanta da zama kuma ta yarda, gashi ita bata kyamata! Kubra tace Aunty Maryam tawuce nan tanada tarbiyyah, bugu da k'ari gata da imani da tausayi mugodewa Allah daya bamu ita a matsayin matan ubanmu kuma ta dawo uwa a garemu.

Aunty Maryam tace kudena yabona haka mana banajin dad'i idan naji kuna fad'an haka, indai kun d'aukeni uwa to kudaina fad'an komai idan na muku dan kun cancanci na mukune.

Kubra tace abun alkairine mutum idan yayi ake saurin fad'a a gabansa ba sharri ba, kinyi kuma dole mu yabeki, fatana Allah ya kawo mana k'anin mu ko k'anwan mu duniya lafiya danni nafison 'ya mace kodan mu k'aru ko Zainab! Itadai Zainab murmushi take musu kawai dan har yanzu batajin walwala a zuciyar ta.

Lauwani yana futa gidan sa ya nufa yana shiga gidan ya tadda labarin mai dad'i a wajen mutanen anguwa, suna ganinsa sukace dama munaso mu kiraka sai mukaji tsoron kar idan kazo a had'a dakai.

Lauwani yace Allah yasa lafiya?

Suka cigaba da cewa lafiya ba lafiya ba wasu yarane sukazo da wuk'aken su wai yaran wajenka sunje sunyi luwad'i da k'annansu yanzu haka yaran suna asibiti tuburansu yana zubar da jini, shine sukazo kaima sai sun maka abunda sukama k'annan su, shine basu same kaba munaji d'aya daga cikinsu yana cewa akanme zasu rama akan ubansu ai gwanda su rama a wajen da zasufi jin zafi, shine suka koma ciki wajen su goma sha bakwai sukama Baba Abu fad'e yanzu haka tana ciki matan mu sunce gabanta yana futar da jini ba inda take tanata cewa akira mata kai.

Lauwani yayi murmushi yace Allah nagodema daka sakamun abunda Abu tama 'ya'yana, itama aka mata ya shiga ciki tanajin muryansa tace Lauwani kaga abunda tsinannun yaran nan suka jamun ko, sunzo sunfi su d'ari uku sukamun fad'e yanzu haka sunmun kaca² da k'asata ta fashe da kuka.

Mutanen wajen sukace Baba Abu ana cikin wani hali ma ana k'arya yaran nan su goma sha bakwaine fa mun k'irga su.

Ga mamakinta sai taji Lauwani ya kwashe da dariya yace bakiga komai bama Abu ina rayuwar nawa 'ya'yan kikeso kiga bayansu to insha Allahu da sannu sauran zasu shiryu kaman yadda Kubra da Zainab suka gane gaskiya, dan haka banga amfaninki a tare damu ba, nabaki awa biyu ki tattara kibarmun gidana na sakeki saki uku, yana gama fad'an haka ya shige d'akin Zainab ya kwaso kayanta duka ya futa yanajin yadda Baba Abu take zaginsa tana tsine masa.

Mutanen anguwa dama sun gaji da halinta sukayi murna sosai da sakin daya mata......


Mmn Ashfaq

Bissimillahi Rahmanin Rahim.

🅿️109
Da kansu suka taro keke napep suka biyasa kafin suka shiga suka tattarota suka saka a napep, kwatancen gidan suka mishi kafin yaja suka tafi, suna ganin keke napep d'in ya tashi sukayi hamdala sukace yau mun rabu da annoba a anguwan mu.

Suna isa gidan mai napep d'in da kansa ya ciccib'eta ya shiga da ita gidan, matan k'annan ta suka futo suna tambayan sa lafiya, accident sukayi ne?

Yace ita ya kamata ku tambaya baniba domin nima bansan komai ba, tai makonta nayi nashigo da ita, bayan futan sa dakyar Baba Abu ta iya musu bayanin abunda ya faru, sukace madalla bake duk abunda 'ya'yanki sukayi shine daidai a wajenki ba yanzuma bakiga komai ba kijira na gaba.

 Duk da tana cikin mawuyacin hali bai hanata tsine musu ba tace kowata shegiya ta b'ace mata akai, suka koma d'akinsu suna mamakin irin taurin kanta.

Koda 'yan uwanta suka dawo, sukaji abunda ya faru da ita sun tausaya mata, suka bata d'aki ta zauna da yake tarbiyyah sun duk iri d'ayane, sai sukayita zagin mutanen da suka mata fad'e da Lauwani.

Lauwani yana zuwa gida ya tadda ba kowa sai yace hala Maryam takai Zainab asibiti ne yaji dad'in yadda take kula da yaransa, shiga d'akin yayi ya kwanta yau ya kasa zuwa kasuwa sai yaran shagonsa sane suka bud'e shagon.

Can yamma Zainab ta dawo gidan ta samesa tace Baba yau baka futa bane? Be b'oye mata abunda ya faruba ya fad'a mata komai, baki ya sake yana kallonta ganin tana dariya harda rik'e ciki shima dariyan yayi cikin dariya yace Zainab Mamanki nefa?

Tace Baba dakaga yadda takejin kyamata sai ka rantse ba ita ta aifeni ba ko wajen dana taka bata bi kullun zagin safe daban na rana daban na dare ma daban, wataran har cikin dare inta farka yin futsari sai tayita zagina banida sake kwata² nida gidan ubana kuma wai da uwata nake rayuwa a ciki, wlh naji dad'in abunda aka mata, Baba saida nace mata ta tuba ta koma ga Allah, tace mun rayuwa kam yanzu ta farasa, bataga komai bama, Lauwani yace Allah ya sauwak'a nagodema Allah daya ganar mun daku ina Auntyn kune?

Tace laa namata ban fad'a maka ba d'azu da Kubra tazo ta ganni shine ganin halin da nake ciki ya d'aga mata hankali wanda yayi sanadiyan d'ago mata nak'uda amma yanzu Alhamdulillah ta aihu ta aifi d'a namiji.

Lauwani ba k'aramun murna yayi ba wai yau shine yayi jika, ranan suna yaron yaci sunan Lauwani ( Muh'd Auwal ) anyi taro lafiya anwatse.

Bayan suna da kwana biyar Maryam itama ta aihu ta aifi yaranta 'yan biyu dukansu mata murna wajen Lauwani ba'acewa komai Kubra burinta ya cika an aifi yara mata kaman yadda takeso bayan anyi suna, Maryam ta raka Zainab karb'an magani, cikin k'ank'anin lokaci maganin ya fara bin jikinta ta murmure tayi kyau kaman ba itaba, yanzu saika lura dakyau zaka gane tana d'ingishi, awajen karb'an maganin ta had'u da wani dattijo yace yana sonta da aure, saidai bazai b'oye mata ba a garin yawon barikinsa ya kwaso ciwo duka matansa biyu sun mutu sun barsa da yara takwas inzata iya auran sa a haka shikenan.

Ba b'ata lokaci ta yarda akayi auransu kuma Alhamdulillah suna zaman lafiya.

Yakubu dan gidan Lauwani ya kamu da ciwon tubura inda tsutsotsi da kashi suke zubowa ta wajen, ganin haka yasa Mubarak da Babawo ba shiri suka daina aikata munanan laifin da suke aikatawa sukaje suka nemi gafaran ma'aifinsu yaji dad'in da komai yake zuwa masa cikin sauk'i yanzu.

Yakubu bai d'auki lokaci yana ciwo ba Allah ya masa rasuwa, sunyi kukan mutuwan sa sosai, daga baya suka dangana.

Baba Abu tunda aka mata fad'e dama matan 'yan uwanta sunce ba ruwansu da ita, abu kaman wasa saita fara futar da ruwa mai wari ta gabanta kowa ya gujeta kota k'ofan d'akinta basubi, k'aninta ya gina mata bayan gida a d'akinta yace karta sake shiga musu bayan gida dan suna gudun kartasa musu cuta, abinci kuma idan ya samu za'ana kawo mata k'ofan d'akinta ta d'auka, rayuwa ta juye mata a lokacin da bata zataba yanzu ta dawo kaman kurma wannan tsiwa da izgilin kwanta² babu, idan kaganta akace maka Baba Abu ne bazaka yarda ba dan yanzu tafi fulawa laushi.

Yau Zainab! Kubra! Babawo da Mubarak suka kawo mata ziyara, shima saida Aunty Maryam ta dage musu da fad'a kafin sukazo, sunji tausayin ta da suka ganta, Zainab tace saida nace ki tuba kafin nadama ya cimmaki kikak'i, yanzu wa gari ya waya, tayita kuka tana neman gafaran su, asibiti suka kaita cikin k'ank'anin lokaci tafara samu sauk'i, kafin suka dawo da ita gidan su, yanzu ba laifi suna kula da ita.

Safiyyah tana kwance a kan gado zazzab'i takeji irin na cikin k'ashin nan ta rufu har kanta Faisal ya shigo ya sameta yace Momin Safiyyah harkin kwanta ne yau"

Tace honey yau tun tashina banajin dad'i saidai bansan me yake damuna ba, ya hauro kan gadon ya jawota jikinsa yace maybe dan kwana biyu ban karb'a hakkina bane shiyasa kike jinki wani irin, amma yau ki shirya biyan bashi yanzu, ya fara shafa jikinta yana mata wani salon wasa me wuyan fassarawa, kasa jure abunda yake mata tayi tafara mayar mishi da martani, sunyi nisa wajen jiyarda juna dad'i kafin suka lula duniyar ma'aurata basu jima da fara sex ba Safiyyah ta rik'e dantsan hanun sa da k'arfi tana juya kai.

Tsayawa yayi da abunda yakeyi yace meya faru sweet honey?

Tace honey idan kana motsi ji nakeyi kaman marata zata fashe kayi hakuri ka sauk'a haka"

Yace bazan iyaba idan na sauk'a zan shiga damuwa kiyi hakuri saura kad'an, idan kuma bakison lafiyata saina sauk'a, juya mishi kai tayi tace dan Allah kamun a hankali, cikin rawan murya yace to, yana ganin yadda take rintse idonta halamun yau tanajin jiki, dazai iya daya barta amma yasan inya kyaleta ba k'aramun cutuwa zaiyi ba.

Bayan komai ya lafa ya rungumota jikinsa yace Safiyyah da akwai abunda yafi honey dad'i da sainace ke kinfisu komai, bansan meye dad'in aureba bansan me akeji akan sex ba sai akanki kullun ina addu'an Allah ya nunamun ranan da zaki bani kyautan farin cikina wato yarinya mai kama dake nima nasaka mata sunanki.

Duk da tana cikin azaban ciwon mara amma saida tace masa nazaci sunan Zainab zakasa wa 'yar da zamu aifa"

Tunda suke bata tab'a ganin b'acin ransa ba sai yau ya tashi zai sauk'a a gadon batare dayace mata komai ba, ganin haka tayi k'ara tareda rik'e cikinta tana cewa wayyo cikina honey zan mutu ka taimake ni!

Ba shiri ya dawo yana cewa maiya sameki inane yake miki ciwo tambayoyi yake watso mata amma bata basa amsan ko d'aya ba ganin haka yasa ya d'auketa cak yaje ya mata wanka kafin ya futo da ita yasaka mata kaya shima yasa, kin motansa ya d'auka ya futa da ita saida ya sakata a mota kafin ya koma ya fad'awa Umma itama ta biyosa suka shiga motan suka tafi Asibitin suna zuwa aka turasu scanning bayan anyi, likita ya bud'e yace congratulation ina tayaka murnan matanka tana da cikin wata uku da sati uku, sannan inaga kana damunta da sex sosai ko kuma kana mata cikin kuzari shiyasa takejin zafi a maranta, so kad'an d'aga mata k'afa kona sati biyu zuwa uku ne zai daina.

Duk bayanin da likitan yakeyi bai fahinci ko kalma d'aya ba, har likitan ya rubuta mata antibiotics yace kuje kusiya wannan maganin tana sha, ita ta karb'a shikam besan abunda ake cewa bama, ya k'ura mata ido kaman wanda za'a kwace masa ita, sai da ta tashi zata futa kafin ya dawo hayyacinsa ya mik'e da sauri zai rungume ta tafuta da sauri tana dariya shima ya mik'awa likitan hanu yabi bayanta da sauri, tana futa tacewa Umma muje sun rubuta magani, sunce ya tsaya zasu masa bayani.

Umma tace sannu Safiyyah Allah ya baki lafiya suka tafi mota suna jiransa, daya futo ya duba ko ina be gansu ba saiya nufi motan yana bud'ewa yaganta ta jingina da jikin kujeran motan idonta a lumshe tana murmushi bataji shigowan saba saiji tayi an rungume ta yana cewa nagode honey Allah ya miki albarka, kingama bani farin ciki a rayuwata ki fad'a duk abunda kikeso zan baki komai girmansa.

Umma tayi gyaran murya kafin tace cikine da Safiyyah?

Ya futo yazo wajen da Umma take itama ya rungume ta yace Umma na Safiyyah tabani farin ciki dayawa a rayuwata, ta jiyar dani dad'in da bansan dashi ba a rayuwata, likita yace mun tana d'auke da cikina harna wajen wata hud'u Umma maizan bata a duniya nasaka mata abunda tamun?

Tace karka damu muje gida tukunna dan maganan sa ba k'aramun kunya ya bata ba, Safiyyah ma ta sunkuyar da kanta, Umma tace sannu Safiyyah Allah ya rabaku lafiya"

Sun shiga gida ta futa da sauri zata tafi d'aki bai gama fakinba ya futo da gudu ya d'agata cak yace ban yafeba idan kika sake sauri irin haka, ko so kike little Safiyyata ta wahala, Umma dai da ido kawai take kallonsu tana jin dad'i irin kulawan da sukeba juna.

Suna shiga d'akin sukaga Sumayyah rungume da little Safiyyah a hanunta tana ganinsu tace subbahanallahi Yaya Faisal meya faru bata da lafiya ne?

Ya ajiyeta akan kujera yace Sumayyah ki kulamun da ita bazanje nasiyo mata magani.

Bayan futansa Umma ta shigo Sumayyah tace "Umma meya sameta, na shigone yau naji shuru batazo tama 'yarta sallama ba, shine nazo na dubata ashe bata da lafiya."

Ita dai Safiyyah ta sunkuyar da kanta, Umma tace eh bata da lafiya yanzu ma daga asibiti muke, ta dafa kan Safiyyah tace sannu Safiyyah Allah ya miki albarka bazanje na kwanta idan kinji wani abu ki kirani a waya kinji?

Ta d'aga mata kai tace nagode, Umma konazo muje mu kwana tarene?

Umma tace rufamun asiri da futunan mijin kinnan, yau inda yake d'aukin nan yazo bai ganki ba inaga kuka zaiyi tamun, bayan futan Umma Sumayyah tace JININA meyake faruwa ne?

Saida ta karb'a little Safiyyah kafin tace Sumayyah Faisal yau ya kunyata ni a gaban Umma wlh har banaso mu had'a ido da ita! Tace meya miki dahar zakiji kunyan Umma.

Tace ko kene aka miki haka nasan wlh sai kinji kaman ki nitse, daga likita yace inada ciki kinga abunda yayita mun wlh ba k'aramun kunya najiba.

Sumayyah ta rungume ta tace dan Allah kina da ciki kai Alhamdulillah gidan mu zai cika da yara Allah yasa ki aifa mun 'yan bakwai

Me kikeso kici yanzu na miki shi?

Safiyyah tayi dariya tace keda Faisal d'aukin ku yayi yawa sai kace akaina aka fara samun ciki, ni kubarni naji da abunda yake damuna.

Sumayyah tace dan Allah JININA ki fad'amun abunda kikeso kici nasan rabanki da abinci tun safe, kitaimake ni kici wani abu, duk yadda taso Safiyyah ta fad'a mata abunda zataci amma tak'i haka ta kyaleta badan taso ba.

Faisal ya shigo da magani dasu kayan kwalama, Sumayyah naganin ya shigo ta karb'a little Safiyyah tamusu sallama.

Ya matso kusa da ita yace sannu sweet honey Allah ya miki albarka kingama mun komai a duniyan nan, washe gari Faisal beje ko inaba yace yau ranan kula da itane, tana kwance a falo yana mata danna sukaji sallama a tsakar gidan Sumayyah ce ta amsa tace sannu da zuwa suka gaisa.

Sumayyah tace saidai bangane kiba wa kike nema?

Ta d'ago idonta ta kalli Sumayyah take hawaye yafara gangarowa a fuskan ta tace dama nazo wajenku ne Faisal yana nan?

Sumayyah ta k'ura mata ido tanaso ta tuna inda tasanta amma ta kasa sai tace barin duba miki, tayi sallamah a k'ofan d'akin tace Yaya Faisal kayi bak'uwa!

Safiyyah tayi saurin bud'e ido tana kallonsa shima ita yake kallo domin yaga kishi k'arara a fuskan ta yayi k'arfin halin cewa kice ta shigo, tajuya tama bak'uwan iso zata juya tace kiyi hakuri mu shiga tare Sumayyah.

Sumayyah mamakine ya kamata jin sunanta a bakin matan sai ta wuce gaba matan ta biyota a baya suka shiga, Faisal da Safiyyah idonsu yanakan k'ofa Safiyyah naganinta ta ganeta sai tayi murmushi ta gyara kwanciyanta a jikin Faisal tace sannu da zuwa big body.

Daga Sumayyah har Faisal maimaita sunan sukayi sai data kira sunanta Faisal ya ganeta ya d'aure fuskansa tamau, Sumayyah tace a ina kika ganta?

Tace gatanan a bayanki ta yunk'ura zata tashi ya mayar da ita yace kinsan banaso kina wahala ko!

Zainab ta tsuguna tana kuka tace nasan ban canci ku yafemun ba saboda namuku abunda ko'a hanya kuka ganni zaku iya k'urmusheni kaman kiyashi, amma ko Allah muna masa laifi kuma mu nemi gafaran sa ya yafe mana, dan Allah kuyi hakuri ku yafemun badan zunu ban dana aikata muku ba ku yafemun domin girman Allah.

Safiyyah tace Zainab kidaina kuka indai mune munjima da yafe miki, duk wanda ya ganki a wannan halin da kike ciki dole ya yafe miki, honey nina ma ya yafe miki Allah ya dad'a shiryar dake.

Sumayyah tace hakane Allah ya yafemu duka, Faisal yace zaki iya tafiya tunda kinji abunda ya kawoki!

Tace nagode Sumayyah idan Fa'izu ya dawo kice shima dan Allah ya yafemun, sai kuma ina neman alfarman ku nunamun inda Umma take naje na nemi gafaranta.

Cikin b'acin rai Faisal yace......

Cikin b'acin rai Faisal yace munce an yafe miki ki futa kibamu waje da Allah, Safiyyah tace haba honey banaso kana irin wannan fushi, idan bazaka iya mata magana me dad'iba kayi shuru kawai, kuma inaso dan Allah kace ka yafe mata da bakinka cikin lafazi mai dad'i bada fad'a ba, yayi shuru yana kallonta ta kawar da fuskanta tace Sumayyah tashi ki rakata d'akin Umma kinji!

Har zasu tashi yace Zainab na yafe miki Allah ya baki lafiya ya kuma dad'a shiryar dake.

Cikin kuka tace nagode Allah ya saka da alkairi ta dubi Safiyyah tace nagode Safiyyah Allah ya baki yara masu tausayi da imani irinki Allah ya rabaki da cikin da yake jikinki lafiya ya d'aukaka zuri'anki yasa kufi k'arfin zuciyar ku da shed'an.

Faisal yaji dad'in addu'an ta ya ciro kud'in dashi kansan besan yawansu ba ya bata tayi ta godiya kafin suka futa.

D'akin Umma suka nufa itama bata ganeta daga farko ba saida Sumayyah ta fad'a mata, cikin kuka ta nemi gafaran ta, itama Umma ta yafe mata dazata tafi ta mata kyautan kud'i da kayan abinci, Sumayyah ma tabata kyautan turmin zannuwa guda uku ta futa tana kuka da takaicin son zuciyar data aikata da yanzu itace a wannan makeke gidan da yanzu itace a kwance a jikin Faisal yana mata tausa, har ta isa gida tana kuka mijinta yace basu yafe miki bako?

Saida taci kuka mai isarta kafin ta masa bayanin yadda sukayi tabashi kud'in data samu a gidan tace gashi kaja jari, yayi murna sosai kafin suka fara lissafin wani irin sana'a zaiyi.

Zainab tunda taje ta nemi gafaran su tajita sakayau kaman an sauk'e mata dutse a kanta, suka cigaba da rayuwarsu itada mijinta da 'yayan sa, cikin ikon Allah ta samu ciki, tayita murna, dawuri suka fara zuwa asibiti domin nemawa d'an cikinta kariya.

Bayan sun gaisa Alh Sulaiman yace ba komai yasa na kira kaba Faisal sai dan inaso muyi magana akan kamfanina, duba da gaskiyan ka da rik'on amanan ka yasa na yanke shawaran sayar maka da kamfanina a farashi mai sauk'i, kuma banaso muyi jayayya dakai Faisal dan inhar ka nunamun k'in amuncewar ka raina zaiyi mungun b'aci, idan ka duba rayuwata Faisal, barin dukiya bata da wani amfani, duk yawancin wanda zasu gajeni sun mutu, kuma Allah be tab'a bani aihuwa ba, idan na mutu nabar dukiya da yawa matana zasuyi ta masifa kan dukiyan, inaso kona mutu nabar musu abunda ko an raba musu ba wanda zatayi ta k'orafi akai, ya d'auko wani file yace duk wani abunda kamfani ta k'umsa yana cikin wannan file d'in zakasa hanu anan, agaban mutane zamu zab'a saidu wanda zasu sheda nasiyar maka da kamfani, amma tsaka nina dakai da ubangijin mu ba siya kayi ba ni Alh Sulaiman na mallaka maka kamfanina kyauta, ban hanaka ka sanarwa iyayenka ba, amma kasani mayarmun da kyautan dana maka zaifi komai k'onamun zuciya, yanzu ka tashi mutafi kamfani kakira k'aninka tunda hukumane muyi komai a gabansa, nima na kira lauyoyina suna jirammu a kamfani.

Fa'izu ya bud'e baki zayi magana Faisal yace kajira nabaku labarin yadda komai ya kasance ko sarkin d'auki, sukayi dariya dukansu, nan Faisal ya zayyane musu duk yadda sukayi da Alh Sulaiman, kabbara sukayita sauk'ewa dukansu sunama ubangijin mu kirari, Safiyyah tace Umma bakici na zama ba tashi kuje akan k'afanku kuma Alh godiya inyaso muma zamuje daga baya.

Sunyi na'am da shawaranta suka tafi gidan Alh Sulaiman, yaji dad'in yadda suka nuna farin cikinsu a fili, sukama juna godiya da fatan gamawa da duniya lafiya, kafin suka dawo gida.

Ta k'araso kusa dashi ta tab'asa yana jinta yak'i bud'e idonsa tace wato bacci kayi kabarni da damuwa a marata kenan, ta tashi ta shige toilet domin tayi wanka kafin ta futo har bacci ya d'aukesa na gaskiya gashi dagashi sai towel itama ta kunce a garin juyin bacci, tana futowa ta tsane ruwan jikinta tazo ta zauna abbakin gado ta kalli abunsa tai mata kallon farilla tace gaskiya Allah yayiwa Faisal d'ina baiwar halitta, takai hannu ta kama, tajita lab'ab'o ba karfi, bata jima da fara tab'a taba saiya farka ya bud'e idon sa kadan yaga itane sannan ya rufe idonsa saita fara wasa da shi a hankali harta kaita bakinta ta fara tsotsa, yaja dogon numfashi, harta tsorata ta zaci ya farkane, sai taga yaci gaba da barcin sa, taci gaba da tsotsa, shiko wani dadi yakeji wanda ya kasa misalta irinsa, nan danan ta miqe tsaye sanb'al, yana son yayi sex amma yanaso yaga gudun ruwanta, sai yaji ta sakesa, harya fara cewa a zuciyar sa karfa yarinyar nan ta tafi ta barni da tsayayyen abu, sai yaga ta cire towel din jikinta, tayi zigidir itama kamar yadda yayi, tazo ta hau kansa ta saita cikin kasanta, wayyo Allah tun kafin ta sauk'ama yaji wani sanyi ya rufe jikin sa, gami da gamshasshen qarfi a jikin abunsa, ta sake turawa ta shiga, da kyar take shiga, ihu take amma tak'i hakura.

Yace aina barki naga iya jarumtar kine, yanzu lokaci nane ya mayar da ita kasan shi ya fara bata hot romance ganin yana abu zafi² tace honey babyn ka fa!

Cikin buk'atuwa yace karki damu itama tana buk'ata na, d'aga k'afanta yayi sama ya saita abunsa...ganin haka yasa najawo musu k'ofa nace baza'ayi wannan rashin imanin a gabana ba, fatana Allah yasa Faisal karya d'ago mata nak'udan daba shiri.....

Bugu da k'ari zakuji yadda FARHANA tayi rayuwa acikin daji tare da sarkin Aljanu kudai kubiyoni domin jin yadda labarin zai kaya.
